WITH down_ports_by_tenant AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, b.HOST AS host, b.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ia.IP_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT inner_cte.tenant_id, COUNT(DISTINCT inner_cte.port_id) AS total_down_ports, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT inner_cte.ip_address) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_ip_allocations FROM inner_cte GROUP BY inner_cte.tenant_id ORDER BY total_down_ports DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10), ipv4_subnets_by_tenant AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.NAME, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY sa.UPDATED_AT NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4) SELECT s.TENANT_ID, COUNT(DISTINCT s.ID) AS total_ipv4_subnets, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT sa.ID) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_any_updated_standard_attribute FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID JOIN inner_cte AS ic ON ic.NAME = s.NAME WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND NOT sa.UPDATED_AT IS NULL GROUP BY s.TENANT_ID ORDER BY total_ipv4_subnets DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10) SELECT d.tenant_id, d.total_down_ports, d.has_ip_allocations, i.total_ipv4_subnets, i.has_any_updated_standard_attribute FROM down_ports_by_tenant AS d JOIN ipv4_subnets_by_tenant AS i ON d.tenant_id = i.tenant_id WHERE (CAST(d.total_down_ports * 1.0 AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(i.total_ipv4_subnets, 0)) > 0.75 ORDER BY (CAST(d.total_down_ports * 1.0 AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(i.total_ipv4_subnets, 0)) DESC NULLS LAST, d.total_down_ports DESC NULLS LAST
WITH sg_admin_up_ports AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.name AS port_name, COUNT(sgpb.security_group_id) AS security_group_binding_count, SUM(CASE WHEN pd.port_id IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END) AS dns_record_count FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id LEFT JOIN PORTDNSES AS pd ON p.id = pd.port_id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.name), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(security_group_binding_count) AS avg_sg_binding_count FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.port_name, i.security_group_binding_count, i.dns_record_count, (i.security_group_binding_count - a.avg_sg_binding_count) AS deviation_from_avg_sg_binding_count FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS a), sg_down_ports_above_avg AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, sgpb.security_group_id, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.security_group_id ORDER BY p.id NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN'), sg_counts AS (SELECT security_group_id, COUNT(*) AS down_ports FROM inner_cte GROUP BY security_group_id) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.mac_address, i.security_group_id, i.window_column, c.down_ports FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN sg_counts AS c ON i.security_group_id = c.security_group_id WHERE c.down_ports > (SELECT AVG(down_ports) FROM sg_counts)) SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT a.port_id) AS admin_up_ports_with_sg_bindings_count, COUNT(DISTINCT d.port_id) AS down_ports_in_security_groups_above_avg_count FROM sg_admin_up_ports AS a CROSS JOIN sg_down_ports_above_avg AS d
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT e.NETWORK_ID, n.NAME, n.STATUS, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP, AVG(n.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (PARTITION BY n.STATUS ORDER BY n.NAME NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM EXTERNALNETWORKS AS e JOIN NETWORKS AS n ON e.NETWORK_ID = n.ID WHERE e.IS_DEFAULT = 0) SELECT inner_cte.NETWORK_ID, inner_cte.NAME, inner_cte.STATUS, inner_cte.ADMIN_STATE_UP, inner_cte.window_column, overall_avg.avg_of_status_avg_admin_state_up FROM inner_cte, (SELECT AVG(status_avg_admin_state_up) AS avg_of_status_avg_admin_state_up FROM (SELECT STATUS, AVG(ADMIN_STATE_UP) AS status_avg_admin_state_up FROM inner_cte GROUP BY STATUS) AS sub) AS overall_avg ORDER BY inner_cte.NAME NULLS FIRST), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT e.NETWORK_ID, n.NAME, n.STATUS, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP, AVG(n.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (PARTITION BY n.STATUS ORDER BY n.NAME NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM EXTERNALNETWORKS AS e JOIN NETWORKS AS n ON e.NETWORK_ID = n.ID WHERE e.IS_DEFAULT = 0), benchmark_cte AS (SELECT AVG(ADMIN_STATE_UP) AS benchmark_admin_state_up FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.NETWORK_ID, i.NAME, i.STATUS, i.ADMIN_STATE_UP, i.window_column, b.benchmark_admin_state_up AS benchmark, CASE WHEN i.ADMIN_STATE_UP > b.benchmark_admin_state_up THEN 'ABOVE_BENCHMARK' WHEN i.ADMIN_STATE_UP < b.benchmark_admin_state_up THEN 'BELOW_BENCHMARK' ELSE 'AT_BENCHMARK' END AS benchmark_category FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN benchmark_cte AS b ORDER BY i.NAME NULLS FIRST) SELECT q1.NETWORK_ID, q1.NAME, q1.STATUS, q1.ADMIN_STATE_UP, q1.window_column AS avg_admin_state_up_within_status_ordered_by_name, q1.avg_of_status_avg_admin_state_up AS overall_avg_of_per_status_avgs, q2.benchmark AS overall_benchmark_admin_state_up, q2.benchmark_category FROM q1 JOIN q2 ON q1.NETWORK_ID = q2.NETWORK_ID ORDER BY q1.NAME NULLS FIRST
WITH DnsOrderRangeTop10 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.name AS subnet_name, MAX(d.ORDER) - MIN(d.ORDER) AS order_range, VAR_SAMP(d.ORDER) AS order_variance FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN DNSNAMESERVERS AS d ON s.id = d.subnet_id WHERE s.ip_version = 4 GROUP BY s.name ORDER BY order_range DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT i.subnet_name, i.order_range, i.order_variance, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT d2.address) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_dns_nameservers FROM inner_cte AS i LEFT JOIN SUBNETS AS s2 ON s2.name = i.subnet_name LEFT JOIN DNSNAMESERVERS AS d2 ON d2.subnet_id = s2.id GROUP BY i.subnet_name, i.order_range, i.order_variance ORDER BY i.order_range DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10), SubnetAllocationPoolMinFirstIp AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.NAME, s.TENANT_ID, s.NETWORK_ID, s.IP_VERSION, s.CIDR, s.GATEWAY_IP, s.ENABLE_DHCP, p.FIRST_IP, p.LAST_IP, MAX(s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (ORDER BY s.ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_standard_attr_id_next2 FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN IPALLOCATIONPOOLS AS p ON s.ID = p.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4) SELECT i.NAME, i.TENANT_ID, i.NETWORK_ID, i.IP_VERSION, i.CIDR, i.GATEWAY_IP, i.ENABLE_DHCP, i.FIRST_IP, i.LAST_IP, i.max_standard_attr_id_next2 FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN SUBNETS AS s2 ON s2.TENANT_ID = i.TENANT_ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONPOOLS AS p2 ON p2.SUBNET_ID = s2.ID WHERE i.FIRST_IP = (SELECT MIN(p3.FIRST_IP) FROM IPALLOCATIONPOOLS AS p3 WHERE p3.SUBNET_ID = s2.ID)) SELECT sap.NAME AS subnet_name, sap.TENANT_ID, sap.NETWORK_ID, sap.IP_VERSION, sap.CIDR, sap.GATEWAY_IP, sap.ENABLE_DHCP, sap.FIRST_IP AS allocation_pool_first_ip, sap.LAST_IP AS allocation_pool_last_ip, sap.max_standard_attr_id_next2, dor.order_range, dor.order_variance, dor.has_dns_nameservers FROM SubnetAllocationPoolMinFirstIp AS sap JOIN DnsOrderRangeTop10 AS dor ON dor.subnet_name = sap.NAME WHERE dor.has_dns_nameservers = 'Yes' ORDER BY dor.order_range DESC NULLS LAST, sap.NAME NULLS FIRST
WITH port_stats AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(port_count) AS avg_port_count FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.port_status, i.device_owner, i.port_count, i.earliest_standard_attr_update, (i.port_count - a.avg_port_count) AS deviation_from_avg_port_count FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS a), active_network_rbac AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.name, n.status, n.admin_state_up, r.action, r.target_tenant, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY n.id ORDER BY n.id NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM networksecuritybindings AS b JOIN networks AS n ON b.network_id = n.id JOIN networkrbacs AS r ON n.id = r.object_id WHERE n.status = 'ACTIVE'), rbac_counts AS (SELECT n.id AS network_id, COUNT(DISTINCT r.target_tenant) AS distinct_target_tenants FROM networksecuritybindings AS b JOIN networks AS n ON b.network_id = n.id JOIN networkrbacs AS r ON n.id = r.object_id WHERE n.status = 'ACTIVE' GROUP BY n.id) SELECT i.name, i.status, i.admin_state_up, i.action, i.target_tenant, c.distinct_target_tenants, i.window_column FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN rbac_counts AS c ON c.network_id = (SELECT n2.id FROM networks AS n2 WHERE n2.name = i.name AND n2.status = i.status AND n2.admin_state_up = i.admin_state_up LIMIT 1)) SELECT anr.name AS network_name, anr.status AS network_status, anr.admin_state_up, anr.action AS rbac_action, anr.target_tenant AS rbac_target_tenant, anr.distinct_target_tenants, anr.window_column, ps.port_status, ps.device_owner, ps.port_count, ps.earliest_standard_attr_update, ps.deviation_from_avg_port_count FROM active_network_rbac AS anr CROSS JOIN port_stats AS ps
WITH DownPortTenantUsage AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, b.HOST AS host, b.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ia.IP_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), TenantDownPorts AS (SELECT tenant_id, COUNT(DISTINCT port_id) AS total_down_ports FROM inner_cte GROUP BY tenant_id), AverageDownPorts AS (SELECT AVG(total_down_ports) AS avg_down_ports FROM TenantDownPorts) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.host, i.vif_type, i.ip_address, i.window_column, tdp.total_down_ports, adp.avg_down_ports AS benchmark, CASE WHEN tdp.total_down_ports > adp.avg_down_ports * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_USER' WHEN tdp.total_down_ports < adp.avg_down_ports * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_USER' ELSE 'AVERAGE_USER' END AS user_category FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN TenantDownPorts AS tdp ON i.tenant_id = tdp.tenant_id CROSS JOIN AverageDownPorts AS adp), DownPortStandardAttrWindow AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(sa.ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sa.RESOURCE_TYPE ORDER BY sa.ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), ResourceTypeAverages AS (SELECT sa.RESOURCE_TYPE, AVG(sa.ID) AS avg_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' GROUP BY sa.RESOURCE_TYPE) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.mac_address, i.window_column FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN PORTS AS p2 ON p2.ID = i.port_id JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa2 ON sa2.ID = p2.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa2.ID > (SELECT avg_standard_attr_id FROM ResourceTypeAverages AS rta WHERE rta.RESOURCE_TYPE = sa2.RESOURCE_TYPE)), CombinedDownPorts AS (SELECT u.port_id, u.tenant_id, u.host, u.vif_type, u.ip_address, u.window_column AS ip_row_number_window, u.total_down_ports, u.benchmark, u.user_category, s.mac_address, s.window_column AS standard_attr_running_sum FROM DownPortTenantUsage AS u JOIN DownPortStandardAttrWindow AS s ON s.port_id = u.port_id AND s.tenant_id = u.tenant_id) SELECT * FROM CombinedDownPorts ORDER BY tenant_id NULLS FIRST, port_id NULLS FIRST
WITH port_level AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(ia.ip_address) AS ip_allocation_count, SUM(CASE WHEN NOT ia.subnet_id IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS subnet_id_rows FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON ia.port_id = p.id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND NOT ia.ip_address IS NULL GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(ip_allocation_count) AS avg_ip_allocation_count FROM inner_cte) SELECT ic.port_id, ic.tenant_id, ic.mac_address, ic.ip_allocation_count, ic.subnet_id_rows, (ic.ip_allocation_count - a.avg_ip_allocation_count) AS deviation_from_avg_ip_allocations FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS a), top_tenants AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(ia.ip_address) AS ip_allocation_count, SUM(CASE WHEN NOT ia.subnet_id IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS subnet_id_rows FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON ia.port_id = p.id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND NOT ia.ip_address IS NULL GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address) SELECT i.tenant_id, COUNT(DISTINCT i.port_id) AS total_ports, SUM(i.ip_allocation_count) AS total_ip_allocations, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT f.id) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_floating_ips FROM inner_cte AS i LEFT JOIN ports AS p ON p.id = i.port_id LEFT JOIN floatingips AS f ON p.id = f.fixed_port_id GROUP BY i.tenant_id ORDER BY total_ip_allocations DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10) SELECT tt.tenant_id, tt.total_ports, tt.total_ip_allocations, tt.has_floating_ips, AVG(pl.deviation_from_avg_ip_allocations) AS avg_port_deviation_from_global_avg_ip_allocations FROM top_tenants AS tt JOIN port_level AS pl ON pl.tenant_id = tt.tenant_id GROUP BY tt.tenant_id, tt.total_ports, tt.total_ip_allocations, tt.has_floating_ips ORDER BY tt.total_ip_allocations DESC NULLS LAST
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, b.HOST AS host, b.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ia.IP_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT inner_cte.port_id, inner_cte.tenant_id, inner_cte.host, inner_cte.vif_type, inner_cte.ip_address, inner_cte.window_column, tenant_counts.total_down_ports, avg_down.avg_down_port_count FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT tenant_id, COUNT(port_id) AS total_down_ports FROM inner_cte GROUP BY tenant_id) AS tenant_counts ON inner_cte.tenant_id = tenant_counts.tenant_id JOIN (SELECT AVG(down_port_count) AS avg_down_port_count FROM (SELECT COUNT(port_id) AS down_port_count FROM inner_cte GROUP BY tenant_id) AS sub) AS avg_down ON 1 = 1), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), SecurityGroupDownCounts AS (SELECT sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID AS security_group_id, COUNT(*) AS down_ports_in_group FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' GROUP BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID), AverageDownCounts AS (SELECT AVG(down_ports_in_group) AS avg_down_ports_in_group FROM SecurityGroupDownCounts) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.mac_address, i.window_column, sgdc.down_ports_in_group, (sgdc.down_ports_in_group - adc.avg_down_ports_in_group) AS deviation_from_avg_down_ports FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb ON sgpb.PORT_ID = i.port_id JOIN SecurityGroupDownCounts AS sgdc ON sgdc.security_group_id = sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID CROSS JOIN AverageDownCounts AS adc) SELECT q1.port_id, q1.tenant_id, q1.host, q1.vif_type, q1.ip_address, q1.window_column AS ip_row_number_window, q1.total_down_ports AS total_down_ports_for_tenant, q1.avg_down_port_count AS avg_down_ports_across_tenants, q2.mac_address, q2.window_column AS running_sum_standard_attr_id_within_security_group, q2.down_ports_in_group, q2.deviation_from_avg_down_ports FROM q1 JOIN q2 ON q1.port_id = q2.port_id AND q1.tenant_id = q2.tenant_id ORDER BY q1.ip_address NULLS FIRST, q1.port_id NULLS FIRST
WITH sg_geom AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, EXP(AVG(LN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID))) AS geometric_mean_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID > 0 GROUP BY s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY geometric_mean_standard_attr_id DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT inner_cte.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, inner_cte.geometric_mean_standard_attr_id, avg_sg.avg_geometric_mean_standard_attr_id FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT AVG(geometric_mean_standard_attr_id) AS avg_geometric_mean_standard_attr_id FROM inner_cte) AS avg_sg ON 1 = 1 ORDER BY inner_cte.geometric_mean_standard_attr_id DESC NULLS LAST), port_aap AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT aap.MAC_ADDRESS AS allowed_mac_address, aap.IP_ADDRESS AS allowed_ip_address, p.ID AS port_id, sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID AS security_group_id, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY p.ID ORDER BY aap.IP_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN ALLOWEDADDRESSPAIRS AS aap ON p.ID = aap.PORT_ID WHERE p.ID = '25d421c1-c76d-4c26-9550-9f871a1d5034' ORDER BY aap.IP_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST) SELECT allowed_mac_address, allowed_ip_address, port_id, security_group_id, window_column FROM inner_cte WHERE window_column > 0 ORDER BY allowed_ip_address NULLS FIRST) SELECT port_aap.allowed_mac_address, port_aap.allowed_ip_address, port_aap.port_id, port_aap.security_group_id, port_aap.window_column AS running_avg_standard_attr_id, sg_geom.geometric_mean_standard_attr_id, sg_geom.avg_geometric_mean_standard_attr_id FROM port_aap JOIN sg_geom ON port_aap.security_group_id = sg_geom.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY port_aap.allowed_ip_address NULLS FIRST
WITH DownPortsWindowed AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(sa.ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sa.RESOURCE_TYPE ORDER BY sa.ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), ResourceTypeAverages AS (SELECT sa.RESOURCE_TYPE, AVG(sa.ID) AS avg_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' GROUP BY sa.RESOURCE_TYPE) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.mac_address, i.window_column FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN PORTS AS p2 ON p2.ID = i.port_id JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa2 ON sa2.ID = p2.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa2.ID > (SELECT avg_standard_attr_id FROM ResourceTypeAverages AS rta WHERE rta.RESOURCE_TYPE = sa2.RESOURCE_TYPE)), TopTenantsByIpAllocations AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(ia.ip_address) AS ip_allocation_count, SUM(CASE WHEN NOT ia.subnet_id IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS subnet_id_rows FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON ia.port_id = p.id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND NOT ia.ip_address IS NULL GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address) SELECT i.tenant_id, COUNT(DISTINCT i.port_id) AS total_ports, SUM(i.ip_allocation_count) AS total_ip_allocations, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT f.id) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_floating_ips FROM inner_cte AS i LEFT JOIN ports AS p ON p.id = i.port_id LEFT JOIN floatingips AS f ON p.id = f.fixed_port_id GROUP BY i.tenant_id ORDER BY total_ip_allocations DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10), TenantDownPortShare AS (SELECT p.tenant_id, COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN p.status = 'DOWN' THEN p.id END) AS down_port_count, COUNT(DISTINCT p.id) AS total_port_count FROM ports AS p GROUP BY p.tenant_id) SELECT t.tenant_id, t.total_ports, t.total_ip_allocations, t.has_floating_ips, COUNT(DISTINCT d.port_id) AS qualifying_down_ports, AVG(d.window_column) AS avg_down_port_running_sum_standard_attr_ids FROM TopTenantsByIpAllocations AS t JOIN TenantDownPortShare AS s ON s.tenant_id = t.tenant_id LEFT JOIN DownPortsWindowed AS d ON d.tenant_id = t.tenant_id WHERE (CAST(s.down_port_count * 1.0 AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(s.total_port_count, 0), 0)) > 0.75 GROUP BY t.tenant_id, t.total_ports, t.total_ip_allocations, t.has_floating_ips ORDER BY t.total_ip_allocations DESC NULLS LAST
WITH down_ports AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, b.HOST AS host, b.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ia.IP_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT inner_cte.port_id, inner_cte.tenant_id, inner_cte.host, inner_cte.vif_type, inner_cte.ip_address, inner_cte.window_column, tenant_counts.total_down_ports, avg_down.avg_down_port_count FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT tenant_id, COUNT(port_id) AS total_down_ports FROM inner_cte GROUP BY tenant_id) AS tenant_counts ON inner_cte.tenant_id = tenant_counts.tenant_id JOIN (SELECT AVG(down_port_count) AS avg_down_port_count FROM (SELECT COUNT(port_id) AS down_port_count FROM inner_cte GROUP BY tenant_id) AS sub) AS avg_down ON 1 = 1), top_tenants_admin_up AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(ia.ip_address) AS ip_allocation_count, SUM(CASE WHEN NOT ia.subnet_id IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS subnet_id_rows FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON ia.port_id = p.id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND NOT ia.ip_address IS NULL GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address) SELECT i.tenant_id, COUNT(DISTINCT i.port_id) AS total_ports, SUM(i.ip_allocation_count) AS total_ip_allocations, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT f.id) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_floating_ips FROM inner_cte AS i LEFT JOIN ports AS p ON p.id = i.port_id LEFT JOIN floatingips AS f ON p.id = f.fixed_port_id GROUP BY i.tenant_id ORDER BY total_ip_allocations DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10) SELECT dp.port_id, dp.tenant_id, dp.host, dp.vif_type, dp.ip_address, dp.window_column, dp.total_down_ports, dp.avg_down_port_count, tt.total_ports AS admin_up_total_ports, tt.total_ip_allocations AS admin_up_total_ip_allocations, tt.has_floating_ips FROM down_ports AS dp JOIN top_tenants_admin_up AS tt ON dp.tenant_id = tt.tenant_id
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.name AS subnet_name, MAX(d.ORDER) - MIN(d.ORDER) AS order_range, VAR_SAMP(d.ORDER) AS order_variance FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN DNSNAMESERVERS AS d ON s.id = d.subnet_id WHERE s.ip_version = 4 GROUP BY s.name ORDER BY order_range DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT subnet_name, order_range, order_variance FROM inner_cte WHERE order_range > (SELECT AVG(order_range) FROM inner_cte) ORDER BY order_range DESC NULLS LAST), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.name AS subnet_name, CAST(STDDEV(d.ORDER) AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(AVG(d.ORDER), 0), 0) AS coefficient_of_variation FROM subnets AS s JOIN dnsnameservers AS d ON d.subnet_id = s.id JOIN ipallocationpools AS p ON p.subnet_id = s.id WHERE s.ip_version = 4 GROUP BY s.name HAVING AVG(d.ORDER) > 0 ORDER BY coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(coefficient_of_variation) AS avg_coefficient_of_variation FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.subnet_name, i.coefficient_of_variation, (i.coefficient_of_variation - a.avg_coefficient_of_variation) AS deviation_from_avg FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS a ORDER BY deviation_from_avg DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT q1.subnet_name, q1.order_range, q1.order_variance, q2.coefficient_of_variation, q2.deviation_from_avg FROM q1 JOIN q2 ON q1.subnet_name = q2.subnet_name ORDER BY q1.order_range DESC NULLS LAST, q2.deviation_from_avg DESC NULLS LAST
WITH down_ports_detail AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, b.HOST AS host, b.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ia.IP_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), TenantDownPorts AS (SELECT tenant_id, COUNT(DISTINCT port_id) AS total_down_ports FROM inner_cte GROUP BY tenant_id), AverageDownPorts AS (SELECT AVG(total_down_ports) AS avg_down_ports FROM TenantDownPorts) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.host, i.vif_type, i.ip_address, i.window_column, tdp.total_down_ports, adp.avg_down_ports AS benchmark, CASE WHEN tdp.total_down_ports > adp.avg_down_ports * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_USER' WHEN tdp.total_down_ports < adp.avg_down_ports * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_USER' ELSE 'AVERAGE_USER' END AS user_category FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN TenantDownPorts AS tdp ON i.tenant_id = tdp.tenant_id CROSS JOIN AverageDownPorts AS adp), top10_tenants_by_ip_len_cv AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.status, CAST(STDDEV(LENGTH(ia.ip_address)) AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(AVG(LENGTH(ia.ip_address)), 0), 0) AS coefficient_of_variation FROM portsecuritybindings AS psb JOIN ports AS p ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON p.id = ia.port_id WHERE psb.port_security_enabled = 1 GROUP BY p.status HAVING AVG(LENGTH(ia.ip_address)) > 0 ORDER BY coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT p.tenant_id, CAST(STDDEV(LENGTH(ia.ip_address)) AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(AVG(LENGTH(ia.ip_address)), 0), 0) AS tenant_coefficient_of_variation, CASE WHEN SUM(CASE WHEN p.status = 'ACTIVE' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_active_ports, CASE WHEN SUM(CASE WHEN p.status = 'DOWN' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_down_ports, CASE WHEN SUM(CASE WHEN p.status = 'BUILD' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_build_ports FROM portsecuritybindings AS psb JOIN ports AS p ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON p.id = ia.port_id JOIN inner_cte AS ic ON ic.status = p.status WHERE psb.port_security_enabled = 1 GROUP BY p.tenant_id HAVING AVG(LENGTH(ia.ip_address)) > 0 ORDER BY tenant_coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10) SELECT t10.tenant_id, t10.tenant_coefficient_of_variation, t10.has_active_ports, t10.has_down_ports, t10.has_build_ports, dpd.port_id, dpd.host, dpd.vif_type, dpd.ip_address, dpd.window_column, dpd.total_down_ports, dpd.benchmark, dpd.user_category FROM top10_tenants_by_ip_len_cv AS t10 JOIN down_ports_detail AS dpd ON dpd.tenant_id = t10.tenant_id ORDER BY t10.tenant_coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST, dpd.ip_address NULLS FIRST
WITH ip4_subnet_attr_rank AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.NAME, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY sa.UPDATED_AT NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4) SELECT i.NAME, i.window_column, sa.RESOURCE_TYPE FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON s.NAME = i.NAME JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID), shared_subnet_rbac_stats AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.NAME AS subnet_name, s.CIDR AS subnet_cidr, n.TARGET_TENANT AS rbac_target_tenant, SUM(s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY s.IP_VERSION ORDER BY s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS n ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared') SELECT i.subnet_name, i.subnet_cidr, i.rbac_target_tenant, i.window_column, tgt_counts.total_shared_subnets, avg_counts.avg_shared_subnets_per_target FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN (SELECT rbac_target_tenant, COUNT(*) AS total_shared_subnets FROM inner_cte GROUP BY rbac_target_tenant) AS tgt_counts ON tgt_counts.rbac_target_tenant = i.rbac_target_tenant, (SELECT AVG(total_shared_subnets) AS avg_shared_subnets_per_target FROM (SELECT rbac_target_tenant, COUNT(*) AS total_shared_subnets FROM inner_cte GROUP BY rbac_target_tenant) AS per_target) AS avg_counts) SELECT s2.subnet_name, s2.subnet_cidr, s2.rbac_target_tenant, s2.window_column AS running_sum_standard_attr_ids_within_ip_version, s2.total_shared_subnets, s2.avg_shared_subnets_per_target, s1.RESOURCE_TYPE AS ip4_standard_attribute_resource_type, s1.window_column AS ip4_dense_rank_by_standardattribute_updated_at FROM shared_subnet_rbac_stats AS s2 JOIN ip4_subnet_attr_rank AS s1 ON s1.NAME = s2.subnet_name JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON s.NAME = s2.subnet_name WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4
WITH sg_ports_detail AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.STATUS AS port_status, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY p.NETWORK_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID = '0008e629-5619-41e0-b86c-4eecfeaacb6c') SELECT inner_cte.port_id, inner_cte.port_status, inner_cte.ip_address, inner_cte.window_column, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, COUNT(p.ID) AS total_ports_for_tenant_in_sg, avg_ports.avg_port_count AS avg_ports_per_tenant_in_sg FROM inner_cte JOIN PORTS AS p ON inner_cte.port_id = p.ID JOIN (SELECT AVG(port_count) AS avg_port_count FROM (SELECT COUNT(p2.ID) AS port_count FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb2 JOIN PORTS AS p2 ON sgpb2.PORT_ID = p2.ID WHERE sgpb2.SECURITY_GROUP_ID = '0008e629-5619-41e0-b86c-4eecfeaacb6c' GROUP BY p2.TENANT_ID) AS sub) AS avg_ports ON 1 = 1 GROUP BY inner_cte.port_id, inner_cte.port_status, inner_cte.ip_address, inner_cte.window_column, p.TENANT_ID, avg_ports.avg_port_count), status_cov AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.status, CAST(STDDEV(p.standard_attr_id) AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(AVG(p.standard_attr_id), 0), 0) AS coefficient_of_variation FROM securitygroupportbindings AS sgpb JOIN ports AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id GROUP BY p.status HAVING AVG(p.standard_attr_id) > 0 ORDER BY coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT inner_cte.status, inner_cte.coefficient_of_variation, avg_cov.avg_coefficient_of_variation FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT AVG(coefficient_of_variation) AS avg_coefficient_of_variation FROM inner_cte) AS avg_cov ON 1 = 1 ORDER BY inner_cte.coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT d.port_id, d.port_status, d.ip_address, d.window_column, d.tenant_id, d.total_ports_for_tenant_in_sg, d.avg_ports_per_tenant_in_sg, c.coefficient_of_variation, c.avg_coefficient_of_variation FROM sg_ports_detail AS d JOIN status_cov AS c ON d.port_status = c.status
WITH subq1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.NAME AS subnet_name, s.CIDR AS subnet_cidr, n.TARGET_TENANT AS rbac_target_tenant, SUM(s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY s.IP_VERSION ORDER BY s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS n ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared') SELECT i.subnet_name, i.subnet_cidr, i.rbac_target_tenant, i.window_column, tgt_counts.total_shared_subnets, avg_counts.avg_shared_subnets_per_target FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN (SELECT rbac_target_tenant, COUNT(*) AS total_shared_subnets FROM inner_cte GROUP BY rbac_target_tenant) AS tgt_counts ON tgt_counts.rbac_target_tenant = i.rbac_target_tenant, (SELECT AVG(total_shared_subnets) AS avg_shared_subnets_per_target FROM (SELECT rbac_target_tenant, COUNT(*) AS total_shared_subnets FROM inner_cte GROUP BY rbac_target_tenant) AS per_target) AS avg_counts), subq2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE') SELECT tenant_id, MAX(standard_attr_updated_at) AS latest_standard_attr_updated_at FROM inner_cte GROUP BY tenant_id) SELECT s1.subnet_name, s1.subnet_cidr, s1.rbac_target_tenant, s1.window_column AS running_sum_standard_attr_ids_within_ip_version, s1.total_shared_subnets, s1.avg_shared_subnets_per_target, s2.latest_standard_attr_updated_at FROM subq1 AS s1 JOIN subq2 AS s2 ON s2.tenant_id = s1.rbac_target_tenant
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT COUNT(*) AS online_haproxy_loadbalancers FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb JOIN PROVIDERRESOURCEASSOCIATIONS AS pra ON lb.ID = pra.RESOURCE_ID WHERE lb.OPERATING_STATUS = 'ONLINE' AND pra.PROVIDER_NAME = 'haproxy') SELECT lb.TENANT_ID, COUNT(DISTINCT lb.ID) AS total_online_haproxy_loadbalancers, inner_cte.online_haproxy_loadbalancers AS overall_online_haproxy_loadbalancers FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb JOIN PROVIDERRESOURCEASSOCIATIONS AS pra ON lb.ID = pra.RESOURCE_ID CROSS JOIN inner_cte WHERE lb.OPERATING_STATUS = 'ONLINE' AND pra.PROVIDER_NAME = 'haproxy' GROUP BY lb.TENANT_ID, inner_cte.online_haproxy_loadbalancers ORDER BY total_online_haproxy_loadbalancers DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT lb.NAME AS loadbalancer_name, lb.VIP_ADDRESS AS vip_address, stats.BYTES_IN AS bytes_in, stats.TOTAL_CONNECTIONS AS total_connections, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY lb.NAME NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCER_STATISTICS AS stats JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb ON stats.LOADBALANCER_ID = lb.ID JOIN PROVIDERRESOURCEASSOCIATIONS AS pra ON lb.ID = pra.RESOURCE_ID WHERE pra.PROVIDER_NAME = 'haproxy') SELECT inner_cte.loadbalancer_name, inner_cte.vip_address, inner_cte.bytes_in, inner_cte.total_connections, inner_cte.window_column, lb.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, tenant_counts.haproxy_lb_count AS tenant_haproxy_loadbalancer_count, avg_counts.avg_haproxy_lb_count AS avg_haproxy_loadbalancers_per_tenant FROM inner_cte JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb ON lb.NAME = inner_cte.loadbalancer_name AND lb.VIP_ADDRESS = inner_cte.vip_address JOIN (SELECT lb2.TENANT_ID, COUNT(lb2.ID) AS haproxy_lb_count FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb2 JOIN PROVIDERRESOURCEASSOCIATIONS AS pra2 ON lb2.ID = pra2.RESOURCE_ID WHERE pra2.PROVIDER_NAME = 'haproxy' GROUP BY lb2.TENANT_ID) AS tenant_counts ON tenant_counts.TENANT_ID = lb.TENANT_ID JOIN (SELECT AVG(sub.haproxy_lb_count) AS avg_haproxy_lb_count FROM (SELECT lb3.TENANT_ID, COUNT(lb3.ID) AS haproxy_lb_count FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb3 JOIN PROVIDERRESOURCEASSOCIATIONS AS pra3 ON lb3.ID = pra3.RESOURCE_ID WHERE pra3.PROVIDER_NAME = 'haproxy' GROUP BY lb3.TENANT_ID) AS sub) AS avg_counts ON 1 = 1) SELECT q1.TENANT_ID, q1.total_online_haproxy_loadbalancers, q1.overall_online_haproxy_loadbalancers, q2.loadbalancer_name, q2.vip_address, q2.bytes_in, q2.total_connections, q2.window_column, q2.tenant_haproxy_loadbalancer_count, q2.avg_haproxy_loadbalancers_per_tenant FROM q1 JOIN q2 ON q2.tenant_id = q1.TENANT_ID ORDER BY q1.total_online_haproxy_loadbalancers DESC NULLS LAST, q2.loadbalancer_name NULLS FIRST
WITH subq1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.status, CAST(STDDEV(p.standard_attr_id) AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(AVG(p.standard_attr_id), 0), 0) AS coefficient_of_variation FROM securitygroupportbindings AS sgpb JOIN ports AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id GROUP BY p.status HAVING AVG(p.standard_attr_id) > 0 ORDER BY coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT i.status, i.coefficient_of_variation, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT f.id) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_floating_ips FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN ports AS p ON p.status = i.status JOIN securitygroupportbindings AS sgpb ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON psb.port_id = p.id LEFT JOIN floatingips AS f ON f.fixed_port_id = p.id GROUP BY i.status, i.coefficient_of_variation ORDER BY i.coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10), subq2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.status, CAST(STDDEV(LENGTH(ia.ip_address)) AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(AVG(LENGTH(ia.ip_address)), 0), 0) AS coefficient_of_variation FROM portsecuritybindings AS psb JOIN ports AS p ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON p.id = ia.port_id WHERE psb.port_security_enabled = 1 GROUP BY p.status HAVING AVG(LENGTH(ia.ip_address)) > 0 ORDER BY coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT p.tenant_id, CAST(STDDEV(LENGTH(ia.ip_address)) AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(AVG(LENGTH(ia.ip_address)), 0), 0) AS tenant_coefficient_of_variation, CASE WHEN SUM(CASE WHEN p.status = 'ACTIVE' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_active_ports, CASE WHEN SUM(CASE WHEN p.status = 'DOWN' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_down_ports, CASE WHEN SUM(CASE WHEN p.status = 'BUILD' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_build_ports FROM portsecuritybindings AS psb JOIN ports AS p ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON p.id = ia.port_id JOIN inner_cte AS ic ON ic.status = p.status WHERE psb.port_security_enabled = 1 GROUP BY p.tenant_id HAVING AVG(LENGTH(ia.ip_address)) > 0 ORDER BY tenant_coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10) SELECT s1.status, s1.coefficient_of_variation, s1.has_floating_ips, COUNT(DISTINCT s2.tenant_id) AS top_tenants_with_ports_in_that_status FROM subq1 AS s1 JOIN ports AS p ON p.status = s1.status JOIN subq2 AS s2 ON s2.tenant_id = p.tenant_id GROUP BY s1.status, s1.coefficient_of_variation, s1.has_floating_ips ORDER BY s1.coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST
WITH down_secure_ports_by_tenant AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT COUNT(*) AS down_ports_with_port_security_enabled FROM PORTS AS p JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND psb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED = 1) SELECT p.tenant_id AS ports_tenant_id, COUNT(p.id) AS total_down_ports_with_port_security_enabled, avg_counts.avg_down_secure_port_count FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id JOIN (SELECT AVG(port_count) AS avg_down_secure_port_count FROM (SELECT p2.tenant_id, COUNT(p2.id) AS port_count FROM ports AS p2 JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb2 ON p2.id = psb2.port_id WHERE p2.status = 'DOWN' AND psb2.port_security_enabled = 1 GROUP BY p2.tenant_id) AS sub) AS avg_counts ON 1 = 1 CROSS JOIN inner_cte WHERE p.status = 'DOWN' AND psb.port_security_enabled = 1 GROUP BY p.tenant_id, avg_counts.avg_down_secure_port_count), sg_admin_up_ports_by_tenant AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.name AS port_name, COUNT(sgpb.security_group_id) AS security_group_binding_count, SUM(CASE WHEN pd.port_id IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END) AS dns_record_count FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id LEFT JOIN PORTDNSES AS pd ON p.id = pd.port_id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.name) SELECT inner_cte.tenant_id, COUNT(inner_cte.port_id) AS total_ports_with_sg_binding_admin_up, SUM(inner_cte.security_group_binding_count) AS total_security_group_bindings, SUM(inner_cte.dns_record_count) AS total_dns_records, avg_ports.avg_port_count AS avg_ports_with_sg_binding_admin_up_per_tenant FROM inner_cte, (SELECT AVG(port_count) AS avg_port_count FROM (SELECT COUNT(port_id) AS port_count FROM inner_cte GROUP BY tenant_id) AS sub) AS avg_ports GROUP BY inner_cte.tenant_id, avg_ports.avg_port_count) SELECT ds.ports_tenant_id AS tenant_id, ds.total_down_ports_with_port_security_enabled, ds.avg_down_secure_port_count, sa.total_ports_with_sg_binding_admin_up, sa.total_security_group_bindings, sa.total_dns_records, sa.avg_ports_with_sg_binding_admin_up_per_tenant FROM down_secure_ports_by_tenant AS ds JOIN sg_admin_up_ports_by_tenant AS sa ON sa.tenant_id = ds.ports_tenant_id
WITH port_sg_dns_deviation AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.name AS port_name, COUNT(sgpb.security_group_id) AS security_group_binding_count, SUM(CASE WHEN pd.port_id IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END) AS dns_record_count FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id LEFT JOIN PORTDNSES AS pd ON p.id = pd.port_id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.name), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(security_group_binding_count) AS avg_sg_binding_count FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.port_name, i.security_group_binding_count, i.dns_record_count, (i.security_group_binding_count - a.avg_sg_binding_count) AS deviation_from_avg_sg_binding_count FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS a), tenant_active_network_latest_update AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE') SELECT tenant_id, MAX(standard_attr_updated_at) AS latest_standard_attr_updated_at FROM inner_cte GROUP BY tenant_id) SELECT psdd.tenant_id, tanlu.latest_standard_attr_updated_at, COUNT(DISTINCT psdd.port_id) AS port_count, AVG(psdd.security_group_binding_count) AS avg_security_group_binding_count_per_port, SUM(psdd.dns_record_count) AS total_dns_record_count_across_ports, AVG(psdd.deviation_from_avg_sg_binding_count) AS avg_deviation_from_avg_sg_binding_count FROM port_sg_dns_deviation AS psdd JOIN tenant_active_network_latest_update AS tanlu ON psdd.tenant_id = tanlu.tenant_id GROUP BY psdd.tenant_id, tanlu.latest_standard_attr_updated_at ORDER BY port_count DESC NULLS LAST
WITH SubnetDetails AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.NAME, s.TENANT_ID, s.NETWORK_ID, s.IP_VERSION, s.CIDR, s.GATEWAY_IP, s.ENABLE_DHCP, p.FIRST_IP, p.LAST_IP, MAX(s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (ORDER BY s.ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_standard_attr_id_next2 FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN IPALLOCATIONPOOLS AS p ON s.ID = p.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4), DhcpAverages AS (SELECT ENABLE_DHCP, AVG(max_standard_attr_id_next2) AS avg_max_next2 FROM inner_cte GROUP BY ENABLE_DHCP) SELECT i.NAME, i.TENANT_ID, i.NETWORK_ID, i.IP_VERSION, i.CIDR, i.GATEWAY_IP, i.ENABLE_DHCP, i.FIRST_IP, i.LAST_IP, i.max_standard_attr_id_next2 FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN DhcpAverages AS d ON i.ENABLE_DHCP = d.ENABLE_DHCP WHERE i.max_standard_attr_id_next2 > d.avg_max_next2), TopSharedNetworks AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.OBJECT_ID AS network_id, COUNT(*) AS subnet_count FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY n.OBJECT_ID ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 3), AvgTop3 AS (SELECT AVG(subnet_count) AS avg_subnet_count FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.network_id, i.subnet_count, COUNT(DISTINCT r.TARGET_TENANT) AS distinct_target_tenants FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r ON r.OBJECT_ID = i.network_id AND r.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY i.network_id, i.subnet_count HAVING i.subnet_count > (SELECT avg_subnet_count FROM AvgTop3) ORDER BY i.subnet_count DESC NULLS LAST), CombinedResults AS (SELECT sd.NETWORK_ID AS network_id, sd.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, sd.NAME AS subnet_name, sd.IP_VERSION AS ip_version, sd.CIDR AS cidr, sd.GATEWAY_IP AS gateway_ip, sd.ENABLE_DHCP AS enable_dhcp, sd.FIRST_IP AS first_ip, sd.LAST_IP AS last_ip, sd.max_standard_attr_id_next2 AS max_standard_attr_id_next2, tsn.subnet_count AS shared_network_subnet_count, tsn.distinct_target_tenants AS shared_network_distinct_target_tenants FROM SubnetDetails AS sd JOIN TopSharedNetworks AS tsn ON tsn.network_id = sd.NETWORK_ID) SELECT * FROM CombinedResults ORDER BY network_id NULLS FIRST, subnet_name NULLS FIRST
WITH HighStdAttrPortsInNetwork AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.STATUS AS port_status, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY p.NETWORK_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID = '0008e629-5619-41e0-b86c-4eecfeaacb6c') SELECT inner_cte.port_id, inner_cte.port_status, inner_cte.ip_address, inner_cte.window_column FROM inner_cte JOIN PORTS AS p2 ON p2.ID = inner_cte.port_id WHERE p2.STANDARD_ATTR_ID > (SELECT AVG(p3.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) FROM PORTS AS p3 WHERE p3.NETWORK_ID = p2.NETWORK_ID)), TenantPortStatsInSG AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.STATUS AS port_status, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY p.NETWORK_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID = '0008e629-5619-41e0-b86c-4eecfeaacb6c') SELECT inner_cte.port_id, inner_cte.port_status, inner_cte.ip_address, inner_cte.window_column, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, COUNT(p.ID) AS total_ports_for_tenant_in_sg, avg_ports.avg_port_count AS avg_ports_per_tenant_in_sg FROM inner_cte JOIN PORTS AS p ON inner_cte.port_id = p.ID JOIN (SELECT AVG(port_count) AS avg_port_count FROM (SELECT COUNT(p2.ID) AS port_count FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb2 JOIN PORTS AS p2 ON sgpb2.PORT_ID = p2.ID WHERE sgpb2.SECURITY_GROUP_ID = '0008e629-5619-41e0-b86c-4eecfeaacb6c' GROUP BY p2.TENANT_ID) AS sub) AS avg_ports ON 1 = 1 GROUP BY inner_cte.port_id, inner_cte.port_status, inner_cte.ip_address, inner_cte.window_column, p.TENANT_ID, avg_ports.avg_port_count) SELECT tps.port_id, tps.port_status, tps.ip_address, tps.window_column, tps.tenant_id, tps.total_ports_for_tenant_in_sg, tps.avg_ports_per_tenant_in_sg FROM TenantPortStatsInSG AS tps WHERE tps.port_id IN (SELECT port_id FROM HighStdAttrPortsInNetwork) ORDER BY tps.tenant_id NULLS FIRST, tps.port_id NULLS FIRST
WITH sg_spread_stats AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, MAX(port_pair_count) - MIN(port_pair_count) AS pair_count_range, VAR_SAMP(port_pair_count) AS pair_count_variance, STDDEV(port_pair_count) AS pair_count_stddev FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, COUNT(*) AS port_pair_count FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ALLOWEDADDRESSPAIRS AS aap ON p.ID = aap.PORT_ID GROUP BY p.ID) AS port_stats ON sgpb.PORT_ID = port_stats.port_id GROUP BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY pair_count_range DESC NULLS LAST), AverageRange AS (SELECT AVG(pair_count_range) AS avg_pair_count_range FROM inner_cte) SELECT ic.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, ic.pair_count_range, ic.pair_count_variance, ic.pair_count_stddev, ar.avg_pair_count_range AS benchmark, CASE WHEN ic.pair_count_range > ar.avg_pair_count_range * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_SPREAD' WHEN ic.pair_count_range < ar.avg_pair_count_range * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_SPREAD' ELSE 'AVERAGE_SPREAD' END AS spread_category FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN AverageRange AS ar), port_25d421c1_allowed_pairs AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT aap.MAC_ADDRESS AS allowed_mac_address, aap.IP_ADDRESS AS allowed_ip_address, p.ID AS port_id, sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID AS security_group_id, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY p.ID ORDER BY aap.IP_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN ALLOWEDADDRESSPAIRS AS aap ON p.ID = aap.PORT_ID WHERE p.ID = '25d421c1-c76d-4c26-9550-9f871a1d5034' ORDER BY aap.IP_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(window_column) AS avg_window_column FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.allowed_mac_address, i.allowed_ip_address, i.port_id, i.security_group_id, i.window_column, (i.window_column - a.avg_window_column) AS deviation_from_avg_window FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS a ORDER BY i.allowed_ip_address NULLS FIRST) SELECT p.allowed_mac_address, p.allowed_ip_address, p.port_id, p.security_group_id, p.window_column, p.deviation_from_avg_window, s.pair_count_range, s.pair_count_variance, s.pair_count_stddev, s.benchmark AS avg_pair_count_range_benchmark, s.spread_category FROM port_25d421c1_allowed_pairs AS p JOIN sg_spread_stats AS s ON s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID = p.security_group_id ORDER BY p.allowed_ip_address NULLS FIRST
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, b.HOST AS host, b.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ia.IP_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), HostCounts AS (SELECT host, COUNT(DISTINCT ip_address) AS down_allocated_ips FROM inner_cte GROUP BY host), AverageHostCounts AS (SELECT AVG(down_allocated_ips) AS avg_down_allocated_ips FROM HostCounts) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.host, i.vif_type, i.ip_address, i.window_column, hc.down_allocated_ips, (hc.down_allocated_ips - ahc.avg_down_allocated_ips) AS deviation_from_avg FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN HostCounts AS hc ON i.host = hc.host CROSS JOIN AverageHostCounts AS ahc), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT COUNT(*) AS down_ports_with_port_security_enabled FROM PORTS AS p JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND psb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED = 1), TenantDownSecurePorts AS (SELECT p.TENANT_ID, COUNT(*) AS down_secure_ports FROM PORTS AS p JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND psb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED = 1 GROUP BY p.TENANT_ID), AverageDownSecurePorts AS (SELECT AVG(down_secure_ports) AS avg_down_secure_ports FROM TenantDownSecurePorts) SELECT tdsp.TENANT_ID, tdsp.down_secure_ports, adsp.avg_down_secure_ports AS benchmark, CASE WHEN tdsp.down_secure_ports > adsp.avg_down_secure_ports * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_USER' WHEN tdsp.down_secure_ports < adsp.avg_down_secure_ports * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_USER' ELSE 'AVERAGE_USER' END AS user_category, inner_cte.down_ports_with_port_security_enabled AS overall_down_secure_ports FROM TenantDownSecurePorts AS tdsp CROSS JOIN AverageDownSecurePorts AS adsp CROSS JOIN inner_cte) SELECT q1.tenant_id, q2.user_category, q2.down_secure_ports, q2.benchmark AS avg_down_secure_ports_benchmark, q2.overall_down_secure_ports, q1.port_id, q1.host, q1.vif_type, q1.ip_address, q1.window_column, q1.down_allocated_ips, q1.deviation_from_avg FROM q1 JOIN q2 ON q1.tenant_id = q2.TENANT_ID ORDER BY q2.user_category NULLS FIRST, q1.tenant_id NULLS FIRST, q1.ip_address NULLS FIRST
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), SecurityGroupDownCounts AS (SELECT sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID AS security_group_id, COUNT(*) AS down_ports_in_group FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' GROUP BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID), AverageDownCounts AS (SELECT AVG(down_ports_in_group) AS avg_down_ports_in_group FROM SecurityGroupDownCounts) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.mac_address, i.window_column, sgdc.down_ports_in_group, (sgdc.down_ports_in_group - adc.avg_down_ports_in_group) AS deviation_from_avg_down_ports FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb ON sgpb.PORT_ID = i.port_id JOIN SecurityGroupDownCounts AS sgdc ON sgdc.security_group_id = sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID CROSS JOIN AverageDownCounts AS adc), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT COUNT(*) AS down_ports_with_port_security_enabled FROM PORTS AS p JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND psb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED = 1), TenantDownSecurePorts AS (SELECT p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, COUNT(*) AS down_secure_ports FROM PORTS AS p JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND psb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED = 1 GROUP BY p.TENANT_ID), AverageTenantCounts AS (SELECT AVG(down_secure_ports) AS avg_down_secure_ports FROM TenantDownSecurePorts) SELECT tdsp.tenant_id, tdsp.down_secure_ports, (tdsp.down_secure_ports - atc.avg_down_secure_ports) AS deviation_from_avg, inner_cte.down_ports_with_port_security_enabled AS total_down_ports_with_port_security_enabled FROM TenantDownSecurePorts AS tdsp CROSS JOIN AverageTenantCounts AS atc CROSS JOIN inner_cte) SELECT q1.port_id, q1.tenant_id, q1.mac_address, q1.window_column, q1.down_ports_in_group, q1.deviation_from_avg_down_ports, q2.down_secure_ports, q2.deviation_from_avg, q2.total_down_ports_with_port_security_enabled FROM q1 JOIN q2 ON q2.tenant_id = q1.tenant_id
WITH SubnetRouteRangeStats AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.NAME AS subnet_name, MAX(route_count) - MIN(route_count) AS route_count_range, VAR_SAMP(route_count) AS route_count_variance, STDDEV(route_count) AS route_count_stddev FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN (SELECT sr.SUBNET_ID, COUNT(*) AS route_count FROM SUBNETROUTES AS sr GROUP BY sr.SUBNET_ID, sr.NEXTHOP) AS route_stats ON s.ID = route_stats.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 GROUP BY s.NAME ORDER BY route_count_range DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT inner_cte.subnet_name, inner_cte.route_count_range, inner_cte.route_count_variance, inner_cte.route_count_stddev, s.tenant_id, CASE WHEN s.enable_dhcp = 1 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS dhcp_enabled FROM inner_cte JOIN subnets AS s ON s.name = inner_cte.subnet_name WHERE s.ip_version = 4 ORDER BY inner_cte.route_count_range DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10), ActiveNetworkRbacAndMtuStats AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.name, n.status, n.admin_state_up, r.action, r.target_tenant, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY n.id ORDER BY n.id NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM networksecuritybindings AS b JOIN networks AS n ON b.network_id = n.id JOIN networkrbacs AS r ON n.id = r.object_id WHERE n.status = 'ACTIVE'), ActiveNetworkMTU AS (SELECT n.id AS network_id, n.mtu FROM networks AS n WHERE n.status = 'ACTIVE'), AverageActiveMTU AS (SELECT AVG(mtu) AS avg_mtu FROM ActiveNetworkMTU) SELECT i.name, i.status, i.admin_state_up, i.action, i.target_tenant, i.window_column, anm.mtu, (anm.mtu - aam.avg_mtu) AS deviation_from_avg_mtu FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN networks AS n ON n.name = i.name AND n.status = i.status AND n.admin_state_up = i.admin_state_up JOIN ActiveNetworkMTU AS anm ON anm.network_id = n.id CROSS JOIN AverageActiveMTU AS aam) SELECT srrs.subnet_name, srrs.route_count_range, srrs.route_count_variance, srrs.route_count_stddev, srrs.tenant_id AS subnet_tenant_id, srrs.dhcp_enabled, anrms.name AS network_name, anrms.status AS network_status, anrms.admin_state_up, anrms.action AS rbac_action, anrms.target_tenant AS rbac_target_tenant, anrms.window_column AS rbac_dense_rank_within_network, anrms.mtu AS network_mtu, anrms.deviation_from_avg_mtu FROM SubnetRouteRangeStats AS srrs JOIN subnets AS s ON s.name = srrs.subnet_name AND s.ip_version = 4 JOIN ActiveNetworkRbacAndMtuStats AS anrms ON anrms.status = 'ACTIVE' JOIN networkrbacs AS r ON r.action = anrms.action AND r.target_tenant = anrms.target_tenant JOIN networks AS n ON n.id = r.object_id AND n.name = anrms.name AND n.status = anrms.status AND n.admin_state_up = anrms.admin_state_up JOIN subnets AS s2 ON s2.network_id = n.id AND s2.ip_version = 4 AND s2.name = srrs.subnet_name ORDER BY srrs.route_count_range DESC NULLS LAST, anrms.deviation_from_avg_mtu DESC NULLS LAST
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.name, n.status, n.admin_state_up, r.action, r.target_tenant, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY n.id ORDER BY n.id NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM (PARTITION BY n.id ORDER BY n.id NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM networksecuritybindings AS b JOIN networks AS n ON b.network_id = n.id JOIN networkrbacs AS r ON n.id = r.object_id WHERE n.status = networkrbacs AS r ON n.id = r.object_id WHERE n.status = 'ACTIVE' GROUP BY n.id) SELECT i.name, i.status, i.admin_state_up, i.action, i.target_tenant, c.distinct_target_tenants, i.window_column FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN rbac_counts AS c ON c.network_id = (SELECT n2.id FROM networks AS n2 WHERE n2.name = i.name AND n2.status = i.status AND n2.admin_state_up = i.admin_state_up LIMIT 1)) SELECT q1.name, q1.status, q1.admin_state_up, q1.action, q1.target_tenant, q2.distinct_target_tenants, q1.window_column FROM q1 JOIN q2 ON q1.name = q2.name AND q1.status = q2.status AND q1.admin_state_up = q2.admin_state_up AND q1.action = q2.action AND q1.target_tenant = q2.target_tenant AND q1.window_column = q2.window_column
WITH down_ports AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.NAME AS port_name, p.NETWORK_ID AS network_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY p.NETWORK_ID ORDER BY p.ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), NetworkDownAllocations AS (SELECT network_id, COUNT(DISTINCT ip_address) AS down_allocated_ips FROM inner_cte GROUP BY network_id), AverageNetworkDownAllocations AS (SELECT AVG(down_allocated_ips) AS avg_down_allocated_ips FROM NetworkDownAllocations) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.port_name, i.network_id, i.mac_address, i.device_owner, i.ip_address, i.window_column, nda.down_allocated_ips, (nda.down_allocated_ips - anda.avg_down_allocated_ips) AS deviation_from_avg FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN NetworkDownAllocations AS nda ON i.network_id = nda.network_id CROSS JOIN AverageNetworkDownAllocations AS anda), active_networks AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE'), AvgMtu AS (SELECT AVG(mtu) AS avg_mtu FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.network_id, i.network_name, i.tenant_id, i.admin_state_up, i.mtu, i.standard_attr_updated_at, i.window_column, a.avg_mtu AS benchmark_avg_mtu, CASE WHEN i.mtu > a.avg_mtu THEN 'ABOVE_AVG_MTU' WHEN i.mtu < a.avg_mtu THEN 'BELOW_AVG_MTU' ELSE 'AT_AVG_MTU' END AS mtu_category FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN AvgMtu AS a) SELECT an.network_id, an.network_name, an.tenant_id AS network_tenant_id, an.admin_state_up, an.mtu, an.standard_attr_updated_at, an.window_column AS network_row_number_within_tenant, an.benchmark_avg_mtu, an.mtu_category, dp.port_id, dp.tenant_id AS port_tenant_id, dp.port_name, dp.mac_address, dp.device_owner, dp.ip_address, dp.window_column AS port_row_number_within_network, dp.down_allocated_ips, dp.deviation_from_avg AS deviation_from_avg_down_allocated_ips FROM active_networks AS an JOIN down_ports AS dp ON dp.network_id = an.network_id
WITH down_ports AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.NAME AS port_name, p.NETWORK_ID AS network_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY p.NETWORK_ID ORDER BY p.ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), NetworkDownAllocations AS (SELECT network_id, COUNT(DISTINCT ip_address) AS down_allocated_ips FROM inner_cte GROUP BY network_id), AverageNetworkDownAllocations AS (SELECT AVG(down_allocated_ips) AS avg_down_allocated_ips FROM NetworkDownAllocations) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.port_name, i.network_id, i.mac_address, i.device_owner, i.ip_address, i.window_column, nda.down_allocated_ips, (nda.down_allocated_ips - anda.avg_down_allocated_ips) AS deviation_from_avg FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN NetworkDownAllocations AS nda ON i.network_id = nda.network_id CROSS JOIN AverageNetworkDownAllocations AS anda), drive59_tenants AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, p.TENANT_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE b.HOST = 'drive-59') SELECT i.TENANT_ID, COUNT(DISTINCT i.PORT_ID) AS total_ports, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT psb.PORT_ID) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_port_security_enabled FROM inner_cte AS i LEFT JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON i.PORT_ID = psb.PORT_ID GROUP BY i.TENANT_ID ORDER BY total_ports DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT dp.port_id, dp.tenant_id, dp.port_name, dp.network_id, dp.mac_address, dp.device_owner, dp.ip_address, dp.window_column, dp.down_allocated_ips, dp.deviation_from_avg, dt.total_ports AS drive59_total_ports_for_tenant, dt.has_port_security_enabled FROM down_ports AS dp JOIN drive59_tenants AS dt ON dp.tenant_id = dt.tenant_id WHERE NOT dp.tenant_id IS NULL AND dt.total_ports >= 1 ORDER BY dt.total_ports DESC NULLS LAST, dp.network_id NULLS FIRST, dp.window_column NULLS FIRST
WITH down_ports_by_network AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.NAME AS port_name, p.NETWORK_ID AS network_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY p.NETWORK_ID ORDER BY p.ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), NetworkDownAllocations AS (SELECT network_id, COUNT(DISTINCT ip_address) AS down_allocated_ips FROM inner_cte GROUP BY network_id), AverageNetworkDownAllocations AS (SELECT AVG(down_allocated_ips) AS avg_down_allocated_ips FROM NetworkDownAllocations) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.port_name, i.network_id, i.mac_address, i.device_owner, i.ip_address, i.window_column, nda.down_allocated_ips, (nda.down_allocated_ips - anda.avg_down_allocated_ips) AS deviation_from_avg FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN NetworkDownAllocations AS nda ON i.network_id = nda.network_id CROSS JOIN AverageNetworkDownAllocations AS anda), top_hosts_by_variance AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT b.HOST, VAR_SAMP(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS standard_attr_id_variance FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND NOT ia.PORT_ID IS NULL GROUP BY b.HOST ORDER BY standard_attr_id_variance DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT i.HOST, i.standard_attr_id_variance, COUNT(DISTINCT p2.ID) AS total_down_ports_with_ip, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT p3.ID) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS tenant_has_any_ports FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b2 ON i.HOST = b2.HOST JOIN PORTS AS p2 ON b2.PORT_ID = p2.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia2 ON p2.ID = ia2.PORT_ID LEFT JOIN PORTS AS p3 ON p3.TENANT_ID = p2.TENANT_ID WHERE p2.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND NOT ia2.PORT_ID IS NULL GROUP BY i.HOST, i.standard_attr_id_variance ORDER BY i.standard_attr_id_variance DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10), down_ports_with_hosts AS (SELECT dpbn.port_id, dpbn.tenant_id, dpbn.port_name, dpbn.network_id, dpbn.mac_address, dpbn.device_owner, dpbn.ip_address, dpbn.window_column, dpbn.down_allocated_ips, dpbn.deviation_from_avg, b.HOST FROM down_ports_by_network AS dpbn JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON b.PORT_ID = dpbn.port_id) SELECT dph.HOST, thbv.standard_attr_id_variance, thbv.total_down_ports_with_ip, thbv.tenant_has_any_ports, dph.port_id, dph.tenant_id, dph.port_name, dph.network_id, dph.mac_address, dph.device_owner, dph.ip_address, dph.window_column, dph.down_allocated_ips, dph.deviation_from_avg FROM down_ports_with_hosts AS dph JOIN top_hosts_by_variance AS thbv ON dph.HOST = thbv.HOST ORDER BY thbv.standard_attr_id_variance DESC NULLS LAST, dph.HOST NULLS FIRST, dph.network_id NULLS FIRST, dph.window_column NULLS FIRST
WITH port_status_owner_stats AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(port_count) AS avg_port_count FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.port_status, i.device_owner, i.port_count, i.earliest_standard_attr_update, (i.port_count - a.avg_port_count) AS deviation_from_avg_port_count FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS a), default_external_networks_by_tenant AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT e.NETWORK_ID, e.IS_DEFAULT, n.TENANT_ID, n.NAME, n.STATUS, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP, n.MTU, n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID, MIN(n.MTU) OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.TENANT_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM EXTERNALNETWORKS AS e JOIN NETWORKS AS n ON e.NETWORK_ID = n.ID WHERE e.IS_DEFAULT = 0) SELECT inner_cte.NETWORK_ID, inner_cte.IS_DEFAULT, inner_cte.TENANT_ID, inner_cte.NAME, inner_cte.STATUS, inner_cte.ADMIN_STATE_UP, inner_cte.MTU, inner_cte.STANDARD_ATTR_ID, inner_cte.window_column, tenant_counts.total_default_external_networks, avg_counts.avg_default_external_networks_per_tenant FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT TENANT_ID, COUNT(NETWORK_ID) AS total_default_external_networks FROM inner_cte GROUP BY TENANT_ID) AS tenant_counts ON inner_cte.TENANT_ID = tenant_counts.TENANT_ID JOIN (SELECT AVG(cnt) AS avg_default_external_networks_per_tenant FROM (SELECT COUNT(NETWORK_ID) AS cnt FROM inner_cte GROUP BY TENANT_ID) AS sub) AS avg_counts ON 1 = 1) SELECT den.TENANT_ID, den.NETWORK_ID, den.IS_DEFAULT, den.NAME AS network_name, den.STATUS AS network_status, den.ADMIN_STATE_UP, den.MTU, den.STANDARD_ATTR_ID AS network_standard_attr_id, den.window_column AS min_mtu_within_tenant, den.total_default_external_networks, den.avg_default_external_networks_per_tenant, pso.port_status, pso.device_owner, pso.port_count, pso.earliest_standard_attr_update, pso.deviation_from_avg_port_count FROM default_external_networks_by_tenant AS den LEFT JOIN PORTS AS p ON p.NETWORK_ID = den.NETWORK_ID LEFT JOIN port_status_owner_stats AS pso ON pso.port_status = p.STATUS AND pso.device_owner = p.DEVICE_OWNER ORDER BY den.TENANT_ID NULLS FIRST, den.NETWORK_ID NULLS FIRST, pso.port_count DESC NULLS LAST
WITH shared_subnets AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.NAME AS subnet_name, s.CIDR AS subnet_cidr, n.TARGET_TENANT AS rbac_target_tenant, SUM(s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY s.IP_VERSION ORDER BY s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS n ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared') SELECT i.subnet_name, i.subnet_cidr, i.rbac_target_tenant, i.window_column, tgt_counts.total_shared_subnets, avg_counts.avg_shared_subnets_per_target FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN (SELECT rbac_target_tenant, COUNT(*) AS total_shared_subnets FROM inner_cte GROUP BY rbac_target_tenant) AS tgt_counts ON tgt_counts.rbac_target_tenant = i.rbac_target_tenant, (SELECT AVG(total_shared_subnets) AS avg_shared_subnets_per_target FROM (SELECT rbac_target_tenant, COUNT(*) AS total_shared_subnets FROM inner_cte GROUP BY rbac_target_tenant) AS per_target) AS avg_counts), active_network_rbac_mtu AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.name, n.status, n.admin_state_up, r.action, r.target_tenant, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY n.id ORDER BY n.id NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM networksecuritybindings AS b JOIN networks AS n ON b.network_id = n.id JOIN networkrbacs AS r ON n.id = r.object_id WHERE n.status = 'ACTIVE'), ActiveNetworkMTU AS (SELECT n.id AS network_id, n.mtu FROM networks AS n WHERE n.status = 'ACTIVE'), AverageActiveMTU AS (SELECT AVG(mtu) AS avg_mtu FROM ActiveNetworkMTU) SELECT i.name, i.status, i.admin_state_up, i.action, i.target_tenant, i.window_column, anm.mtu, (anm.mtu - aam.avg_mtu) AS deviation_from_avg_mtu FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN networks AS n ON n.name = i.name AND n.status = i.status AND n.admin_state_up = i.admin_state_up JOIN ActiveNetworkMTU AS anm ON anm.network_id = n.id CROSS JOIN AverageActiveMTU AS aam), active_shared_networks AS (SELECT n.id AS network_id, n.name AS network_name, n.status AS network_status, n.admin_state_up, r.action AS rbac_action, r.target_tenant AS rbac_target_tenant FROM networks AS n JOIN networkrbacs AS r ON r.object_id = n.id WHERE n.status = 'ACTIVE' AND r.action = 'access_as_shared') SELECT asn.network_name, asn.network_status, asn.admin_state_up, asn.rbac_action, asn.rbac_target_tenant, anrm.window_column AS network_dense_rank_within_id, anrm.mtu, anrm.deviation_from_avg_mtu, ss.subnet_name, ss.subnet_cidr, ss.window_column AS running_sum_subnet_standard_attr_id_within_ip_version, ss.total_shared_subnets, ss.avg_shared_subnets_per_target FROM active_shared_networks AS asn JOIN active_network_rbac_mtu AS anrm ON anrm.name = asn.network_name AND anrm.status = asn.network_status AND anrm.admin_state_up = asn.admin_state_up AND anrm.action = asn.rbac_action AND anrm.target_tenant = asn.rbac_target_tenant JOIN networkrbacs AS r2 ON r2.object_id = asn.network_id AND r2.action = 'access_as_shared' AND r2.target_tenant = asn.rbac_target_tenant JOIN subnets AS s ON s.network_id = asn.network_id JOIN shared_subnets AS ss ON ss.subnet_name = s.name AND ss.subnet_cidr = s.cidr AND ss.rbac_target_tenant = asn.rbac_target_tenant ORDER BY asn.rbac_target_tenant NULLS FIRST, asn.network_name NULLS FIRST, ss.subnet_name NULLS FIRST
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT COUNT(*) AS down_ports_with_port_security_enabled FROM PORTS AS p JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND psb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED = 1), TenantDownSecurePorts AS (SELECT p.TENANT_ID, COUNT(*) AS down_secure_ports FROM PORTS AS p JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND psb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED = 1 GROUP BY p.TENANT_ID), AverageDownSecurePorts AS (SELECT AVG(down_secure_ports) AS avg_down_secure_ports FROM TenantDownSecurePorts) SELECT tdsp.TENANT_ID, tdsp.down_secure_ports, adsp.avg_down_secure_ports AS benchmark, CASE WHEN tdsp.down_secure_ports > adsp.avg_down_secure_ports * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_USER' WHEN tdsp.down_secure_ports < adsp.avg_down_secure_ports * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_USER' ELSE 'AVERAGE_USER' END AS user_category, inner_cte.down_ports_with_port_security_enabled AS overall_down_secure_ports FROM TenantDownSecurePorts AS tdsp CROSS JOIN AverageDownSecurePorts AS adsp CROSS JOIN inner_cte), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, p.TENANT_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE b.HOST = 'drive-59') SELECT i.TENANT_ID, COUNT(DISTINCT i.PORT_ID) AS total_ports, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT psb.PORT_ID) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_port_security_enabled FROM inner_cte AS i LEFT JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON i.PORT_ID = psb.PORT_ID GROUP BY i.TENANT_ID ORDER BY total_ports DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT q2.TENANT_ID) AS tenants_on_drive_59, COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN q1.user_category = 'HIGH_USER' THEN q2.TENANT_ID END) AS high_user_tenants_on_drive_59, COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN q2.has_port_security_enabled = 'Yes' THEN q2.TENANT_ID END) AS tenants_on_drive_59_with_port_security_enabled, MAX(q1.overall_down_secure_ports) AS overall_down_secure_ports FROM q2 JOIN q1 ON q2.TENANT_ID = q1.TENANT_ID
WITH sg_dns_stats AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.name AS port_name, COUNT(sgpb.security_group_id) AS security_group_binding_count, SUM(CASE WHEN pd.port_id IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END) AS dns_record_count FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id LEFT JOIN PORTDNSES AS pd ON p.id = pd.port_id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.name), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(security_group_binding_count) AS avg_sg_binding_count FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.port_name, i.security_group_binding_count, i.dns_record_count, (i.security_group_binding_count - a.avg_sg_binding_count) AS deviation_from_avg_sg_binding_count FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS a), down_port_details AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, b.HOST AS host, b.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ia.IP_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT inner_cte.port_id, inner_cte.tenant_id, inner_cte.host, inner_cte.vif_type, inner_cte.ip_address, inner_cte.window_column, tenant_counts.total_down_ports, avg_down.avg_down_port_count FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT tenant_id, COUNT(port_id) AS total_down_ports FROM inner_cte GROUP BY tenant_id) AS tenant_counts ON inner_cte.tenant_id = tenant_counts.tenant_id JOIN (SELECT AVG(down_port_count) AS avg_down_port_count FROM (SELECT COUNT(port_id) AS down_port_count FROM inner_cte GROUP BY tenant_id) AS sub) AS avg_down ON 1 = 1) SELECT d.port_id, d.tenant_id, s.port_name, d.host, d.vif_type, d.ip_address, d.window_column, s.security_group_binding_count, s.dns_record_count, s.deviation_from_avg_sg_binding_count, d.total_down_ports, d.avg_down_port_count FROM down_port_details AS d JOIN sg_dns_stats AS s ON d.port_id = s.port_id WHERE s.tenant_id = d.tenant_id
WITH PortStatusVariance AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.STATUS, VAR_SAMP(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS standard_attr_id_variance FROM PORTS AS p JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE NOT p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID IS NULL GROUP BY p.STATUS ORDER BY standard_attr_id_variance DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT i.STATUS, i.standard_attr_id_variance, COUNT(DISTINCT p2.ID) AS total_ports, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT f.ID) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_floating_ips FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN PORTS AS p2 ON p2.STATUS = i.STATUS JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia2 ON ia2.PORT_ID = p2.ID LEFT JOIN FLOATINGIPS AS f ON f.FIXED_PORT_ID = p2.ID WHERE NOT p2.STANDARD_ATTR_ID IS NULL GROUP BY i.STATUS, i.standard_attr_id_variance ORDER BY i.standard_attr_id_variance DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10), TopSecurityGroupsDownPorts AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, sgpb.security_group_id, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.security_group_id ORDER BY p.id NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN') SELECT inner_cte.security_group_id, COUNT(DISTINCT inner_cte.port_id) AS total_down_ports, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT t.tenant_id) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_tenant_with_multiple_down_ports FROM inner_cte LEFT JOIN (SELECT tenant_id FROM inner_cte GROUP BY tenant_id HAVING COUNT(DISTINCT port_id) > 1) AS t ON inner_cte.tenant_id = t.tenant_id GROUP BY inner_cte.security_group_id ORDER BY total_down_ports DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10) SELECT tsg.security_group_id, tsg.total_down_ports, tsg.has_tenant_with_multiple_down_ports, psv.STATUS AS port_status, psv.standard_attr_id_variance, psv.total_ports, psv.has_floating_ips FROM TopSecurityGroupsDownPorts AS tsg JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb ON sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID = tsg.security_group_id JOIN PORTS AS p ON p.ID = sgpb.PORT_ID JOIN PortStatusVariance AS psv ON psv.STATUS = p.STATUS WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' ORDER BY tsg.total_down_ports DESC NULLS LAST, psv.standard_attr_id_variance DESC NULLS LAST
WITH down_ports_windowed AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(sa.ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sa.RESOURCE_TYPE ORDER BY sa.ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), ResourceTypeAverages AS (SELECT sa.RESOURCE_TYPE, AVG(sa.ID) AS avg_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' GROUP BY sa.RESOURCE_TYPE) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.mac_address, i.window_column FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN PORTS AS p2 ON p2.ID = i.port_id JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa2 ON sa2.ID = p2.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa2.ID > (SELECT avg_standard_attr_id FROM ResourceTypeAverages AS rta WHERE rta.RESOURCE_TYPE = sa2.RESOURCE_TYPE)), top_shared_network_subnets AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.OBJECT_ID AS network_id, COUNT(*) AS subnet_count FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY n.OBJECT_ID ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 3) SELECT CASE WHEN LAG(inner_cte.network_id) OVER (ORDER BY inner_cte.network_id NULLS FIRST) = inner_cte.network_id THEN NULL ELSE inner_cte.network_id END AS network_id, inner_cte.subnet_count, s.TENANT_ID AS subnet_tenant_id FROM inner_cte JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON s.NETWORK_ID = inner_cte.network_id ORDER BY inner_cte.network_id NULLS FIRST, s.TENANT_ID NULLS FIRST), down_ports_on_top_shared_networks AS (SELECT dpw.port_id, dpw.tenant_id, dpw.mac_address, dpw.window_column, tsn.network_id, tsn.subnet_count, tsn.subnet_tenant_id FROM down_ports_windowed AS dpw JOIN PORTS AS p ON p.ID = dpw.port_id JOIN top_shared_network_subnets AS tsn ON tsn.network_id = p.NETWORK_ID) SELECT dptsn.network_id, dptsn.subnet_count, dptsn.subnet_tenant_id, COUNT(DISTINCT dptsn.port_id) AS down_port_count, AVG(dptsn.window_column) AS avg_down_port_running_sum_standard_attr_ids FROM down_ports_on_top_shared_networks AS dptsn GROUP BY dptsn.network_id, dptsn.subnet_count, dptsn.subnet_tenant_id ORDER BY dptsn.subnet_count DESC NULLS LAST, dptsn.network_id NULLS FIRST, dptsn.subnet_tenant_id NULLS FIRST
WITH DownPortTenants AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(sa.ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sa.RESOURCE_TYPE ORDER BY sa.ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT i.tenant_id, COUNT(DISTINCT i.port_id) AS total_down_ports, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT sa2.ID) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_ports_resource_type FROM inner_cte AS i LEFT JOIN PORTS AS p2 ON p2.ID = i.port_id LEFT JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa2 ON sa2.ID = p2.STANDARD_ATTR_ID AND sa2.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' GROUP BY i.tenant_id ORDER BY total_down_ports DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10), NonDefaultExternalNetworkTenants AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.NAME, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY r.ACTION NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM EXTERNALNETWORKS AS e JOIN NETWORKS AS n ON e.NETWORK_ID = n.ID JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r ON n.ID = r.OBJECT_ID WHERE e.IS_DEFAULT = 0) SELECT n.TENANT_ID, COUNT(DISTINCT n.ID) AS total_nondefault_external_networks, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT r.ID) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_any_rbac FROM EXTERNALNETWORKS AS e JOIN NETWORKS AS n ON e.NETWORK_ID = n.ID LEFT JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r ON n.ID = r.OBJECT_ID JOIN inner_cte AS ic ON ic.NAME = n.NAME WHERE e.IS_DEFAULT = 0 GROUP BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY total_nondefault_external_networks DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10), CombinedTopTenants AS (SELECT dpt.tenant_id AS tenant_id, 'Top DOWN Ports' AS reason, dpt.total_down_ports AS metric_count, dpt.has_ports_resource_type AS flag FROM DownPortTenants AS dpt UNION ALL SELECT ndent.tenant_id AS tenant_id, 'Top Non-default External Networks' AS reason, ndent.total_nondefault_external_networks AS metric_count, ndent.has_any_rbac AS flag FROM NonDefaultExternalNetworkTenants AS ndent) SELECT tenant_id, reason, metric_count, flag FROM CombinedTopTenants ORDER BY tenant_id NULLS FIRST, reason NULLS FIRST
