WITH down_port_ip_details AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, b.HOST AS host, b.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ia.IP_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), HostCounts AS (SELECT host, COUNT(DISTINCT ip_address) AS down_allocated_ips FROM inner_cte GROUP BY host), AverageHostCounts AS (SELECT AVG(down_allocated_ips) AS avg_down_allocated_ips FROM HostCounts) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.host, i.vif_type, i.ip_address, i.window_column, hc.down_allocated_ips, (hc.down_allocated_ips - ahc.avg_down_allocated_ips) AS deviation_from_avg FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN HostCounts AS hc ON i.host = hc.host CROSS JOIN AverageHostCounts AS ahc), tenant_down_port_benchmark AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (ORDER BY p.ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), TenantDownPorts AS (SELECT p.tenant_id, COUNT(p.id) AS total_down_ports FROM PORTS AS p JOIN inner_cte AS ic ON ic.MAC_ADDRESS = p.MAC_ADDRESS GROUP BY p.tenant_id), AverageDownPorts AS (SELECT AVG(total_down_ports) AS avg_down_ports FROM TenantDownPorts) SELECT tdp.tenant_id, tdp.total_down_ports, adp.avg_down_ports AS benchmark, CASE WHEN tdp.total_down_ports > adp.avg_down_ports * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_USER' WHEN tdp.total_down_ports < adp.avg_down_ports * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_USER' ELSE 'AVERAGE_USER' END AS user_category FROM TenantDownPorts AS tdp CROSS JOIN AverageDownPorts AS adp) SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT dpipd.tenant_id) AS tenants_with_down_port_ip_allocations, COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN tdpb.user_category = 'HIGH_USER' THEN tdpb.tenant_id END) AS high_user_tenants_among_down_port_tenants FROM down_port_ip_details AS dpipd JOIN tenant_down_port_benchmark AS tdpb ON dpipd.tenant_id = tdpb.tenant_id
WITH Top10PortStatusCoV AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.status, CAST(STDDEV(p.standard_attr_id) AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(AVG(p.standard_attr_id), 0), 0) AS coefficient_of_variation FROM securitygroupportbindings AS sgpb JOIN ports AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id GROUP BY p.status HAVING AVG(p.standard_attr_id) > 0 ORDER BY coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT i.status, i.coefficient_of_variation, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT f.id) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_floating_ips FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN ports AS p ON p.status = i.status JOIN securitygroupportbindings AS sgpb ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON psb.port_id = p.id LEFT JOIN floatingips AS f ON f.fixed_port_id = p.id GROUP BY i.status, i.coefficient_of_variation ORDER BY i.coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10), AllStatusCoVWithAvg AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.status, CAST(STDDEV(p.standard_attr_id) AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(AVG(p.standard_attr_id), 0), 0) AS coefficient_of_variation FROM securitygroupportbindings AS sgpb JOIN ports AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id GROUP BY p.status HAVING AVG(p.standard_attr_id) > 0 ORDER BY coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT inner_cte.status, inner_cte.coefficient_of_variation, avg_cov.avg_coefficient_of_variation FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT AVG(coefficient_of_variation) AS avg_coefficient_of_variation FROM inner_cte) AS avg_cov ON 1 = 1 ORDER BY inner_cte.coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST), CombinedStatusCoV AS (SELECT t.status, t.coefficient_of_variation, a.avg_coefficient_of_variation, t.has_floating_ips, 'Top 10 CoV Statuses (with Floating IP flag)' AS reason FROM Top10PortStatusCoV AS t JOIN AllStatusCoVWithAvg AS a ON a.status = t.status UNION ALL SELECT a.status, a.coefficient_of_variation, a.avg_coefficient_of_variation, NULL AS has_floating_ips, 'All Statuses (with overall average CoV)' AS reason FROM AllStatusCoVWithAvg AS a) SELECT status, coefficient_of_variation, avg_coefficient_of_variation, has_floating_ips, reason FROM CombinedStatusCoV ORDER BY reason NULLS FIRST, coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST, status NULLS FIRST
WITH down_ports_ipallocs AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, b.HOST AS host, b.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ia.IP_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT inner_cte.tenant_id, COUNT(DISTINCT inner_cte.port_id) AS total_down_ports, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT inner_cte.ip_address) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_ip_allocations FROM inner_cte GROUP BY inner_cte.tenant_id ORDER BY total_down_ports DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10), down_secure_ports_by_tenant AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT COUNT(*) AS down_ports_with_port_security_enabled FROM PORTS AS p JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND psb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED = 1) SELECT p.tenant_id AS ports_tenant_id, COUNT(p.id) AS total_down_ports_with_port_security_enabled, avg_counts.avg_down_secure_port_count FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id JOIN (SELECT AVG(port_count) AS avg_down_secure_port_count FROM (SELECT p2.tenant_id, COUNT(p2.id) AS port_count FROM ports AS p2 JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb2 ON p2.id = psb2.port_id WHERE p2.status = 'DOWN' AND psb2.port_security_enabled = 1 GROUP BY p2.tenant_id) AS sub) AS avg_counts ON 1 = 1 CROSS JOIN inner_cte WHERE p.status = 'DOWN' AND psb.port_security_enabled = 1 GROUP BY p.tenant_id, avg_counts.avg_down_secure_port_count) SELECT dpi.tenant_id, dpi.total_down_ports, dpi.has_ip_allocations, dsp.total_down_ports_with_port_security_enabled, dsp.avg_down_secure_port_count FROM down_ports_ipallocs AS dpi JOIN down_secure_ports_by_tenant AS dsp ON dsp.ports_tenant_id = dpi.tenant_id ORDER BY dpi.total_down_ports DESC NULLS LAST
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.id AS network_id, SUM(1) AS total_subnets FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r ON n.id = r.object_id JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON r.object_id = s.network_id WHERE n.status = 'ACTIVE' GROUP BY n.id HAVING SUM(1) > (SELECT AVG(subnet_cnt) + STDDEV(subnet_cnt) FROM (SELECT n2.id AS network_id, SUM(1) AS subnet_cnt FROM NETWORKS AS n2 JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r2 ON n2.id = r2.object_id JOIN SUBNETS AS s2 ON r2.object_id = s2.network_id WHERE n2.status = 'ACTIVE' GROUP BY n2.id) AS per_network)), benchmark_cte AS (SELECT AVG(subnet_cnt) + STDDEV(subnet_cnt) AS benchmark_threshold FROM (SELECT n2.id AS network_id, SUM(1) AS subnet_cnt FROM NETWORKS AS n2 JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r2 ON n2.id = r2.object_id JOIN SUBNETS AS s2 ON r2.object_id = s2.network_id WHERE n2.status = 'ACTIVE' GROUP BY n2.id) AS per_network) SELECT i.network_id, i.total_subnets, b.benchmark_threshold AS benchmark, CASE WHEN i.total_subnets > b.benchmark_threshold THEN 'ABOVE_1STD' ELSE 'NOT_ABOVE_1STD' END AS subnet_usage_category FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN benchmark_cte AS b), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.OBJECT_ID AS network_id, COUNT(*) AS subnet_count FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY n.OBJECT_ID ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 3), AverageSubnets AS (SELECT AVG(subnet_count) AS avg_subnet_count FROM inner_cte) SELECT ic.network_id, ic.subnet_count, a.avg_subnet_count AS benchmark, CASE WHEN ic.subnet_count > a.avg_subnet_count * 1.5 THEN 'ABOVE_BENCHMARK' WHEN ic.subnet_count < a.avg_subnet_count * 0.5 THEN 'BELOW_BENCHMARK' ELSE 'NEAR_BENCHMARK' END AS subnet_category FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN AverageSubnets AS a) SELECT q2.network_id, q2.subnet_count, q2.benchmark AS top3_shared_benchmark, q2.subnet_category, q1.total_subnets AS active_total_subnets, q1.benchmark AS active_avg_plus_stddev_benchmark, q1.subnet_usage_category FROM q2 JOIN q1 ON q2.network_id = q1.network_id JOIN NETWORKS AS n ON n.id = q2.network_id WHERE n.status = 'ACTIVE' ORDER BY q2.subnet_count DESC NULLS LAST
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.STATUS, VAR_SAMP(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS standard_attr_id_variance FROM PORTS AS p JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE NOT p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID IS NULL GROUP BY p.STATUS ORDER BY standard_attr_id_variance DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT i.STATUS, i.standard_attr_id_variance, COUNT(DISTINCT p2.ID) AS total_ports, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT f.ID) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_floating_ips FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN PORTS AS p2 ON p2.STATUS = i.STATUS JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia2 ON ia2.PORT_ID = p2.ID LEFT JOIN FLOATINGIPS AS f ON f.FIXED_PORT_ID = p2.ID WHERE NOT p2.STANDARD_ATTR_ID IS NULL GROUP BY i.STATUS, i.standard_attr_id_variance ORDER BY i.standard_attr_id_variance DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, b.HOST AS host, b.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ia.IP_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT inner_cte.port_id, inner_cte.tenant_id, inner_cte.host, inner_cte.vif_type, inner_cte.ip_address, inner_cte.window_column, tenant_counts.total_down_ports, avg_down.avg_down_port_count FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT tenant_id, COUNT(port_id) AS total_down_ports FROM inner_cte GROUP BY tenant_id) AS tenant_counts ON inner_cte.tenant_id = tenant_counts.tenant_id JOIN (SELECT AVG(down_port_count) AS avg_down_port_count FROM (SELECT COUNT(port_id) AS down_port_count FROM inner_cte GROUP BY tenant_id) AS sub) AS avg_down ON 1 = 1) SELECT q2.port_id, q2.tenant_id, q2.host, q2.vif_type, q2.ip_address, q2.window_column, q2.total_down_ports, q2.avg_down_port_count, q1.standard_attr_id_variance AS down_status_standard_attr_id_variance, q1.total_ports AS down_status_total_ports_with_ipalloc, q1.has_floating_ips AS down_status_has_any_floating_ips FROM q2 JOIN q1 ON q1.STATUS = 'DOWN'
WITH RouteRangeTop10 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.NAME AS subnet_name, MAX(route_count) - MIN(route_count) AS route_count_range, VAR_SAMP(route_count) AS route_count_variance, STDDEV(route_count) AS route_count_stddev FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN (SELECT sr.SUBNET_ID, COUNT(*) AS route_count FROM SUBNETROUTES AS sr GROUP BY sr.SUBNET_ID, sr.NEXTHOP) AS route_stats ON s.ID = route_stats.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 GROUP BY s.NAME ORDER BY route_count_range DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT inner_cte.subnet_name, inner_cte.route_count_range, inner_cte.route_count_variance, inner_cte.route_count_stddev, s.tenant_id, CASE WHEN s.enable_dhcp = 1 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS dhcp_enabled FROM inner_cte JOIN subnets AS s ON s.name = inner_cte.subnet_name WHERE s.ip_version = 4 ORDER BY inner_cte.route_count_range DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10), HighDNSVarianceSubnets AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.NAME AS subnet_name, AVG(d.ORDER) AS avg_dns_order, VAR_SAMP(d.ORDER) AS dns_order_variance FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN DNSNAMESERVERS AS d ON s.ID = d.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 GROUP BY s.NAME ORDER BY dns_order_variance DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT subnet_name, avg_dns_order, dns_order_variance FROM inner_cte WHERE dns_order_variance > (SELECT AVG(dns_order_variance) FROM inner_cte) ORDER BY dns_order_variance DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT rrt.subnet_name, rrt.tenant_id, rrt.dhcp_enabled, rrt.route_count_range, rrt.route_count_variance, rrt.route_count_stddev, hdv.avg_dns_order, hdv.dns_order_variance FROM RouteRangeTop10 AS rrt JOIN HighDNSVarianceSubnets AS hdv ON rrt.subnet_name = hdv.subnet_name ORDER BY rrt.route_count_range DESC NULLS LAST, hdv.dns_order_variance DESC NULLS LAST
WITH Above1StdActiveNetworks AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.id AS network_id, SUM(1) AS total_subnets FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r ON n.id = r.object_id JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON r.object_id = s.network_id WHERE n.status = 'ACTIVE' GROUP BY n.id HAVING SUM(1) > (SELECT AVG(subnet_cnt) + STDDEV(subnet_cnt) FROM (SELECT n2.id AS network_id, SUM(1) AS subnet_cnt FROM NETWORKS AS n2 JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r2 ON n2.id = r2.object_id JOIN SUBNETS AS s2 ON r2.object_id = s2.network_id WHERE n2.status = 'ACTIVE' GROUP BY n2.id) AS per_network)), benchmark_cte AS (SELECT AVG(subnet_cnt) + STDDEV(subnet_cnt) AS benchmark_threshold FROM (SELECT n2.id AS network_id, SUM(1) AS subnet_cnt FROM NETWORKS AS n2 JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r2 ON n2.id = r2.object_id JOIN SUBNETS AS s2 ON r2.object_id = s2.network_id WHERE n2.status = 'ACTIVE' GROUP BY n2.id) AS per_network) SELECT i.network_id, i.total_subnets, b.benchmark_threshold AS benchmark, CASE WHEN i.total_subnets > b.benchmark_threshold THEN 'ABOVE_1STD' ELSE 'NOT_ABOVE_1STD' END AS subnet_usage_category FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN benchmark_cte AS b), Top3SharedNetworks AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.OBJECT_ID AS network_id, COUNT(*) AS subnet_count FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY n.OBJECT_ID ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 3), AverageSubnets AS (SELECT AVG(subnet_count) AS avg_subnet_count FROM inner_cte) SELECT ic.network_id, ic.subnet_count, a.avg_subnet_count AS benchmark, CASE WHEN ic.subnet_count > a.avg_subnet_count * 1.5 THEN 'ABOVE_BENCHMARK' WHEN ic.subnet_count < a.avg_subnet_count * 0.5 THEN 'BELOW_BENCHMARK' ELSE 'NEAR_BENCHMARK' END AS subnet_category FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN AverageSubnets AS a), CombinedNetworks AS (SELECT a.network_id AS network_id, a.total_subnets AS subnet_metric, a.benchmark AS benchmark_value, a.subnet_usage_category AS category, 'ACTIVE_ABOVE_1STD' AS reason FROM Above1StdActiveNetworks AS a UNION ALL SELECT t.network_id AS network_id, t.subnet_count AS subnet_metric, t.benchmark AS benchmark_value, t.subnet_category AS category, 'TOP3_SHARED_BY_SUBNETS' AS reason FROM Top3SharedNetworks AS t) SELECT network_id, subnet_metric, benchmark_value, category, reason FROM CombinedNetworks ORDER BY network_id NULLS FIRST, reason NULLS FIRST
WITH cv_stats AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.name AS subnet_name, CAST(STDDEV(d.ORDER) AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(AVG(d.ORDER), 0), 0) AS coefficient_of_variation FROM subnets AS s JOIN dnsnameservers AS d ON d.subnet_id = s.id JOIN ipallocationpools AS p ON p.subnet_id = s.id WHERE s.ip_version = 4 GROUP BY s.name HAVING AVG(d.ORDER) > 0 ORDER BY coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(coefficient_of_variation) AS avg_coefficient_of_variation FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.subnet_name, i.coefficient_of_variation, (i.coefficient_of_variation - a.avg_coefficient_of_variation) AS deviation_from_avg FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS a ORDER BY deviation_from_avg DESC NULLS LAST), pool_stats AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.name AS subnet_name, MAX(pool_count) - MIN(pool_count) AS pool_count_range, VAR_SAMP(pool_count) AS pool_count_variance, STDDEV(pool_count) AS pool_count_stddev FROM subnets AS s JOIN dnsnameservers AS d ON d.subnet_id = s.id JOIN (SELECT s2.id AS subnet_id, COUNT(*) AS pool_count FROM subnets AS s2 JOIN ipallocationpools AS p ON p.subnet_id = s2.id GROUP BY s2.id, p.id) AS pool_stats ON pool_stats.subnet_id = s.id GROUP BY s.name ORDER BY pool_count_range DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT inner_cte.subnet_name, inner_cte.pool_count_range, inner_cte.pool_count_variance, inner_cte.pool_count_stddev, avg_stats.avg_pool_count_range FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT AVG(pool_count_range) AS avg_pool_count_range FROM inner_cte) AS avg_stats ON 1 = 1 ORDER BY inner_cte.pool_count_range DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT cv_stats.subnet_name, cv_stats.coefficient_of_variation, cv_stats.deviation_from_avg, pool_stats.pool_count_range, pool_stats.pool_count_variance, pool_stats.pool_count_stddev, pool_stats.avg_pool_count_range FROM cv_stats JOIN pool_stats ON cv_stats.subnet_name = pool_stats.subnet_name ORDER BY cv_stats.deviation_from_avg DESC NULLS LAST
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, b.HOST AS host, b.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ia.IP_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT inner_cte.port_id, inner_cte.tenant_id, inner_cte.host, inner_cte.vif_type, inner_cte.ip_address, inner_cte.window_column, tenant_counts.total_down_ports, avg_down.avg_down_port_count FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT tenant_id, COUNT(port_id) AS total_down_ports FROM inner_cte GROUP BY tenant_id) AS tenant_counts ON inner_cte.tenant_id = tenant_counts.tenant_id JOIN (SELECT AVG(down_port_count) AS avg_down_port_count FROM (SELECT COUNT(port_id) AS down_port_count FROM inner_cte GROUP BY tenant_id) AS sub) AS avg_down ON 1 = 1), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), SecurityGroupDownCounts AS (SELECT sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID AS security_group_id, COUNT(*) AS down_ports_in_group FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' GROUP BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID), AverageDownCounts AS (SELECT AVG(down_ports_in_group) AS avg_down_ports_in_group FROM SecurityGroupDownCounts) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.mac_address, i.window_column, sgdc.down_ports_in_group, (sgdc.down_ports_in_group - adc.avg_down_ports_in_group) AS deviation_from_avg_down_ports FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb ON sgpb.PORT_ID = i.port_id JOIN SecurityGroupDownCounts AS sgdc ON sgdc.security_group_id = sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID CROSS JOIN AverageDownCounts AS adc) SELECT q1.port_id, q1.tenant_id, q1.host, q1.vif_type, q1.ip_address, q1.window_column AS ip_row_number_window, q1.total_down_ports AS tenant_total_down_ports, q1.avg_down_port_count AS avg_down_ports_per_tenant, q2.mac_address, q2.window_column AS security_group_running_sum_standard_attr_id, q2.down_ports_in_group, q2.deviation_from_avg_down_ports FROM q1 JOIN q2 ON q1.port_id = q2.port_id AND q1.tenant_id = q2.tenant_id ORDER BY q1.ip_address NULLS FIRST
WITH TopTenTenants AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT lb.name AS loadbalancer_name, MAX(lb.admin_state_up) AS max_admin_state_up, lb.operating_status FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb JOIN PROVIDERRESOURCEASSOCIATIONS AS pra ON lb.id = pra.resource_id WHERE pra.provider_name = 'haproxy' GROUP BY lb.name, lb.operating_status HAVING MAX(lb.admin_state_up) = 1) SELECT lb.tenant_id, COUNT(DISTINCT lb.id) AS total_qualifying_loadbalancers, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN inner_cte.operating_status = 'ONLINE' THEN lb.id END) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_any_online FROM inner_cte JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb ON lb.name = inner_cte.loadbalancer_name AND lb.operating_status = inner_cte.operating_status GROUP BY lb.tenant_id ORDER BY total_qualifying_loadbalancers DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10), TenantHaproxyCountsAndAverage AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT lb.name AS loadbalancer_name, MAX(lb.admin_state_up) AS max_admin_state_up, lb.operating_status FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb JOIN PROVIDERRESOURCEASSOCIATIONS AS pra ON lb.id = pra.resource_id WHERE pra.provider_name = 'haproxy' GROUP BY lb.name, lb.operating_status HAVING MAX(lb.admin_state_up) = 1) SELECT lb.tenant_id AS tenant_id, COUNT(inner_cte.loadbalancer_name) AS total_haproxy_loadbalancers, avg_counts.avg_lb_count FROM inner_cte JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb ON lb.name = inner_cte.loadbalancer_name AND lb.operating_status = inner_cte.operating_status, (SELECT AVG(lb_count) AS avg_lb_count FROM (SELECT lb2.tenant_id, COUNT(*) AS lb_count FROM (SELECT lb.name AS loadbalancer_name, MAX(lb.admin_state_up) AS max_admin_state_up, lb.operating_status FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb JOIN PROVIDERRESOURCEASSOCIATIONS AS pra ON lb.id = pra.resource_id WHERE pra.provider_name = 'haproxy' GROUP BY lb.name, lb.operating_status HAVING MAX(lb.admin_state_up) = 1) AS inner2 JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb2 ON lb2.name = inner2.loadbalancer_name AND lb2.operating_status = inner2.operating_status GROUP BY lb2.tenant_id) AS sub) AS avg_counts GROUP BY lb.tenant_id, avg_counts.avg_lb_count) SELECT ttt.tenant_id, ttt.total_qualifying_loadbalancers, tha.avg_lb_count, ttt.has_any_online FROM TopTenTenants AS ttt JOIN TenantHaproxyCountsAndAverage AS tha ON ttt.tenant_id = tha.tenant_id WHERE ttt.total_qualifying_loadbalancers > tha.avg_lb_count ORDER BY ttt.total_qualifying_loadbalancers DESC NULLS LAST
WITH down_ports_ip_window AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, b.HOST AS host, b.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ia.IP_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT inner_cte.port_id, inner_cte.tenant_id, inner_cte.host, inner_cte.vif_type, inner_cte.ip_address, inner_cte.window_column, tenant_counts.total_down_ports, avg_down.avg_down_port_count FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT tenant_id, COUNT(port_id) AS total_down_ports FROM inner_cte GROUP BY tenant_id) AS tenant_counts ON inner_cte.tenant_id = tenant_counts.tenant_id JOIN (SELECT AVG(down_port_count) AS avg_down_port_count FROM (SELECT COUNT(port_id) AS down_port_count FROM inner_cte GROUP BY tenant_id) AS sub) AS avg_down ON 1 = 1), down_ports_admin_window_filtered AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, AVG(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST), VifTypeAverages AS (SELECT VIF_TYPE, AVG(window_column) AS avg_window_column FROM inner_cte GROUP BY VIF_TYPE) SELECT i.NAME, i.MAC_ADDRESS, i.ADMIN_STATE_UP, i.HOST, i.VIF_TYPE, i.window_column FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN VifTypeAverages AS v ON i.VIF_TYPE = v.VIF_TYPE WHERE i.window_column > v.avg_window_column ORDER BY i.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST) SELECT dipw.port_id, dipw.tenant_id, dipw.host, dipw.vif_type, dipw.ip_address, dipw.window_column AS ip_row_number_window, dipw.total_down_ports, dipw.avg_down_port_count, dapwf.NAME AS port_name, dapwf.MAC_ADDRESS, dapwf.ADMIN_STATE_UP, dapwf.window_column AS admin_state_up_window_avg FROM down_ports_ip_window AS dipw JOIN PORTS AS p ON p.ID = dipw.port_id JOIN down_ports_admin_window_filtered AS dapwf ON dapwf.MAC_ADDRESS = p.MAC_ADDRESS AND dapwf.HOST = dipw.host AND dapwf.VIF_TYPE = dipw.vif_type
WITH sg_admin_up_ports AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.name AS port_name, COUNT(sgpb.security_group_id) AS security_group_binding_count, SUM(CASE WHEN pd.port_id IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END) AS dns_record_count FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id LEFT JOIN PORTDNSES AS pd ON p.id = pd.port_id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.name), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(security_group_binding_count) AS avg_sg_binding_count FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.port_name, i.security_group_binding_count, i.dns_record_count, (i.security_group_binding_count - a.avg_sg_binding_count) AS deviation_from_avg_sg_binding_count FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS a), down_ports_tenant_benchmark AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), TenantDownPorts AS (SELECT tenant_id, COUNT(DISTINCT port_id) AS total_down_ports FROM inner_cte GROUP BY tenant_id), AverageDownPorts AS (SELECT AVG(total_down_ports) AS avg_down_ports FROM TenantDownPorts) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.mac_address, i.window_column, tdp.total_down_ports, adp.avg_down_ports AS benchmark, CASE WHEN tdp.total_down_ports > adp.avg_down_ports * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_USER' WHEN tdp.total_down_ports < adp.avg_down_ports * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_USER' ELSE 'AVERAGE_USER' END AS user_category FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN TenantDownPorts AS tdp ON i.tenant_id = tdp.tenant_id CROSS JOIN AverageDownPorts AS adp) SELECT sap.port_id, sap.tenant_id, sap.port_name, sap.security_group_binding_count, sap.dns_record_count, sap.deviation_from_avg_sg_binding_count, dptb.mac_address, dptb.window_column, dptb.total_down_ports, dptb.benchmark, dptb.user_category FROM sg_admin_up_ports AS sap JOIN down_ports_tenant_benchmark AS dptb ON sap.port_id = dptb.port_id WHERE NOT sap.tenant_id IS NULL
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.STATUS, VAR_SAMP(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS standard_attr_id_variance FROM PORTS AS p JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE NOT p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID IS NULL GROUP BY p.STATUS ORDER BY standard_attr_id_variance DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT i.STATUS, i.standard_attr_id_variance, COUNT(DISTINCT p2.ID) AS total_ports, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT f.ID) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_floating_ips FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN PORTS AS p2 ON p2.STATUS = i.STATUS JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia2 ON ia2.PORT_ID = p2.ID LEFT JOIN FLOATINGIPS AS f ON f.FIXED_PORT_ID = p2.ID WHERE NOT p2.STANDARD_ATTR_ID IS NULL GROUP BY i.STATUS, i.standard_attr_id_variance ORDER BY i.standard_attr_id_variance DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS min_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS spb ON p.ID = spb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), AveragePorts AS (SELECT AVG(port_count) AS avg_ports FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.device_owner, i.port_count, i.min_standard_attr_id, ap.avg_ports AS benchmark, CASE WHEN i.port_count > ap.avg_ports * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_USER' WHEN i.port_count < ap.avg_ports * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_USER' ELSE 'AVERAGE_USER' END AS user_category FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN AveragePorts AS ap) SELECT q1.STATUS, q1.standard_attr_id_variance, q1.total_ports, q1.has_floating_ips, COUNT(DISTINCT q2.device_owner) AS down_device_owners_bound_to_sg, AVG(q2.port_count) AS avg_down_ports_per_device_owner, MAX(q2.port_count) AS max_down_ports_per_device_owner, COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN q2.user_category = 'HIGH_USER' THEN q2.device_owner END) AS high_user_device_owners, COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN q2.user_category = 'LOW_USER' THEN q2.device_owner END) AS low_user_device_owners FROM q1 LEFT JOIN q2 ON q1.STATUS = 'DOWN' GROUP BY q1.STATUS, q1.standard_attr_id_variance, q1.total_ports, q1.has_floating_ips ORDER BY q1.standard_attr_id_variance DESC NULLS LAST
WITH RangeVarianceSubnets AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.name AS subnet_name, MAX(d.ORDER) - MIN(d.ORDER) AS order_range, VAR_SAMP(d.ORDER) AS order_variance FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN DNSNAMESERVERS AS d ON s.id = d.subnet_id WHERE s.ip_version = 4 GROUP BY s.name ORDER BY order_range DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT subnet_name, order_range, order_variance FROM inner_cte WHERE order_range > (SELECT AVG(order_range) FROM inner_cte) ORDER BY order_range DESC NULLS LAST), CoeffVarDeviationSubnets AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.name AS subnet_name, CAST(STDDEV(d.ORDER) AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(AVG(d.ORDER), 0), 0) AS coefficient_of_variation FROM subnets AS s JOIN dnsnameservers AS d ON d.subnet_id = s.id JOIN ipallocationpools AS p ON p.subnet_id = s.id WHERE s.ip_version = 4 GROUP BY s.name HAVING AVG(d.ORDER) > 0 ORDER BY coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(coefficient_of_variation) AS avg_coefficient_of_variation FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.subnet_name, i.coefficient_of_variation, (i.coefficient_of_variation - a.avg_coefficient_of_variation) AS deviation_from_avg FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS a ORDER BY deviation_from_avg DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT rvs.subnet_name, rvs.order_range, rvs.order_variance, cvd.coefficient_of_variation, cvd.deviation_from_avg FROM RangeVarianceSubnets AS rvs JOIN CoeffVarDeviationSubnets AS cvd ON rvs.subnet_name = cvd.subnet_name ORDER BY rvs.order_range DESC NULLS LAST, cvd.deviation_from_avg DESC NULLS LAST
WITH DownSecureNetworks AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT COUNT(*) AS down_ports_with_port_security_enabled FROM PORTS AS p JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND psb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED = 1), NetworkDownSecureCounts AS (SELECT p.network_id, COUNT(*) AS down_secure_ports FROM PORTS AS p JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND psb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED = 1 GROUP BY p.network_id), AvgDownSecure AS (SELECT AVG(down_secure_ports) AS avg_down_secure_ports FROM NetworkDownSecureCounts) SELECT ndsc.network_id, ndsc.down_secure_ports, (SELECT down_ports_with_port_security_enabled FROM inner_cte) AS total_down_secure_ports_overall FROM NetworkDownSecureCounts AS ndsc WHERE ndsc.down_secure_ports > (SELECT avg_down_secure_ports FROM AvgDownSecure)), AllowedAddressPairStatsByNetwork AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, MAX(port_pair_count) - MIN(port_pair_count) AS pair_count_range, VAR_SAMP(port_pair_count) AS pair_count_variance, STDDEV(port_pair_count) AS pair_count_stddev FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, COUNT(*) AS port_pair_count FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ALLOWEDADDRESSPAIRS AS aap ON p.ID = aap.PORT_ID GROUP BY p.ID) AS port_stats ON sgpb.PORT_ID = port_stats.port_id GROUP BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY pair_count_range DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT p.NETWORK_ID, MAX(inner_cte.pair_count_range) AS max_pair_count_range, MAX(inner_cte.pair_count_variance) AS max_pair_count_variance, MAX(inner_cte.pair_count_stddev) AS max_pair_count_stddev FROM inner_cte JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb ON inner_cte.SECURITY_GROUP_ID = sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID GROUP BY p.NETWORK_ID), CombinedNetworkMetrics AS (SELECT dsn.network_id, dsn.down_secure_ports, dsn.total_down_secure_ports_overall, aapsbn.max_pair_count_range, aapsbn.max_pair_count_variance, aapsbn.max_pair_count_stddev FROM DownSecureNetworks AS dsn JOIN AllowedAddressPairStatsByNetwork AS aapsbn ON dsn.network_id = aapsbn.network_id) SELECT * FROM CombinedNetworkMetrics ORDER BY network_id NULLS FIRST
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.STATUS, VAR_SAMP(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS standard_attr_id_variance FROM PORTS AS p JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE NOT p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID IS NULL GROUP BY p.STATUS ORDER BY standard_attr_id_variance DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT i.STATUS, i.standard_attr_id_variance, COUNT(DISTINCT p2.ID) AS total_ports, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT f.ID) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_floating_ips FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN PORTS AS p2 ON p2.STATUS = i.STATUS JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia2 ON ia2.PORT_ID = p2.ID LEFT JOIN FLOATINGIPS AS f ON f.FIXED_PORT_ID = p2.ID WHERE NOT p2.STANDARD_ATTR_ID IS NULL GROUP BY i.STATUS, i.standard_attr_id_variance ORDER BY i.standard_attr_id_variance DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, AVG(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST), VifTypeAverages AS (SELECT VIF_TYPE, AVG(window_column) AS avg_window_column FROM inner_cte GROUP BY VIF_TYPE) SELECT i.NAME, i.MAC_ADDRESS, i.ADMIN_STATE_UP, i.HOST, i.VIF_TYPE, i.window_column FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN VifTypeAverages AS v ON i.VIF_TYPE = v.VIF_TYPE WHERE i.window_column > v.avg_window_column ORDER BY i.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST) SELECT q2.NAME, q2.MAC_ADDRESS, q2.ADMIN_STATE_UP, q2.HOST, q2.VIF_TYPE, q2.window_column, q1.standard_attr_id_variance, q1.total_ports, q1.has_floating_ips FROM q2 JOIN q1 ON q1.STATUS = 'DOWN' ORDER BY q1.standard_attr_id_variance DESC NULLS LAST, q2.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.STATUS, VAR_SAMP(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS standard_attr_id_variance FROM PORTS AS p JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE NOT p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID IS NULL GROUP BY p.STATUS ORDER BY standard_attr_id_variance DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT i.STATUS, i.standard_attr_id_variance, COUNT(DISTINCT p2.ID) AS total_ports, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT f.ID) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_floating_ips FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN PORTS AS p2 ON p2.STATUS = i.STATUS JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia2 ON ia2.PORT_ID = p2.ID LEFT JOIN FLOATINGIPS AS f ON f.FIXED_PORT_ID = p2.ID WHERE NOT p2.STANDARD_ATTR_ID IS NULL GROUP BY i.STATUS, i.standard_attr_id_variance ORDER BY i.standard_attr_id_variance DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.status, CAST(STDDEV(p.standard_attr_id) AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(AVG(p.standard_attr_id), 0), 0) AS coefficient_of_variation FROM securitygroupportbindings AS sgpb JOIN ports AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id GROUP BY p.status HAVING AVG(p.standard_attr_id) > 0 ORDER BY coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT inner_cte.status, inner_cte.coefficient_of_variation, avg_cov.avg_coefficient_of_variation FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT AVG(coefficient_of_variation) AS avg_coefficient_of_variation FROM inner_cte) AS avg_cov ON 1 = 1 ORDER BY inner_cte.coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT q1.status, q1.standard_attr_id_variance, q1.total_ports, q1.has_floating_ips, q2.coefficient_of_variation, q2.avg_coefficient_of_variation FROM q1 JOIN q2 ON q1.status = q2.status WHERE q1.has_floating_ips = 'Yes' ORDER BY q1.standard_attr_id_variance DESC NULLS LAST
WITH pool_range_stats AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.name AS subnet_name, MAX(pool_count) - MIN(pool_count) AS pool_count_range, VAR_SAMP(pool_count) AS pool_count_variance, STDDEV(pool_count) AS pool_count_stddev FROM subnets AS s JOIN dnsnameservers AS d ON d.subnet_id = s.id JOIN (SELECT s2.id AS subnet_id, COUNT(*) AS pool_count FROM subnets AS s2 JOIN ipallocationpools AS p ON p.subnet_id = s2.id GROUP BY s2.id, p.id) AS pool_stats ON pool_stats.subnet_id = s.id GROUP BY s.name ORDER BY pool_count_range DESC NULLS LAST), avg_ranges AS (SELECT AVG(pool_count_range) AS avg_pool_count_range FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.subnet_name, i.pool_count_range, i.pool_count_variance, i.pool_count_stddev, (i.pool_count_range - a.avg_pool_count_range) AS deviation_from_avg_pool_count_range FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_ranges AS a ORDER BY i.pool_count_range DESC NULLS LAST), dns_order_cv AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.name AS subnet_name, CAST(STDDEV(d.ORDER) AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(AVG(d.ORDER), 0), 0) AS coefficient_of_variation FROM subnets AS s JOIN dnsnameservers AS d ON d.subnet_id = s.id JOIN ipallocationpools AS p ON p.subnet_id = s.id WHERE s.ip_version = 4 GROUP BY s.name HAVING AVG(d.ORDER) > 0 ORDER BY coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(coefficient_of_variation) AS avg_coefficient_of_variation FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.subnet_name, i.coefficient_of_variation, (i.coefficient_of_variation - a.avg_coefficient_of_variation) AS deviation_from_avg FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS a ORDER BY deviation_from_avg DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT pr.subnet_name, pr.pool_count_range, pr.pool_count_variance, pr.pool_count_stddev, pr.deviation_from_avg_pool_count_range, dc.coefficient_of_variation, dc.deviation_from_avg AS deviation_from_avg_dns_order_cv FROM pool_range_stats AS pr JOIN dns_order_cv AS dc ON dc.subnet_name = pr.subnet_name JOIN subnets AS s ON s.name = pr.subnet_name WHERE s.ip_version = 4 ORDER BY pr.pool_count_range DESC NULLS LAST
WITH down_ports_top10 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, sgpb.security_group_id, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.security_group_id ORDER BY p.id NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN') SELECT inner_cte.security_group_id, COUNT(DISTINCT inner_cte.port_id) AS total_down_ports, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT t.tenant_id) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_tenant_with_multiple_down_ports FROM inner_cte LEFT JOIN (SELECT tenant_id FROM inner_cte GROUP BY tenant_id HAVING COUNT(DISTINCT port_id) > 1) AS t ON inner_cte.tenant_id = t.tenant_id GROUP BY inner_cte.security_group_id ORDER BY total_down_ports DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10), sg_geomean AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, EXP(AVG(LN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID))) AS geometric_mean_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID > 0 GROUP BY s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY geometric_mean_standard_attr_id DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT inner_cte.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, inner_cte.geometric_mean_standard_attr_id, avg_sg.avg_geometric_mean_standard_attr_id FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT AVG(geometric_mean_standard_attr_id) AS avg_geometric_mean_standard_attr_id FROM inner_cte) AS avg_sg ON 1 = 1 ORDER BY inner_cte.geometric_mean_standard_attr_id DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT dpt.security_group_id, dpt.total_down_ports, dpt.has_tenant_with_multiple_down_ports, sg.geometric_mean_standard_attr_id, sg.avg_geometric_mean_standard_attr_id FROM down_ports_top10 AS dpt LEFT JOIN sg_geomean AS sg ON dpt.security_group_id = sg.security_group_id ORDER BY dpt.total_down_ports DESC NULLS LAST
WITH down_ports_sg AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, sgpb.security_group_id, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.security_group_id ORDER BY p.id NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN'), sg_counts AS (SELECT security_group_id, COUNT(*) AS down_ports FROM inner_cte GROUP BY security_group_id) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.mac_address, i.security_group_id, i.window_column, c.down_ports FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN sg_counts AS c ON i.security_group_id = c.security_group_id WHERE c.down_ports > (SELECT AVG(down_ports) FROM sg_counts)), host_drive59_tenants AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, p.TENANT_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE b.HOST = 'drive-59') SELECT i.TENANT_ID, COUNT(DISTINCT i.PORT_ID) AS total_ports, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT psb.PORT_ID) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_port_security_enabled FROM inner_cte AS i LEFT JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON i.PORT_ID = psb.PORT_ID GROUP BY i.TENANT_ID ORDER BY total_ports DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT d.port_id, d.tenant_id, d.mac_address, d.security_group_id, d.window_column AS port_rank_within_security_group_by_port_id, d.down_ports AS down_ports_in_security_group, h.total_ports AS total_ports_on_drive_59_for_tenant, h.has_port_security_enabled FROM down_ports_sg AS d JOIN host_drive59_tenants AS h ON d.tenant_id = h.tenant_id
WITH active_network_latest_stdattr AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE') SELECT tenant_id, MAX(standard_attr_updated_at) AS latest_standard_attr_updated_at FROM inner_cte GROUP BY tenant_id), top10_default_external_tenants AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT e.NETWORK_ID, e.IS_DEFAULT, n.TENANT_ID, n.NAME, n.STATUS, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP, n.MTU, n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID, MIN(n.MTU) OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.TENANT_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM EXTERNALNETWORKS AS e JOIN NETWORKS AS n ON e.NETWORK_ID = n.ID WHERE e.IS_DEFAULT = 0) SELECT i.TENANT_ID, COUNT(DISTINCT i.NETWORK_ID) AS total_default_external_networks, MIN(i.MTU) AS min_mtu_in_tenant FROM inner_cte AS i GROUP BY i.TENANT_ID ORDER BY total_default_external_networks DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10) SELECT t.TENANT_ID, t.total_default_external_networks, t.min_mtu_in_tenant, a.latest_standard_attr_updated_at FROM top10_default_external_tenants AS t JOIN active_network_latest_stdattr AS a ON t.TENANT_ID = a.tenant_id ORDER BY t.total_default_external_networks DESC NULLS LAST
WITH subq1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.STATUS AS port_status, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY p.NETWORK_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID = '0008e629-5619-41e0-b86c-4eecfeaacb6c') SELECT inner_cte.port_id, inner_cte.port_status, inner_cte.ip_address, inner_cte.window_column, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, COUNT(p.ID) AS total_ports_for_tenant_in_sg, avg_ports.avg_port_count AS avg_ports_per_tenant_in_sg FROM inner_cte JOIN PORTS AS p ON inner_cte.port_id = p.ID JOIN (SELECT AVG(port_count) AS avg_port_count FROM (SELECT COUNT(p2.ID) AS port_count FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb2 JOIN PORTS AS p2 ON sgpb2.PORT_ID = p2.ID WHERE sgpb2.SECURITY_GROUP_ID = '0008e629-5619-41e0-b86c-4eecfeaacb6c' GROUP BY p2.TENANT_ID) AS sub) AS avg_ports ON 1 = 1 GROUP BY inner_cte.port_id, inner_cte.port_status, inner_cte.ip_address, inner_cte.window_column, p.TENANT_ID, avg_ports.avg_port_count), subq2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.status, CAST(STDDEV(p.standard_attr_id) AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(AVG(p.standard_attr_id), 0), 0) AS coefficient_of_variation FROM securitygroupportbindings AS sgpb JOIN ports AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id GROUP BY p.status HAVING AVG(p.standard_attr_id) > 0 ORDER BY coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT inner_cte.status, inner_cte.coefficient_of_variation, avg_cov.avg_coefficient_of_variation FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT AVG(coefficient_of_variation) AS avg_coefficient_of_variation FROM inner_cte) AS avg_cov ON 1 = 1 ORDER BY inner_cte.coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT subq1.port_id, subq1.port_status, subq1.ip_address, subq1.window_column, subq1.tenant_id, subq1.total_ports_for_tenant_in_sg, subq1.avg_ports_per_tenant_in_sg, subq2.coefficient_of_variation, subq2.avg_coefficient_of_variation FROM subq1 JOIN subq2 ON subq1.port_status = subq2.status
WITH sg_geom AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, EXP(AVG(LN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID))) AS geometric_mean_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID > 0 GROUP BY s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY geometric_mean_standard_attr_id DESC NULLS LAST), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(geometric_mean_standard_attr_id) AS avg_geometric_mean_standard_attr_id FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, i.geometric_mean_standard_attr_id, (i.geometric_mean_standard_attr_id - a.avg_geometric_mean_standard_attr_id) AS deviation_from_avg_geometric_mean FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS a ORDER BY deviation_from_avg_geometric_mean DESC NULLS LAST), port_aap_window AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT aap.MAC_ADDRESS AS allowed_mac_address, aap.IP_ADDRESS AS allowed_ip_address, p.ID AS port_id, sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID AS security_group_id, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY p.ID ORDER BY aap.IP_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN ALLOWEDADDRESSPAIRS AS aap ON p.ID = aap.PORT_ID WHERE p.ID = '25d421c1-c76d-4c26-9550-9f871a1d5034' ORDER BY aap.IP_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(window_column) AS avg_window_column FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.allowed_mac_address, i.allowed_ip_address, i.port_id, i.security_group_id, i.window_column, (i.window_column - a.avg_window_column) AS deviation_from_avg_window FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS a ORDER BY i.allowed_ip_address NULLS FIRST) SELECT p.allowed_mac_address, p.allowed_ip_address, p.port_id, p.security_group_id, p.window_column, p.deviation_from_avg_window, g.geometric_mean_standard_attr_id, g.deviation_from_avg_geometric_mean FROM port_aap_window AS p JOIN sg_geom AS g ON p.security_group_id = g.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY p.allowed_ip_address NULLS FIRST
WITH tenant_ipv4 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.NAME, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY sa.UPDATED_AT NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4) SELECT s.TENANT_ID, COUNT(DISTINCT s.ID) AS total_ipv4_subnets, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT sa.ID) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_any_updated_standard_attribute FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID JOIN inner_cte AS ic ON ic.NAME = s.NAME WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND NOT sa.UPDATED_AT IS NULL GROUP BY s.TENANT_ID ORDER BY total_ipv4_subnets DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10), active_network_rbac AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.name, n.status, n.admin_state_up, r.action, r.target_tenant, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY n.id ORDER BY n.id NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM networksecuritybindings AS b JOIN networks AS n ON b.network_id = n.id JOIN networkrbacs AS r ON n.id = r.object_id WHERE n.status = 'ACTIVE'), rbac_counts AS (SELECT n.id AS network_id, COUNT(DISTINCT r.target_tenant) AS distinct_target_tenants FROM networksecuritybindings AS b JOIN networks AS n ON b.network_id = n.id JOIN networkrbacs AS r ON n.id = r.object_id WHERE n.status = 'ACTIVE' GROUP BY n.id) SELECT i.name, i.status, i.admin_state_up, i.action, i.target_tenant, c.distinct_target_tenants, i.window_column FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN rbac_counts AS c ON c.network_id = (SELECT n2.id FROM networks AS n2 WHERE n2.name = i.name AND n2.status = i.status AND n2.admin_state_up = i.admin_state_up LIMIT 1)), network_tenant_map AS (SELECT DISTINCT n.id AS network_id, n.tenant_id FROM networks AS n WHERE n.status = 'ACTIVE') SELECT anr.name AS network_name, anr.status AS network_status, anr.admin_state_up, anr.action AS rbac_action, anr.target_tenant, anr.distinct_target_tenants, anr.window_column AS network_dense_rank, t.total_ipv4_subnets, t.has_any_updated_standard_attribute FROM active_network_rbac AS anr JOIN networks AS n ON n.name = anr.name AND n.status = anr.status AND n.admin_state_up = anr.admin_state_up JOIN network_tenant_map AS ntm ON ntm.network_id = n.id JOIN tenant_ipv4 AS t ON t.tenant_id = ntm.tenant_id WHERE n.status = 'ACTIVE'
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.name AS subnet_name, CAST(STDDEV(d.ORDER) AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(AVG(d.ORDER), 0), 0) AS coefficient_of_variation FROM subnets AS s JOIN dnsnameservers AS d ON d.subnet_id = s.id JOIN ipallocationpools AS p ON p.subnet_id = s.id WHERE s.ip_version = 4 GROUP BY s.name HAVING AVG(d.ORDER) > 0 ORDER BY coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(coefficient_of_variation) AS avg_coefficient_of_variation FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.subnet_name, i.coefficient_of_variation, (i.coefficient_of_variation - a.avg_coefficient_of_variation) AS deviation_from_avg FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS a ORDER BY deviation_from_avg DESC NULLS LAST), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.name, MAX(s.ip_version) AS max_ip_version, s.gateway_ip FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.object_id = s.network_id WHERE n.action = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY s.name, s.gateway_ip HAVING MAX(s.ip_version) > 3), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(max_ip_version) AS avg_max_ip_version, COUNT(*) AS total_groups FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.name, i.gateway_ip, i.max_ip_version, a.total_groups, (i.max_ip_version - a.avg_max_ip_version) AS deviation_from_avg_max_ip_version FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS a) SELECT q2.name AS subnet_name, q2.gateway_ip, q2.max_ip_version, q2.total_groups, q2.deviation_from_avg_max_ip_version, q1.coefficient_of_variation, q1.deviation_from_avg FROM q2 JOIN q1 ON q1.subnet_name = q2.name ORDER BY q2.deviation_from_avg_max_ip_version DESC NULLS LAST, q1.deviation_from_avg DESC NULLS LAST
WITH down_ports_window AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(sa.ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sa.RESOURCE_TYPE ORDER BY sa.ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT port_id, tenant_id, mac_address, window_column FROM inner_cte WHERE window_column > (SELECT AVG(window_column) FROM inner_cte)), top_tenants_binding_fips AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.status, MAX(ps.binding_count) - MIN(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_range, VAR_SAMP(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_variance, STDDEV(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_stddev FROM ports AS p JOIN (SELECT p2.id AS port_id, COUNT(*) AS binding_count FROM ports AS p2 JOIN portsecuritybindings AS b ON p2.id = b.port_id GROUP BY p2.id) AS ps ON p.id = ps.port_id GROUP BY p.status ORDER BY binding_count_range DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT p.tenant_id, MAX(inner_cte.binding_count_range) AS max_binding_count_range_across_statuses, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT f.id) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_floating_ips FROM ports AS p JOIN (SELECT p2.id AS port_id, COUNT(*) AS binding_count FROM ports AS p2 JOIN portsecuritybindings AS b ON p2.id = b.port_id GROUP BY p2.id) AS ps ON p.id = ps.port_id JOIN inner_cte ON p.status = inner_cte.status LEFT JOIN floatingips AS f ON p.id = f.fixed_port_id GROUP BY p.tenant_id ORDER BY max_binding_count_range_across_statuses DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10) SELECT dpw.port_id, dpw.tenant_id, dpw.mac_address, dpw.window_column AS running_sum_standard_attr_ids, ttb.max_binding_count_range_across_statuses, ttb.has_floating_ips FROM down_ports_window AS dpw JOIN top_tenants_binding_fips AS ttb ON dpw.tenant_id = ttb.tenant_id ORDER BY ttb.max_binding_count_range_across_statuses DESC NULLS LAST, dpw.window_column DESC NULLS LAST
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1), VifCounts AS (SELECT VIF_TYPE, COUNT(*) AS vif_port_count FROM inner_cte GROUP BY VIF_TYPE), OverallAvg AS (SELECT AVG(vif_port_count) AS avg_vif_port_count FROM VifCounts) SELECT i.PORT_ID, i.HOST, i.VIF_TYPE, i.VNIC_TYPE, i.TENANT_ID, i.NETWORK_ID, i.MAC_ADDRESS, i.STATUS, i.DEVICE_OWNER, i.window_column FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN VifCounts AS vc ON i.VIF_TYPE = vc.VIF_TYPE CROSS JOIN OverallAvg AS oa WHERE vc.vif_port_count > oa.avg_vif_port_count), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, AVG(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST) SELECT VIF_TYPE, MAX(window_column) AS max_windowed_avg_admin_state_up FROM inner_cte GROUP BY VIF_TYPE) SELECT q1.PORT_ID, q1.HOST, q1.VIF_TYPE, q1.VNIC_TYPE, q1.TENANT_ID, q1.NETWORK_ID, q1.MAC_ADDRESS, q1.STATUS, q1.DEVICE_OWNER, q1.window_column AS avg_standard_attr_id_window_2p2f, q2.max_windowed_avg_admin_state_up FROM q1 JOIN q2 ON q1.VIF_TYPE = q2.VIF_TYPE
WITH TopRouteRangeSubnets AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.NAME AS subnet_name, MAX(route_count) - MIN(route_count) AS route_count_range, VAR_SAMP(route_count) AS route_count_variance, STDDEV(route_count) AS route_count_stddev FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN (SELECT sr.SUBNET_ID, COUNT(*) AS route_count FROM SUBNETROUTES AS sr GROUP BY sr.SUBNET_ID, sr.NEXTHOP) AS route_stats ON s.ID = route_stats.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 GROUP BY s.NAME ORDER BY route_count_range DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT inner_cte.subnet_name, inner_cte.route_count_range, inner_cte.route_count_variance, inner_cte.route_count_stddev, s.tenant_id, CASE WHEN s.enable_dhcp = 1 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS dhcp_enabled FROM inner_cte JOIN subnets AS s ON s.name = inner_cte.subnet_name WHERE s.ip_version = 4 ORDER BY inner_cte.route_count_range DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10), HighDnsVarianceSubnets AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.NAME AS subnet_name, AVG(d.ORDER) AS avg_dns_order, VAR_SAMP(d.ORDER) AS dns_order_variance FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN DNSNAMESERVERS AS d ON s.ID = d.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 GROUP BY s.NAME ORDER BY dns_order_variance DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT subnet_name, avg_dns_order, dns_order_variance FROM inner_cte WHERE dns_order_variance > (SELECT AVG(dns_order_variance) FROM inner_cte) ORDER BY dns_order_variance DESC NULLS LAST), CombinedSubnets AS (SELECT trrs.subnet_name, trrs.tenant_id, trrs.dhcp_enabled, trrs.route_count_range, trrs.route_count_variance, trrs.route_count_stddev, NULL AS avg_dns_order, NULL AS dns_order_variance, 'Top Route-Count Range' AS reason FROM TopRouteRangeSubnets AS trrs UNION ALL SELECT hdvs.subnet_name, s.tenant_id, CASE WHEN s.enable_dhcp = 1 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS dhcp_enabled, NULL AS route_count_range, NULL AS route_count_variance, NULL AS route_count_stddev, hdvs.avg_dns_order, hdvs.dns_order_variance, 'High DNS Order Variance' AS reason FROM HighDnsVarianceSubnets AS hdvs JOIN subnets AS s ON s.name = hdvs.subnet_name AND s.ip_version = 4) SELECT * FROM CombinedSubnets ORDER BY subnet_name NULLS FIRST, reason NULLS FIRST
WITH deviation_by_status_owner AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(port_count) AS avg_port_count FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.port_status, i.device_owner, i.port_count, i.earliest_standard_attr_update, (i.port_count - a.avg_port_count) AS deviation_from_avg_port_count FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS a), above_avg_groups AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), GroupAverage AS (SELECT AVG(port_count) AS avg_port_count FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.port_status, i.device_owner, i.port_count, i.earliest_standard_attr_update FROM inner_cte AS i WHERE i.port_count > (SELECT avg_port_count FROM GroupAverage)) SELECT a.port_status, a.device_owner, a.port_count, a.earliest_standard_attr_update, d.deviation_from_avg_port_count FROM above_avg_groups AS a JOIN deviation_by_status_owner AS d ON d.port_status = a.port_status AND d.device_owner = a.device_owner AND d.port_count = a.port_count AND d.earliest_standard_attr_update = a.earliest_standard_attr_update
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE'), AvgMtu AS (SELECT AVG(mtu) AS avg_mtu FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.network_id, i.network_name, i.tenant_id, i.admin_state_up, i.mtu, i.standard_attr_updated_at, i.window_column, a.avg_mtu AS benchmark_avg_mtu, CASE WHEN i.mtu > a.avg_mtu THEN 'ABOVE_AVG_MTU' WHEN i.mtu < a.avg_mtu THEN 'BELOW_AVG_MTU' ELSE 'AT_AVG_MTU' END AS mtu_category FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN AvgMtu AS a), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, AVG(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST), VifTypeAverages AS (SELECT VIF_TYPE, AVG(window_column) AS avg_window_column FROM inner_cte GROUP BY VIF_TYPE) SELECT i.NAME, i.MAC_ADDRESS, i.ADMIN_STATE_UP, i.HOST, i.VIF_TYPE, i.window_column FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN VifTypeAverages AS v ON i.VIF_TYPE = v.VIF_TYPE WHERE i.window_column > v.avg_window_column ORDER BY i.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST), q2_ports AS (SELECT p.NETWORK_ID AS network_id, q2.NAME AS port_name, q2.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, q2.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS port_admin_state_up, q2.HOST AS binding_host, q2.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, q2.window_column AS port_admin_state_up_window_avg FROM q2 JOIN PORTS AS p ON p.MAC_ADDRESS = q2.MAC_ADDRESS AND p.NAME = q2.NAME WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT q1.network_id, q1.network_name, q1.tenant_id, q1.admin_state_up AS network_admin_state_up, q1.mtu, q1.standard_attr_updated_at, q1.window_column AS tenant_network_name_rownum, q1.benchmark_avg_mtu, q1.mtu_category, q2_ports.port_name, q2_ports.mac_address, q2_ports.port_admin_state_up, q2_ports.binding_host, q2_ports.vif_type, q2_ports.port_admin_state_up_window_avg FROM q1 JOIN q2_ports ON q1.network_id = q2_ports.network_id ORDER BY q1.tenant_id NULLS FIRST, q1.network_name NULLS FIRST, q2_ports.mac_address NULLS FIRST
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT lb.ID AS loadbalancer_id, lb.NAME AS loadbalancer_name, pra.PROVIDER_NAME AS provider_name, lb.VIP_ADDRESS AS vip_address, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY pra.PROVIDER_NAME ORDER BY stats.TOTAL_CONNECTIONS NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCER_STATISTICS AS stats JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb ON stats.LOADBALANCER_ID = lb.ID JOIN PROVIDERRESOURCEASSOCIATIONS AS pra ON lb.ID = pra.RESOURCE_ID WHERE lb.OPERATING_STATUS = 'ONLINE'), ProviderAverages AS (SELECT pra.PROVIDER_NAME AS provider_name, AVG(stats.TOTAL_CONNECTIONS) AS avg_total_connections FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCER_STATISTICS AS stats JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb ON stats.LOADBALANCER_ID = lb.ID JOIN PROVIDERRESOURCEASSOCIATIONS AS pra ON lb.ID = pra.RESOURCE_ID WHERE lb.OPERATING_STATUS = 'ONLINE' GROUP BY pra.PROVIDER_NAME) SELECT i.loadbalancer_id, i.loadbalancer_name, i.provider_name, i.vip_address, i.window_column FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCER_STATISTICS AS stats ON stats.LOADBALANCER_ID = i.loadbalancer_id WHERE stats.TOTAL_CONNECTIONS > (SELECT pa.avg_total_connections FROM ProviderAverages AS pa WHERE pa.provider_name = i.provider_name)), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT lb.NAME AS loadbalancer_name, lb.VIP_ADDRESS AS vip_address, stats.BYTES_IN AS bytes_in, stats.TOTAL_CONNECTIONS AS total_connections, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY lb.NAME NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCER_STATISTICS AS stats JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb ON stats.LOADBALANCER_ID = lb.ID JOIN PROVIDERRESOURCEASSOCIATIONS AS pra ON lb.ID = pra.RESOURCE_ID WHERE pra.PROVIDER_NAME = 'haproxy'), haproxy_avg AS (SELECT AVG(stats2.BYTES_IN * 1.0) AS avg_bytes_in FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCER_STATISTICS AS stats2 JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb2 ON stats2.LOADBALANCER_ID = lb2.ID JOIN PROVIDERRESOURCEASSOCIATIONS AS pra2 ON lb2.ID = pra2.RESOURCE_ID WHERE pra2.PROVIDER_NAME = 'haproxy') SELECT i.loadbalancer_name, i.vip_address, i.bytes_in, i.total_connections, i.window_column FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN haproxy_avg AS a WHERE i.bytes_in > a.avg_bytes_in ORDER BY i.loadbalancer_name NULLS FIRST) SELECT q1.loadbalancer_id, q1.loadbalancer_name, q1.provider_name, q1.vip_address, q1.window_column AS provider_rank_by_total_connections, q2.bytes_in, q2.total_connections, q2.window_column AS haproxy_row_number_window FROM q1 JOIN q2 ON q1.loadbalancer_name = q2.loadbalancer_name AND q1.vip_address = q2.vip_address WHERE q1.provider_name = 'haproxy'
WITH RankAboveAvg AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.NAME, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY r.ACTION NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM EXTERNALNETWORKS AS e JOIN NETWORKS AS n ON e.NETWORK_ID = n.ID JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r ON n.ID = r.OBJECT_ID WHERE e.IS_DEFAULT = 0) SELECT NAME, window_column FROM inner_cte WHERE window_column > (SELECT AVG(window_column) FROM inner_cte)), AllDefaultExternalNetworkRanks AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.NAME, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY r.ACTION NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM EXTERNALNETWORKS AS e JOIN NETWORKS AS n ON e.NETWORK_ID = n.ID JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r ON n.ID = r.OBJECT_ID WHERE e.IS_DEFAULT = 0) SELECT inner_cte.NAME, inner_cte.window_column, COUNT(*) AS total_networks, avg_counts.avg_networks_per_action FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT AVG(action_count) AS avg_networks_per_action FROM (SELECT COUNT(*) AS action_count FROM inner_cte GROUP BY window_column) AS sub) AS avg_counts ON 1 = 1 GROUP BY inner_cte.NAME, inner_cte.window_column, avg_counts.avg_networks_per_action) SELECT a.NAME, a.window_column, a.total_networks, a.avg_networks_per_action FROM AllDefaultExternalNetworkRanks AS a JOIN RankAboveAvg AS r ON r.NAME = a.NAME AND r.window_column = a.window_column
WITH sg_admin_ports AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.name AS port_name, COUNT(sgpb.security_group_id) AS security_group_binding_count, SUM(CASE WHEN pd.port_id IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END) AS dns_record_count FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id LEFT JOIN PORTDNSES AS pd ON p.id = pd.port_id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.name), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(security_group_binding_count) AS avg_sg_binding_count FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.port_name, i.security_group_binding_count, i.dns_record_count, (i.security_group_binding_count - a.avg_sg_binding_count) AS deviation_from_avg_sg_binding_count FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS a), down_ports_by_sg AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), TenantDownPorts AS (SELECT tenant_id, COUNT(DISTINCT port_id) AS total_down_ports FROM inner_cte GROUP BY tenant_id), AverageDownPorts AS (SELECT AVG(total_down_ports) AS avg_down_ports FROM TenantDownPorts) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.mac_address, i.window_column, tdp.total_down_ports, adp.avg_down_ports AS benchmark, CASE WHEN tdp.total_down_ports > adp.avg_down_ports * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_USER' WHEN tdp.total_down_ports < adp.avg_down_ports * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_USER' ELSE 'AVERAGE_USER' END AS user_category FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN TenantDownPorts AS tdp ON i.tenant_id = tdp.tenant_id CROSS JOIN AverageDownPorts AS adp) SELECT sap.port_id, sap.tenant_id, sap.port_name, sap.security_group_binding_count, sap.dns_record_count, sap.deviation_from_avg_sg_binding_count, dps.mac_address, dps.window_column, dps.total_down_ports, dps.benchmark, dps.user_category FROM sg_admin_ports AS sap JOIN down_ports_by_sg AS dps ON sap.port_id = dps.port_id AND sap.tenant_id = dps.tenant_id
WITH port_vif_running_sums AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, p.TENANT_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE b.HOST = 'drive-59'), VifTypeStats AS (SELECT b.VIF_TYPE, COUNT(*) AS ports_in_vif_type, AVG(ic.window_column) AS avg_running_sum_in_vif_type FROM inner_cte AS ic JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON b.PORT_ID = ic.PORT_ID GROUP BY b.VIF_TYPE) SELECT ic.PORT_ID, ic.TENANT_ID, ic.MAC_ADDRESS, ic.window_column AS running_sum_standard_attr_id, vts.ports_in_vif_type, (ic.window_column - vts.avg_running_sum_in_vif_type) AS deviation_from_avg_running_sum_in_vif_type FROM inner_cte AS ic JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON b.PORT_ID = ic.PORT_ID JOIN VifTypeStats AS vts ON vts.VIF_TYPE = b.VIF_TYPE), tenant_down_secure_port_stats AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT COUNT(*) AS down_ports_with_port_security_enabled FROM PORTS AS p JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND psb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED = 1), TenantDownSecurePorts AS (SELECT p.TENANT_ID, COUNT(*) AS down_secure_ports FROM PORTS AS p JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND psb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED = 1 GROUP BY p.TENANT_ID), AverageDownSecurePorts AS (SELECT AVG(down_secure_ports) AS avg_down_secure_ports FROM TenantDownSecurePorts) SELECT tdsp.TENANT_ID, tdsp.down_secure_ports, adsp.avg_down_secure_ports AS benchmark, CASE WHEN tdsp.down_secure_ports > adsp.avg_down_secure_ports * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_USER' WHEN tdsp.down_secure_ports < adsp.avg_down_secure_ports * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_USER' ELSE 'AVERAGE_USER' END AS user_category, inner_cte.down_ports_with_port_security_enabled AS overall_down_secure_ports FROM TenantDownSecurePorts AS tdsp CROSS JOIN AverageDownSecurePorts AS adsp CROSS JOIN inner_cte) SELECT pvrs.PORT_ID, pvrs.TENANT_ID, pvrs.MAC_ADDRESS, pvrs.running_sum_standard_attr_id, pvrs.ports_in_vif_type, pvrs.deviation_from_avg_running_sum_in_vif_type, tdsp.down_secure_ports, tdsp.benchmark, tdsp.user_category, tdsp.overall_down_secure_ports FROM port_vif_running_sums AS pvrs JOIN tenant_down_secure_port_stats AS tdsp ON tdsp.TENANT_ID = pvrs.TENANT_ID
WITH CoeffVariationSubnets AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.name AS subnet_name, CAST(STDDEV(d.ORDER) AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(AVG(d.ORDER), 0), 0) AS coefficient_of_variation FROM subnets AS s JOIN dnsnameservers AS d ON d.subnet_id = s.id JOIN ipallocationpools AS p ON p.subnet_id = s.id WHERE s.ip_version = 4 GROUP BY s.name HAVING AVG(d.ORDER) > 0 ORDER BY coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST), AvgCoeff AS (SELECT AVG(coefficient_of_variation) AS avg_coefficient_of_variation FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.subnet_name, i.coefficient_of_variation, a.avg_coefficient_of_variation AS benchmark, CASE WHEN i.coefficient_of_variation > a.avg_coefficient_of_variation * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_VARIATION' WHEN i.coefficient_of_variation < a.avg_coefficient_of_variation * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_VARIATION' ELSE 'AVERAGE_VARIATION' END AS variation_category FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN AvgCoeff AS a ORDER BY i.coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST), HighRangeSubnets AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.name AS subnet_name, MAX(d.ORDER) - MIN(d.ORDER) AS order_range, VAR_SAMP(d.ORDER) AS order_variance FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN DNSNAMESERVERS AS d ON s.id = d.subnet_id WHERE s.ip_version = 4 GROUP BY s.name ORDER BY order_range DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT subnet_name, order_range, order_variance FROM inner_cte WHERE order_range > (SELECT AVG(order_range) FROM inner_cte) ORDER BY order_range DESC NULLS LAST), CombinedSubnetFindings AS (SELECT subnet_name, 'High Coefficient of Variation vs Benchmark' AS reason FROM CoeffVariationSubnets WHERE variation_category = 'HIGH_VARIATION' UNION ALL SELECT subnet_name, 'Order Range Above IPv4 Average' AS reason FROM HighRangeSubnets) SELECT subnet_name, reason FROM CombinedSubnetFindings ORDER BY subnet_name NULLS FIRST, reason NULLS FIRST
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, AVG(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST), VifTypeCounts AS (SELECT VIF_TYPE, COUNT(*) AS down_ports_in_vif_type FROM inner_cte GROUP BY VIF_TYPE), AverageVifTypeCounts AS (SELECT AVG(down_ports_in_vif_type) AS avg_down_ports_per_vif_type FROM VifTypeCounts) SELECT i.NAME, i.MAC_ADDRESS, i.ADMIN_STATE_UP, i.HOST, i.VIF_TYPE, i.window_column, vtc.down_ports_in_vif_type, (vtc.down_ports_in_vif_type - avc.avg_down_ports_per_vif_type) AS deviation_from_avg FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN VifTypeCounts AS vtc ON i.VIF_TYPE = vtc.VIF_TYPE CROSS JOIN AverageVifTypeCounts AS avc ORDER BY i.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), TenantDownPorts AS (SELECT tenant_id, COUNT(DISTINCT port_id) AS total_down_ports FROM inner_cte GROUP BY tenant_id), AverageDownPorts AS (SELECT AVG(total_down_ports) AS avg_down_ports FROM TenantDownPorts) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.mac_address, i.window_column, tdp.total_down_ports, adp.avg_down_ports AS benchmark, CASE WHEN tdp.total_down_ports > adp.avg_down_ports * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_USER' WHEN tdp.total_down_ports < adp.avg_down_ports * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_USER' ELSE 'AVERAGE_USER' END AS user_category FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN TenantDownPorts AS tdp ON i.tenant_id = tdp.tenant_id CROSS JOIN AverageDownPorts AS adp) SELECT q2.port_id, q1.NAME AS port_name, q2.tenant_id, q1.MAC_ADDRESS, q1.ADMIN_STATE_UP, q1.HOST AS binding_host, q1.VIF_TYPE, q1.window_column AS avg_admin_state_up_window_2_pre_2_fol_by_vif, q1.down_ports_in_vif_type, q1.deviation_from_avg AS vif_type_down_port_count_deviation_from_avg, q2.window_column AS running_sum_standard_attr_id_within_security_group, q2.total_down_ports AS tenant_total_down_ports, q2.benchmark AS avg_down_ports_across_tenants_benchmark, q2.user_category AS tenant_user_category FROM q2 JOIN q1 ON q2.mac_address = q1.MAC_ADDRESS ORDER BY q1.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST
WITH sg_stats AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, MAX(port_pair_count) - MIN(port_pair_count) AS pair_count_range, VAR_SAMP(port_pair_count) AS pair_count_variance, STDDEV(port_pair_count) AS pair_count_stddev FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, COUNT(*) AS port_pair_count FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ALLOWEDADDRESSPAIRS AS aap ON p.ID = aap.PORT_ID GROUP BY p.ID) AS port_stats ON sgpb.PORT_ID = port_stats.port_id GROUP BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY pair_count_range DESC NULLS LAST), SecurityGroupAverages AS (SELECT sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID AS security_group_id, AVG(port_stats.port_pair_count) AS avg_pairs_per_port FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, COUNT(*) AS port_pair_count FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ALLOWEDADDRESSPAIRS AS aap ON p.ID = aap.PORT_ID GROUP BY p.ID) AS port_stats ON sgpb.PORT_ID = port_stats.port_id GROUP BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID), OverallAverage AS (SELECT AVG(avg_pairs_per_port) AS overall_avg_pairs_per_port FROM SecurityGroupAverages) SELECT i.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, i.pair_count_range, i.pair_count_variance, i.pair_count_stddev, sga.avg_pairs_per_port, (sga.avg_pairs_per_port - oa.overall_avg_pairs_per_port) AS deviation_from_overall_avg FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN SecurityGroupAverages AS sga ON i.SECURITY_GROUP_ID = sga.security_group_id CROSS JOIN OverallAverage AS oa ORDER BY i.pair_count_range DESC NULLS LAST), port_aap_stats AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT aap.MAC_ADDRESS AS allowed_mac_address, aap.IP_ADDRESS AS allowed_ip_address, p.ID AS port_id, sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID AS security_group_id, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY p.ID ORDER BY aap.IP_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN ALLOWEDADDRESSPAIRS AS aap ON p.ID = aap.PORT_ID WHERE p.ID = '25d421c1-c76d-4c26-9550-9f871a1d5034' ORDER BY aap.IP_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(window_column) AS avg_window_column FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.allowed_mac_address, i.allowed_ip_address, i.port_id, i.security_group_id, i.window_column, (i.window_column - a.avg_window_column) AS deviation_from_avg_window FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS a ORDER BY i.allowed_ip_address NULLS FIRST) SELECT pas.allowed_mac_address, pas.allowed_ip_address, pas.port_id, pas.security_group_id, pas.window_column, pas.deviation_from_avg_window, sgs.pair_count_range, sgs.pair_count_variance, sgs.pair_count_stddev, sgs.avg_pairs_per_port, sgs.deviation_from_overall_avg FROM port_aap_stats AS pas JOIN sg_stats AS sgs ON pas.security_group_id = sgs.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY pas.allowed_ip_address NULLS FIRST
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.ACTION, AVG(s.IP_VERSION) AS avg_ip_version, VAR_SAMP(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_variance, STDDEV(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_stddev FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 GROUP BY n.ACTION ORDER BY ip_version_variance DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT inner_cte.action, inner_cte.avg_ip_version, inner_cte.ip_version_variance, inner_cte.ip_version_stddev, overall_avg.avg_of_action_avg_ip_version FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT AVG(avg_ip_version) AS avg_of_action_avg_ip_version FROM inner_cte) AS overall_avg ON 1 = 1), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.ACTION, VAR_SAMP(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_variance FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY n.ACTION ORDER BY ip_version_variance DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT i.ACTION, i.ip_version_variance, COUNT(DISTINCT n.OBJECT_ID) AS total_networks_referenced, COUNT(DISTINCT s.ID) AS total_subnets_on_referenced_networks FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS n ON i.ACTION = n.ACTION JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY i.ACTION, i.ip_version_variance ORDER BY i.ip_version_variance DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10) SELECT q1.action, q1.avg_ip_version, q1.ip_version_variance, q1.ip_version_stddev, q1.avg_of_action_avg_ip_version, q2.total_networks_referenced, q2.total_subnets_on_referenced_networks FROM q1 JOIN q2 ON q1.action = q2.action WHERE q1.action = 'access_as_shared' ORDER BY q1.ip_version_variance DESC NULLS LAST
WITH RouteDetails AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT r.DESTINATION, r.NEXTHOP, s.NAME AS subnet_name, s.CIDR AS subnet_cidr, p.FIRST_IP, p.LAST_IP, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY r.SUBNET_ID ORDER BY r.SUBNET_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SUBNETROUTES AS r JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON r.SUBNET_ID = s.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONPOOLS AS p ON s.ID = p.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4), SubnetRouteCounts AS (SELECT s.ID AS subnet_id, COUNT(r.DESTINATION) AS total_routes FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN SUBNETROUTES AS r ON r.SUBNET_ID = s.ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 GROUP BY s.ID), AverageRoutes AS (SELECT AVG(total_routes) AS avg_routes FROM SubnetRouteCounts) SELECT i.DESTINATION, i.NEXTHOP, i.subnet_name, i.subnet_cidr, i.FIRST_IP, i.LAST_IP, i.window_column, src.total_routes, ar.avg_routes AS benchmark, CASE WHEN src.total_routes > ar.avg_routes * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_ROUTE_SUBNET' WHEN src.total_routes < ar.avg_routes * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_ROUTE_SUBNET' ELSE 'AVERAGE_ROUTE_SUBNET' END AS route_category FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON s.CIDR = i.subnet_cidr AND s.NAME = i.subnet_name JOIN SubnetRouteCounts AS src ON src.subnet_id = s.ID CROSS JOIN AverageRoutes AS ar), ActiveNetworkDetails AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE'), AvgMtu AS (SELECT AVG(mtu) AS avg_mtu FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.network_id, i.network_name, i.tenant_id, i.admin_state_up, i.mtu, i.standard_attr_updated_at, i.window_column, a.avg_mtu AS benchmark_avg_mtu, CASE WHEN i.mtu > a.avg_mtu THEN 'ABOVE_AVG_MTU' WHEN i.mtu < a.avg_mtu THEN 'BELOW_AVG_MTU' ELSE 'AT_AVG_MTU' END AS mtu_category FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN AvgMtu AS a) SELECT rd.DESTINATION, rd.NEXTHOP, rd.subnet_name, rd.subnet_cidr, rd.FIRST_IP, rd.LAST_IP, rd.window_column AS subnet_dense_rank, rd.total_routes, rd.benchmark AS avg_routes_benchmark, rd.route_category, andt.network_id, andt.network_name, andt.tenant_id, andt.admin_state_up, andt.mtu, andt.standard_attr_updated_at, andt.window_column AS tenant_network_rownum, andt.benchmark_avg_mtu, andt.mtu_category FROM RouteDetails AS rd JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON s.CIDR = rd.subnet_cidr AND s.NAME = rd.subnet_name JOIN ActiveNetworkDetails AS andt ON andt.network_id = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 ORDER BY andt.tenant_id NULLS FIRST, andt.network_name NULLS FIRST, rd.subnet_name NULLS FIRST, rd.DESTINATION NULLS FIRST
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, b.HOST AS host, b.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ia.IP_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), HostCounts AS (SELECT host, COUNT(DISTINCT ip_address) AS down_allocated_ips FROM inner_cte GROUP BY host), AverageHostCounts AS (SELECT AVG(down_allocated_ips) AS avg_down_allocated_ips FROM HostCounts) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.host, i.vif_type, i.ip_address, i.window_column, hc.down_allocated_ips, (hc.down_allocated_ips -
