ahc.avg_down_allocated_ips) AS deviation_from_avg FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN HostCounts AS hc ON i.host = hc.host CROSS JOIN AverageHostCounts AS ahc), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT COUNT(*) AS down_ports_with_port_security_enabled FROM PORTS AS p JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND psb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED = 1), TenantDownSecurePorts AS (SELECT p.TENANT_ID, COUNT(*) AS down_secure_ports FROM PORTS AS p JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND psb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED = 1 GROUP BY p.TENANT_ID), AverageDownSecurePorts AS (SELECT AVG(down_secure_ports) AS avg_down_secure_ports FROM TenantDownSecurePorts) SELECT tdsp.TENANT_ID, tdsp.down_secure_ports, adsp.avg_down_secure_ports AS benchmark, CASE WHEN tdsp.down_secure_ports > adsp.avg_down_secure_ports * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_USER' WHEN tdsp.down_secure_ports < adsp.avg_down_secure_ports * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_USER' ELSE 'AVERAGE_USER' END AS user_category, inner_cte.down_ports_with_port_security_enabled AS overall_down_secure_ports FROM TenantDownSecurePorts AS tdsp CROSS JOIN AverageDownSecurePorts AS adsp CROSS JOIN inner_cte) SELECT q1.port_id, q1.tenant_id, q1.host, q1.vif_type, q1.ip_address, q1.window_column, q1.down_allocated_ips, q1.deviation_from_avg, q2.down_secure_ports, q2.benchmark AS avg_down_secure_ports_benchmark, q2.user_category, q2.overall_down_secure_ports FROM q1 JOIN q2 ON q1.tenant_id = q2.tenant_id ORDER BY q1.host NULLS FIRST, q1.ip_address NULLS FIRST
WITH down_ports AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.NAME AS port_name, p.NETWORK_ID AS network_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY p.NETWORK_ID ORDER BY p.ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), NetworkDownAllocations AS (SELECT network_id, COUNT(DISTINCT ip_address) AS down_allocated_ips FROM inner_cte GROUP BY network_id), AverageNetworkDownAllocations AS (SELECT AVG(down_allocated_ips) AS avg_down_allocated_ips FROM NetworkDownAllocations) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.port_name, i.network_id, i.mac_address, i.device_owner, i.ip_address, i.window_column, nda.down_allocated_ips, (nda.down_allocated_ips - anda.avg_down_allocated_ips) AS deviation_from_avg FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN NetworkDownAllocations AS nda ON i.network_id = nda.network_id CROSS JOIN AverageNetworkDownAllocations AS anda), active_networks AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE'), AvgMtu AS (SELECT AVG(mtu) AS avg_mtu FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.network_id, i.network_name, i.tenant_id, i.admin_state_up, i.mtu, i.standard_attr_updated_at, i.window_column, a.avg_mtu AS benchmark_avg_mtu, CASE WHEN i.mtu > a.avg_mtu THEN 'ABOVE_AVG_MTU' WHEN i.mtu < a.avg_mtu THEN 'BELOW_AVG_MTU' ELSE 'AT_AVG_MTU' END AS mtu_category FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN AvgMtu AS a) SELECT dp.port_id, dp.tenant_id AS port_tenant_id, dp.port_name, dp.network_id, an.network_name, an.tenant_id AS network_tenant_id, an.admin_state_up, an.mtu, an.standard_attr_updated_at, an.window_column AS network_rownum_within_tenant, an.benchmark_avg_mtu, an.mtu_category, dp.mac_address, dp.device_owner, dp.ip_address, dp.window_column AS port_rownum_within_network, dp.down_allocated_ips, dp.deviation_from_avg FROM down_ports AS dp JOIN active_networks AS an ON dp.network_id = an.network_id
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (ORDER BY p.ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.NETWORK_ID IN ('0a1d0a27-cffa-4de3-92c5-9d3fd3f2e74d')) SELECT inner_cte.NAME, inner_cte.NETWORK_ID, inner_cte.MAC_ADDRESS, inner_cte.ADMIN_STATE_UP, inner_cte.STATUS, inner_cte.DEVICE_OWNER, inner_cte.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, inner_cte.window_column, owner_stats.total_ports_per_owner, avg_owner_stats.avg_ports_per_owner FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT DEVICE_OWNER, COUNT(*) AS total_ports_per_owner FROM inner_cte GROUP BY DEVICE_OWNER) AS owner_stats ON inner_cte.DEVICE_OWNER = owner_stats.DEVICE_OWNER JOIN (SELECT AVG(port_count) AS avg_ports_per_owner FROM (SELECT COUNT(*) AS port_count FROM inner_cte GROUP BY DEVICE_OWNER) AS sub) AS avg_owner_stats ON 1 = 1), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS min_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS spb ON p.ID = spb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(port_count) AS avg_port_count FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.port_status, i.device_owner, i.port_count, i.min_standard_attr_id FROM inner_cte AS i WHERE i.port_count > (SELECT avg_port_count FROM avg_cte)) SELECT q1.NAME, q1.NETWORK_ID, q1.MAC_ADDRESS, q1.ADMIN_STATE_UP, q1.STATUS, q1.DEVICE_OWNER, q1.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, q1.window_column, q1.total_ports_per_owner, q1.avg_ports_per_owner, q2.port_count, q2.min_standard_attr_id FROM q1 JOIN q2 ON q1.STATUS = q2.port_status AND q1.DEVICE_OWNER = q2.device_owner
WITH subq1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' AND NOT p.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER) SELECT inner_cte.port_status, inner_cte.device_owner, inner_cte.port_count, inner_cte.earliest_standard_attr_update, avg_groups.avg_port_count_across_groups FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT AVG(port_count) AS avg_port_count_across_groups FROM inner_cte) AS avg_groups ON 1 = 1), subq2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' AND NOT p.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(port_count) AS avg_port_count FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.port_status, i.device_owner, i.port_count, i.earliest_standard_attr_update, (i.port_count - a.avg_port_count) AS deviation_from_avg_port_count FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS a) SELECT s1.port_status, s1.device_owner, s1.port_count, s1.earliest_standard_attr_update, s1.avg_port_count_across_groups, s2.deviation_from_avg_port_count FROM subq1 AS s1 JOIN subq2 AS s2 ON s1.port_status = s2.port_status AND s1.device_owner = s2.device_owner

owa.overall_avg_window_column) AS deviation_from_overall_window_avg FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN VifTypeCounts AS vtc ON i.VIF_TYPE = vtc.VIF_TYPE CROSS JOIN OverallWindowAvg AS owa), down_ports AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, sgpb.security_group_id, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.security_group_id ORDER BY p.id NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN' AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT inner_cte.port_id, inner_cte.tenant_id, inner_cte.mac_address, inner_cte.security_group_id, inner_cte.window_column, tenant_down.total_down_ports_for_tenant, avg_down.avg_down_ports_per_tenant FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT tenant_id, COUNT(*) AS total_down_ports_for_tenant FROM ports WHERE status = 'DOWN' GROUP BY tenant_id) AS tenant_down ON inner_cte.tenant_id = tenant_down.tenant_id JOIN (SELECT AVG(down_port_count) AS avg_down_ports_per_tenant FROM (SELECT tenant_id, COUNT(*) AS down_port_count FROM ports WHERE status = 'DOWN' GROUP BY tenant_id) AS sub) AS avg_down ON 1 = 1) SELECT aup.PORT_ID AS admin_up_port_id, aup.HOST, aup.VIF_TYPE, aup.VNIC_TYPE, aup.TENANT_ID, aup.NETWORK_ID, aup.MAC_ADDRESS, aup.STATUS AS admin_up_port_status, aup.DEVICE_OWNER, aup.window_column AS admin_up_window_avg_standard_attr_id, aup.ports_in_vif_type, aup.deviation_from_overall_window_avg, dp.security_group_id, dp.window_column AS down_port_rank_within_security_group, dp.total_down_ports_for_tenant, dp.avg_down_ports_per_tenant FROM admin_up_ports AS aup JOIN down_ports AS dp ON aup.TENANT_ID = dp.tenant_id AND aup.MAC_ADDRESS = dp.mac_address
WITH down_ports_by_sg AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, sgpb.security_group_id, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.security_group_id ORDER BY p.id NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN' AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), SecurityGroupPorts AS (SELECT security_group_id, COUNT(port_id) AS total_ports FROM inner_cte GROUP BY security_group_id), AveragePorts AS (SELECT 'AVERAGE_USER' END AS user_category FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN SecurityGroupPorts AS sgp ON i.security_group_id = sgp.security_group_id CROSS JOIN AveragePorts AS ap), admin_up_ports_by_vif AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = i.DEVICE_OWNER, i.window_column, vtc.ports_in_vif_type, (i.window_column - owa.overall_avg_window_column) AS deviation_from_overall_window_avg FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN VifTypeCounts AS vtc ON i.VIF_TYPE = vtc.VIF_TYPE CROSS JOIN OverallWindowAvg AS owa) SELECT a.PORT_ID, a.TENANT_ID, a.NETWORK_ID, a.MAC_ADDRESS, a.HOST, a.VIF_TYPE, a.VNIC_TYPE, a.STATUS, a.DEVICE_OWNER, a.window_column AS WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.ACTION AS rbac_action, AVG(s.IP_VERSION) AS avg_ip_version, MAX(s.IP_VERSION) - MIN(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_range, VAR_SAMP(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_variance FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION IN (4, 6) AND n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY n.ACTION ORDER BY ip_version_range DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT rbac_action, avg_ip_version, ip_version_range, ip_version_variance FROM inner_cte WHERE avg_ip_version > 4 ORDER BY ip_version_range DESC NULLS LAST), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.ACTION AS rbac_action, AVG(s.IP_VERSION) AS avg_ip_version, MAX(s.IP_VERSION) - MIN(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_range, VAR_SAMP(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_variance FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION IN (4, 6) AND n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY n.ACTION ORDER BY ip_version_range DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT i.rbac_action, i.avg_ip_version, i.ip_version_range, i.ip_version_variance, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN s.ENABLE_DHCP = 1 THEN s.ID END) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_dhcp_enabled_subnets FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS n ON n.ACTION = i.rbac_action JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON s.NETWORK_ID = n.OBJECT_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION IN (4, 6) AND n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY i.rbac_action, i.avg_ip_version, i.ip_version_range, i.ip_version_variance ORDER BY i.ip_version_range DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10) SELECT q2.rbac_action, q2.avg_ip_version, q2.ip_version_range, q2.ip_version_variance, q2.has_dhcp_enabled_subnets FROM q2 JOIN q1 ON q1.rbac_action = q2.rbac_action ORDER BY q2.ip_version_range DESC NULLS LAST
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.name AS subnet_name, CAST(STDDEV(d.order) AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(AVG(d.order), 0), 0) AS coefficient_of_variation FROM subnets AS s JOIN dnsnameservers AS d ON d.subnet_id = s.id JOIN ipallocationpools AS p ON p.subnet_id = s.id JOIN networks AS n ON n.id = s.network_id WHERE s.ip_version = 4 AND NOT n.id IN (SELECT r.object_id FROM networkrbacs AS r WHERE r.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (r.target_tenant = '*' OR r.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY s.name HAVING AVG(d.order) > 0 ORDER BY coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT inner_cte.subnet_name, inner_cte.coefficient_of_variation, avg_cv.avg_coefficient_of_variation FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT AVG(coefficient_of_variation) AS avg_coefficient_of_variation FROM inner_cte) AS avg_cv ON 1 = 1 ORDER BY inner_cte.coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.name AS subnet_name, CAST(STDDEV(d.order) AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(AVG(d.order), 0), 0) AS coefficient_of_variation FROM subnets AS s JOIN dnsnameservers AS d ON d.subnet_id = s.id JOIN ipallocationpools AS p ON p.subnet_id = s.id JOIN networks AS n ON n.id = s.network_id WHERE s.ip_version = 4 AND NOT n.id IN (SELECT r.object_id FROM networkrbacs AS r WHERE r.action = SELECT i.subnet_name, i.coefficient_of_variation FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN subnets AS s ON s.name = i.subnet_name WHERE i.coefficient_of_variation > (SELECT AVG(coefficient_of_variation) FROM inner_cte) ORDER BY i.coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT q1.subnet_name, q1.coefficient_of_variation, q1.avg_coefficient_of_variation, q2.coefficient_of_variation AS above_avg_coefficient_of_variation FROM q1 LEFT JOIN q2 ON q2.subnet_name = q1.subnet_name ORDER BY q1.coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.ACTION, AVG(s.IP_VERSION) AS avg_ip_version, VAR_SAMP(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_variance, STDDEV(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_stddev FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY n.ACTION ORDER BY ip_version_variance DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT inner_cte.ACTION, inner_cte.avg_ip_version, inner_cte.ip_version_variance, inner_cte.ip_version_stddev, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN s.ENABLE_DHCP = 1 THEN s.ID END) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_dhcp_enabled_subnets FROM inner_cte JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS n ON n.ACTION = inner_cte.ACTION JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON s.NETWORK_ID = n.OBJECT_ID WHERE n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY inner_cte.ACTION, inner_cte.avg_ip_version, inner_cte.ip_version_variance, inner_cte.ip_version_stddev ORDER BY inner_cte.ip_version_variance DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.ACTION AS rbac_action, AVG(s.IP_VERSION) AS avg_ip_version, MAX(s.IP_VERSION) - MIN(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_range, VAR_SAMP(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_variance FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION IN (4, 6) AND n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY n.ACTION ORDER BY ip_version_range DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT i.rbac_action, i.avg_ip_version, i.ip_version_range, i.ip_version_variance, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN s.ENABLE_DHCP = 1 THEN s.ID END) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_dhcp_enabled_subnets FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS n ON n.ACTION = i.rbac_action JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON s.NETWORK_ID = n.OBJECT_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION IN (4, 6) AND n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY i.rbac_action, i.avg_ip_version, i.ip_version_range, i.ip_version_variance ORDER BY i.ip_version_range DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10) SELECT q2.rbac_action, q2.avg_ip_version, q2.ip_version_range, q2.ip_version_variance, q2.has_dhcp_enabled_subnets, q1.ip_version_stddev AS ipv4_only_ip_version_stddev FROM q2 JOIN q1 ON q1.ACTION = q2.rbac_action WHERE q2.ip_version_range > 0 ORDER BY q2.ip_version_range DESC NULLS LAST
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.status, MAX(ps.binding_count) - MIN(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_range, VAR_SAMP(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_variance, STDDEV(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_stddev FROM ports AS p JOIN (SELECT p2.id AS port_id, COUNT(*) AS binding_count FROM ports AS p2 JOIN portsecuritybindings AS b ON p2.id = b.port_id WHERE p2.tenant_id IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p2.id) AS ps ON p.id = ps.port_id GROUP BY p.status ORDER BY binding_count_range DESC NULLS LAST), status_network_ranges AS (SELECT p.status, p.network_id, MAX(ps.binding_count) - MIN(ps.binding_count) AS network_binding_count_range FROM ports AS p JOIN (SELECT p2.id AS port_id, COUNT(*) AS binding_count FROM ports AS p2 JOIN portsecuritybindings AS b ON p2.id = b.port_id WHERE p2.tenant_id IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p2.id) AS ps ON p.id = ps.port_id GROUP BY p.status, p.network_id) SELECT i.status, MAX(snr.network_binding_count_range) AS max_network_binding_count_range FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN status_network_ranges AS snr ON i.status = snr.status GROUP BY i.status ORDER BY max_network_binding_count_range DESC NULLS LAST), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.STATUS, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) AS min_admin_state_up, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS avg_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE b.VIF_TYPE = 'ovs' AND p.DEVICE_ID IN ('da811c75-0f9b-462c-b020-8ea55f6ca7c7') GROUP BY p.STATUS) SELECT i.status, COUNT(DISTINCT p2.id) AS total_ports, i.min_admin_state_up, i.avg_standard_attr_id FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN ports AS p2 ON p2.status = i.status JOIN ml2_port_bindings AS b2 ON p2.id = b2.port_id WHERE b2.vif_type = 'ovs' AND p2.device_id IN ('da811c75-0f9b-462c-b020-8ea55f6ca7c7') GROUP BY i.status, i.min_admin_state_up, i.avg_standard_attr_id ORDER BY total_ports DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10) SELECT q2.status, q2.total_ports, q2.min_admin_state_up, q2.avg_standard_attr_id, q1.max_network_binding_count_range FROM q2 LEFT JOIN q1 ON q2.status = q1.status ORDER BY q2.total_ports DESC NULLS LAST
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(ia.ip_address) AS ip_allocation_count, SUM(CASE WHEN NOT ia.subnet_id IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS subnet_id_rows FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON ia.port_id = p.id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND NOT ia.ip_address IS NULL AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address) SELECT p.network_id, MAX(inner_cte.ip_allocation_count) AS max_ip_allocations_on_a_port FROM inner_cte JOIN ports AS p ON p.id = inner_cte.port_id GROUP BY p.network_id), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.status, CAST(STDDEV(LENGTH(ia.ip_address)) AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(AVG(LENGTH(ia.ip_address)), 0), 0) AS coefficient_of_variation FROM portsecuritybindings AS psb JOIN ports AS p ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON p.id = ia.port_id WHERE psb.port_security_enabled = 1 AND ia.ip_address IN ('10.150.64.113/8') GROUP BY p.status HAVING AVG(LENGTH(ia.ip_address)) > 0 ORDER BY coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT ia.network_id, MAX(inner_cte.coefficient_of_variation) AS max_coefficient_of_variation FROM inner_cte JOIN ports AS p ON p.status = inner_cte.status JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON ia.port_id = p.id GROUP BY ia.network_id) SELECT q1.network_id, q1.max_ip_allocations_on_a_port, q2.max_coefficient_of_variation FROM q1 JOIN q2 ON q2.network_id = q1.network_id
WITH subq1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (ORDER BY p.ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.NETWORK_ID IN ('0a1d0a27-cffa-4de3-92c5-9d3fd3f2e74d')), avg_window AS (SELECT AVG(window_column) AS avg_window_column FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.NAME, i.NETWORK_ID, i.MAC_ADDRESS, i.ADMIN_STATE_UP, i.STATUS, i.DEVICE_OWNER, i.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, i.window_column, (i.window_column - a.avg_window_column) AS deviation_from_avg_window_min FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_window AS a), subq2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT inner_cte.port_id, inner_cte.tenant_id, inner_cte.mac_address, inner_cte.window_column, tenant_counts.total_down_ports_for_tenant, avg_counts.avg_down_ports_across_tenants FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT p.tenant_id AS tenant_id, COUNT(p.id) AS total_down_ports_for_tenant FROM ports AS p WHERE p.status = 'DOWN' GROUP BY p.tenant_id) AS tenant_counts ON inner_cte.tenant_id = tenant_counts.tenant_id JOIN (SELECT AVG(port_count) AS avg_down_ports_across_tenants FROM (SELECT COUNT(p.id) AS port_count FROM ports AS p WHERE p.status = 'DOWN' GROUP BY p.tenant_id) AS sub) AS avg_counts ON 1 = 1) SELECT subq1.NETWORK_ID, subq2.tenant_id, COUNT(*) AS joined_down_port_rows, AVG(subq1.deviation_from_avg_window_min) AS avg_deviation_from_avg_window_min, AVG(subq2.window_column) AS avg_running_sum_standard_attr_id_within_sg, MAX(subq2.total_down_ports_for_tenant) AS total_down_ports_for_tenant, MAX(subq2.avg_down_ports_across_tenants) AS avg_down_ports_across_tenants FROM subq1 JOIN subq2 ON subq1.MAC_ADDRESS = subq2.mac_address GROUP BY subq1.NETWORK_ID, subq2.tenant_id
WITH subq1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' AND NOT p.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER) SELECT inner_cte.port_status, inner_cte.device_owner, inner_cte.port_count, inner_cte.earliest_standard_attr_update, avg_groups.avg_port_count_across_groups FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT AVG(port_count) AS avg_port_count_across_groups FROM inner_cte) AS avg_groups ON 1 = 1), subq2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' AND NOT p.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(port_count) AS avg_port_count FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.port_status, i.device_owner, i.port_count, i.earliest_standard_attr_update, (i.port_count - a.avg_port_count) AS deviation_from_avg_port_count FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS a) SELECT s2.port_status, s2.device_owner, s2.port_count, s2.earliest_standard_attr_update, s1.avg_port_count_across_groups, s2.deviation_from_avg_port_count FROM subq2 AS s2 JOIN subq1 AS s1 ON s2.port_status = s1.port_status AND ((s2.device_owner = s1.device_owner) OR (s2.device_owner IS NULL AND s1.device_owner IS NULL)) WHERE s2.port_count > s1.avg_port_count_across_groups ORDER BY s2.port_count DESC NULLS LAST
WITH DnsPerPort AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.name AS port_name, COUNT(sgpb.security_group_id) AS security_group_binding_count, SUM(CASE WHEN pd.port_id IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END) AS dns_record_count FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id LEFT JOIN PORTDNSES AS pd ON p.id = pd.port_id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.name), AverageDns AS (SELECT AVG(dns_record_count) AS avg_dns_records FROM inner_cte) SELECT ic.port_id, ic.tenant_id, ic.port_name, ic.security_group_binding_count, ic.dns_record_count, ad.avg_dns_records AS benchmark, CASE WHEN ic.dns_record_count > ad.avg_dns_records * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_DNS' WHEN ic.dns_record_count < ad.avg_dns_records * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_DNS' ELSE 'AVERAGE_DNS' END AS dns_category FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN AverageDns AS ad), IpPerPort AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(ia.ip_address) AS ip_allocation_count, SUM(CASE WHEN NOT ia.subnet_id IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS subnet_id_rows FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON ia.port_id = p.id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND NOT ia.ip_address IS NULL AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address), AverageAllocations AS (SELECT AVG(ip_allocation_count) AS avg_ip_allocations FROM inner_cte) SELECT ic.port_id, ic.tenant_id, ic.mac_address, ic.ip_allocation_count, ic.subnet_id_rows, aa.avg_ip_allocations AS benchmark, CASE WHEN ic.ip_allocation_count > aa.avg_ip_allocations * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_IP' WHEN ic.ip_allocation_count < aa.avg_ip_allocations * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_IP' ELSE 'AVERAGE_IP' END AS ip_usage_category FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN AverageAllocations AS aa), CombinedPortSignals AS (SELECT dpp.port_id, dpp.tenant_id, 'DNS' AS signal_type, dpp.dns_category AS category, dpp.dns_record_count AS signal_count, dpp.benchmark AS benchmark FROM DnsPerPort AS dpp UNION ALL SELECT ipp.port_id, ipp.tenant_id, 'IP' AS signal_type, ipp.ip_usage_category AS category, ipp.ip_allocation_count AS signal_count, ipp.benchmark AS benchmark FROM IpPerPort AS ipp) SELECT port_id, tenant_id, signal_type, category, signal_count, benchmark FROM CombinedPortSignals ORDER BY tenant_id NULLS FIRST, port_id NULLS FIRST, signal_type NULLS FIRST

inner_cte AS i JOIN VifTypeAverages AS v ON v.VIF_TYPE = i.VIF_TYPE WHERE i.window_column > v.avg_window_column), DownPortsHighStdAttrInSecurityGroups AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, sgpb.security_group_id, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.security_group_id ORDER BY p.id NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p3 ON sgpb3.port_id = p3.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb3 ON p3.id = psb3.port_id GROUP BY sgpb3.security_group_id) AS sg_totals ON sg_totals.security_group_id = i.security_group_id WHERE sg_totals.total_ports_in_sg > (SELECT avg_ports_per_sg FROM SecurityGroupAverages)) SELECT nmd.network_id, nmd.network_name, nmd.tenant_id, nmd.admin_state_up AS network_admin_state_up, nmd.mtu, nmd.standard_attr_updated_at AS network_standard_attr_updated_at, nmd.window_column AS network_row_number_within_tenant, nmd.deviation_from_avg_mtu, psr.port_id, psr.mac_address, psr.security_group_id, psr.window_column AS port_rank_within_security_group FROM networks_mtu_detail AS nmd JOIN PORTS AS p ON p.network_id = nmd.network_id AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') JOIN ports_sg_ranked AS psr ON psr.port_id = p.id AND psr.tenant_id = p.tenant_id ORDER BY nmd.network_name NULLS FIRST, psr.security_group_id NULLS FIRST, psr.port_id NULLS FIRST
WITH port_security_stats AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.status, MAX(ps.binding_count) - MIN(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_range, VAR_SAMP(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_variance, STDDEV(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_stddev FROM ports AS p JOIN (SELECT p2.id AS port_id, COUNT(*) AS binding_count FROM ports AS p2 JOIN portsecuritybindings AS b ON p2.id = b.port_id WHERE p2.tenant_id IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p2.id) AS ps ON p.id = ps.port_id GROUP BY p.status ORDER BY binding_count_range DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT inner_cte.status, inner_cte.binding_count_range, inner_cte.binding_count_variance, inner_cte.binding_count_stddev, avg_bindings.avg_binding_count FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT AVG(binding_count) AS avg_binding_count FROM (SELECT p2.id AS port_id, COUNT(*) AS binding_count FROM ports AS p2 JOIN portsecuritybindings AS b ON p2.id = b.port_id WHERE p2.tenant_id IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p2.id) AS sub) AS avg_bindings ON 1 = 1 ORDER BY inner_cte.binding_count_range DESC NULLS LAST), security_group_port_stats AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS min_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS spb ON p.ID = spb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(port_count) AS avg_port_count FROM inner_cte) SELECT ic.port_status, ic.device_owner, ic.port_count, ic.min_standard_attr_id, ac.avg_port_count AS benchmark, CASE WHEN ic.port_count > ac.avg_port_count * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_GROUP' WHEN ic.port_count < ac.avg_port_count * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_GROUP' ELSE 'AVERAGE_GROUP' END AS group_category FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS ac) SELECT sgps.port_status, sgps.device_owner, sgps.port_count, sgps.min_standard_attr_id, sgps.benchmark AS avg_port_count_benchmark, sgps.group_category, pss.binding_count_range, pss.binding_count_variance, pss.binding_count_stddev, pss.avg_binding_count FROM security_group_port_stats AS sgps JOIN port_security_stats AS pss ON sgps.port_status = pss.status ORDER BY sgps.port_count DESC NULLS LAST
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.ACTION AS rbac_action, AVG(s.IP_VERSION) AS avg_ip_version, MAX(s.IP_VERSION) - MIN(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_range, VAR_SAMP(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_variance FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION IN (4, 6) AND n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY n.ACTION ORDER BY ip_version_range DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT rbac_action, avg_ip_version, ip_version_range, ip_version_variance FROM inner_cte WHERE avg_ip_version > 4 ORDER BY ip_version_range DESC NULLS LAST), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.ACTION, AVG(s.IP_VERSION) AS avg_ip_version, VAR_SAMP(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_variance, STDDEV(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_stddev FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY n.ACTION ORDER BY ip_version_variance DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT inner_cte.ACTION, inner_cte.avg_ip_version, inner_cte.ip_version_variance, inner_cte.ip_version_stddev, overall_avg.overall_avg_of_action_avgs FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT AVG(avg_ip_version) AS overall_avg_of_action_avgs FROM inner_cte) AS overall_avg ON 1 = 1) SELECT q1.rbac_action, q1.avg_ip_version AS avg_ip_version_ip4_or_ip6, q1.ip_version_range, q1.ip_version_variance AS ip_version_variance_ip4_or_ip6, q2.avg_ip_version AS avg_ip_version_ipv4_only, q2.ip_version_variance AS ip_version_variance_ipv4_only, q2.ip_version_stddev AS ip_version_stddev_ipv4_only, q2.overall_avg_of_action_avgs FROM q1 JOIN q2 ON q1.rbac_action = q2.ACTION
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.ACTION AS rbac_action, AVG(s.IP_VERSION) AS avg_ip_version, MAX(s.IP_VERSION) - MIN(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_range, VAR_SAMP(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_variance FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION IN (4, 6) AND n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY n.ACTION ORDER BY ip_version_range DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT i.rbac_action, i.avg_ip_version, i.ip_version_range, i.ip_version_variance, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN s.ENABLE_DHCP = 1 THEN s.ID END) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_dhcp_enabled_subnets FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS n ON n.ACTION = i.rbac_action JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON s.NETWORK_ID = n.OBJECT_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION IN (4, 6) AND n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY i.rbac_action, i.avg_ip_version, i.ip_version_range, i.ip_version_variance ORDER BY i.ip_version_range DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.ACTION, AVG(s.IP_VERSION) AS avg_ip_version, VAR_SAMP(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_variance, STDDEV(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_stddev FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY n.ACTION ORDER BY ip_version_variance DESC NULLS LAST), overall_avgs AS (SELECT AVG(avg_ip_version) AS overall_avg_ip_version, AVG(ip_version_variance) AS overall_avg_variance, AVG(ip_version_stddev) AS overall_avg_stddev FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.ACTION, i.avg_ip_version, i.ip_version_variance, i.ip_version_stddev, (i.avg_ip_version - o.overall_avg_ip_version) AS deviation_avg_ip_version, (i.ip_version_variance - o.overall_avg_variance) AS deviation_ip_version_variance, (i.ip_version_stddev - o.overall_avg_stddev) AS deviation_ip_version_stddev FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN overall_avgs AS o) SELECT q1.rbac_action, q1.avg_ip_version AS avg_ip_version_across_ip4_or_ip6, q1.ip_version_range, q1.ip_version_variance AS ip_version_variance_across_ip4_or_ip6, q1.has_dhcp_enabled_subnets, q2.avg_ip_version AS avg_ip_version_ip4_only, q2.ip_version_variance AS ip_version_variance_ip4_only, q2.ip_version_stddev AS ip_version_stddev_ip4_only, q2.deviation_avg_ip_version, q2.deviation_ip_version_variance, q2.deviation_ip_version_stddev FROM q1 JOIN q2 ON q2.ACTION = q1.rbac_action ORDER BY q1.ip_version_range DESC NULLS LAST
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' AND NOT p.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER) SELECT i.port_status, i.device_owner, i.port_count, i.earliest_standard_attr_update, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT f.id) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_floating_ips FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN ports AS p ON p.status = i.port_status AND p.device_owner = i.device_owner JOIN standardattributes AS sa ON sa.id = p.standard_attr_id LEFT JOIN floatingips AS f ON p.id = f.fixed_port_id WHERE sa.resource_type = 'ports' AND NOT p.network_id IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY i.port_status, i.device_owner, i.port_count, i.earliest_standard_attr_update ORDER BY i.port_count DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.status, MAX(ps.binding_count) - MIN(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_range, VAR_SAMP(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_variance, STDDEV(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_stddev FROM ports AS p JOIN (SELECT p2.id AS port_id, COUNT(*) AS binding_count FROM ports AS p2 JOIN portsecuritybindings AS b ON p2.id = b.port_id WHERE p2.tenant_id IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p2.id) AS ps ON p.id = ps.port_id GROUP BY p.status ORDER BY binding_count_range DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT i.status, i.binding_count_range, i.binding_count_variance, i.binding_count_stddev, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT f.id) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_floating_ips FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN ports AS p ON p.status = i.status LEFT JOIN floatingips AS f ON p.id = f.fixed_port_id WHERE p.tenant_id IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY i.status, i.binding_count_range, i.binding_count_variance, i.binding_count_stddev ORDER BY i.binding_count_range DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT q1.port_status, q1.device_owner, q1.port_count, q1.earliest_standard_attr_update, q1.has_floating_ips AS top10_combo_has_floating_ips, q2.binding_count_range, q2.binding_count_variance, q2.binding_count_stddev, q2.has_floating_ips AS tenant_ports_in_status_have_floating_ips FROM q1 JOIN q2 ON q2.status = q1.port_status ORDER BY q1.port_count DESC NULLS LAST
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.NAME AS subnet_name, s.CIDR AS subnet_cidr, n.TARGET_TENANT AS rbac_target_tenant, SUM(s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY s.IP_VERSION ORDER BY s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS n ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' AND n.TARGET_TENANT IN ('1ee6b5b4fe8c45cfa16c3ecdbb8ee02b')), AvgStdAttr AS (SELECT AVG(s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS avg_standard_attr_id FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS n ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' AND n.TARGET_TENANT IN ('1ee6b5b4fe8c45cfa16c3ecdbb8ee02b')) SELECT i.subnet_name, i.subnet_cidr, i.rbac_target_tenant, i.window_column, (s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID - a.avg_standard_attr_id) AS deviation_from_avg_standard_attr_id FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON s.NAME = i.subnet_name AND s.CIDR = i.subnet_cidr CROSS JOIN AvgStdAttr AS a), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.NAME AS subnet_name, s.CIDR AS subnet_cidr, n.TARGET_TENANT AS rbac_target_tenant, SUM(s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY s.IP_VERSION ORDER BY s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS n ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' AND n.TARGET_TENANT IN ('1ee6b5b4fe8c45cfa16c3ecdbb8ee02b')), IpVersionCounts AS (SELECT s.IP_VERSION, COUNT(*) AS total_subnets FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS n ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' AND n.TARGET_TENANT IN ('1ee6b5b4fe8c45cfa16c3ecdbb8ee02b') GROUP BY s.IP_VERSION), AverageSubnets AS (SELECT AVG(total_subnets) AS avg_subnets FROM IpVersionCounts) SELECT i.subnet_name, i.subnet_cidr, i.rbac_target_tenant, i.window_column, c.IP_VERSION, c.total_subnets, a.avg_subnets AS benchmark, CASE WHEN c.total_subnets > a.avg_subnets * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_SUBNET_USAGE' WHEN c.total_subnets < a.avg_subnets * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_SUBNET_USAGE' ELSE 'AVERAGE_SUBNET_USAGE' END AS usage_category FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN SUBNETS AS s2 ON s2.NAME = i.subnet_name AND s2.CIDR = i.subnet_cidr JOIN IpVersionCounts AS c ON c.IP_VERSION = s2.IP_VERSION CROSS JOIN AverageSubnets AS a) SELECT q2.IP_VERSION, q2.usage_category, COUNT(*) AS total_filtered_subnets, AVG(q1.deviation_from_avg_standard_attr_id) AS avg_deviation_from_avg_standard_attr_id, MAX(q2.window_column) AS max_running_sum_standard_attr_id, AVG(q2.benchmark) AS avg_benchmark_subnets_per_ip_version FROM q1 JOIN q2 ON q1.subnet_name = q2.subnet_name AND q1.subnet_cidr = q2.subnet_cidr AND q1.rbac_target_tenant = q2.rbac_target_tenant AND q1.window_column = q2.window_column GROUP BY q2.IP_VERSION, q2.usage_category ORDER BY total_filtered_subnets DESC NULLS LAST
WITH DownPortsAboveAvgRunningSum AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT port_id, tenant_id, mac_address, window_column FROM inner_cte WHERE window_column > (SELECT AVG(window_column) FROM inner_cte)), AdminUpPortsAboveVifTypeWindowAvg AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), VifTypeAverages AS (SELECT VIF_TYPE, AVG(window_column) AS avg_window_column FROM inner_cte GROUP BY VIF_TYPE) SELECT i.PORT_ID, i.HOST, i.VIF_TYPE, i.VNIC_TYPE, i.TENANT_ID, i.NETWORK_ID, i.MAC_ADDRESS, i.STATUS, i.DEVICE_OWNER, i.window_column FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN VifTypeAverages AS v ON v.VIF_TYPE = i.VIF_TYPE WHERE i.window_column > v.avg_window_column) SELECT a.PORT_ID, a.HOST, a.VIF_TYPE, a.VNIC_TYPE, a.TENANT_ID, a.NETWORK_ID, a.MAC_ADDRESS, a.STATUS, a.DEVICE_OWNER, a.window_column AS admin_up_window_avg_standard_attr_id FROM AdminUpPortsAboveVifTypeWindowAvg AS a WHERE a.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') AND a.PORT_ID IN (SELECT port_id FROM DownPortsAboveAvgRunningSum) ORDER BY a.NETWORK_ID NULLS FIRST, a.PORT_ID NULLS FIRST
WITH ip_status_variance AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.STATUS, VAR_SAMP(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS standard_attr_id_variance FROM PORTS AS p JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE NOT p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID IS NULL AND ia.IP_ADDRESS IN ('10.150.64.113/8') GROUP BY p.STATUS ORDER BY standard_attr_id_variance DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT inner_cte.status, inner_cte.standard_attr_id_variance, avg_vars.avg_standard_attr_id_variance FROM inner_cte, (SELECT AVG(standard_attr_id_variance) AS avg_standard_attr_id_variance FROM inner_cte) AS avg_vars), status_owner_volume AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' AND NOT p.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(port_count) AS avg_port_count FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.port_status, i.device_owner, i.port_count, i.earliest_standard_attr_update, a.avg_port_count AS benchmark, CASE WHEN i.port_count > a.avg_port_count * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_VOLUME' WHEN i.port_count < a.avg_port_count * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_VOLUME' ELSE 'AVERAGE_VOLUME' END AS volume_category FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS a) SELECT sov.port_status, sov.device_owner, sov.port_count, sov.earliest_standard_attr_update, sov.benchmark, sov.volume_category, isv.standard_attr_id_variance, isv.avg_standard_attr_id_variance FROM status_owner_volume AS sov JOIN ip_status_variance AS isv ON isv.status = sov.port_status
WITH SubnetRbacWindowAndDeviation AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.NAME AS subnet_name, s.CIDR AS subnet_cidr, n.TARGET_TENANT AS rbac_target_tenant, SUM(s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY s.IP_VERSION ORDER BY s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS n ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' AND n.TARGET_TENANT IN ('1ee6b5b4fe8c45cfa16c3ecdbb8ee02b')), AvgStdAttr AS (SELECT AVG(s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS avg_standard_attr_id FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS n ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' AND n.TARGET_TENANT IN ('1ee6b5b4fe8c45cfa16c3ecdbb8ee02b')) SELECT i.subnet_name, i.subnet_cidr, i.rbac_target_tenant, i.window_column, (s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID - a.avg_standard_attr_id) AS deviation_from_avg_standard_attr_id FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON s.NAME = i.subnet_name AND s.CIDR = i.subnet_cidr CROSS JOIN AvgStdAttr AS a), SubnetDnsCvAboveAvg AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.name AS subnet_name, CAST(STDDEV(d.order) AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(AVG(d.order), 0), 0) AS coefficient_of_variation FROM subnets AS s JOIN dnsnameservers AS d ON d.subnet_id = s.id JOIN ipallocationpools AS p ON p.subnet_id = s.id JOIN networks AS n ON n.id = s.network_id WHERE s.ip_version = 4 AND NOT n.id IN (SELECT r.object_id FROM networkrbacs AS r WHERE r.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (r.target_tenant = '*' OR r.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY s.name HAVING AVG(d.order) > 0 ORDER BY coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT i.subnet_name, i.coefficient_of_variation FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN subnets AS s ON s.name = i.subnet_name WHERE i.coefficient_of_variation > (SELECT AVG(coefficient_of_variation) FROM inner_cte) ORDER BY i.coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT s1.subnet_name, s1.subnet_cidr, s1.rbac_target_tenant, s1.window_column, s1.deviation_from_avg_standard_attr_id, s2.coefficient_of_variation FROM SubnetRbacWindowAndDeviation AS s1 JOIN SubnetDnsCvAboveAvg AS s2 ON s2.subnet_name = s1.subnet_name WHERE s1.rbac_target_tenant IN ('1ee6b5b4fe8c45cfa16c3ecdbb8ee02b') ORDER BY s2.coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST
WITH network_mtu_details AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE ELSE 'AVERAGE_MTU' END AS mtu_category FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN AverageMtu AS am), port_vif_details AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), VifTypeCounts AS (SELECT VIF_TYPE, COUNT(*) AS total_ports FROM inner_cte GROUP BY VIF_TYPE), AverageVifTypePorts AS (SELECT AVG(total_ports) AS avg_ports FROM VifTypeCounts) SELECT i.PORT_ID, i.HOST, i.VIF_TYPE, i.VNIC_TYPE, i.TENANT_ID, i.NETWORK_ID, i.MAC_ADDRESS, i.STATUS, i.DEVICE_OWNER, i.window_column, vtc.total_ports, avp.avg_ports AS benchmark, CASE WHEN vtc.total_ports > avp.avg_ports * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_USER' WHEN vtc.total_ports < avp.avg_ports * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_USER' ELSE 'AVERAGE_USER' END AS user_category FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN VifTypeCounts AS vtc ON i.VIF_TYPE = vtc.VIF_TYPE CROSS JOIN AverageVifTypePorts AS avp) SELECT nmd.network_id, nmd.network_name, nmd.tenant_id, nmd.admin_state_up, nmd.mtu, nmd.standard_attr_updated_at, nmd.window_column AS network_row_number_within_tenant, nmd.benchmark_mtu, nmd.mtu_category, pvd.PORT_ID, pvd.HOST, pvd.VIF_TYPE, pvd.VNIC_TYPE, pvd.MAC_ADDRESS, pvd.STATUS AS port_status, pvd.DEVICE_OWNER, pvd.window_column AS port_std_attr_id_window_avg, pvd.total_ports AS total_ports_for_vif_type, pvd.benchmark AS avg_ports_per_vif_type_benchmark, pvd.user_category AS vif_type_user_category FROM network_mtu_details AS nmd JOIN port_vif_details AS pvd ON pvd.NETWORK_ID = nmd.network_id AND pvd.TENANT_ID = nmd.tenant_id WHERE nmd.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')
WITH down_ports AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT port_id, tenant_id, mac_address, window_column FROM inner_cte WHERE window_column > (SELECT AVG(window_column) FROM inner_cte)), admin_up_ports AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), VifTypeAverages AS (SELECT VIF_TYPE, AVG(window_column) AS avg_window_column FROM inner_cte GROUP BY VIF_TYPE) SELECT i.PORT_ID, i.HOST, i.VIF_TYPE, i.VNIC_TYPE, i.TENANT_ID, i.NETWORK_ID, i.MAC_ADDRESS, i.STATUS, i.DEVICE_OWNER, i.window_column FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN VifTypeAverages AS v ON v.VIF_TYPE = i.VIF_TYPE WHERE i.window_column > v.avg_window_column) SELECT a.PORT_ID, a.HOST, a.VIF_TYPE, a.VNIC_TYPE, a.TENANT_ID, a.NETWORK_ID, a.MAC_ADDRESS, a.STATUS, a.DEVICE_OWNER, a.window_column AS admin_up_windowed_avg_standard_attr_id, d.window_column AS down_running_sum_standard_attr_id FROM admin_up_ports AS a JOIN down_ports AS d ON d.port_id = a.PORT_ID ORDER BY a.window_column DESC NULLS LAST, d.window_column DESC NULLS LAST
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.ACTION AS rbac_action, AVG(s.IP_VERSION) AS avg_ip_version, MAX(s.IP_VERSION) - MIN(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_range, VAR_SAMP(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_variance FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION IN (4, 6) AND n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY n.ACTION ORDER BY ip_version_range DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT rbac_action, avg_ip_version, ip_version_range, ip_version_variance FROM inner_cte WHERE avg_ip_version > 4 ORDER BY ip_version_range DESC NULLS LAST), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.ACTION, AVG(s.IP_VERSION) AS avg_ip_version, VAR_SAMP(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_variance, STDDEV(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_stddev FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY n.ACTION ORDER BY ip_version_variance DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT i.ACTION, MAX(i.avg_ip_version) AS max_avg_ip_version, MAX(i.ip_version_variance) AS max_ip_version_variance, MAX(i.ip_version_stddev) AS max_ip_version_stddev FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS n2 ON n2.ACTION = i.ACTION GROUP BY i.ACTION) SELECT q1.rbac_action, q1.avg_ip_version, q1.ip_version_range, q1.ip_version_variance, q2.max_avg_ip_version, q2.max_ip_version_variance, q2.max_ip_version_stddev FROM q1 JOIN q2 ON q2.ACTION = q1.rbac_action ORDER BY q1.ip_version_range DESC NULLS LAST

subnet_id_rows FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON ia.port_id = p.id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND NOT ia.ip_address IS NULL AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.mac_address, i.ip_allocation_count, i.subnet_id_rows, CASE WHEN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p.tenant_id, inner_cte.down_ports_with_port_security_enabled) SELECT s1.port_id, s1.tenant_id AS tenant_id_admin_up_top_ip_allocations, s1.mac_address, s2.has_floating_ips AS down_ports_have_any_floating_ips FROM subq1 AS s1 CROSS JOIN subq2 AS s2
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.STATUS, VAR_SAMP(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS standard_attr_id_variance FROM PORTS AS p JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE NOT p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID IS NULL AND ia.IP_ADDRESS IN ('10.150.64.113/8') GROUP BY p.STATUS ORDER BY standard_attr_id_variance DESC NULLS LAST), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(standard_attr_id_variance) AS avg_variance FROM inner_cte), variance_by_status AS (SELECT i.STATUS, i.standard_attr_id_variance, a.avg_variance AS benchmark, CASE WHEN i.standard_attr_id_variance > a.avg_variance * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_VARIANCE' WHEN i.standard_attr_id_variance < a.avg_variance * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_VARIANCE' ELSE 'AVERAGE_VARIANCE' END AS variance_category FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS a), inner_cte2 AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' AND NOT p.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), above_avg_status_owner AS (SELECT port_status, device_owner, port_count, earliest_standard_attr_update FROM inner_cte2 WHERE port_count > (SELECT AVG(port_count) FROM inner_cte2)) SELECT v.STATUS AS port_status, v.standard_attr_id_variance, v.benchmark, v.variance_category, a.device_owner, a.port_count, a.earliest_standard_attr_update FROM variance_by_status AS v JOIN above_avg_status_owner AS a ON a.port_status = v.STATUS
WITH up_ports AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), VifTypeCounts AS (SELECT VIF_TYPE, COUNT(*) AS ports_in_vif_type FROM inner_cte GROUP BY VIF_TYPE), OverallWindowAvg AS (SELECT AVG(window_column) AS overall_avg_window_column FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.PORT_ID, i.HOST, i.VIF_TYPE, i.VNIC_TYPE, i.TENANT_ID, i.NETWORK_ID, i.MAC_ADDRESS, i.STATUS, i.DEVICE_OWNER, i.window_column, vtc.ports_in_vif_type, (i.window_column - owa.overall_avg_window_column) AS deviation_from_overall_window_avg FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN VifTypeCounts AS vtc ON i.VIF_TYPE = vtc.VIF_TYPE CROSS JOIN OverallWindowAvg AS owa), down_ports AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, sgpb.security_group_id, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.security_group_id ORDER BY p.id NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN' AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), avg_rank_cte AS (SELECT AVG(window_column) AS avg_rank FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.mac_address, i.security_group_id, i.window_column AS rank_within_security_group, (i.window_column - WITH DownPortsRanked AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, sgpb.security_group_id, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.security_group_id ORDER BY p.id NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN' AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT port_id, tenant_id, mac_address, security_group_id, window_column FROM inner_cte WHERE window_column > (SELECT AVG(window_column) FROM inner_cte)), AdminUpPortsIpStats AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(ia.ip_address) AS ip_allocation_count, SUM(CASE WHEN NOT ia.subnet_id IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS subnet_id_rows FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON ia.port_id = p.id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND NOT ia.ip_address IS NULL AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address) SELECT inner_cte.port_id, inner_cte.tenant_id, inner_cte.mac_address, inner_cte.ip_allocation_count, inner_cte.subnet_id_rows, COUNT(DISTINCT ia2.subnet_id) AS distinct_subnet_count FROM inner_cte JOIN ipallocations AS ia2 ON ia2.port_id = inner_cte.port_id WHERE NOT ia2.ip_address IS NULL GROUP BY inner_cte.port_id, inner_cte.tenant_id, inner_cte.mac_address, inner_cte.ip_allocation_count, inner_cte.subnet_id_rows HAVING inner_cte.ip_allocation_count > 0 AND (CAST(COUNT(DISTINCT ia2.subnet_id) * 1.0 AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(inner_cte.ip_allocation_count, 0)) > 0.75) SELECT (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM DownPortsRanked) AS Down_Port_Count_Above_Avg_Rank, (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM AdminUpPortsIpStats) AS Admin_Up_Port_Count_With_Distinct_Subnet_Ratio_Above_75pct

AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), VifTypeCounts AS (SELECT VIF_TYPE, COUNT(*) AS total_ports FROM inner_cte GROUP BY top10_ovs_device_status_count FROM q1 CROSS JOIN q2
WITH networks_subq AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE' AND n.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), avg_mtu_cte AS (SELECT AVG(mtu) AS avg_mtu FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.network_id, i.network_name, i.tenant_id, i.admin_state_up, i.mtu, i.standard_attr_updated_at, i.window_column, (i.mtu - a.avg_mtu) AS deviation_from_avg_mtu FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_mtu_cte AS a), ports_subq AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), VifTypeCounts AS (SELECT VIF_TYPE, COUNT(*) AS ports_in_vif_type FROM inner_cte GROUP BY VIF_TYPE), OverallWindowAvg AS (SELECT AVG(window_column) AS overall_avg_window_column FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.PORT_ID, i.HOST, i.VIF_TYPE, i.VNIC_TYPE, i.TENANT_ID, i.NETWORK_ID, i.MAC_ADDRESS, i.STATUS, i.DEVICE_OWNER, i.window_column, vtc.ports_in_vif_type, (i.window_column -
