WITH shared_subnet_outliers AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.id AS network_id, SUM(1) AS total_subnets FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r ON n.id = r.object_id JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON r.object_id = s.network_id WHERE n.status = 'ACTIVE' AND r.action = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY n.id HAVING SUM(1) > (SELECT AVG(subnet_cnt) + STDDEV(subnet_cnt) FROM (SELECT n2.id AS network_id, SUM(1) AS subnet_cnt FROM NETWORKS AS n2 JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r2 ON n2.id = r2.object_id JOIN SUBNETS AS s2 ON r2.object_id = s2.network_id WHERE n2.status = 'ACTIVE' AND r2.action = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY n2.id) AS per_network)) SELECT inner_cte.network_id, inner_cte.total_subnets, stats.avg_subnet_count FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT AVG(subnet_cnt) AS avg_subnet_count FROM (SELECT n2.id AS network_id, SUM(1) AS subnet_cnt FROM NETWORKS AS n2 JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r2 ON n2.id = r2.object_id JOIN SUBNETS AS s2 ON r2.object_id = s2.network_id WHERE n2.status = 'ACTIVE' AND r2.action = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY n2.id) AS per_network) AS stats ON 1 = 1), nonshared_active_network_ranks AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.name, n.status, n.admin_state_up, r.action, r.target_tenant, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY n.id ORDER BY n.id NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM networksecuritybindings AS b JOIN networks AS n ON b.network_id = n.id JOIN networkrbacs AS r ON n.id = r.object_id WHERE n.status = 'ACTIVE' AND NOT n.id IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b'))) SELECT n.id AS network_id, MAX(inner_cte.window_column) AS max_dense_rank FROM inner_cte JOIN networks AS n ON n.name = inner_cte.name AND n.status = inner_cte.status AND n.admin_state_up = inner_cte.admin_state_up GROUP BY n.id) SELECT sso.network_id, sso.total_subnets, sso.avg_subnet_count, nar.max_dense_rank FROM shared_subnet_outliers AS sso JOIN nonshared_active_network_ranks AS nar ON nar.network_id = sso.network_id
WITH PortDnsAndSg AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.name AS port_name, COUNT(sgpb.security_group_id) AS security_group_binding_count, SUM(CASE WHEN pd.port_id IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END) AS dns_record_count FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id LEFT JOIN PORTDNSES AS pd ON p.id = pd.port_id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.name), AverageDns AS (SELECT AVG(dns_record_count) AS avg_dns_records FROM inner_cte) SELECT ic.port_id, ic.tenant_id, ic.port_name, ic.security_group_binding_count, ic.dns_record_count, ad.avg_dns_records AS benchmark, CASE WHEN ic.dns_record_count > ad.avg_dns_records * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_DNS' WHEN ic.dns_record_count < ad.avg_dns_records * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_DNS' ELSE 'AVERAGE_DNS' END AS dns_category FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN AverageDns AS ad), ActiveNetworksMtu AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE' AND n.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), avg_mtu_cte AS (SELECT AVG(mtu) AS avg_mtu FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.network_id, i.network_name, i.tenant_id, i.admin_state_up, i.mtu, i.standard_attr_updated_at, i.window_column, (i.mtu - a.avg_mtu) AS deviation_from_avg_mtu FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_mtu_cte AS a) SELECT an.network_id, an.network_name, an.tenant_id, an.admin_state_up AS network_admin_state_up, an.mtu, an.standard_attr_updated_at, an.window_column, an.deviation_from_avg_mtu, COUNT(DISTINCT pds.port_id) AS admin_up_sg_bound_port_count, AVG(pds.dns_record_count) AS avg_dns_records_per_port, SUM(CASE WHEN pds.dns_category = 'HIGH_DNS' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS high_dns_port_count, SUM(CASE WHEN pds.dns_category = 'LOW_DNS' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS low_dns_port_count, SUM(CASE WHEN pds.dns_category = 'AVERAGE_DNS' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS average_dns_port_count FROM ActiveNetworksMtu AS an JOIN PORTS AS p ON p.network_id = an.network_id AND p.tenant_id = an.tenant_id AND p.admin_state_up = 1 JOIN PortDnsAndSg AS pds ON pds.port_id = p.id AND pds.tenant_id = an.tenant_id GROUP BY an.network_id, an.network_name, an.tenant_id, an.admin_state_up, an.mtu, an.standard_attr_updated_at, an.window_column, an.deviation_from_avg_mtu ORDER BY an.tenant_id NULLS FIRST, an.network_name NULLS FIRST
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (ORDER BY p.ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.NETWORK_ID IN ('0a1d0a27-cffa-4de3-92c5-9d3fd3f2e74d')) SELECT inner_cte.NAME, inner_cte.NETWORK_ID, inner_cte.MAC_ADDRESS, inner_cte.ADMIN_STATE_UP, inner_cte.STATUS, inner_cte.DEVICE_OWNER, inner_cte.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, inner_cte.window_column, owner_stats.total_ports_per_owner, avg_owner_stats.avg_ports_per_owner FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT DEVICE_OWNER, COUNT(*) AS total_ports_per_owner FROM inner_cte GROUP BY DEVICE_OWNER) AS owner_stats ON inner_cte.DEVICE_OWNER = owner_stats.DEVICE_OWNER JOIN (SELECT AVG(port_count) AS avg_ports_per_owner FROM (SELECT COUNT(*) AS port_count FROM inner_cte GROUP BY DEVICE_OWNER) AS sub) AS avg_owner_stats ON 1 = 1), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT COUNT(*) AS down_ports_with_port_security_enabled FROM PORTS AS p JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND psb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7')), NetworkCounts AS (SELECT p.NETWORK_ID AS network_id, COUNT(*) AS down_ports_with_port_security_enabled FROM PORTS AS p JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND psb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p.NETWORK_ID), AverageCounts AS (SELECT AVG(down_ports_with_port_security_enabled) AS avg_down_ports_with_port_security_enabled FROM NetworkCounts) SELECT nc.network_id, nc.down_ports_with_port_security_enabled, (nc.down_ports_with_port_security_enabled - ac.avg_down_ports_with_port_security_enabled) AS deviation_from_avg FROM NetworkCounts AS nc CROSS JOIN AverageCounts AS ac) SELECT q1.NAME, q1.NETWORK_ID, q1.MAC_ADDRESS, q1.ADMIN_STATE_UP, q1.STATUS, q1.DEVICE_OWNER, q1.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, q1.window_column, q1.total_ports_per_owner, q1.avg_ports_per_owner, q2.down_ports_with_port_security_enabled, q2.deviation_from_avg FROM q1 JOIN q2 ON q1.NETWORK_ID = q2.network_id
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, sgpb.security_group_id, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.security_group_id ORDER BY p.id NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN' AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT CASE WHEN LAG(security_group_id) OVER (ORDER BY security_group_id NULLS FIRST, port_id NULLS FIRST) = security_group_id THEN NULL ELSE security_group_id END AS security_group_id, port_id, tenant_id, mac_address, window_column AS port_rank_within_security_group FROM inner_cte ORDER BY security_group_id NULLS FIRST, port_id NULLS FIRST), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, sgpb.security_group_id, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.security_group_id ORDER BY p.id NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN' AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), SecurityGroupPorts AS (SELECT security_group_id, COUNT(port_id) AS total_ports FROM inner_cte GROUP BY security_group_id), AveragePorts AS (SELECT AVG(total_ports) AS avg_ports FROM SecurityGroupPorts) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.mac_address, i.security_group_id, i.window_column AS port_rank_within_security_group, sgp.total_ports AS total_down_ports_in_security_group, ap.avg_ports AS benchmark, CASE WHEN sgp.total_ports > ap.avg_ports * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_USER' WHEN sgp.total_ports < ap.avg_ports * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_USER' ELSE 'AVERAGE_USER' END AS user_category FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN SecurityGroupPorts AS sgp ON i.security_group_id = sgp.security_group_id CROSS JOIN AveragePorts AS ap) SELECT q2.security_group_id, q2.total_down_ports_in_security_group, q2.benchmark, q2.user_category, q1.port_id, q1.tenant_id, q1.mac_address, q1.port_rank_within_security_group FROM q2 JOIN q1 ON q2.port_id = q1.port_id ORDER BY q2.security_group_id NULLS FIRST, q1.port_id NULLS FIRST
WITH MaxPortsPerStatus AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' AND NOT p.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER) SELECT port_status, MAX(port_count) AS max_ports_for_any_device_owner FROM inner_cte GROUP BY port_status), PortSecurityBindingSpreadByStatus AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.status, MAX(ps.binding_count) - MIN(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_range, VAR_SAMP(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_variance, STDDEV(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_stddev FROM ports AS p JOIN (SELECT p2.id AS port_id, COUNT(*) AS binding_count FROM ports AS p2 JOIN portsecuritybindings AS b ON p2.id = b.port_id WHERE p2.tenant_id IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p2.id) AS ps ON p.id = ps.port_id GROUP BY p.status ORDER BY binding_count_range DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT ic.status, ic.binding_count_range, ic.binding_count_variance, ic.binding_count_stddev, CASE WHEN ic.binding_count_range >= 5 THEN 'HIGH_SPREAD' ELSE 'LOW_SPREAD' END AS spread_category FROM inner_cte AS ic JOIN ports AS p3 ON p3.status = ic.status GROUP BY ic.status, ic.binding_count_range, ic.binding_count_variance, ic.binding_count_stddev ORDER BY ic.binding_count_range DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT mpps.port_status, mpps.max_ports_for_any_device_owner, psbs.binding_count_range, psbs.binding_count_variance, psbs.binding_count_stddev, psbs.spread_category FROM MaxPortsPerStatus AS mpps JOIN PortSecurityBindingSpreadByStatus AS psbs ON psbs.status = mpps.port_status ORDER BY psbs.binding_count_range DESC NULLS LAST
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.status, CAST(STDDEV(LENGTH(ia.ip_address)) AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(AVG(LENGTH(ia.ip_address)), 0), 0) AS coefficient_of_variation FROM portsecuritybindings AS psb JOIN ports AS p ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON p.id = ia.port_id WHERE psb.port_security_enabled = 1 AND ia.ip_address IN ('10.150.64.113/8') GROUP BY p.status HAVING AVG(LENGTH(ia.ip_address)) > 0 ORDER BY coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(coefficient_of_variation) AS avg_coefficient_of_variation FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.status, i.coefficient_of_variation, (i.coefficient_of_variation - a.avg_coefficient_of_variation) AS deviation_from_avg_coefficient_of_variation FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS a ORDER BY deviation_from_avg_coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.STATUS, VAR_SAMP(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS standard_attr_id_variance FROM PORTS AS p JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE NOT p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID IS NULL AND ia.IP_ADDRESS IN ('10.150.64.113/8') GROUP BY p.STATUS ORDER BY standard_attr_id_variance DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT inner_cte.status, inner_cte.standard_attr_id_variance, avg_vars.avg_standard_attr_id_variance FROM inner_cte, (SELECT AVG(standard_attr_id_variance) AS avg_standard_attr_id_variance FROM inner_cte) AS avg_vars) SELECT q1.status, q1.coefficient_of_variation, q1.deviation_from_avg_coefficient_of_variation, q2.standard_attr_id_variance, q2.avg_standard_attr_id_variance FROM q1 JOIN q2 ON q1.status = q2.status ORDER BY q1.deviation_from_avg_coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.status, CAST(STDDEV(p.standard_attr_id) AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(AVG(p.standard_attr_id), 0), 0) AS coefficient_of_variation FROM securitygroupportbindings AS sgpb JOIN ports AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.tenant_id IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p.status HAVING AVG(p.standard_attr_id) > 0 ORDER BY coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT i.status, i.coefficient_of_variation, COUNT(DISTINCT p.id) AS total_ports, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT f.id) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_floating_ips FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN ports AS p ON p.status = i.status AND p.tenant_id IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') JOIN securitygroupportbindings AS sgpb ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON psb.port_id = p.id LEFT JOIN floatingips AS f ON f.fixed_port_id = p.id GROUP BY i.status, i.coefficient_of_variation ORDER BY i.coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS min_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS spb ON p.ID = spb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER) SELECT i.port_status, i.device_owner, i.port_count, i.min_standard_attr_id, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT spb2.security_group_id) > COUNT(DISTINCT p2.id) THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_multi_sg_bindings FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN ports AS p2 ON p2.status = i.port_status AND p2.device_owner = i.device_owner AND p2.tenant_id IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') JOIN securitygroupportbindings AS spb2 ON p2.id = spb2.port_id GROUP BY i.port_status, i.device_owner, i.port_count, i.min_standard_attr_id ORDER BY i.port_count DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10) SELECT q2.port_status, q2.device_owner, q2.port_count, q2.min_standard_attr_id, q2.has_multi_sg_bindings, q1.coefficient_of_variation, q1.total_ports, q1.has_floating_ips FROM q2 JOIN q1 ON q1.status = q2.port_status ORDER BY q2.port_count DESC NULLS LAST, q2.device_owner NULLS FIRST
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT port_id, tenant_id, mac_address, window_column FROM inner_cte WHERE window_column > (SELECT AVG(window_column) FROM inner_cte)), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT inner_cte.port_id, inner_cte.tenant_id, inner_cte.mac_address, sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID AS security_group_id, inner_cte.window_column FROM inner_cte JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb ON inner_cte.port_id = sgpb.PORT_ID WHERE inner_cte.window_column > 100 ORDER BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID NULLS FIRST, inner_cte.window_column NULLS FIRST) SELECT q2.security_group_id, COUNT(DISTINCT q2.port_id) AS ports_meeting_both_thresholds, AVG(q2.window_column) AS avg_running_sum_for_these_ports FROM q2 JOIN q1 ON q2.port_id = q1.port_id AND q2.tenant_id = q1.tenant_id AND q2.mac_address = q1.mac_address AND q2.window_column = q1.window_column GROUP BY q2.security_group_id ORDER BY ports_meeting_both_thresholds DESC NULLS LAST, q2.security_group_id NULLS FIRST
WITH Subquery1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, p.TENANT_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE b.HOST = 'drive-59' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7')) SELECT i.PORT_ID, i.TENANT_ID, i.MAC_ADDRESS, b.VIF_TYPE, i.window_column, (SELECT AVG(window_column) FROM inner_cte) AS avg_window_column FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON i.PORT_ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE i.window_column > (SELECT AVG(window_column) FROM inner_cte)), Subquery2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(sa.ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sa.RESOURCE_TYPE ORDER BY sa.ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7')) SELECT port_id, tenant_id, mac_address, window_column FROM inner_cte WHERE window_column > 1000) SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT s1.PORT_ID) AS Ports_On_drive_59_Above_Avg_VIF_RunningSum_Count, COUNT(DISTINCT s2.port_id) AS DOWN_Ports_Above_1000_ResourceType_RunningSum_Count FROM Subquery1 AS s1 CROSS JOIN Subquery2 AS s2
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.ACTION, STDDEV(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_stddev FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.ENABLE_DHCP = 1 AND NOT s.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY n.ACTION HAVING COUNT(*) > 5 ORDER BY ip_version_stddev DESC NULLS LAST), inner_cte_2 AS (SELECT n.ACTION AS rbac_action, AVG(s.IP_VERSION) AS avg_ip_version, MAX(s.IP_VERSION) - MIN(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_range, VAR_SAMP(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_variance FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION IN (4, 6) AND n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY n.ACTION ORDER BY ip_version_range DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT q1.ACTION, q1.max_ip_version_stddev, q2.avg_ip_version, q2.ip_version_range, q2.ip_version_variance, q2.has_dhcp_enabled_subnets FROM (SELECT n.ACTION, MAX(inner_cte.ip_version_stddev) AS max_ip_version_stddev FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID JOIN inner_cte ON inner_cte.ACTION = n.ACTION WHERE s.ENABLE_DHCP = 1 AND NOT s.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY n.ACTION ORDER BY max_ip_version_stddev DESC NULLS LAST) AS q1 JOIN (SELECT i.rbac_action, i.avg_ip_version, i.ip_version_range, i.ip_version_variance, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN s.ENABLE_DHCP = 1 THEN s.ID END) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_dhcp_enabled_subnets FROM inner_cte_2 AS i JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS n ON n.ACTION = i.rbac_action JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON s.NETWORK_ID = n.OBJECT_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION IN (4, 6) AND n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY i.rbac_action, i.avg_ip_version, i.ip_version_range, i.ip_version_variance ORDER BY i.ip_version_range DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10) AS q2 ON q1.ACTION = q2.rbac_action ORDER BY q1.max_ip_version_stddev DESC NULLS LAST
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.ACTION, AVG(s.IP_VERSION) AS avg_ip_version, VAR_SAMP(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_variance, STDDEV(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_stddev FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY n.ACTION ORDER BY ip_version_variance DESC NULLS LAST), overall_avgs AS (SELECT AVG(avg_ip_version) AS overall_avg_ip_version, AVG(ip_version_variance) AS overall_avg_variance, AVG(ip_version_stddev) AS overall_avg_stddev FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.ACTION, i.avg_ip_version, i.ip_version_variance, i.ip_version_stddev, (i.avg_ip_version - o.overall_avg_ip_version) AS deviation_avg_ip_version, (i.ip_version_variance - o.overall_avg_variance) AS deviation_ip_version_variance, (i.ip_version_stddev - o.overall_avg_stddev) AS deviation_ip_version_stddev FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN overall_avgs AS o), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.ACTION, VAR_SAMP(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_variance FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' AND n.TARGET_TENANT = '*' GROUP BY n.ACTION ORDER BY ip_version_variance DESC NULLS LAST), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(ip_version_variance) AS avg_ip_version_variance FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.ACTION, i.ip_version_variance, a.avg_ip_version_variance AS benchmark, CASE WHEN i.ip_version_variance > a.avg_ip_version_variance * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_VARIANCE' WHEN i.ip_version_variance < a.avg_ip_version_variance * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_VARIANCE' ELSE 'AVERAGE_VARIANCE' END AS variance_category FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS a) SELECT q1.ACTION, q1.avg_ip_version, q1.ip_version_variance AS ip_version_variance_ipv4_only, q1.ip_version_stddev, q1.deviation_avg_ip_version, q1.deviation_ip_version_variance, q1.deviation_ip_version_stddev, q2.ip_version_variance AS ip_version_variance_public_access, q2.benchmark AS variance_benchmark_across_actions, q2.variance_category FROM q1 JOIN q2 ON q1.ACTION = q2.ACTION ORDER BY q2.ip_version_variance DESC NULLS LAST
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), VifTypeCounts AS (SELECT VIF_TYPE, COUNT(*) AS total_ports FROM inner_cte GROUP BY VIF_TYPE), AverageVifTypePorts AS (SELECT AVG(total_ports) AS avg_ports FROM VifTypeCounts) SELECT i.PORT_ID, i.HOST, i.VIF_TYPE, i.VNIC_TYPE, i.TENANT_ID, i.NETWORK_ID, i.MAC_ADDRESS, i.STATUS, i.DEVICE_OWNER, i.window_column, vtc.total_ports, avp.avg_ports AS benchmark, CASE WHEN vtc.total_ports > avp.avg_ports * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_USER' WHEN vtc.total_ports < avp.avg_ports * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_USER' ELSE 'AVERAGE_USER' END AS user_category FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN VifTypeCounts AS vtc ON i.VIF_TYPE = vtc.VIF_TYPE CROSS JOIN AverageVifTypePorts AS avp), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.STATUS, VAR_SAMP(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS standard_attr_id_variance FROM PORTS AS p JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE NOT p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID IS NULL AND ia.IP_ADDRESS IN ('10.150.64.113/8') GROUP BY p.STATUS ORDER BY standard_attr_id_variance DESC NULLS LAST), StatusPortCounts AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS status, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS distinct_ports FROM PORTS AS p JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE NOT p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID IS NULL AND ia.IP_ADDRESS IN ('10.150.64.113/8') GROUP BY p.STATUS), AverageVariance AS (SELECT AVG(standard_attr_id_variance) AS avg_variance FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.STATUS, i.standard_attr_id_variance, spc.distinct_ports, (i.standard_attr_id_variance - av.avg_variance) AS deviation_from_avg_variance FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN StatusPortCounts AS spc ON i.STATUS = spc.status CROSS JOIN AverageVariance AS av ORDER BY i.standard_attr_id_variance DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT q1.PORT_ID, q1.HOST, q1.VIF_TYPE, q1.VNIC_TYPE, q1.TENANT_ID, q1.NETWORK_ID, q1.MAC_ADDRESS, q1.STATUS, q1.DEVICE_OWNER, q1.window_column, q1.total_ports, q1.benchmark, q1.user_category, q2.standard_attr_id_variance, q2.distinct_ports, q2.deviation_from_avg_variance FROM q1 JOIN q2 ON q1.STATUS = q2.STATUS
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' AND NOT p.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(port_count) AS avg_port_count FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.port_status, i.device_owner, i.port_count, i.earliest_standard_attr_update, (i.port_count - a.avg_port_count) AS deviation_from_avg_port_count FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS a), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS min_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS spb ON p.ID = spb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER) SELECT i.port_status, i.device_owner, i.port_count, i.min_standard_attr_id, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT spb2.security_group_id) > COUNT(DISTINCT p2.id) THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_multi_sg_bindings FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN ports AS p2 ON p2.status = i.port_status AND p2.device_owner = i.device_owner AND p2.tenant_id IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') JOIN securitygroupportbindings AS spb2 ON p2.id = spb2.port_id GROUP BY i.port_status, i.device_owner, i.port_count, i.min_standard_attr_id ORDER BY i.port_count DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10) SELECT q2.port_status, q2.device_owner, q2.port_count AS distinct_port_count, q2.min_standard_attr_id, q2.has_multi_sg_bindings, q1.earliest_standard_attr_update, q1.deviation_from_avg_port_count FROM q2 JOIN q1 ON q1.port_status = q2.port_status AND q1.device_owner = q2.device_owner ORDER BY q2.port_count DESC NULLS LAST
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' AND NOT p.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(port_count) AS avg_port_count FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.port_status, i.device_owner, i.port_count, i.earliest_standard_attr_update, (i.port_count - a.avg_port_count) AS deviation_from_avg_port_count FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS a), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS min_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS spb ON p.ID = spb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER) SELECT i.port_status, i.device_owner, i.port_count, i.min_standard_attr_id, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT spb2.security_group_id) > COUNT(DISTINCT p2.id) THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_multi_sg_bindings FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN ports AS p2 ON p2.status = i.port_status AND p2.device_owner = i.device_owner AND p2.tenant_id IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') JOIN securitygroupportbindings AS spb2 ON p2.id = spb2.port_id GROUP BY i.port_status, i.device_owner, i.port_count, i.min_standard_attr_id ORDER BY i.port_count DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10) SELECT q2.port_status, q2.device_owner, q2.port_count AS distinct_down_ports_with_sg_count, q2.min_standard_attr_id, q2.has_multi_sg_bindings, q1.port_count AS all_ports_count_excluding_shared_networks, q1.earliest_standard_attr_update, q1.deviation_from_avg_port_count FROM q2 LEFT JOIN q1 ON q1.port_status = q2.port_status AND q1.device_owner = q2.device_owner ORDER BY q2.port_count DESC NULLS LAST
WITH sg_admin_ports_above_avg AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.name AS port_name, COUNT(sgpb.security_group_id) AS security_group_binding_count, SUM(CASE WHEN pd.port_id IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END) AS dns_record_count FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id LEFT JOIN PORTDNSES AS pd ON p.id = pd.port_id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.name) SELECT port_id, tenant_id, port_name, security_group_binding_count, dns_record_count FROM inner_cte WHERE security_group_binding_count > (SELECT AVG(security_group_binding_count) FROM inner_cte)), down_ports_window_stats AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT inner_cte.port_id, inner_cte.tenant_id, inner_cte.mac_address, inner_cte.window_column, tenant_counts.total_down_ports_for_tenant, avg_counts.avg_down_ports_across_tenants FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT p.tenant_id AS tenant_id, COUNT(p.id) AS total_down_ports_for_tenant FROM ports AS p WHERE p.status = 'DOWN' GROUP BY p.tenant_id) AS tenant_counts ON inner_cte.tenant_id = tenant_counts.tenant_id JOIN (SELECT AVG(port_count) AS avg_down_ports_across_tenants FROM (SELECT COUNT(p.id) AS port_count FROM ports AS p WHERE p.status = 'DOWN' GROUP BY p.tenant_id) AS sub) AS avg_counts ON 1 = 1) SELECT s.port_id, s.tenant_id, s.port_name, s.security_group_binding_count, s.dns_record_count, d.mac_address, d.window_column, d.total_down_ports_for_tenant, d.avg_down_ports_across_tenants FROM sg_admin_ports_above_avg AS s JOIN down_ports_window_stats AS d ON s.port_id = d.port_id AND s.tenant_id = d.tenant_id
WITH TenantPortSummary AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (ORDER BY p.ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.NETWORK_ID IN ('0a1d0a27-cffa-4de3-92c5-9d3fd3f2e74d')) SELECT p.tenant_id, COUNT(DISTINCT p.id) AS total_ports, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT inner_cte.security_group_id) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_security_groups FROM ports AS p LEFT JOIN inner_cte ON p.network_id = inner_cte.network_id AND p.mac_address = inner_cte.mac_address GROUP BY p.tenant_id ORDER BY total_ports DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10), TenantDownPortWindowed AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), TenantAvg AS (SELECT p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS avg_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.TENANT_ID) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.mac_address, i.window_column FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN PORTS AS p2 ON p2.ID = i.port_id JOIN TenantAvg AS ta ON ta.tenant_id = i.tenant_id WHERE p2.STANDARD_ATTR_ID > ta.avg_standard_attr_id), TenantDownPortCounts AS (SELECT p.tenant_id, COUNT(DISTINCT p.id) AS down_ports_in_network FROM PORTS AS p WHERE p.status = 'DOWN' AND p.network_id IN ('0a1d0a27-cffa-4de3-92c5-9d3fd3f2e74d') GROUP BY p.tenant_id) SELECT tps.tenant_id, tps.total_ports, tps.has_security_groups, tdc.down_ports_in_network, COUNT(DISTINCT tdpw.port_id) AS qualifying_down_ports_for_tenant_98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57 FROM TenantPortSummary AS tps JOIN TenantDownPortCounts AS tdc ON tps.tenant_id = tdc.tenant_id LEFT JOIN TenantDownPortWindowed AS tdpw ON tps.tenant_id = tdpw.tenant_id WHERE (CAST(tdc.down_ports_in_network * 1.0 AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(tps.total_ports, 0), 0)) > 0.75 GROUP BY tps.tenant_id, tps.total_ports, tps.has_security_groups, tdc.down_ports_in_network ORDER BY tps.total_ports DESC NULLS LAST
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(ia.ip_address) AS ip_allocation_count, SUM(CASE WHEN NOT ia.subnet_id IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS subnet_id_rows FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON ia.port_id = p.id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND NOT ia.ip_address IS NULL AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address) SELECT inner_cte.port_id, inner_cte.tenant_id, inner_cte.mac_address, inner_cte.ip_allocation_count, inner_cte.subnet_id_rows, avg_allocs.avg_ip_allocation_count FROM inner_cte, (SELECT AVG(ip_allocation_count) AS avg_ip_allocation_count FROM inner_cte) AS avg_allocs), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(ia.ip_address) AS ip_allocation_count, SUM(CASE WHEN NOT ia.subnet_id IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS subnet_id_rows FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON ia.port_id = p.id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND NOT ia.ip_address IS NULL AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(ip_allocation_count) AS avg_ip_allocation_count FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.mac_address, i.ip_allocation_count, COUNT(DISTINCT ia2.subnet_id) AS distinct_subnet_count, (i.ip_allocation_count - a.avg_ip_allocation_count) AS deviation_from_avg_ip_allocations FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN ports AS p2 ON p2.id = i.port_id JOIN ipallocations AS ia2 ON ia2.port_id = p2.id CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS a WHERE NOT ia2.ip_address IS NULL GROUP BY i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.mac_address, i.ip_allocation_count, a.avg_ip_allocation_count), CombinedPortMetrics AS (SELECT q1.port_id, q1.tenant_id, q1.mac_address, q1.ip_allocation_count, q1.subnet_id_rows AS subnet_metric, q1.avg_ip_allocation_count AS metric_value, 'Average IP Allocation Count' AS metric_type FROM q1 UNION ALL SELECT q2.port_id, q2.tenant_id, q2.mac_address, q2.ip_allocation_count, q2.distinct_subnet_count AS subnet_metric, q2.deviation_from_avg_ip_allocations AS metric_value, 'Deviation From Average IP Allocation Count' AS metric_type FROM q2) SELECT port_id, tenant_id, mac_address, ip_allocation_count, subnet_metric, metric_value, metric_type FROM CombinedPortMetrics ORDER BY tenant_id NULLS FIRST, port_id NULLS FIRST, metric_type NULLS FIRST
WITH subq1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' AND NOT p.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER) SELECT inner_cte.port_status, inner_cte.device_owner, inner_cte.port_count, inner_cte.earliest_standard_attr_update, avg_groups.avg_port_count_across_groups FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT AVG(port_count) AS avg_port_count_across_groups FROM inner_cte) AS avg_groups ON 1 = 1), subq2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' AND NOT p.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(port_count) AS avg_port_count FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.port_status, i.device_owner, i.port_count, i.earliest_standard_attr_update, (i.port_count - a.avg_port_count) AS deviation_from_avg_port_count FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS a) SELECT s1.port_status, s1.device_owner, s1.port_count, s1.earliest_standard_attr_update, s1.avg_port_count_across_groups, s2.deviation_from_avg_port_count FROM subq1 AS s1 JOIN subq2 AS s2 ON s1.port_status = s2.port_status AND s1.device_owner = s2.device_owner ORDER BY s1.port_status NULLS FIRST, s1.device_owner NULLS FIRST
WITH rbac_action_ip_version_stats AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.ACTION, AVG(s.IP_VERSION) AS avg_ip_version, VAR_SAMP(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_variance, STDDEV(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_stddev FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY n.ACTION ORDER BY ip_version_variance DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT inner_cte.ACTION, inner_cte.avg_ip_version, inner_cte.ip_version_variance, inner_cte.ip_version_stddev, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN s.ENABLE_DHCP = 1 THEN s.ID END) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_dhcp_enabled_subnets FROM inner_cte JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS n ON n.ACTION = inner_cte.ACTION JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON s.NETWORK_ID = n.OBJECT_ID WHERE n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY inner_cte.ACTION, inner_cte.avg_ip_version, inner_cte.ip_version_variance, inner_cte.ip_version_stddev ORDER BY inner_cte.ip_version_variance DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10), public_shared_subnet_pairs AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.name, MAX(s.ip_version) AS max_ip_version, s.gateway_ip FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.object_id = s.network_id WHERE n.action = 'access_as_shared' AND n.target_tenant = '*' GROUP BY s.name, s.gateway_ip HAVING MAX(s.ip_version) > 3) SELECT i.name, i.gateway_ip, i.max_ip_version, CASE WHEN MAX(s.enable_dhcp) = 1 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_dhcp_enabled FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON s.name = i.name AND s.gateway_ip = i.gateway_ip GROUP BY i.name, i.gateway_ip, i.max_ip_version ORDER BY i.max_ip_version DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10) SELECT p.name AS subnet_name, p.gateway_ip, p.max_ip_version, p.has_dhcp_enabled, a.ACTION, a.avg_ip_version, a.ip_version_variance, a.ip_version_stddev, a.has_dhcp_enabled_subnets FROM public_shared_subnet_pairs AS p JOIN rbac_action_ip_version_stats AS a ON a.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' ORDER BY p.max_ip_version DESC NULLS LAST, p.name NULLS FIRST, p.gateway_ip NULLS FIRST
WITH networks_subq AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE' AND n.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT inner_cte.network_id, inner_cte.network_name, inner_cte.tenant_id, inner_cte.admin_state_up, inner_cte.mtu, inner_cte.standard_attr_updated_at, inner_cte.window_column, tenant_counts.total_active_networks, avg_counts.avg_active_networks FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT tenant_id, COUNT(network_id) AS total_active_networks FROM inner_cte GROUP BY tenant_id) AS tenant_counts ON tenant_counts.tenant_id = inner_cte.tenant_id JOIN (SELECT AVG(active_network_count) AS avg_active_networks FROM (SELECT tenant_id, COUNT(network_id) AS active_network_count FROM inner_cte GROUP BY tenant_id) AS sub) AS avg_counts ON 1 = 1), ports_subq AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(ia.ip_address) AS ip_allocation_count, SUM(CASE WHEN NOT ia.subnet_id IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS subnet_id_rows FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON ia.port_id = p.id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND NOT ia.ip_address IS NULL AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(ip_allocation_count) AS avg_ip_allocation_count FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.mac_address, i.ip_allocation_count, COUNT(DISTINCT ia2.subnet_id) AS distinct_subnet_count, (i.ip_allocation_count - a.avg_ip_allocation_count) AS deviation_from_avg_ip_allocations FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN ports AS p2 ON p2.id = i.port_id JOIN ipallocations AS ia2 ON ia2.port_id = p2.id CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS a WHERE NOT ia2.ip_address IS NULL GROUP BY i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.mac_address, i.ip_allocation_count, a.avg_ip_allocation_count) SELECT n.network_id, n.network_name, n.tenant_id, n.admin_state_up AS network_admin_state_up, n.mtu, n.standard_attr_updated_at AS network_standard_attr_updated_at, n.window_column AS network_row_number_within_tenant, n.total_active_networks, n.avg_active_networks, p.port_id, p.mac_address, p.ip_allocation_count, p.distinct_subnet_count, p.deviation_from_avg_ip_allocations FROM networks_subq AS n JOIN ports AS prt ON prt.network_id = n.network_id AND prt.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') JOIN ports_subq AS p ON p.port_id = prt.id AND p.tenant_id = n.tenant_id ORDER BY n.tenant_id NULLS FIRST, n.network_name NULLS FIRST, p.port_id NULLS FIRST
WITH port_rank_cte AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, sgpb.security_group_id, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.security_group_id ORDER BY p.id NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN' AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT port_id, tenant_id, mac_address, security_group_id, window_column FROM inner_cte WHERE window_column > (SELECT AVG(window_column) FROM inner_cte)), active_networks_cte AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE' AND n.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT inner_cte.network_id, inner_cte.network_name, inner_cte.tenant_id, inner_cte.admin_state_up, inner_cte.mtu, inner_cte.standard_attr_updated_at, inner_cte.window_column, tenant_counts.total_active_networks, avg_counts.avg_active_networks FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT tenant_id, COUNT(network_id) AS total_active_networks FROM inner_cte GROUP BY tenant_id) AS tenant_counts ON tenant_counts.tenant_id = inner_cte.tenant_id JOIN (SELECT AVG(active_network_count) AS avg_active_networks FROM (SELECT tenant_id, COUNT(network_id) AS active_network_count FROM inner_cte GROUP BY tenant_id) AS sub) AS avg_counts ON 1 = 1) SELECT an.network_id, an.network_name, an.tenant_id, an.admin_state_up, an.mtu, an.standard_attr_updated_at, an.window_column AS network_row_number_within_tenant, an.total_active_networks, an.avg_active_networks, pr.port_id, pr.mac_address, pr.security_group_id, pr.window_column AS port_rank_within_security_group FROM active_networks_cte AS an JOIN PORTS AS p ON p.tenant_id = an.tenant_id AND p.network_id = an.network_id AND p.status = 'DOWN' JOIN port_rank_cte AS pr ON pr.port_id = p.id AND pr.tenant_id = an.tenant_id ORDER BY an.total_active_networks DESC NULLS LAST, an.network_name NULLS FIRST, pr.security_group_id NULLS FIRST, pr.port_id NULLS FIRST
WITH port_dns_security AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.name AS port_name, COUNT(sgpb.security_group_id) AS security_group_binding_count, SUM(CASE WHEN pd.port_id IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END) AS dns_record_count FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id LEFT JOIN PORTDNSES AS pd ON p.id = pd.port_id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.name), AverageDns AS (SELECT AVG(dns_record_count) AS avg_dns_records FROM inner_cte) SELECT ic.port_id, ic.tenant_id, ic.port_name, ic.security_group_binding_count, ic.dns_record_count, ad.avg_dns_records AS benchmark, CASE WHEN ic.dns_record_count > ad.avg_dns_records * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_DNS' WHEN ic.dns_record_count < ad.avg_dns_records * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_DNS' ELSE 'AVERAGE_DNS' END AS dns_category FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN AverageDns AS ad), active_network_mtu AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE' AND n.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), avg_mtu_cte AS (SELECT AVG(mtu) AS avg_mtu FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.network_id, i.network_name, i.tenant_id, i.admin_state_up, i.mtu, i.standard_attr_updated_at, i.window_column, (i.mtu - a.avg_mtu) AS deviation_from_avg_mtu FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_mtu_cte AS a), port_network_bridge AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.network_id FROM PORTS AS p WHERE p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT anm.network_id, anm.network_name, anm.tenant_id, anm.admin_state_up AS network_admin_state_up, anm.mtu, anm.standard_attr_updated_at, anm.window_column, anm.deviation_from_avg_mtu, pds.port_id, pds.port_name, pds.security_group_binding_count, pds.dns_record_count, pds.benchmark AS avg_dns_records_benchmark, pds.dns_category FROM active_network_mtu AS anm JOIN port_network_bridge AS pnb ON pnb.network_id = anm.network_id JOIN port_dns_security AS pds ON pds.port_id = pnb.port_id WHERE anm.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, sgpb.security_group_id, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.security_group_id ORDER BY p.id NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN' AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), SecurityGroupPorts AS (SELECT security_group_id, COUNT(port_id) AS total_ports FROM inner_cte GROUP BY security_group_id), AveragePorts AS (SELECT AVG(total_ports) AS avg_ports FROM SecurityGroupPorts) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.mac_address, i.security_group_id, i.window_column AS port_rank_within_security_group, sgp.total_ports AS total_down_ports_in_security_group, ap.avg_ports AS benchmark, CASE WHEN sgp.total_ports > ap.avg_ports * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_USER' WHEN sgp.total_ports < ap.avg_ports * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_USER' ELSE 'AVERAGE_USER' END AS user_category FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN SecurityGroupPorts AS sgp ON i.security_group_id = sgp.security_group_id CROSS JOIN AveragePorts AS ap), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), AverageStdAttr AS (SELECT AVG(p2.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS avg_standard_attr_id FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb2 JOIN PORTS AS p2 ON sgpb2.PORT_ID = p2.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb2 ON p2.ID = psb2.PORT_ID WHERE p2.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p2.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.mac_address, i.window_column, (p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID - a.avg_standard_attr_id) AS deviation_from_avg_standard_attr_id FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN PORTS AS p ON p.ID = i.port_id CROSS JOIN AverageStdAttr AS a) SELECT q1.port_id, q1.tenant_id, q1.mac_address, q1.security_group_id, q1.port_rank_within_security_group, q1.total_down_ports_in_security_group, q1.benchmark AS avg_down_ports_benchmark_across_security_groups, q1.user_category, q2.window_column AS running_sum_standard_attr_id_within_security_group, q2.deviation_from_avg_standard_attr_id FROM q1 JOIN q2 ON q1.port_id = q2.port_id AND q1.tenant_id = q2.tenant_id AND q1.mac_address = q2.mac_address
WITH SubnetRbacStdAttr AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.NAME AS subnet_name, s.CIDR AS subnet_cidr, n.TARGET_TENANT AS rbac_target_tenant, SUM(s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY s.IP_VERSION ORDER BY s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS n ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' AND n.TARGET_TENANT IN ('1ee6b5b4fe8c45cfa16c3ecdbb8ee02b')), AvgStdAttr AS (SELECT AVG(s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS avg_standard_attr_id FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS n ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' AND n.TARGET_TENANT IN ('1ee6b5b4fe8c45cfa16c3ecdbb8ee02b')) SELECT i.subnet_name, i.subnet_cidr, i.rbac_target_tenant, i.window_column, (s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID - a.avg_standard_attr_id) AS deviation_from_avg_standard_attr_id FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON s.NAME = i.subnet_name AND s.CIDR = i.subnet_cidr CROSS JOIN AvgStdAttr AS a), SubnetDnsCv AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.name AS subnet_name, CAST(STDDEV(d.order) AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(AVG(d.order), 0), 0) AS coefficient_of_variation FROM subnets AS s JOIN dnsnameservers AS d ON d.subnet_id = s.id JOIN ipallocationpools AS p ON p.subnet_id = s.id JOIN networks AS n ON n.id = s.network_id WHERE s.ip_version = 4 AND NOT n.id IN (SELECT r.object_id FROM networkrbacs AS r WHERE r.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (r.target_tenant = '*' OR r.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY s.name HAVING AVG(d.order) > 0 ORDER BY coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT i.subnet_name, i.coefficient_of_variation FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN subnets AS s ON s.name = i.subnet_name WHERE i.coefficient_of_variation > (SELECT AVG(coefficient_of_variation) FROM inner_cte) ORDER BY i.coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT s1.subnet_name, s1.subnet_cidr, s1.rbac_target_tenant, s1.window_column, s1.deviation_from_avg_standard_attr_id, s2.coefficient_of_variation, ROUND(CAST(CAST(s2.coefficient_of_variation AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(AVG(s2.coefficient_of_variation) OVER (), 0), 0) AS DECIMAL), 4) AS normalized_coefficient_of_variation_ratio FROM SubnetRbacStdAttr AS s1 JOIN SubnetDnsCv AS s2 ON s2.subnet_name = s1.subnet_name WHERE s1.rbac_target_tenant IN ('1ee6b5b4fe8c45cfa16c3ecdbb8ee02b') ORDER BY normalized_coefficient_of_variation_ratio DESC NULLS LAST, s2.coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST
WITH networks_subq AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE' AND n.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), avg_mtu_cte AS (SELECT AVG(mtu) AS avg_mtu FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.network_id, i.network_name, i.tenant_id, i.admin_state_up, i.mtu, i.standard_attr_updated_at, i.window_column, (i.mtu - a.avg_mtu) AS deviation_from_avg_mtu FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_mtu_cte AS a), ports_subq AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), VifTypeCounts AS (SELECT VIF_TYPE, COUNT(*) AS ports_in_vif_type FROM inner_cte GROUP BY VIF_TYPE), OverallWindowAvg AS (SELECT AVG(window_column) AS overall_avg_window_column FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.PORT_ID, i.HOST, i.VIF_TYPE, i.VNIC_TYPE, i.TENANT_ID, i.NETWORK_ID, i.MAC_ADDRESS, i.STATUS, i.DEVICE_OWNER, i.window_column, vtc.ports_in_vif_type, (i.window_column - owa.overall_avg_window_column) AS deviation_from_overall_window_avg FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN VifTypeCounts AS vtc ON i.VIF_TYPE = vtc.VIF_TYPE CROSS JOIN OverallWindowAvg AS owa) SELECT n.network_id, n.network_name, n.tenant_id, n.admin_state_up AS network_admin_state_up, n.mtu, n.standard_attr_updated_at, n.window_column AS network_row_number_within_tenant_by_name, n.deviation_from_avg_mtu, p.PORT_ID, p.HOST, p.VIF_TYPE, p.VNIC_TYPE, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER, p.window_column AS port_windowed_avg_standard_attr_id, p.ports_in_vif_type, p.deviation_from_overall_window_avg FROM networks_subq AS n JOIN ports_subq AS p ON p.NETWORK_ID = n.network_id AND p.TENANT_ID = n.tenant_id
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.ACTION, AVG(s.IP_VERSION) AS avg_ip_version, VAR_SAMP(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_variance, STDDEV(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_stddev FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY n.ACTION ORDER BY ip_version_variance DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT inner_cte.ACTION, inner_cte.avg_ip_version, inner_cte.ip_version_variance, inner_cte.ip_version_stddev, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN s.ENABLE_DHCP = 1 THEN s.ID END) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_dhcp_enabled_subnets FROM inner_cte JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS n ON n.ACTION = inner_cte.ACTION JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON s.NETWORK_ID = n.OBJECT_ID WHERE n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY inner_cte.ACTION, inner_cte.avg_ip_version, inner_cte.ip_version_variance, inner_cte.ip_version_stddev ORDER BY inner_cte.ip_version_variance DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.ACTION, VAR_SAMP(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_variance FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' AND n.TARGET_TENANT = '*' GROUP BY n.ACTION ORDER BY ip_version_variance DESC NULLS LAST), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(ip_version_variance) AS avg_ip_version_variance FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.ACTION, i.ip_version_variance, a.avg_ip_version_variance AS benchmark, CASE WHEN i.ip_version_variance > a.avg_ip_version_variance * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_VARIANCE' WHEN i.ip_version_variance < a.avg_ip_version_variance * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_VARIANCE' ELSE 'AVERAGE_VARIANCE' END AS variance_category FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS a) SELECT q1.ACTION, q1.avg_ip_version, q1.ip_version_variance AS top10_ip_version_variance, q1.ip_version_stddev, q1.has_dhcp_enabled_subnets, q2.benchmark AS avg_variance_benchmark_across_actions, q2.variance_category FROM q1 JOIN q2 ON q2.ACTION = q1.ACTION ORDER BY q1.ip_version_variance DESC NULLS LAST
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, sgpb.security_group_id, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.security_group_id ORDER BY p.id NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN') SELECT inner_cte.tenant_id, COUNT(inner_cte.port_id) AS total_down_ports, avg_down.avg_down_port_count FROM inner_cte, (SELECT AVG(down_port_count) AS avg_down_port_count FROM (SELECT tenant_id, COUNT(port_id) AS down_port_count FROM inner_cte GROUP BY tenant_id) AS sub) AS avg_down GROUP BY inner_cte.tenant_id, avg_down.avg_down_port_count
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT lb.NAME AS loadbalancer_name, lb.VIP_ADDRESS AS vip_address, stats.BYTES_IN AS bytes_in, stats.TOTAL_CONNECTIONS AS total_connections, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY lb.NAME NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCER_STATISTICS AS stats JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb ON stats.LOADBALANCER_ID = lb.ID JOIN PROVIDERRESOURCEASSOCIATIONS AS pra ON lb.ID = pra.RESOURCE_ID WHERE pra.PROVIDER_NAME = 'haproxy') SELECT inner_cte.loadbalancer_name, inner_cte.vip_address, inner_cte.bytes_in, inner_cte.total_connections, inner_cte.window_column, lb.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, tenant_counts.haproxy_lb_count AS tenant_haproxy_loadbalancer_count, avg_counts.avg_haproxy_lb_count AS avg_haproxy_loadbalancers_per_tenant FROM inner_cte JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb ON lb.NAME = inner_cte.loadbalancer_name AND lb.VIP_ADDRESS = inner_cte.vip_address JOIN (SELECT lb2.TENANT_ID, COUNT(lb2.ID) AS haproxy_lb_count FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb2 JOIN PROVIDERRESOURCEASSOCIATIONS AS pra2 ON lb2.ID = pra2.RESOURCE_ID WHERE pra2.PROVIDER_NAME = 'haproxy' GROUP BY lb2.TENANT_ID) AS tenant_counts ON tenant_counts.TENANT_ID = lb.TENANT_ID JOIN (SELECT AVG(sub.haproxy_lb_count) AS avg_haproxy_lb_count FROM (SELECT lb3.TENANT_ID, COUNT(lb3.ID) AS haproxy_lb_count FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb3 JOIN PROVIDERRESOURCEASSOCIATIONS AS pra3 ON lb3.ID = pra3.RESOURCE_ID WHERE pra3.PROVIDER_NAME = 'haproxy' GROUP BY lb3.TENANT_ID) AS sub) AS avg_counts ON 1 = 1
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT COUNT(*) AS active_shared_rbac_networks FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r ON n.ID = r.OBJECT_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE' AND r.ACTION = 'access_as_shared') SELECT n.tenant_id, COUNT(DISTINCT n.id) AS total_active_shared_networks, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN r.target_tenant <> '*' THEN r.id END) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_specific_target_tenant_share FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r ON n.ID = r.OBJECT_ID CROSS JOIN inner_cte WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE' AND r.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY n.tenant_id ORDER BY total_active_shared_networks DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.STATUS AS network_status, VAR_SAMP(nsb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED) AS port_security_enabled_variance, STDDEV(nsb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED) AS port_security_enabled_stddev FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN NETWORKSECURITYBINDINGS AS nsb ON n.ID = nsb.NETWORK_ID GROUP BY n.STATUS ORDER BY port_security_enabled_variance DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT inner_cte.network_status, inner_cte.port_security_enabled_variance, inner_cte.port_security_enabled_stddev, avg_vars.avg_variance_across_statuses FROM inner_cte, (SELECT AVG(port_security_enabled_variance) AS avg_variance_across_statuses FROM inner_cte) AS avg_vars ORDER BY inner_cte.port_security_enabled_variance DESC NULLS LAST
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.name AS subnet_name, CAST(STDDEV(d.ORDER) AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(AVG(d.ORDER), 0), 0) AS coefficient_of_variation FROM subnets AS s JOIN dnsnameservers AS d ON d.subnet_id = s.id JOIN ipallocationpools AS p ON p.subnet_id = s.id WHERE s.ip_version = 4 GROUP BY s.name HAVING AVG(d.ORDER) > 0 ORDER BY coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT inner_cte.subnet_name, inner_cte.coefficient_of_variation, COUNT(DISTINCT p.id) AS ip_allocation_pool_count FROM inner_cte JOIN subnets AS s ON s.name = inner_cte.subnet_name JOIN ipallocationpools AS p ON p.subnet_id = s.id WHERE inner_cte.coefficient_of_variation > 0.5 GROUP BY inner_cte.subnet_name, inner_cte.coefficient_of_variation ORDER BY inner_cte.coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT COUNT(*) AS online_haproxy_loadbalancers FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb JOIN PROVIDERRESOURCEASSOCIATIONS AS pra ON lb.ID = pra.RESOURCE_ID WHERE lb.OPERATING_STATUS = 'ONLINE' AND pra.PROVIDER_NAME = 'haproxy'), TenantCounts AS (SELECT lb.TENANT_ID, COUNT(*) AS tenant_online_haproxy_count FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb JOIN PROVIDERRESOURCEASSOCIATIONS AS pra ON lb.ID = pra.RESOURCE_ID WHERE lb.OPERATING_STATUS = 'ONLINE' AND pra.PROVIDER_NAME = 'haproxy' GROUP BY lb.TENANT_ID), AvgTenantCount AS (SELECT AVG(tenant_online_haproxy_count) AS avg_tenant_online_haproxy_count FROM TenantCounts) SELECT tc.TENANT_ID, tc.tenant_online_haproxy_count FROM TenantCounts AS tc CROSS JOIN inner_cte CROSS JOIN AvgTenantCount AS atc WHERE tc.tenant_online_haproxy_count > atc.avg_tenant_online_haproxy_count
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.name AS network_name, SUM(1) AS total_security_bindings FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN NETWORKSECURITYBINDINGS AS nsb ON n.id = nsb.network_id WHERE n.status = 'ACTIVE' GROUP BY n.name HAVING SUM(1) > 1) SELECT MAX(total_security_bindings) AS max_total_security_bindings FROM inner_cte
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT aap.MAC_ADDRESS AS allowed_mac_address, aap.IP_ADDRESS AS allowed_ip_address, p.ID AS port_id, sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID AS security_group_id, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY p.ID ORDER BY aap.IP_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN ALLOWEDADDRESSPAIRS AS aap ON p.ID = aap.PORT_ID WHERE p.ID = '25d421c1-c76d-4c26-9550-9f871a1d5034' ORDER BY aap.IP_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(window_column) AS avg_window_column FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.allowed_mac_address, i.allowed_ip_address, i.port_id, i.security_group_id, i.window_column, (i.window_column - a.avg_window_column) AS deviation_from_avg_window FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS a ORDER BY i.allowed_ip_address NULLS FIRST
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, b.HOST AS host, b.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ia.IP_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), HostCounts AS (SELECT host, COUNT(DISTINCT ip_address) AS down_allocated_ips FROM inner_cte GROUP BY host), AverageHostCounts AS (SELECT AVG(down_allocated_ips) AS avg_down_allocated_ips FROM HostCounts) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.host, i.vif_type, i.ip_address, i.window_column, hc.down_allocated_ips, (hc.down_allocated_ips - ahc.avg_down_allocated_ips) AS deviation_from_avg FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN HostCounts AS hc ON i.host = hc.host CROSS JOIN AverageHostCounts AS ahc

WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (ORDER BY p.ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT NAME, NETWORK_ID, MAC_ADDRESS, ADMIN_STATE_UP, STATUS, DEVICE_OWNER, SECURITY_GROUP_ID, window_column FROM inner_cte WHERE SECURITY_GROUP_ID IN (SELECT SECURITY_GROUP_ID FROM inner_cte GROUP BY SECURITY_GROUP_ID HAVING COUNT(*) > 5)
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, EXP(AVG(LN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID))) AS geometric_mean_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID > 0 GROUP BY s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY geometric_mean_standard_attr_id DESC NULLS LAST), NetworkSecurityGroupGeoMean AS (SELECT p.NETWORK_ID, s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, i.geometric_mean_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID JOIN inner_cte AS i ON i.SECURITY_GROUP_ID = s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID) SELECT NETWORK_ID, MAX(geometric_mean_standard_attr_id) AS max_security_group_geometric_mean_standard_attr_id FROM NetworkSecurityGroupGeoMean GROUP BY NETWORK_ID
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT COUNT(*) AS down_ports_with_port_security_enabled FROM PORTS AS p JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND psb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED = 1), NetworkDownSecurePorts AS (SELECT p.NETWORK_ID AS network_id, COUNT(*) AS total_down_ports_with_port_security_enabled FROM PORTS AS p JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND psb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED = 1 GROUP BY p.NETWORK_ID) SELECT network_id, total_down_ports_with_port_security_enabled FROM NetworkDownSecurePorts WHERE total_down_ports_with_port_security_enabled > (SELECT AVG(total_down_ports_with_port_security_enabled) FROM NetworkDownSecurePorts)
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.NAME AS subnet_name, s.CIDR AS subnet_cidr, n.TARGET_TENANT AS rbac_target_tenant, SUM(s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY s.IP_VERSION ORDER BY s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS n ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared') SELECT subnet_name, subnet_cidr, rbac_target_tenant, window_column FROM inner_cte WHERE window_column > (SELECT AVG(window_column) FROM inner_cte)
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) AS min_admin_state_up, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS avg_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE b.VIF_TYPE = 'ovs' GROUP BY p.STATUS) SELECT p.STATUS, inner_cte.min_admin_state_up, inner_cte.avg_standard_attr_id FROM inner_cte JOIN PORTS AS p ON 1 = 1 JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE b.VIF_TYPE = 'ovs' GROUP BY p.STATUS, inner_cte.min_admin_state_up, inner_cte.avg_standard_attr_id HAVING inner_cte.avg_standard_attr_id > (SELECT AVG(p2.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) FROM PORTS AS p2 JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b2 ON p2.ID = b2.PORT_ID WHERE b2.VIF_TYPE = 'ovs')

WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(sa.ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sa.RESOURCE_TYPE ORDER BY sa.ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT inner_cte.port_id, inner_cte.tenant_id, inner_cte.mac_address, inner_cte.window_column FROM inner_cte JOIN PORTS AS p2 ON p2.ID = inner_cte.port_id JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa2 ON sa2.ID = p2.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa2.ID > (SELECT AVG(sa3.ID) FROM PORTS AS p3 JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa3 ON sa3.ID = p3.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE p3.STATUS = 'DOWN')
