WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.NAME, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY r.ACTION NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM EXTERNALNETWORKS AS e JOIN NETWORKS AS n ON e.NETWORK_ID = n.ID JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r ON n.ID = r.OBJECT_ID WHERE e.IS_DEFAULT = 0) SELECT NAME, window_column FROM inner_cte WHERE window_column > (SELECT AVG(window_column) FROM inner_cte)
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.STATUS AS network_status, VAR_SAMP(nsb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED) AS port_security_enabled_variance, STDDEV(nsb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED) AS port_security_enabled_stddev FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN NETWORKSECURITYBINDINGS AS nsb ON n.ID = nsb.NETWORK_ID GROUP BY n.STATUS ORDER BY port_security_enabled_variance DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT i.network_status, i.port_security_enabled_variance, i.port_security_enabled_stddev FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN NETWORKS AS n ON n.STATUS = i.network_status JOIN NETWORKSECURITYBINDINGS AS nsb ON n.ID = nsb.NETWORK_ID GROUP BY i.network_status, i.port_security_enabled_variance, i.port_security_enabled_stddev HAVING COUNT(DISTINCT n.ID) >= 3 ORDER BY i.port_security_enabled_variance DESC NULLS LAST
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, AVG(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST), VifDownCounts AS (SELECT VIF_TYPE, COUNT(*) AS total_down_ports FROM inner_cte GROUP BY VIF_TYPE), AverageDownCounts AS (SELECT AVG(total_down_ports) AS avg_down_ports FROM VifDownCounts) SELECT i.NAME, i.MAC_ADDRESS, i.ADMIN_STATE_UP, i.HOST, i.VIF_TYPE, i.window_column, vdc.total_down_ports, adc.avg_down_ports AS benchmark, CASE WHEN vdc.total_down_ports > adc.avg_down_ports * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_DOWN' WHEN vdc.total_down_ports < adc.avg_down_ports * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_DOWN' ELSE 'AVERAGE_DOWN' END AS down_category FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN VifDownCounts AS vdc ON i.VIF_TYPE = vdc.VIF_TYPE CROSS JOIN AverageDownCounts AS adc ORDER BY i.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(ia.ip_address) AS ip_allocation_count, SUM(CASE WHEN NOT ia.subnet_id IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS subnet_id_rows FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON ia.port_id = p.id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND NOT ia.ip_address IS NULL GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(ip_allocation_count) AS avg_ip_allocation_count FROM inner_cte) SELECT ic.port_id, ic.tenant_id, ic.mac_address, ic.ip_allocation_count, ic.subnet_id_rows, (ic.ip_allocation_count - a.avg_ip_allocation_count) AS deviation_from_avg_ip_allocations FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS a
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (ORDER BY p.ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), TenantDownPorts AS (SELECT p.tenant_id, COUNT(p.id) AS total_down_ports FROM PORTS AS p JOIN inner_cte AS ic ON ic.MAC_ADDRESS = p.MAC_ADDRESS GROUP BY p.tenant_id), AverageDownPorts AS (SELECT AVG(total_down_ports) AS avg_down_ports FROM TenantDownPorts) SELECT tdp.tenant_id, tdp.total_down_ports, adp.avg_down_ports AS benchmark, CASE WHEN tdp.total_down_ports > adp.avg_down_ports * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_USER' WHEN tdp.total_down_ports < adp.avg_down_ports * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_USER' ELSE 'AVERAGE_USER' END AS user_category FROM TenantDownPorts AS tdp CROSS JOIN AverageDownPorts AS adp
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.NAME, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY r.ACTION NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM EXTERNALNETWORKS AS e JOIN NETWORKS AS n ON e.NETWORK_ID = n.ID JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r ON n.ID = r.OBJECT_ID WHERE e.IS_DEFAULT = 0) SELECT inner_cte.NAME, inner_cte.window_column, COUNT(*) AS total_networks, avg_counts.avg_networks_per_action FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT AVG(action_count) AS avg_networks_per_action FROM (SELECT COUNT(*) AS action_count FROM inner_cte GROUP BY window_column) AS sub) AS avg_counts ON 1 = 1 GROUP BY inner_cte.NAME, inner_cte.window_column, avg_counts.avg_networks_per_action
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT lb.OPERATING_STATUS, COUNT(*) AS total_loadbalancers FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb JOIN PROVIDERRESOURCEASSOCIATIONS AS pra ON lb.ID = pra.RESOURCE_ID WHERE pra.PROVIDER_NAME = 'haproxy' GROUP BY lb.OPERATING_STATUS HAVING COUNT(*) > 1) SELECT i.OPERATING_STATUS, i.total_loadbalancers FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN (SELECT COUNT(*) AS total_haproxy_loadbalancers FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb2 JOIN PROVIDERRESOURCEASSOCIATIONS AS pra2 ON lb2.ID = pra2.RESOURCE_ID WHERE pra2.PROVIDER_NAME = 'haproxy') AS totals ON 1 = 1 WHERE (CAST(i.total_loadbalancers * 1.0 AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(totals.total_haproxy_loadbalancers, 0)) > 0.50
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), NetworkPortCounts AS (SELECT p.network_id, p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS ports_in_network FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' GROUP BY p.network_id, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER) SELECT npc.port_status, npc.device_owner, MAX(npc.ports_in_network) AS max_ports_in_any_network FROM NetworkPortCounts AS npc JOIN inner_cte AS ic ON ic.port_status = npc.port_status AND ic.device_owner = npc.device_owner GROUP BY npc.port_status, npc.device_owner
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.name AS port_name, COUNT(sgpb.security_group_id) AS security_group_binding_count, SUM(CASE WHEN pd.port_id IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END) AS dns_record_count FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id LEFT JOIN PORTDNSES AS pd ON p.id = pd.port_id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.name) SELECT inner_cte.tenant_id, COUNT(inner_cte.port_id) AS total_ports_with_sg_binding_admin_up, SUM(inner_cte.security_group_binding_count) AS total_security_group_bindings, SUM(inner_cte.dns_record_count) AS total_dns_records, avg_ports.avg_port_count AS avg_ports_with_sg_binding_admin_up_per_tenant FROM inner_cte, (SELECT AVG(port_count) AS avg_port_count FROM (SELECT COUNT(port_id) AS port_count FROM inner_cte GROUP BY tenant_id) AS sub) AS avg_ports GROUP BY inner_cte.tenant_id, avg_ports.avg_port_count
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, AVG(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST) SELECT i.VIF_TYPE, COUNT(*) AS total_down_ports, AVG(i.window_column) AS avg_window_admin_state_up FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b2 ON NOT i.MAC_ADDRESS IS NULL AND i.VIF_TYPE = b2.VIF_TYPE GROUP BY i.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY total_down_ports DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.NAME, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY sa.UPDATED_AT NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4), avg_rank AS (SELECT AVG(window_column) AS avg_window_column FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.NAME, i.window_column, a.avg_window_column AS benchmark, CASE WHEN i.window_column > a.avg_window_column * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_RANK' WHEN i.window_column < a.avg_window_column * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_RANK' ELSE 'AVERAGE_RANK' END AS rank_category FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_rank AS a
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT lb.name AS loadbalancer_name, MAX(lb.admin_state_up) AS max_admin_state_up, lb.operating_status FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb JOIN PROVIDERRESOURCEASSOCIATIONS AS pra ON lb.id = pra.resource_id WHERE pra.provider_name = 'haproxy' GROUP BY lb.name, lb.operating_status HAVING MAX(lb.admin_state_up) = 1) SELECT lb.tenant_id, COUNT(DISTINCT lb.id) AS total_qualifying_loadbalancers, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN inner_cte.operating_status = 'ONLINE' THEN lb.id END) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_any_online FROM inner_cte JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb ON lb.name = inner_cte.loadbalancer_name AND lb.operating_status = inner_cte.operating_status GROUP BY lb.tenant_id ORDER BY total_qualifying_loadbalancers DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.OBJECT_ID AS network_id, COUNT(*) AS subnet_count FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY n.OBJECT_ID ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 3) SELECT CASE WHEN LAG(inner_cte.network_id) OVER (ORDER BY inner_cte.network_id NULLS FIRST) = inner_cte.network_id THEN NULL ELSE inner_cte.network_id END AS network_id, inner_cte.subnet_count, s.TENANT_ID AS subnet_tenant_id FROM inner_cte JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON s.NETWORK_ID = inner_cte.network_id ORDER BY inner_cte.network_id NULLS FIRST, s.TENANT_ID NULLS FIRST
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT COUNT(*) AS down_ports_with_port_security_enabled FROM PORTS AS p JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND psb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED = 1), TenantDownSecurePorts AS (SELECT p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, COUNT(*) AS down_secure_ports FROM PORTS AS p JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND psb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED = 1 GROUP BY p.TENANT_ID), AverageTenantCounts AS (SELECT AVG(down_secure_ports) AS avg_down_secure_ports FROM TenantDownSecurePorts) SELECT tdsp.tenant_id, tdsp.down_secure_ports, (tdsp.down_secure_ports - atc.avg_down_secure_ports) AS deviation_from_avg, inner_cte.down_ports_with_port_security_enabled AS total_down_ports_with_port_security_enabled FROM TenantDownSecurePorts AS tdsp CROSS JOIN AverageTenantCounts AS atc CROSS JOIN inner_cte
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, MAX(port_pair_count) - MIN(port_pair_count) AS pair_count_range, VAR_SAMP(port_pair_count) AS pair_count_variance, STDDEV(port_pair_count) AS pair_count_stddev FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, COUNT(*) AS port_pair_count FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ALLOWEDADDRESSPAIRS AS aap ON p.ID = aap.PORT_ID GROUP BY p.ID) AS port_stats ON sgpb.PORT_ID = port_stats.port_id GROUP BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY pair_count_range DESC NULLS LAST), AverageRange AS (SELECT AVG(pair_count_range) AS avg_pair_count_range FROM inner_cte) SELECT ic.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, ic.pair_count_range, ic.pair_count_variance, ic.pair_count_stddev, ar.avg_pair_count_range AS benchmark, CASE WHEN ic.pair_count_range > ar.avg_pair_count_range * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_SPREAD' WHEN ic.pair_count_range < ar.avg_pair_count_range * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_SPREAD' ELSE 'AVERAGE_SPREAD' END AS spread_category FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN AverageRange AS ar
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) AS min_admin_state_up, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS avg_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE b.VIF_TYPE = 'ovs' GROUP BY p.STATUS), overall_avg AS (SELECT AVG(avg_standard_attr_id) AS overall_avg_standard_attr_id FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.min_admin_state_up, i.avg_standard_attr_id, (i.avg_standard_attr_id - o.overall_avg_standard_attr_id) AS deviation_from_overall_avg_standard_attr_id FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN overall_avg AS o
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT r.DESTINATION, r.NEXTHOP, s.NAME AS subnet_name, s.CIDR AS subnet_cidr, p.FIRST_IP, p.LAST_IP, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY r.SUBNET_ID ORDER BY r.SUBNET_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SUBNETROUTES AS r JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON r.SUBNET_ID = s.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONPOOLS AS p ON s.ID = p.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4), SubnetRouteCounts AS (SELECT s.id AS subnet_id, COUNT(r.destination) AS route_count FROM subnets AS s LEFT JOIN subnetroutes AS r ON r.subnet_id = s.id WHERE s.ip_version = 4 GROUP BY s.id), AverageRouteCounts AS (SELECT AVG(route_count) AS avg_route_count FROM SubnetRouteCounts) SELECT ic.DESTINATION, ic.NEXTHOP, ic.subnet_name, ic.subnet_cidr, ic.FIRST_IP, ic.LAST_IP, ic.window_column, src.route_count, (src.route_count - arc.avg_route_count) AS deviation_from_avg_route_count FROM inner_cte AS ic JOIN subnets AS s ON s.cidr = ic.subnet_cidr AND s.name = ic.subnet_name JOIN SubnetRouteCounts AS src ON src.subnet_id = s.id CROSS JOIN AverageRouteCounts AS arc
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT aap.MAC_ADDRESS AS allowed_mac_address, aap.IP_ADDRESS AS allowed_ip_address, p.ID AS port_id, sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID AS security_group_id, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY p.ID ORDER BY aap.IP_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN ALLOWEDADDRESSPAIRS AS aap ON p.ID = aap.PORT_ID WHERE p.ID = '25d421c1-c76d-4c26-9550-9f871a1d5034' ORDER BY aap.IP_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST), TenantPorts AS (SELECT p2.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, COUNT(p2.ID) AS total_ports FROM PORTS AS p2 GROUP BY p2.TENANT_ID), AveragePorts AS (SELECT AVG(total_ports) AS avg_ports FROM TenantPorts) SELECT i.allowed_mac_address, i.allowed_ip_address, i.port_id, i.security_group_id, i.window_column, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, tp.total_ports, ap.avg_ports AS benchmark, CASE WHEN tp.total_ports > ap.avg_ports * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_USER' WHEN tp.total_ports < ap.avg_ports * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_USER' ELSE 'AVERAGE_USER' END AS user_category FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN PORTS AS p ON p.ID = i.port_id JOIN TenantPorts AS tp ON tp.tenant_id = p.TENANT_ID CROSS JOIN AveragePorts AS ap ORDER BY i.allowed_ip_address NULLS FIRST
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT aap.MAC_ADDRESS AS allowed_mac_address, aap.IP_ADDRESS AS allowed_ip_address, p.ID AS port_id, sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID AS security_group_id, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY p.ID ORDER BY aap.IP_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN ALLOWEDADDRESSPAIRS AS aap ON p.ID = aap.PORT_ID WHERE p.ID = '25d421c1-c76d-4c26-9550-9f871a1d5034' ORDER BY aap.IP_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST) SELECT inner_cte.allowed_mac_address, inner_cte.allowed_ip_address, inner_cte.port_id, inner_cte.security_group_id, inner_cte.window_column, port_sg_counts.total_sg_bindings_on_port, avg_sg.avg_sg_bindings_per_port FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT sgpb.PORT_ID AS port_id, COUNT(*) AS total_sg_bindings_on_port FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb GROUP BY sgpb.PORT_ID) AS port_sg_counts ON port_sg_counts.port_id = inner_cte.port_id JOIN (SELECT AVG(sg_count) AS avg_sg_bindings_per_port FROM (SELECT COUNT(*) AS sg_count FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS GROUP BY PORT_ID) AS sub) AS avg_sg ON 1 = 1 ORDER BY inner_cte.allowed_ip_address NULLS FIRST
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(sa.ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sa.RESOURCE_TYPE ORDER BY sa.ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), ResourceTypeAverages AS (SELECT sa.RESOURCE_TYPE, AVG(sa.ID) AS avg_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' GROUP BY sa.RESOURCE_TYPE) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.mac_address, i.window_column FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN PORTS AS p2 ON p2.ID = i.port_id JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa2 ON sa2.ID = p2.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa2.ID > (SELECT avg_standard_attr_id FROM ResourceTypeAverages AS rta WHERE rta.RESOURCE_TYPE = sa2.RESOURCE_TYPE)
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT lb.NAME AS loadbalancer_name, lb.VIP_ADDRESS AS vip_address, stats.BYTES_IN AS bytes_in, stats.TOTAL_CONNECTIONS AS total_connections, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY lb.NAME NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCER_STATISTICS AS stats JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb ON stats.LOADBALANCER_ID = lb.ID JOIN PROVIDERRESOURCEASSOCIATIONS AS pra ON lb.ID = pra.RESOURCE_ID WHERE pra.PROVIDER_NAME = 'haproxy'), haproxy_avg AS (SELECT AVG(stats2.BYTES_IN * 1.0) AS avg_bytes_in FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCER_STATISTICS AS stats2 JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb2 ON stats2.LOADBALANCER_ID = lb2.ID JOIN PROVIDERRESOURCEASSOCIATIONS AS pra2 ON lb2.ID = pra2.RESOURCE_ID WHERE pra2.PROVIDER_NAME = 'haproxy') SELECT i.loadbalancer_name, i.vip_address, i.bytes_in, i.total_connections, i.window_column FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN haproxy_avg AS a WHERE i.bytes_in > a.avg_bytes_in ORDER BY i.loadbalancer_name NULLS FIRST
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT aap.MAC_ADDRESS AS allowed_mac_address, aap.IP_ADDRESS AS allowed_ip_address, p.ID AS port_id, sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID AS security_group_id, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY p.ID ORDER BY aap.IP_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN ALLOWEDADDRESSPAIRS AS aap ON p.ID = aap.PORT_ID WHERE p.ID = '25d421c1-c76d-4c26-9550-9f871a1d5034' ORDER BY aap.IP_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST) SELECT allowed_mac_address, allowed_ip_address, port_id, security_group_id, window_column FROM inner_cte WHERE window_column > 0 ORDER BY allowed_ip_address NULLS FIRST
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT lb.OPERATING_STATUS, COUNT(*) AS total_loadbalancers FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb JOIN PROVIDERRESOURCEASSOCIATIONS AS pra ON lb.ID = pra.RESOURCE_ID WHERE pra.PROVIDER_NAME = 'haproxy' GROUP BY lb.OPERATING_STATUS HAVING COUNT(*) > 1), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(total_loadbalancers) AS avg_loadbalancers FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.OPERATING_STATUS, i.total_loadbalancers, a.avg_loadbalancers AS benchmark, CASE WHEN i.total_loadbalancers > a.avg_loadbalancers * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_COUNT' WHEN i.total_loadbalancers < a.avg_loadbalancers * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_COUNT' ELSE 'AVERAGE_COUNT' END AS count_category FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS a
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.name AS network_name, SUM(1) AS total_security_bindings FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN NETWORKSECURITYBINDINGS AS nsb ON n.id = nsb.network_id WHERE n.status = 'ACTIVE' GROUP BY n.name HAVING SUM(1) > 1), AverageBindings AS (SELECT AVG(total_security_bindings) AS avg_bindings FROM inner_cte) SELECT ic.network_name, ic.total_security_bindings, ab.avg_bindings AS benchmark, CASE WHEN ic.total_security_bindings > ab.avg_bindings * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_BINDINGS' WHEN ic.total_security_bindings < ab.avg_bindings * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_BINDINGS' ELSE 'AVERAGE_BINDINGS' END AS bindings_category FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN AverageBindings AS ab
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.name, MAX(s.ip_version) AS max_ip_version, s.gateway_ip FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.object_id = s.network_id WHERE n.action = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY s.name, s.gateway_ip HAVING MAX(s.ip_version) > 3), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(max_ip_version) AS avg_max_ip_version, COUNT(*) AS total_groups FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.name, i.gateway_ip, i.max_ip_version, a.total_groups, (i.max_ip_version - a.avg_max_ip_version) AS deviation_from_avg_max_ip_version FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS a
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), AdminUpUtilization AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, SUM(CASE WHEN p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS admin_up_ports, COUNT(p.ID) AS total_ports FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER) SELECT i.port_status, i.device_owner, i.port_count, i.earliest_standard_attr_update FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN AdminUpUtilization AS a ON a.port_status = i.port_status AND a.device_owner = i.device_owner WHERE a.total_ports > 0 AND (CAST(a.admin_up_ports * 1.0 AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(a.total_ports, 0)) >= 0.80
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.name AS subnet_name, MAX(pool_count) - MIN(pool_count) AS pool_count_range, VAR_SAMP(pool_count) AS pool_count_variance, STDDEV(pool_count) AS pool_count_stddev FROM subnets AS s JOIN dnsnameservers AS d ON d.subnet_id = s.id JOIN (SELECT s2.id AS subnet_id, COUNT(*) AS pool_count FROM subnets AS s2 JOIN ipallocationpools AS p ON p.subnet_id = s2.id GROUP BY s2.id, p.id) AS pool_stats ON pool_stats.subnet_id = s.id GROUP BY s.name ORDER BY pool_count_range DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT inner_cte.subnet_name, inner_cte.pool_count_range, inner_cte.pool_count_variance, inner_cte.pool_count_stddev, avg_stats.avg_pool_count_range FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT AVG(pool_count_range) AS avg_pool_count_range FROM inner_cte) AS avg_stats ON 1 = 1 ORDER BY inner_cte.pool_count_range DESC NULLS LAST
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS min_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS spb ON p.ID = spb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER) SELECT inner_cte.port_status, inner_cte.device_owner, inner_cte.port_count, inner_cte.min_standard_attr_id, avg_counts.avg_port_count FROM inner_cte, (SELECT AVG(port_count) AS avg_port_count FROM inner_cte) AS avg_counts
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT lb.NAME, lb.VIP_ADDRESS, stats.TOTAL_CONNECTIONS, stats.BYTES_IN, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY lb.TENANT_ID ORDER BY lb.ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCER_STATISTICS AS stats ON lb.ID = stats.LOADBALANCER_ID WHERE lb.PROVISIONING_STATUS = 'ACTIVE'), tenant_avg AS (SELECT lb.TENANT_ID, AVG(stats.TOTAL_CONNECTIONS) AS avg_total_connections FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCER_STATISTICS AS stats ON lb.ID = stats.LOADBALANCER_ID WHERE lb.PROVISIONING_STATUS = 'ACTIVE' GROUP BY lb.TENANT_ID) SELECT lb.TENANT_ID, i.NAME, i.VIP_ADDRESS, i.TOTAL_CONNECTIONS, i.BYTES_IN, i.window_column, ta.avg_total_connections AS benchmark, CASE WHEN i.TOTAL_CONNECTIONS > ta.avg_total_connections * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_CONN' WHEN i.TOTAL_CONNECTIONS < ta.avg_total_connections * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_CONN' ELSE 'AVG_CONN' END AS connection_category FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb ON lb.NAME = i.NAME AND lb.VIP_ADDRESS = i.VIP_ADDRESS JOIN tenant_avg AS ta ON ta.TENANT_ID = lb.TENANT_ID
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(ia.ip_address) AS ip_allocation_count, SUM(CASE WHEN NOT ia.subnet_id IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS subnet_id_rows FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON ia.port_id = p.id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND NOT ia.ip_address IS NULL GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address) SELECT inner_cte.tenant_id AS ports_tenant_id, COUNT(inner_cte.port_id) AS total_admin_up_ports_with_ip_allocations, SUM(inner_cte.ip_allocation_count) AS total_ip_allocations, SUM(inner_cte.subnet_id_rows) AS total_subnet_id_rows, avg_ports.avg_port_count AS avg_ports_per_tenant FROM inner_cte, (SELECT AVG(port_count) AS avg_port_count FROM (SELECT COUNT(port_id) AS port_count FROM inner_cte GROUP BY tenant_id) AS sub) AS avg_ports GROUP BY inner_cte.tenant_id, avg_ports.avg_port_count
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT COUNT(*) AS active_shared_rbac_networks FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r ON n.ID = r.OBJECT_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE' AND r.ACTION = 'access_as_shared') SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, COUNT(DISTINCT r.TARGET_TENANT) AS distinct_target_tenants FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r ON n.ID = r.OBJECT_ID CROSS JOIN inner_cte WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE' AND r.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY n.ID, n.NAME HAVING COUNT(DISTINCT r.TARGET_TENANT) > 2
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.STATUS AS port_status, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY p.NETWORK_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID = '0008e629-5619-41e0-b86c-4eecfeaacb6c'), NetworkAverages AS (SELECT p.NETWORK_ID AS network_id, AVG(inner_cte.window_column) AS avg_window_column FROM inner_cte JOIN PORTS AS p ON inner_cte.port_id = p.ID GROUP BY p.NETWORK_ID) SELECT inner_cte.port_id, inner_cte.port_status, inner_cte.ip_address, p.NETWORK_ID AS network_id, inner_cte.window_column, na.avg_window_column FROM inner_cte JOIN PORTS AS p ON inner_cte.port_id = p.ID JOIN NetworkAverages AS na ON p.NETWORK_ID = na.network_id WHERE inner_cte.window_column > na.avg_window_column
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.status, CAST(STDDEV(LENGTH(ia.ip_address)) AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(AVG(LENGTH(ia.ip_address)), 0), 0) AS coefficient_of_variation FROM portsecuritybindings AS psb JOIN ports AS p ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON p.id = ia.port_id WHERE psb.port_security_enabled = 1 GROUP BY p.status HAVING AVG(LENGTH(ia.ip_address)) > 0 ORDER BY coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT p.tenant_id, CAST(STDDEV(LENGTH(ia.ip_address)) AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(AVG(LENGTH(ia.ip_address)), 0), 0) AS tenant_coefficient_of_variation, CASE WHEN SUM(CASE WHEN p.status = 'ACTIVE' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_active_ports, CASE WHEN SUM(CASE WHEN p.status = 'DOWN' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_down_ports, CASE WHEN SUM(CASE WHEN p.status = 'BUILD' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_build_ports FROM portsecuritybindings AS psb JOIN ports AS p ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON p.id = ia.port_id JOIN inner_cte AS ic ON ic.status = p.status WHERE psb.port_security_enabled = 1 GROUP BY p.tenant_id HAVING AVG(LENGTH(ia.ip_address)) > 0 ORDER BY tenant_coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.ACTION, STDDEV(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_stddev FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.ENABLE_DHCP = 1 GROUP BY n.ACTION HAVING COUNT(*) > 5 ORDER BY ip_version_stddev DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT inner_cte.ACTION, inner_cte.ip_version_stddev, avg_stddevs.avg_ip_version_stddev FROM inner_cte, (SELECT AVG(ip_version_stddev) AS avg_ip_version_stddev FROM inner_cte) AS avg_stddevs ORDER BY inner_cte.ip_version_stddev DESC NULLS LAST
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS min_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS spb ON p.ID = spb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER) SELECT i.port_status, i.device_owner, i.port_count, i.min_standard_attr_id, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT spb2.PORT_ID) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_security_group_bound_ports FROM inner_cte AS i LEFT JOIN PORTS AS p2 ON p2.STATUS = i.port_status AND p2.DEVICE_OWNER = i.device_owner LEFT JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS spb2 ON p2.ID = spb2.PORT_ID GROUP BY i.port_status, i.device_owner, i.port_count, i.min_standard_attr_id ORDER BY i.port_count DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.ACTION, AVG(s.IP_VERSION) AS avg_ip_version, VAR_SAMP(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_variance, STDDEV(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_stddev FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 GROUP BY n.ACTION ORDER BY ip_version_variance DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT inner_cte.action, inner_cte.avg_ip_version, inner_cte.ip_version_variance, inner_cte.ip_version_stddev, overall_avg.avg_of_action_avg_ip_version FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT AVG(avg_ip_version) AS avg_of_action_avg_ip_version FROM inner_cte) AS overall_avg ON 1 = 1
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.NAME AS subnet_name, AVG(d.ORDER) AS avg_dns_order, VAR_SAMP(d.ORDER) AS dns_order_variance FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN DNSNAMESERVERS AS d ON s.ID = d.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 GROUP BY s.NAME ORDER BY dns_order_variance DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT inner_cte.subnet_name, inner_cte.avg_dns_order, inner_cte.dns_order_variance, overall_avg.avg_of_subnet_avg_dns_order FROM inner_cte, (SELECT AVG(avg_dns_order) AS avg_of_subnet_avg_dns_order FROM inner_cte) AS overall_avg ORDER BY inner_cte.dns_order_variance DESC NULLS LAST
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.status, CAST(STDDEV(p.standard_attr_id) AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(AVG(p.standard_attr_id), 0), 0) AS coefficient_of_variation FROM securitygroupportbindings AS sgpb JOIN ports AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id GROUP BY p.status HAVING AVG(p.standard_attr_id) > 0 ORDER BY coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT inner_cte.status, inner_cte.coefficient_of_variation, avg_cov.avg_coefficient_of_variation FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT AVG(coefficient_of_variation) AS avg_coefficient_of_variation FROM inner_cte) AS avg_cov ON 1 = 1 ORDER BY inner_cte.coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1), VifTypePorts AS (SELECT VIF_TYPE, COUNT(PORT_ID) AS total_ports FROM inner_cte GROUP BY VIF_TYPE), AveragePorts AS (SELECT AVG(total_ports) AS avg_ports FROM VifTypePorts) SELECT vtp.VIF_TYPE, vtp.total_ports, ap.avg_ports AS benchmark, CASE WHEN vtp.total_ports > ap.avg_ports * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_USER' WHEN vtp.total_ports < ap.avg_ports * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_USER' ELSE 'AVERAGE_USER' END AS user_category FROM VifTypePorts AS vtp CROSS JOIN AveragePorts AS ap
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.name AS port_name, COUNT(sgpb.security_group_id) AS security_group_binding_count, SUM(CASE WHEN pd.port_id IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END) AS dns_record_count FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id LEFT JOIN PORTDNSES AS pd ON p.id = pd.port_id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.name), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(security_group_binding_count) AS avg_sg_binding_count FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.port_name, i.security_group_binding_count, i.dns_record_count, (i.security_group_binding_count - a.avg_sg_binding_count) AS deviation_from_avg_sg_binding_count FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS a
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.STATUS, VAR_SAMP(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS standard_attr_id_variance FROM PORTS AS p JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE NOT p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID IS NULL GROUP BY p.STATUS ORDER BY standard_attr_id_variance DESC NULLS LAST), AverageVariance AS (SELECT AVG(standard_attr_id_variance) AS avg_variance FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.STATUS, i.standard_attr_id_variance, av.avg_variance AS benchmark, CASE WHEN i.standard_attr_id_variance > av.avg_variance * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_VARIANCE' WHEN i.standard_attr_id_variance < av.avg_variance * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_VARIANCE' ELSE 'AVERAGE_VARIANCE' END AS variance_category FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN AverageVariance AS av
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, b.HOST AS host, b.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ia.IP_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), TenantDownPorts AS (SELECT tenant_id, COUNT(port_id) AS total_down_ports FROM inner_cte GROUP BY tenant_id) SELECT tenant_id, total_down_ports FROM TenantDownPorts WHERE total_down_ports > (SELECT AVG(total_down_ports) FROM TenantDownPorts)
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.ACTION AS rbac_action, AVG(s.IP_VERSION) AS avg_ip_version, MAX(s.IP_VERSION) - MIN(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_range, VAR_SAMP(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_variance FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION IN (4, 6) GROUP BY n.ACTION ORDER BY ip_version_range DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT i.rbac_action, i.avg_ip_version, i.ip_version_range, i.ip_version_variance, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN s.ENABLE_DHCP = 1 THEN s.ID END) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_dhcp_enabled_subnet FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS n ON n.ACTION = i.rbac_action JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON s.NETWORK_ID = n.OBJECT_ID AND s.IP_VERSION IN (4, 6) GROUP BY i.rbac_action, i.avg_ip_version, i.ip_version_range, i.ip_version_variance ORDER BY i.ip_version_range DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.OBJECT_ID AS network_id, COUNT(*) AS subnet_count FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY n.OBJECT_ID ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 3), AverageSubnets AS (SELECT AVG(subnet_count) AS avg_subnet_count FROM inner_cte) SELECT ic.network_id, ic.subnet_count, a.avg_subnet_count AS benchmark, CASE WHEN ic.subnet_count > a.avg_subnet_count * 1.5 THEN 'ABOVE_BENCHMARK' WHEN ic.subnet_count < a.avg_subnet_count * 0.5 THEN 'BELOW_BENCHMARK' ELSE 'NEAR_BENCHMARK' END AS subnet_category FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN AverageSubnets AS a
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.NAME, s.TENANT_ID, s.NETWORK_ID, s.IP_VERSION, s.CIDR, s.GATEWAY_IP, s.ENABLE_DHCP, p.FIRST_IP, p.LAST_IP, MAX(s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (ORDER BY s.ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_standard_attr_id_next2 FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN IPALLOCATIONPOOLS AS p ON s.ID = p.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4) SELECT i.NAME, i.TENANT_ID, i.NETWORK_ID, i.IP_VERSION, i.CIDR, i.GATEWAY_IP, i.ENABLE_DHCP, i.FIRST_IP, i.LAST_IP, i.max_standard_attr_id_next2 FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN SUBNETS AS s2 ON s2.TENANT_ID = i.TENANT_ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONPOOLS AS p2 ON p2.SUBNET_ID = s2.ID WHERE i.FIRST_IP = (SELECT MIN(p3.FIRST_IP) FROM IPALLOCATIONPOOLS AS p3 WHERE p3.SUBNET_ID = s2.ID)
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT lb.ID AS loadbalancer_id, lb.NAME AS loadbalancer_name, pra.PROVIDER_NAME AS provider_name, lb.VIP_ADDRESS AS vip_address, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY pra.PROVIDER_NAME ORDER BY stats.TOTAL_CONNECTIONS NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCER_STATISTICS AS stats JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb ON stats.LOADBALANCER_ID = lb.ID JOIN PROVIDERRESOURCEASSOCIATIONS AS pra ON lb.ID = pra.RESOURCE_ID WHERE lb.OPERATING_STATUS = 'ONLINE'), provider_avg AS (SELECT pra.PROVIDER_NAME AS provider_name, AVG(stats.TOTAL_CONNECTIONS) AS avg_total_connections FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCER_STATISTICS AS stats JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb ON stats.LOADBALANCER_ID = lb.ID JOIN PROVIDERRESOURCEASSOCIATIONS AS pra ON lb.ID = pra.RESOURCE_ID WHERE lb.OPERATING_STATUS = 'ONLINE' GROUP BY pra.PROVIDER_NAME) SELECT i.loadbalancer_id, i.loadbalancer_name, i.provider_name, i.vip_address, i.window_column AS rank_within_provider_by_total_connections, (stats.TOTAL_CONNECTIONS - pa.avg_total_connections) AS deviation_from_provider_avg_total_connections FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCER_STATISTICS AS stats ON stats.LOADBALANCER_ID = i.loadbalancer_id JOIN provider_avg AS pa ON pa.provider_name = i.provider_name
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, AVG(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST) SELECT VIF_TYPE, MAX(window_column) AS max_windowed_avg_admin_state_up FROM inner_cte GROUP BY VIF_TYPE
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.ACTION, AVG(s.IP_VERSION) AS avg_ip_version, VAR_SAMP(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_variance, STDDEV(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_stddev FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 GROUP BY n.ACTION ORDER BY ip_version_variance DESC NULLS LAST), overall_avg AS (SELECT AVG(avg_ip_version) AS overall_avg_ip_version FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.ACTION, i.avg_ip_version, i.ip_version_variance, i.ip_version_stddev, (i.avg_ip_version - o.overall_avg_ip_version) AS deviation_from_overall_avg FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN overall_avg AS o

WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT lb.NAME AS loadbalancer_name, lb.VIP_ADDRESS AS vip_address, stats.BYTES_IN AS bytes_in, stats.TOTAL_CONNECTIONS AS total_connections, pra.PROVIDER_NAME AS provider_name, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY lb.TENANT_ID ORDER BY lb.ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCER_STATISTICS AS stats JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb ON stats.LOADBALANCER_ID = lb.ID JOIN PROVIDERRESOURCEASSOCIATIONS AS pra ON lb.ID = pra.RESOURCE_ID WHERE lb.PROVISIONING_STATUS = 'ACTIVE'), ProviderAverages AS (SELECT provider_name, AVG(total_connections) AS avg_total_connections FROM inner_cte GROUP BY provider_name) SELECT i.loadbalancer_name, i.vip_address, i.bytes_in, i.total_connections, i.provider_name, i.window_column FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN ProviderAverages AS pa ON i.provider_name = pa.provider_name WHERE i.total_connections > pa.avg_total_connections
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.status, CAST(STDDEV(p.standard_attr_id) AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(AVG(p.standard_attr_id), 0), 0) AS coefficient_of_variation FROM securitygroupportbindings AS sgpb JOIN ports AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id GROUP BY p.status HAVING AVG(p.standard_attr_id) > 0 ORDER BY coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST), avg_cv AS (SELECT AVG(coefficient_of_variation) AS avg_coefficient_of_variation FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.status, i.coefficient_of_variation, a.avg_coefficient_of_variation AS benchmark, CASE WHEN i.coefficient_of_variation > a.avg_coefficient_of_variation * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_VARIATION' WHEN i.coefficient_of_variation < a.avg_coefficient_of_variation * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_VARIATION' ELSE 'AVERAGE_VARIATION' END AS variation_category FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_cv AS a
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS min_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS spb ON p.ID = spb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER) SELECT port_status, device_owner, port_count, min_standard_attr_id FROM inner_cte WHERE port_count > (SELECT AVG(port_count) FROM inner_cte)
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.name AS subnet_name, MAX(pool_count) - MIN(pool_count) AS pool_count_range, VAR_SAMP(pool_count) AS pool_count_variance, STDDEV(pool_count) AS pool_count_stddev FROM subnets AS s JOIN dnsnameservers AS d ON d.subnet_id = s.id JOIN (SELECT s2.id AS subnet_id, COUNT(*) AS pool_count FROM subnets AS s2 JOIN ipallocationpools AS p ON p.subnet_id = s2.id GROUP BY s2.id, p.id) AS pool_stats ON pool_stats.subnet_id = s.id GROUP BY s.name ORDER BY pool_count_range DESC NULLS LAST), avg_ranges AS (SELECT AVG(pool_count_range) AS avg_pool_count_range FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.subnet_name, i.pool_count_range, i.pool_count_variance, i.pool_count_stddev, (i.pool_count_range - a.avg_pool_count_range) AS deviation_from_avg_pool_count_range FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_ranges AS a ORDER BY i.pool_count_range DESC NULLS LAST
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT MIN(t2.ORDER) AS min_dns_order, AVG(t1.IP_VERSION) AS avg_ip_version FROM SUBNETROUTES AS t0 JOIN SUBNETS AS t1 ON t0.SUBNET_ID = t1.ID JOIN DNSNAMESERVERS AS t2 ON t1.ID = t2.SUBNET_ID WHERE t1.IP_VERSION = 4 AND t2.ADDRESS = '10.107.23.242/8' GROUP BY t1.NAME) SELECT inner_cte.min_dns_order, inner_cte.avg_ip_version, overall_avg.avg_min_dns_order FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT AVG(min_dns_order) AS avg_min_dns_order FROM inner_cte) AS overall_avg ON 1 = 1
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.name, n.status, n.admin_state_up, r.action, r.target_tenant, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY n.id ORDER BY n.id NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM networksecuritybindings AS b JOIN networks AS n ON b.network_id = n.id JOIN networkrbacs AS r ON n.id = r.object_id WHERE n.status = 'ACTIVE'), rbac_counts AS (SELECT n.id AS network_id, COUNT(DISTINCT r.target_tenant) AS distinct_target_tenants FROM networksecuritybindings AS b JOIN networks AS n ON b.network_id = n.id JOIN networkrbacs AS r ON n.id = r.object_id WHERE n.status = 'ACTIVE' GROUP BY n.id) SELECT i.name, i.status, i.admin_state_up, i.action, i.target_tenant, c.distinct_target_tenants, i.window_column FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN rbac_counts AS c ON c.network_id = (SELECT n2.id FROM networks AS n2 WHERE n2.name = i.name AND n2.status = i.status AND n2.admin_state_up = i.admin_state_up LIMIT 1)
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.NAME, s.TENANT_ID, s.NETWORK_ID, s.IP_VERSION, s.CIDR, s.GATEWAY_IP, s.ENABLE_DHCP, p.FIRST_IP, p.LAST_IP, MAX(s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (ORDER BY s.ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_standard_attr_id_next2 FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN IPALLOCATIONPOOLS AS p ON s.ID = p.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4), DhcpAverages AS (SELECT ENABLE_DHCP, AVG(max_standard_attr_id_next2) AS avg_max_next2 FROM inner_cte GROUP BY ENABLE_DHCP) SELECT i.NAME, i.TENANT_ID, i.NETWORK_ID, i.IP_VERSION, i.CIDR, i.GATEWAY_IP, i.ENABLE_DHCP, i.FIRST_IP, i.LAST_IP, i.max_standard_attr_id_next2 FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN DhcpAverages AS d ON i.ENABLE_DHCP = d.ENABLE_DHCP WHERE i.max_standard_attr_id_next2 > d.avg_max_next2
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.status, MAX(ps.binding_count) - MIN(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_range, VAR_SAMP(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_variance, STDDEV(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_stddev FROM ports AS p JOIN (SELECT p2.id AS port_id, COUNT(*) AS binding_count FROM ports AS p2 JOIN portsecuritybindings AS b ON p2.id = b.port_id GROUP BY p2.id) AS ps ON p.id = ps.port_id GROUP BY p.status ORDER BY binding_count_range DESC NULLS LAST), NetworkStatusRanges AS (SELECT p.network_id, p.status, MAX(ps.binding_count) - MIN(ps.binding_count) AS network_binding_count_range FROM ports AS p JOIN (SELECT p2.id AS port_id, COUNT(*) AS binding_count FROM ports AS p2 JOIN portsecuritybindings AS b ON p2.id = b.port_id GROUP BY p2.id) AS ps ON p.id = ps.port_id GROUP BY p.network_id, p.status) SELECT i.status, MAX(nsr.network_binding_count_range) AS max_network_binding_count_range FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN NetworkStatusRanges AS nsr ON i.status = nsr.status GROUP BY i.status ORDER BY max_network_binding_count_range DESC NULLS LAST
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT lb.NAME, lb.VIP_ADDRESS, stats.TOTAL_CONNECTIONS, stats.BYTES_IN, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY lb.TENANT_ID ORDER BY lb.ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCER_STATISTICS AS stats ON lb.ID = stats.LOADBALANCER_ID WHERE lb.PROVISIONING_STATUS = 'ACTIVE') SELECT inner_cte.NAME, inner_cte.VIP_ADDRESS, inner_cte.TOTAL_CONNECTIONS, inner_cte.BYTES_IN, inner_cte.window_column, tenant_avg.avg_total_connections FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT lb.TENANT_ID, AVG(stats.TOTAL_CONNECTIONS) AS avg_total_connections FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCER_STATISTICS AS stats ON lb.ID = stats.LOADBALANCER_ID WHERE lb.PROVISIONING_STATUS = 'ACTIVE' GROUP BY lb.TENANT_ID) AS tenant_avg ON tenant_avg.TENANT_ID = (SELECT lb2.TENANT_ID FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb2 WHERE lb2.VIP_ADDRESS = inner_cte.VIP_ADDRESS LIMIT 1)
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, b.HOST AS host, b.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ia.IP_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), HostVifShare AS (SELECT host, COUNT(DISTINCT port_id) AS down_ports, SUM(CASE WHEN vif_type = 'ovs' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS ovs_down_ports FROM inner_cte GROUP BY host) SELECT host, down_ports, ovs_down_ports, (CAST(ovs_down_ports * 1.0 AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(down_ports, 0)) AS ovs_share FROM HostVifShare WHERE down_ports > 0 AND (CAST(ovs_down_ports * 1.0 AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(down_ports, 0)) > 0.60
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT lb.NAME AS loadbalancer_name, lb.VIP_ADDRESS AS vip_address, stats.BYTES_IN AS bytes_in, stats.TOTAL_CONNECTIONS AS total_connections, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY lb.NAME NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCER_STATISTICS AS stats JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb ON stats.LOADBALANCER_ID = lb.ID JOIN PROVIDERRESOURCEASSOCIATIONS AS pra ON lb.ID = pra.RESOURCE_ID WHERE pra.PROVIDER_NAME = 'haproxy') SELECT lb.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, MAX(inner_cte.total_connections) AS max_total_connections FROM inner_cte JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb ON lb.NAME = inner_cte.loadbalancer_name AND lb.VIP_ADDRESS = inner_cte.vip_address GROUP BY lb.TENANT_ID
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.ACTION, AVG(s.IP_VERSION) AS avg_ip_version, VAR_SAMP(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_variance, STDDEV(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_stddev FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 GROUP BY n.ACTION ORDER BY ip_version_variance DESC NULLS LAST), OverallBenchmark AS (SELECT AVG(avg_ip_version) AS benchmark_avg_ip_version FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.ACTION, i.avg_ip_version, i.ip_version_variance, i.ip_version_stddev, ob.benchmark_avg_ip_version AS benchmark, CASE WHEN i.avg_ip_version > ob.benchmark_avg_ip_version * 1.10 THEN 'HIGH_IPV4' WHEN i.avg_ip_version < ob.benchmark_avg_ip_version * 0.90 THEN 'LOW_IPV4' ELSE 'AVERAGE_IPV4' END AS ipv4_category FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN OverallBenchmark AS ob
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.OBJECT_ID AS network_id, COUNT(*) AS subnet_count FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY n.OBJECT_ID ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 3), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(subnet_count) AS avg_subnet_count FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.network_id, i.subnet_count, (i.subnet_count - a.avg_subnet_count) AS deviation_from_avg FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS a
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.name, STDDEV(d.ORDER) AS order_stddev FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN DNSNAMESERVERS AS d ON s.id = d.subnet_id WHERE s.ip_version = 4 GROUP BY s.name HAVING COUNT(*) > 5 ORDER BY order_stddev DESC NULLS LAST), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(order_stddev) AS avg_order_stddev FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.name, i.order_stddev, (i.order_stddev - a.avg_order_stddev) AS deviation_from_avg_order_stddev FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS a ORDER BY deviation_from_avg_order_stddev DESC NULLS LAST
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, p.TENANT_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE b.HOST = 'drive-59') SELECT i.TENANT_ID, COUNT(DISTINCT i.PORT_ID) AS total_ports, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT psb.PORT_ID) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_port_security_enabled FROM inner_cte AS i LEFT JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON i.PORT_ID = psb.PORT_ID GROUP BY i.TENANT_ID ORDER BY total_ports DESC NULLS LAST
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, EXP(AVG(LN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID))) AS geometric_mean_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID > 0 GROUP BY s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY geometric_mean_standard_attr_id DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT SECURITY_GROUP_ID, geometric_mean_standard_attr_id FROM inner_cte WHERE geometric_mean_standard_attr_id > 1000 ORDER BY geometric_mean_standard_attr_id DESC NULLS LAST
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT MIN(t2.ORDER) AS min_dns_order, AVG(t1.IP_VERSION) AS avg_ip_version FROM SUBNETROUTES AS t0 JOIN SUBNETS AS t1 ON t0.SUBNET_ID = t1.ID JOIN DNSNAMESERVERS AS t2 ON t1.ID = t2.SUBNET_ID WHERE t1.IP_VERSION = 4 AND t2.ADDRESS = '10.107.23.242/8' GROUP BY t1.NAME) SELECT min_dns_order, avg_ip_version FROM inner_cte WHERE min_dns_order > 0
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), SecurityGroupDownCounts AS (SELECT sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID AS security_group_id, COUNT(*) AS down_ports_in_group FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' GROUP BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID), AverageDownCounts AS (SELECT AVG(down_ports_in_group) AS avg_down_ports_in_group FROM SecurityGroupDownCounts) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.mac_address, i.window_column, sgdc.down_ports_in_group, (sgdc.down_ports_in_group - adc.avg_down_ports_in_group) AS deviation_from_avg_down_ports FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb ON sgpb.PORT_ID = i.port_id JOIN SecurityGroupDownCounts AS sgdc ON sgdc.security_group_id = sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID CROSS JOIN AverageDownCounts AS adc
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT e.NETWORK_ID, e.IS_DEFAULT, n.TENANT_ID, n.NAME, n.STATUS, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP, n.MTU, n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID, MIN(n.MTU) OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.TENANT_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM EXTERNALNETWORKS AS e JOIN NETWORKS AS n ON e.NETWORK_ID = n.ID WHERE e.IS_DEFAULT = 0) SELECT i.TENANT_ID, COUNT(DISTINCT i.NETWORK_ID) AS total_default_external_networks, MIN(i.MTU) AS min_mtu_in_tenant FROM inner_cte AS i GROUP BY i.TENANT_ID ORDER BY total_default_external_networks DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY psb.port_security_enabled NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN' AND p.device_id IN ('da811c75-0f9b-462c-b020-8ea55f6ca7c7')), TenantPorts AS (SELECT p.tenant_id, COUNT(inner_cte.port_id) AS total_ports FROM inner_cte JOIN ports AS p ON p.id = inner_cte.port_id GROUP BY p.tenant_id), AveragePorts AS (SELECT AVG(total_ports) AS avg_ports FROM TenantPorts) SELECT tp.tenant_id, tp.total_ports, ap.avg_ports AS benchmark, CASE WHEN tp.total_ports > ap.avg_ports * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_USER' WHEN tp.total_ports < ap.avg_ports * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_USER' ELSE 'AVERAGE_USER' END AS user_category FROM TenantPorts AS tp CROSS JOIN AveragePorts AS ap
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT inner_cte.port_id, inner_cte.tenant_id, inner_cte.mac_address, inner_cte.window_column, tenant_counts.total_down_ports_for_tenant, avg_counts.avg_down_ports_across_tenants FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT p.tenant_id AS tenant_id, COUNT(p.id) AS total_down_ports_for_tenant FROM ports AS p WHERE p.status = 'DOWN' GROUP BY p.tenant_id) AS tenant_counts ON inner_cte.tenant_id = tenant_counts.tenant_id JOIN (SELECT AVG(port_count) AS avg_down_ports_across_tenants FROM (SELECT COUNT(p.id) AS port_count FROM ports AS p WHERE p.status = 'DOWN' GROUP BY p.tenant_id) AS sub) AS avg_counts ON 1 = 1
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE' AND n.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT inner_cte.network_id, inner_cte.network_name, inner_cte.tenant_id, inner_cte.admin_state_up, inner_cte.mtu, inner_cte.standard_attr_updated_at, inner_cte.window_column, tenant_counts.total_active_networks, avg_counts.avg_active_networks FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT tenant_id, COUNT(network_id) AS total_active_networks FROM inner_cte GROUP BY tenant_id) AS tenant_counts ON tenant_counts.tenant_id = inner_cte.tenant_id JOIN (SELECT AVG(active_network_count) AS avg_active_networks FROM (SELECT tenant_id, COUNT(network_id) AS active_network_count FROM inner_cte GROUP BY tenant_id) AS sub) AS avg_counts ON 1 = 1
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, p.TENANT_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE b.HOST = 'drive-59' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7')) SELECT b.VIF_TYPE, MAX(inner_cte.window_column) AS max_running_sum_standard_attr_id FROM inner_cte JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON inner_cte.PORT_ID = b.PORT_ID GROUP BY b.VIF_TYPE
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.name AS network_name, r.action AS rbac_action, MAX(n.mtu) AS max_mtu FROM NETWORKSECURITYBINDINGS AS nsb JOIN NETWORKS AS n ON nsb.network_id = n.id JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r ON n.id = r.object_id WHERE n.status = 'ACTIVE' AND r.action IN ('access_as_shared', 'access_as_external') GROUP BY n.name, r.action HAVING MAX(n.mtu) > 8000), TargetTenantCounts AS (SELECT ic.network_name, ic.rbac_action, ic.max_mtu, COUNT(DISTINCT r.target_tenant) AS distinct_target_tenants FROM inner_cte AS ic JOIN NETWORKS AS n ON n.name = ic.network_name JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r ON r.object_id = n.id AND r.action = ic.rbac_action GROUP BY ic.network_name, ic.rbac_action, ic.max_mtu), AverageTargetTenantCounts AS (SELECT AVG(distinct_target_tenants) AS avg_distinct_target_tenants FROM TargetTenantCounts) SELECT ttc.network_name, ttc.rbac_action, ttc.max_mtu, ttc.distinct_target_tenants, (ttc.distinct_target_tenants - atc.avg_distinct_target_tenants) AS deviation_from_avg_distinct_target_tenants FROM TargetTenantCounts AS ttc CROSS JOIN AverageTargetTenantCounts AS atc
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS min_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS spb ON p.ID = spb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER) SELECT inner_cte.port_status, inner_cte.device_owner, inner_cte.port_count, inner_cte.min_standard_attr_id, avg_counts.avg_port_count FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT AVG(port_count) AS avg_port_count FROM inner_cte) AS avg_counts ON 1 = 1
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.name AS subnet_name, CAST(STDDEV(d.order) AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(AVG(d.order), 0), 0) AS coefficient_of_variation FROM subnets AS s JOIN dnsnameservers AS d ON d.subnet_id = s.id JOIN ipallocationpools AS p ON p.subnet_id = s.id JOIN networks AS n ON n.id = s.network_id WHERE s.ip_version = 4 AND NOT n.id IN (SELECT r.object_id FROM networkrbacs AS r WHERE r.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (r.target_tenant = '*' OR r.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY s.name HAVING AVG(d.order) > 0 ORDER BY coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(coefficient_of_variation) AS avg_coefficient_of_variation FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.subnet_name, i.coefficient_of_variation, (i.coefficient_of_variation - a.avg_coefficient_of_variation) AS deviation_from_avg_coefficient_of_variation FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS a ORDER BY deviation_from_avg_coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS min_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS spb ON p.ID = spb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER) SELECT i.port_status, i.device_owner, i.port_count, i.min_standard_attr_id, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT spb2.security_group_id) > COUNT(DISTINCT p2.id) THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_multi_sg_bindings FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN ports AS p2 ON p2.status = i.port_status AND p2.device_owner = i.device_owner AND p2.tenant_id IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') JOIN securitygroupportbindings AS spb2 ON p2.id = spb2.port_id GROUP BY i.port_status, i.device_owner, i.port_count, i.min_standard_attr_id ORDER BY i.port_count DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (ORDER BY p.ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.NETWORK_ID IN ('0a1d0a27-cffa-4de3-92c5-9d3fd3f2e74d')), TenantPortCounts AS (SELECT p.TENANT_ID, COUNT(*) AS total_ports FROM inner_cte AS ic JOIN PORTS AS p ON p.NETWORK_ID = ic.NETWORK_ID AND p.MAC_ADDRESS = ic.MAC_ADDRESS AND p.DEVICE_OWNER = ic.DEVICE_OWNER GROUP BY p.TENANT_ID), AveragePorts AS (SELECT AVG(total_ports) AS avg_ports FROM TenantPortCounts) SELECT tpc.TENANT_ID, tpc.total_ports, ap.avg_ports AS benchmark, CASE WHEN tpc.total_ports > ap.avg_ports * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_USER' WHEN tpc.total_ports < ap.avg_ports * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_USER' ELSE 'AVERAGE_USER' END AS user_category FROM TenantPortCounts AS tpc CROSS JOIN AveragePorts AS ap
