WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.name AS port_name, COUNT(sgpb.security_group_id) AS security_group_binding_count, SUM(CASE WHEN pd.port_id IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END) AS dns_record_count FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id LEFT JOIN PORTDNSES AS pd ON p.id = pd.port_id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.name) SELECT port_id, tenant_id, port_name, security_group_binding_count, dns_record_count FROM inner_cte WHERE security_group_binding_count > (SELECT AVG(security_group_binding_count) FROM inner_cte)
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, p.TENANT_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE b.HOST = 'drive-59' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7')), PortCounts AS (SELECT b.VIF_TYPE, COUNT(*) AS ports_in_vif_type FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN inner_cte AS ic ON ic.PORT_ID = b.PORT_ID GROUP BY b.VIF_TYPE), AvgWindowByVif AS (SELECT b.VIF_TYPE, AVG(ic.window_column) AS avg_window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN inner_cte AS ic ON ic.PORT_ID = b.PORT_ID GROUP BY b.VIF_TYPE) SELECT ic.PORT_ID, ic.TENANT_ID, ic.MAC_ADDRESS, ic.window_column, pc.ports_in_vif_type, (ic.window_column - aw.avg_window_column) AS deviation_from_vif_avg FROM inner_cte AS ic JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON b.PORT_ID = ic.PORT_ID JOIN PortCounts AS pc ON pc.VIF_TYPE = b.VIF_TYPE JOIN AvgWindowByVif AS aw ON aw.VIF_TYPE = b.VIF_TYPE
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.name AS network_name, r.action AS rbac_action, MAX(n.mtu) AS max_mtu FROM NETWORKSECURITYBINDINGS AS nsb JOIN NETWORKS AS n ON nsb.network_id = n.id JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r ON n.id = r.object_id WHERE n.status = 'ACTIVE' AND r.action IN ('access_as_shared', 'access_as_external') GROUP BY n.name, r.action HAVING MAX(n.mtu) > 8000) SELECT inner_cte.rbac_action, MAX(inner_cte.max_mtu) AS highest_network_max_mtu FROM inner_cte GROUP BY inner_cte.rbac_action
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.STATUS, VAR_SAMP(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS standard_attr_id_variance FROM PORTS AS p JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE NOT p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID IS NULL AND ia.IP_ADDRESS IN ('10.150.64.113/8') GROUP BY p.STATUS ORDER BY standard_attr_id_variance DESC NULLS LAST), StatusNetworkVariance AS (SELECT p.status, p.network_id, VAR_SAMP(p.standard_attr_id) AS network_level_variance FROM ports AS p JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON p.id = ia.port_id JOIN inner_cte AS ic ON ic.status = p.status WHERE NOT p.standard_attr_id IS NULL AND ia.ip_address IN ('10.150.64.113/8') GROUP BY p.status, p.network_id) SELECT status, MAX(network_level_variance) AS max_network_standard_attr_id_variance FROM StatusNetworkVariance GROUP BY status ORDER BY max_network_standard_attr_id_variance DESC NULLS LAST
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.STATUS, VAR_SAMP(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS standard_attr_id_variance FROM PORTS AS p JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE NOT p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID IS NULL AND ia.IP_ADDRESS IN ('10.150.64.113/8') GROUP BY p.STATUS ORDER BY standard_attr_id_variance DESC NULLS LAST), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(standard_attr_id_variance) AS avg_variance FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.STATUS, i.standard_attr_id_variance, a.avg_variance AS benchmark, CASE WHEN i.standard_attr_id_variance > a.avg_variance * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_VARIANCE' WHEN i.standard_attr_id_variance < a.avg_variance * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_VARIANCE' ELSE 'AVERAGE_VARIANCE' END AS variance_category FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS a
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.ACTION, STDDEV(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_stddev FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.ENABLE_DHCP = 1 AND NOT s.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY n.ACTION HAVING COUNT(*) > 5 ORDER BY ip_version_stddev DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT n.ACTION, MAX(inner_cte.ip_version_stddev) AS max_ip_version_stddev FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID JOIN inner_cte ON inner_cte.ACTION = n.ACTION WHERE s.ENABLE_DHCP = 1 AND NOT s.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY n.ACTION ORDER BY max_ip_version_stddev DESC NULLS LAST
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT lb.NAME AS loadbalancer_name, lb.VIP_ADDRESS AS vip_address, stats.BYTES_IN AS bytes_in, stats.TOTAL_CONNECTIONS AS total_connections, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY lb.NAME NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCER_STATISTICS AS stats JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb ON stats.LOADBALANCER_ID = lb.ID JOIN PROVIDERRESOURCEASSOCIATIONS AS pra ON lb.ID = pra.RESOURCE_ID WHERE pra.PROVIDER_NAME = 'haproxy' AND lb.VIP_ADDRESS IN ('10.20.127.222/8')) SELECT MAX(bytes_in) AS max_bytes_in FROM inner_cte
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.STATUS, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) AS min_admin_state_up, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS avg_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE b.VIF_TYPE = 'ovs' AND p.DEVICE_ID IN ('da811c75-0f9b-462c-b020-8ea55f6ca7c7') GROUP BY p.STATUS) SELECT i.status, COUNT(DISTINCT p2.id) AS total_ports, i.min_admin_state_up, i.avg_standard_attr_id FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN ports AS p2 ON p2.status = i.status JOIN ml2_port_bindings AS b2 ON p2.id = b2.port_id WHERE b2.vif_type = 'ovs' AND p2.device_id IN ('da811c75-0f9b-462c-b020-8ea55f6ca7c7') GROUP BY i.status, i.min_admin_state_up, i.avg_standard_attr_id ORDER BY total_ports DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT lb.OPERATING_STATUS, SUM(s.BYTES_IN) AS total_bytes_in FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCER_STATISTICS AS s ON lb.ID = s.LOADBALANCER_ID WHERE lb.PROVISIONING_STATUS = 'ACTIVE' AND lb.VIP_SUBNET_ID IN (SELECT subnets.id FROM subnets JOIN networks ON networks.id = subnets.network_id WHERE NOT networks.id IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b'))) GROUP BY lb.OPERATING_STATUS HAVING SUM(s.BYTES_IN) > 1000000) SELECT i.OPERATING_STATUS, i.total_bytes_in, CASE WHEN MAX(CASE WHEN s.TOTAL_CONNECTIONS > 10000 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) = 1 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_any_lb_over_10000_total_connections FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb ON lb.OPERATING_STATUS = i.OPERATING_STATUS JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCER_STATISTICS AS s ON s.LOADBALANCER_ID = lb.ID WHERE lb.PROVISIONING_STATUS = 'ACTIVE' GROUP BY i.OPERATING_STATUS, i.total_bytes_in ORDER BY i.total_bytes_in DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.status, MAX(ps.binding_count) - MIN(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_range, VAR_SAMP(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_variance, STDDEV(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_stddev FROM ports AS p JOIN (SELECT p2.id AS port_id, COUNT(*) AS binding_count FROM ports AS p2 JOIN portsecuritybindings AS b ON p2.id = b.port_id WHERE p2.tenant_id IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p2.id) AS ps ON p.id = ps.port_id GROUP BY p.status ORDER BY binding_count_range DESC NULLS LAST), status_network_ranges AS (SELECT p.status, p.network_id, MAX(ps.binding_count) - MIN(ps.binding_count) AS network_binding_count_range FROM ports AS p JOIN (SELECT p2.id AS port_id, COUNT(*) AS binding_count FROM ports AS p2 JOIN portsecuritybindings AS b ON p2.id = b.port_id WHERE p2.tenant_id IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p2.id) AS ps ON p.id = ps.port_id GROUP BY p.status, p.network_id) SELECT i.status, MAX(snr.network_binding_count_range) AS max_network_binding_count_range FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN status_network_ranges AS snr ON i.status = snr.status GROUP BY i.status ORDER BY max_network_binding_count_range DESC NULLS LAST
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.STATUS, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) AS min_admin_state_up, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS avg_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE b.VIF_TYPE = 'ovs' AND p.DEVICE_ID IN ('da811c75-0f9b-462c-b020-8ea55f6ca7c7') GROUP BY p.STATUS) SELECT STATUS, min_admin_state_up, avg_standard_attr_id FROM inner_cte WHERE avg_standard_attr_id > (SELECT AVG(avg_standard_attr_id) FROM inner_cte)
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.NAME AS subnet_name, s.CIDR AS subnet_cidr, n.TARGET_TENANT AS rbac_target_tenant, SUM(s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY s.IP_VERSION ORDER BY s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS n ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' AND n.TARGET_TENANT IN ('1ee6b5b4fe8c45cfa16c3ecdbb8ee02b')) SELECT inner_cte.subnet_name, inner_cte.subnet_cidr, inner_cte.rbac_target_tenant, inner_cte.window_column, tenant_counts.total_subnets_for_target_tenant, avg_counts.avg_subnets_per_target_tenant FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT rbac_target_tenant, COUNT(*) AS total_subnets_for_target_tenant FROM inner_cte GROUP BY rbac_target_tenant) AS tenant_counts ON tenant_counts.rbac_target_tenant = inner_cte.rbac_target_tenant, (SELECT AVG(subnet_count) AS avg_subnets_per_target_tenant FROM (SELECT rbac_target_tenant, COUNT(*) AS subnet_count FROM inner_cte GROUP BY rbac_target_tenant) AS sub) AS avg_counts
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.name AS port_name, COUNT(sgpb.security_group_id) AS security_group_binding_count, SUM(CASE WHEN pd.port_id IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END) AS dns_record_count FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id LEFT JOIN PORTDNSES AS pd ON p.id = pd.port_id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.name) SELECT p.network_id, MAX(inner_cte.security_group_binding_count) AS max_security_group_bindings_on_a_port, MAX(inner_cte.dns_record_count) AS max_dns_records_on_a_port FROM inner_cte JOIN PORTS AS p ON inner_cte.port_id = p.id GROUP BY p.network_id
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, EXP(AVG(LN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID))) AS geometric_mean_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID > 0 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY geometric_mean_standard_attr_id DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT SECURITY_GROUP_ID, geometric_mean_standard_attr_id FROM inner_cte WHERE geometric_mean_standard_attr_id > 1000 ORDER BY geometric_mean_standard_attr_id DESC NULLS LAST
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.name AS subnet_name, CAST(STDDEV(d.order) AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(AVG(d.order), 0), 0) AS coefficient_of_variation FROM subnets AS s JOIN dnsnameservers AS d ON d.subnet_id = s.id JOIN ipallocationpools AS p ON p.subnet_id = s.id JOIN networks AS n ON n.id = s.network_id WHERE s.ip_version = 4 AND NOT n.id IN (SELECT r.object_id FROM networkrbacs AS r WHERE r.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (r.target_tenant = '*' OR r.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY s.name HAVING AVG(d.order) > 0 ORDER BY coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST), CVAverages AS (SELECT AVG(coefficient_of_variation) AS avg_cv FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.subnet_name, i.coefficient_of_variation FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN subnets AS s ON s.name = i.subnet_name JOIN networks AS n ON n.id = s.network_id WHERE i.coefficient_of_variation > (SELECT avg_cv FROM CVAverages) ORDER BY i.coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.NAME, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY sa.UPDATED_AT NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND s.ID IN ('012993ae-5d73-4aad-834b-e87a31e99d48')) SELECT inner_cte.NAME, inner_cte.window_column, CASE WHEN NOT sa.DESCRIPTION IS NULL AND sa.DESCRIPTION <> '' THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_description FROM inner_cte JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON s.NAME = inner_cte.NAME JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.STATUS, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) AS min_admin_state_up, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS avg_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE b.VIF_TYPE = 'ovs' AND p.DEVICE_ID IN ('da811c75-0f9b-462c-b020-8ea55f6ca7c7') GROUP BY p.STATUS) SELECT b.VIF_TYPE, MAX(inner_cte.min_admin_state_up) AS max_of_min_admin_state_up, MAX(inner_cte.avg_standard_attr_id) AS max_of_avg_standard_attr_id FROM inner_cte JOIN PORTS AS p ON p.STATUS = inner_cte.STATUS JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE p.DEVICE_ID IN ('da811c75-0f9b-462c-b020-8ea55f6ca7c7') GROUP BY b.VIF_TYPE
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE' AND n.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), AverageMtu AS (SELECT AVG(mtu) AS avg_mtu FROM inner_cte) SELECT ic.network_id, ic.network_name, ic.tenant_id, ic.admin_state_up, ic.mtu, ic.standard_attr_updated_at, ic.window_column, am.avg_mtu AS benchmark_mtu, CASE WHEN ic.mtu > am.avg_mtu * 1.1 THEN 'HIGH_MTU' WHEN ic.mtu < am.avg_mtu * 0.9 THEN 'LOW_MTU' ELSE 'AVERAGE_MTU' END AS mtu_category FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN AverageMtu AS am
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT inner_cte.port_id, inner_cte.tenant_id, inner_cte.mac_address, sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID AS security_group_id, inner_cte.window_column FROM inner_cte JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb ON inner_cte.port_id = sgpb.PORT_ID WHERE inner_cte.window_column > 100 ORDER BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID NULLS FIRST, inner_cte.window_column NULLS FIRST
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.ACTION, VAR_SAMP(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_variance FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' AND n.TARGET_TENANT = '*' GROUP BY n.ACTION ORDER BY ip_version_variance DESC NULLS LAST), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(ip_version_variance) AS avg_ip_version_variance FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.ACTION, i.ip_version_variance, a.avg_ip_version_variance AS benchmark, CASE WHEN i.ip_version_variance > a.avg_ip_version_variance * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_VARIANCE' WHEN i.ip_version_variance < a.avg_ip_version_variance * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_VARIANCE' ELSE 'AVERAGE_VARIANCE' END AS variance_category FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS a
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.NAME AS subnet_name, MAX(route_count) - MIN(route_count) AS route_count_range, VAR_SAMP(route_count) AS route_count_variance, STDDEV(route_count) AS route_count_stddev FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN (SELECT sr.SUBNET_ID, COUNT(*) AS route_count FROM SUBNETROUTES AS sr GROUP BY sr.SUBNET_ID, sr.NEXTHOP) AS route_stats ON s.ID = route_stats.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND s.ID IN ('012993ae-5d73-4aad-834b-e87a31e99d48') GROUP BY s.NAME ORDER BY route_count_range DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT s.NETWORK_ID AS network_id, MAX(inner_cte.route_count_range) AS max_route_count_range, MAX(inner_cte.route_count_variance) AS max_route_count_variance, MAX(inner_cte.route_count_stddev) AS max_route_count_stddev FROM inner_cte JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON s.NAME = inner_cte.subnet_name WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND s.ID IN ('012993ae-5d73-4aad-834b-e87a31e99d48') GROUP BY s.NETWORK_ID
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.NAME, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY r.ACTION NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM EXTERNALNETWORKS AS e JOIN NETWORKS AS n ON e.NETWORK_ID = n.ID JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r ON n.ID = r.OBJECT_ID WHERE e.IS_DEFAULT = 0 AND r.ACTION = 'access_as_external') SELECT n.tenant_id, COUNT(DISTINCT n.id) AS total_qualifying_networks, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN r.target_tenant <> '*' THEN r.id END) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_targeted_rbac FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN networks AS n ON n.name = i.name JOIN externalnetworks AS e ON e.network_id = n.id JOIN networkrbacs AS r ON r.object_id = n.id WHERE e.is_default = 0 AND r.action = 'access_as_external' GROUP BY n.tenant_id ORDER BY total_qualifying_networks DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.name, MAX(s.ip_version) AS max_ip_version, s.gateway_ip FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.object_id = s.network_id WHERE n.action = 'access_as_shared' AND n.target_tenant = '*' GROUP BY s.name, s.gateway_ip HAVING MAX(s.ip_version) > 3) SELECT inner_cte.name, inner_cte.gateway_ip, inner_cte.max_ip_version, avg_maxes.avg_max_ip_version FROM inner_cte, (SELECT AVG(max_ip_version) AS avg_max_ip_version FROM inner_cte) AS avg_maxes
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, sgpb.security_group_id, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.security_group_id ORDER BY p.id NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN' AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), SecurityGroupAverages AS (SELECT AVG(sg_port_count) AS avg_ports_per_sg FROM (SELECT sgpb2.security_group_id, COUNT(*) AS sg_port_count FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb2 JOIN PORTS AS p2 ON sgpb2.port_id = p2.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb2 ON p2.id = psb2.port_id GROUP BY sgpb2.security_group_id) AS sg_counts) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.mac_address, i.security_group_id, i.window_column FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN (SELECT sgpb3.security_group_id, COUNT(*) AS total_ports_in_sg FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb3 JOIN PORTS AS p3 ON sgpb3.port_id = p3.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb3 ON p3.id = psb3.port_id GROUP BY sgpb3.security_group_id) AS sg_totals ON sg_totals.security_group_id = i.security_group_id WHERE sg_totals.total_ports_in_sg > (SELECT avg_ports_per_sg FROM SecurityGroupAverages)
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.STATUS AS network_status, VAR_SAMP(nsb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED) AS port_security_enabled_variance, STDDEV(nsb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED) AS port_security_enabled_stddev FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN NETWORKSECURITYBINDINGS AS nsb ON n.ID = nsb.NETWORK_ID WHERE NOT n.ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY n.STATUS ORDER BY port_security_enabled_variance DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT n.STATUS AS network_status, COUNT(DISTINCT n.ID) AS total_networks_with_security_binding, inner_cte.port_security_enabled_variance, inner_cte.port_security_enabled_stddev FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN NETWORKSECURITYBINDINGS AS nsb ON n.ID = nsb.NETWORK_ID JOIN inner_cte ON n.STATUS = inner_cte.network_status WHERE NOT n.ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY n.STATUS, inner_cte.port_security_enabled_variance, inner_cte.port_security_enabled_stddev ORDER BY total_networks_with_security_binding DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(ia.ip_address) AS ip_allocation_count, SUM(CASE WHEN NOT ia.subnet_id IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS subnet_id_rows FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON ia.port_id = p.id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND NOT ia.ip_address IS NULL AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address), TenantAverages AS (SELECT tenant_id, AVG(ip_allocation_count) AS avg_ip_allocs FROM inner_cte GROUP BY tenant_id) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.mac_address, i.ip_allocation_count, i.subnet_id_rows FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN TenantAverages AS ta ON ta.tenant_id = i.tenant_id WHERE i.ip_allocation_count > ta.avg_ip_allocs
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.status, MAX(ps.binding_count) - MIN(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_range, VAR_SAMP(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_variance, STDDEV(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_stddev FROM ports AS p JOIN (SELECT p2.id AS port_id, COUNT(*) AS binding_count FROM ports AS p2 JOIN portsecuritybindings AS b ON p2.id = b.port_id WHERE p2.tenant_id IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p2.id) AS ps ON p.id = ps.port_id GROUP BY p.status ORDER BY binding_count_range DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT inner_cte.status, inner_cte.binding_count_range, inner_cte.binding_count_variance, inner_cte.binding_count_stddev, avg_bindings.avg_binding_count FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT AVG(binding_count) AS avg_binding_count FROM (SELECT p2.id AS port_id, COUNT(*) AS binding_count FROM ports AS p2 JOIN portsecuritybindings AS b ON p2.id = b.port_id WHERE p2.tenant_id IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p2.id) AS sub) AS avg_bindings ON 1 = 1 ORDER BY inner_cte.binding_count_range DESC NULLS LAST
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.id AS network_id, SUM(1) AS total_subnets FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r ON n.id = r.object_id JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON r.object_id = s.network_id WHERE n.status = 'ACTIVE' AND r.action = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY n.id HAVING SUM(1) > (SELECT AVG(subnet_cnt) + STDDEV(subnet_cnt) FROM (SELECT n2.id AS network_id, SUM(1) AS subnet_cnt FROM NETWORKS AS n2 JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r2 ON n2.id = r2.object_id JOIN SUBNETS AS s2 ON r2.object_id = s2.network_id WHERE n2.status = 'ACTIVE' AND r2.action = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY n2.id) AS per_network)), benchmark_cte AS (SELECT AVG(subnet_cnt) + STDDEV(subnet_cnt) AS threshold FROM (SELECT n2.id AS network_id, SUM(1) AS subnet_cnt FROM NETWORKS AS n2 JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r2 ON n2.id = r2.object_id JOIN SUBNETS AS s2 ON r2.object_id = s2.network_id WHERE n2.status = 'ACTIVE' AND r2.action = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY n2.id) AS per_network) SELECT i.network_id, i.total_subnets, b.threshold AS benchmark_threshold, CASE WHEN i.total_subnets > b.threshold THEN 'ABOVE_THRESHOLD' ELSE 'NOT_ABOVE_THRESHOLD' END AS subnet_usage_category FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN benchmark_cte AS b
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(ia.ip_address) AS ip_allocation_count, SUM(CASE WHEN NOT ia.subnet_id IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS subnet_id_rows FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON ia.port_id = p.id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND NOT ia.ip_address IS NULL AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address) SELECT p.network_id, MAX(inner_cte.ip_allocation_count) AS max_ip_allocations_on_a_port FROM inner_cte JOIN ports AS p ON p.id = inner_cte.port_id GROUP BY p.network_id
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT lb.OPERATING_STATUS, SUM(s.BYTES_IN) AS total_bytes_in FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCER_STATISTICS AS s ON lb.ID = s.LOADBALANCER_ID WHERE lb.PROVISIONING_STATUS = 'ACTIVE' AND lb.VIP_SUBNET_ID IN (SELECT subnets.id FROM subnets JOIN networks ON networks.id = subnets.network_id WHERE NOT networks.id IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b'))) GROUP BY lb.OPERATING_STATUS HAVING SUM(s.BYTES_IN) > 1000000) SELECT lb.TENANT_ID, MAX(inner_cte.total_bytes_in) AS max_total_bytes_in_across_statuses FROM inner_cte JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb ON lb.OPERATING_STATUS = inner_cte.OPERATING_STATUS WHERE lb.PROVISIONING_STATUS = 'ACTIVE' GROUP BY lb.TENANT_ID HAVING MAX(inner_cte.total_bytes_in) > 1000000
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.name, MAX(s.ip_version) AS max_ip_version, s.gateway_ip FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.object_id = s.network_id WHERE n.action = 'access_as_shared' AND n.target_tenant = '*' GROUP BY s.name, s.gateway_ip HAVING MAX(s.ip_version) > 3) SELECT CASE WHEN LAG(name) OVER (ORDER BY name NULLS FIRST, gateway_ip NULLS FIRST) = name THEN NULL ELSE name END AS name, max_ip_version, gateway_ip FROM inner_cte ORDER BY name NULLS FIRST, gateway_ip NULLS FIRST
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), TenantAvg AS (SELECT p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS avg_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.TENANT_ID) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.mac_address, i.window_column FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN PORTS AS p2 ON p2.ID = i.port_id JOIN TenantAvg AS ta ON ta.tenant_id = i.tenant_id WHERE p2.STANDARD_ATTR_ID > ta.avg_standard_attr_id
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, sgpb.security_group_id, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.security_group_id ORDER BY p.id NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN' AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT inner_cte.port_id, inner_cte.tenant_id, inner_cte.mac_address, inner_cte.security_group_id, inner_cte.window_column, tenant_down.total_down_ports_for_tenant, avg_down.avg_down_ports_per_tenant FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT tenant_id, COUNT(*) AS total_down_ports_for_tenant FROM ports WHERE status = 'DOWN' GROUP BY tenant_id) AS tenant_down ON inner_cte.tenant_id = tenant_down.tenant_id JOIN (SELECT AVG(down_port_count) AS avg_down_ports_per_tenant FROM (SELECT tenant_id, COUNT(*) AS down_port_count FROM ports WHERE status = 'DOWN' GROUP BY tenant_id) AS sub) AS avg_down ON 1 = 1
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' AND NOT p.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER) SELECT i.port_status, i.device_owner, i.port_count, i.earliest_standard_attr_update, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT f.id) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_floating_ips FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN ports AS p ON p.status = i.port_status AND p.device_owner = i.device_owner JOIN standardattributes AS sa ON sa.id = p.standard_attr_id LEFT JOIN floatingips AS f ON p.id = f.fixed_port_id WHERE sa.resource_type = 'ports' AND NOT p.network_id IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY i.port_status, i.device_owner, i.port_count, i.earliest_standard_attr_update ORDER BY i.port_count DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.NAME AS subnet_name, s.CIDR AS subnet_cidr, n.TARGET_TENANT AS rbac_target_tenant, SUM(s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY s.IP_VERSION ORDER BY s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS n ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' AND n.TARGET_TENANT IN ('1ee6b5b4fe8c45cfa16c3ecdbb8ee02b')) SELECT s.IP_VERSION, MAX(inner_cte.window_column) AS max_running_sum_standard_attr_id FROM inner_cte JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON s.NAME = inner_cte.subnet_name AND s.CIDR = inner_cte.subnet_cidr GROUP BY s.IP_VERSION
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.OBJECT_ID AS network_id, COUNT(*) AS subnet_count FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' AND n.TARGET_TENANT = '*' GROUP BY n.OBJECT_ID ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 3) SELECT i.network_id, i.subnet_count, COUNT(DISTINCT s.TENANT_ID) AS distinct_subnet_tenants FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON s.NETWORK_ID = i.network_id GROUP BY i.network_id, i.subnet_count ORDER BY i.subnet_count DESC NULLS LAST
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, sgpb.security_group_id, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.security_group_id ORDER BY p.id NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN' AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT i.security_group_id, COUNT(DISTINCT i.port_id) AS total_down_ports, MIN(i.port_id) AS sample_port_id, MIN(i.tenant_id) AS tenant_id, MIN(i.mac_address) AS sample_mac_address, MIN(i.window_column) AS best_rank_in_group FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb2 ON sgpb2.security_group_id = i.security_group_id JOIN PORTS AS p2 ON p2.id = sgpb2.port_id GROUP BY i.security_group_id ORDER BY total_down_ports DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10

WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), VifTypeAverages AS (SELECT VIF_TYPE, AVG(window_column) AS avg_window_column FROM inner_cte GROUP BY VIF_TYPE) SELECT i.PORT_ID, i.HOST, i.VIF_TYPE, i.VNIC_TYPE, i.TENANT_ID, i.NETWORK_ID, i.MAC_ADDRESS, i.STATUS, i.DEVICE_OWNER, i.window_column FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN VifTypeAverages AS v ON v.VIF_TYPE = i.VIF_TYPE WHERE i.window_column > v.avg_window_column

WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.name, MAX(s.ip_version) AS max_ip_version, s.gateway_ip FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.object_id = s.network_id WHERE n.action = 'access_as_shared' AND n.target_tenant = '*' GROUP BY s.name, s.gateway_ip HAVING MAX(s.ip_version) > 3) SELECT i.name, i.gateway_ip, i.max_ip_version, CASE WHEN MAX(s.enable_dhcp) = 1 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_dhcp_enabled FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON s.name = i.name AND s.gateway_ip = i.gateway_ip GROUP BY i.name, i.gateway_ip, i.max_ip_version ORDER BY i.max_ip_version DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.name AS subnet_name, CAST(STDDEV(d.order) AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(AVG(d.order), 0), 0) AS coefficient_of_variation FROM subnets AS s JOIN dnsnameservers AS d ON d.subnet_id = s.id JOIN ipallocationpools AS p ON p.subnet_id = s.id JOIN networks AS n ON n.id = s.network_id WHERE s.ip_version = 4 AND NOT n.id IN (SELECT r.object_id FROM networkrbacs AS r WHERE r.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (r.target_tenant = '*' OR r.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY s.name HAVING AVG(d.order) > 0 ORDER BY coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT inner_cte.subnet_name, inner_cte.coefficient_of_variation, avg_cv.avg_coefficient_of_variation FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT AVG(coefficient_of_variation) AS avg_coefficient_of_variation FROM inner_cte) AS avg_cv ON 1 = 1 ORDER BY inner_cte.coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST

WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), VifTypeCounts AS (SELECT VIF_TYPE, COUNT(*) AS ports_in_vif_type FROM inner_cte GROUP BY VIF_TYPE), OverallWindowAvg AS (SELECT AVG(window_column) AS overall_avg_window_column FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.PORT_ID, i.HOST, i.VIF_TYPE, i.VNIC_TYPE, i.TENANT_ID, i.NETWORK_ID, i.MAC_ADDRESS, i.STATUS, i.DEVICE_OWNER, i.window_column, vtc.ports_in_vif_type, (i.window_column - owa.overall_avg_window_column) AS deviation_from_overall_window_avg FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN VifTypeCounts AS vtc ON i.VIF_TYPE = vtc.VIF_TYPE CROSS JOIN OverallWindowAvg AS owa

WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.NAME, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY r.ACTION NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM EXTERNALNETWORKS AS e JOIN NETWORKS AS n ON e.NETWORK_ID = n.ID JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r ON n.ID = r.OBJECT_ID WHERE e.IS_DEFAULT = 0 AND r.ACTION = 'access_as_external'), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(window_column) AS avg_window_column FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.NAME, i.window_column, (i.window_column - a.avg_window_column) AS deviation_from_avg_window_column FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS a
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.ACTION, AVG(s.IP_VERSION) AS avg_ip_version, VAR_SAMP(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_variance, STDDEV(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_stddev FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY n.ACTION ORDER BY ip_version_variance DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT inner_cte.ACTION, inner_cte.avg_ip_version, inner_cte.ip_version_variance, inner_cte.ip_version_stddev, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN s.ENABLE_DHCP = 1 THEN s.ID END) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_dhcp_enabled_subnets FROM inner_cte JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS n ON n.ACTION = inner_cte.ACTION JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON s.NETWORK_ID = n.OBJECT_ID WHERE n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY inner_cte.ACTION, inner_cte.avg_ip_version, inner_cte.ip_version_variance, inner_cte.ip_version_stddev ORDER BY inner_cte.ip_version_variance DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10

WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, EXP(AVG(LN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID))) AS geometric_mean_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID > 0 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY geometric_mean_standard_attr_id DESC NULLS LAST), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(geometric_mean_standard_attr_id) AS avg_geometric_mean_standard_attr_id FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, i.geometric_mean_standard_attr_id, (i.geometric_mean_standard_attr_id - a.avg_geometric_mean_standard_attr_id) AS deviation_from_avg_geometric_mean FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS a ORDER BY i.geometric_mean_standard_attr_id DESC NULLS LAST
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS min_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS spb ON p.ID = spb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(port_count) AS avg_port_count FROM inner_cte) SELECT ic.port_status, ic.device_owner, ic.port_count, ic.min_standard_attr_id, ac.avg_port_count AS benchmark, CASE WHEN ic.port_count > ac.avg_port_count * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_GROUP' WHEN ic.port_count < ac.avg_port_count * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_GROUP' ELSE 'AVERAGE_GROUP' END AS group_category FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS ac
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT r.DESTINATION, r.NEXTHOP, s.NAME AS subnet_name, s.CIDR AS subnet_cidr, p.FIRST_IP, p.LAST_IP, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY r.SUBNET_ID ORDER BY r.SUBNET_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SUBNETROUTES AS r JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON r.SUBNET_ID = s.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONPOOLS AS p ON s.ID = p.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND s.ID IN ('012993ae-5d73-4aad-834b-e87a31e99d48')), SubnetRouteCounts AS (SELECT s.ID AS subnet_id, COUNT(r.DESTINATION) AS total_routes FROM SUBNETS AS s LEFT JOIN SUBNETROUTES AS r ON r.SUBNET_ID = s.ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 GROUP BY s.ID), AverageRoutes AS (SELECT AVG(total_routes) AS avg_routes FROM SubnetRouteCounts) SELECT i.DESTINATION, i.NEXTHOP, i.subnet_name, i.subnet_cidr, i.FIRST_IP, i.LAST_IP, i.window_column, src.total_routes, ar.avg_routes AS benchmark, CASE WHEN src.total_routes > ar.avg_routes * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_ROUTE_SUBNET' WHEN src.total_routes < ar.avg_routes * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_ROUTE_SUBNET' ELSE 'AVERAGE_ROUTE_SUBNET' END AS route_category FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON s.NAME = i.subnet_name AND s.CIDR = i.subnet_cidr JOIN SubnetRouteCounts AS src ON src.subnet_id = s.ID CROSS JOIN AverageRoutes AS ar
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.NAME, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY r.ACTION NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM EXTERNALNETWORKS AS e JOIN NETWORKS AS n ON e.NETWORK_ID = n.ID JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r ON n.ID = r.OBJECT_ID WHERE e.IS_DEFAULT = 0 AND r.ACTION = 'access_as_external'), tenant_counts AS (SELECT n.TENANT_ID, COUNT(*) AS tenant_network_count FROM EXTERNALNETWORKS AS e JOIN NETWORKS AS n ON e.NETWORK_ID = n.ID JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r ON n.ID = r.OBJECT_ID WHERE e.IS_DEFAULT = 0 AND r.ACTION = 'access_as_external' GROUP BY n.TENANT_ID), avg_counts AS (SELECT AVG(tenant_network_count) AS avg_networks_per_tenant FROM tenant_counts) SELECT inner_cte.NAME, inner_cte.window_column, totals.total_matching_networks, avg_counts.avg_networks_per_tenant FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT COUNT(*) AS total_matching_networks FROM EXTERNALNETWORKS AS e JOIN NETWORKS AS n ON e.NETWORK_ID = n.ID JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r ON n.ID = r.OBJECT_ID WHERE e.IS_DEFAULT = 0 AND r.ACTION = 'access_as_external') AS totals ON 1 = 1 JOIN avg_counts ON 1 = 1
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.NAME AS subnet_name, MAX(route_count) - MIN(route_count) AS route_count_range, VAR_SAMP(route_count) AS route_count_variance, STDDEV(route_count) AS route_count_stddev FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN (SELECT sr.SUBNET_ID, COUNT(*) AS route_count FROM SUBNETROUTES AS sr GROUP BY sr.SUBNET_ID, sr.NEXTHOP) AS route_stats ON s.ID = route_stats.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND s.ID IN ('012993ae-5d73-4aad-834b-e87a31e99d48') GROUP BY s.NAME ORDER BY route_count_range DESC NULLS LAST), avg_range AS (SELECT AVG(route_count_range) AS avg_route_count_range FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.subnet_name, i.route_count_range, i.route_count_variance, i.route_count_stddev, a.avg_route_count_range AS benchmark, CASE WHEN i.route_count_range > a.avg_route_count_range * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_VARIABILITY' WHEN i.route_count_range < a.avg_route_count_range * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_VARIABILITY' ELSE 'AVERAGE_VARIABILITY' END AS variability_category FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_range AS a
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.status, CAST(STDDEV(LENGTH(ia.ip_address)) AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(AVG(LENGTH(ia.ip_address)), 0), 0) AS coefficient_of_variation FROM portsecuritybindings AS psb JOIN ports AS p ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON p.id = ia.port_id WHERE psb.port_security_enabled = 1 AND ia.ip_address IN ('10.150.64.113/8') GROUP BY p.status HAVING AVG(LENGTH(ia.ip_address)) > 0 ORDER BY coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(coefficient_of_variation) AS avg_coefficient_of_variation FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.status, i.coefficient_of_variation, (i.coefficient_of_variation - a.avg_coefficient_of_variation) AS deviation_from_avg_coefficient_of_variation FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS a ORDER BY deviation_from_avg_coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(sa.ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sa.RESOURCE_TYPE ORDER BY sa.ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7')), TenantDownPorts AS (SELECT p.tenant_id, COUNT(p.id) AS total_down_ports FROM ports AS p JOIN standardattributes AS sa ON sa.id = p.standard_attr_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN' GROUP BY p.tenant_id), AverageDownPorts AS (SELECT AVG(total_down_ports) AS avg_down_ports FROM TenantDownPorts) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.mac_address, i.window_column, tdp.total_down_ports, adp.avg_down_ports AS benchmark, CASE WHEN tdp.total_down_ports > adp.avg_down_ports * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_USER' WHEN tdp.total_down_ports < adp.avg_down_ports * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_USER' ELSE 'AVERAGE_USER' END AS user_category FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN TenantDownPorts AS tdp ON tdp.tenant_id = i.tenant_id CROSS JOIN AverageDownPorts AS adp
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, EXP(AVG(LN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID))) AS geometric_mean_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID > 0 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY geometric_mean_standard_attr_id DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT SECURITY_GROUP_ID, geometric_mean_standard_attr_id FROM inner_cte WHERE geometric_mean_standard_attr_id > (SELECT AVG(geometric_mean_standard_attr_id) FROM inner_cte) ORDER BY geometric_mean_standard_attr_id DESC NULLS LAST
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.ACTION, AVG(s.IP_VERSION) AS avg_ip_version, VAR_SAMP(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_variance, STDDEV(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_stddev FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY n.ACTION ORDER BY ip_version_variance DESC NULLS LAST), overall_avgs AS (SELECT AVG(avg_ip_version) AS overall_avg_ip_version, AVG(ip_version_variance) AS overall_avg_variance, AVG(ip_version_stddev) AS overall_avg_stddev FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.ACTION, i.avg_ip_version, i.ip_version_variance, i.ip_version_stddev, (i.avg_ip_version - o.overall_avg_ip_version) AS deviation_avg_ip_version, (i.ip_version_variance - o.overall_avg_variance) AS deviation_ip_version_variance, (i.ip_version_stddev - o.overall_avg_stddev) AS deviation_ip_version_stddev FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN overall_avgs AS o
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.NAME, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY sa.UPDATED_AT NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND s.ID IN ('012993ae-5d73-4aad-834b-e87a31e99d48')) SELECT s.NETWORK_ID, MAX(inner_cte.window_column) AS max_window_dense_rank, MIN(inner_cte.NAME) AS example_subnet_name FROM inner_cte JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON s.NAME = inner_cte.NAME GROUP BY s.NETWORK_ID
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, EXP(AVG(LN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID))) AS geometric_mean_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID > 0 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY geometric_mean_standard_attr_id DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT i.security_group_id, i.geometric_mean_standard_attr_id, COUNT(DISTINCT p.id) AS total_ports, CASE WHEN SUM(CASE WHEN p.admin_state_up = 1 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_any_admin_up_ports FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN securitygroupportbindings AS s ON i.security_group_id = s.security_group_id JOIN ports AS p ON p.id = s.port_id WHERE p.standard_attr_id > 0 AND p.tenant_id IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY i.security_group_id, i.geometric_mean_standard_attr_id ORDER BY i.geometric_mean_standard_attr_id DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.STATUS, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) AS min_admin_state_up, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS avg_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE b.VIF_TYPE = 'ovs' AND p.DEVICE_ID IN ('da811c75-0f9b-462c-b020-8ea55f6ca7c7') GROUP BY p.STATUS), overall_avg AS (SELECT AVG(avg_standard_attr_id) AS overall_avg_standard_attr_id FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.STATUS, i.min_admin_state_up, i.avg_standard_attr_id, (i.avg_standard_attr_id - o.overall_avg_standard_attr_id) AS deviation_from_overall_avg FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN overall_avg AS o

WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.name AS network_name, r.action AS rbac_action, MAX(n.mtu) AS max_mtu FROM NETWORKSECURITYBINDINGS AS nsb JOIN NETWORKS AS n ON nsb.network_id = n.id JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r ON n.id = r.object_id WHERE n.status = 'ACTIVE' AND r.action IN ('access_as_shared', 'access_as_external') GROUP BY n.name, r.action HAVING MAX(n.mtu) > 8000) SELECT i.network_name, i.rbac_action, i.max_mtu, COUNT(DISTINCT r2.target_tenant) AS distinct_target_tenants FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN NETWORKS AS n2 ON n2.name = i.network_name JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r2 ON r2.object_id = n2.id AND r2.action = i.rbac_action GROUP BY i.network_name, i.rbac_action, i.max_mtu

WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.NAME AS subnet_name, MAX(route_count) - MIN(route_count) AS route_count_range, VAR_SAMP(route_count) AS route_count_variance, STDDEV(route_count) AS route_count_stddev FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN (SELECT sr.SUBNET_ID, COUNT(*) AS route_count FROM SUBNETROUTES AS sr GROUP BY sr.SUBNET_ID, sr.NEXTHOP) AS route_stats ON s.ID = route_stats.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND s.ID IN ('012993ae-5d73-4aad-834b-e87a31e99d48') GROUP BY s.NAME ORDER BY route_count_range DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT s.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, COUNT(sr.DESTINATION) AS total_routes, MAX(inner_cte.route_count_range) AS route_count_range, MAX(inner_cte.route_count_variance) AS route_count_variance, MAX(inner_cte.route_count_stddev) AS route_count_stddev FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN SUBNETROUTES AS sr ON s.ID = sr.SUBNET_ID JOIN inner_cte ON inner_cte.subnet_name = s.NAME WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND s.ID IN ('012993ae-5d73-4aad-834b-e87a31e99d48') GROUP BY s.TENANT_ID ORDER BY total_routes DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10

SELECT MIN(t0.ID) AS min_rbac_id, AVG(t1.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS avg_standard_attr_id FROM NETWORKRBACS AS t0 JOIN SUBNETS AS t1 ON t0.OBJECT_ID = t1.NETWORK_ID WHERE t0.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' AND t0.TARGET_TENANT = '*' GROUP BY t1.NETWORK_ID
SELECT n.NAME, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY r.ACTION NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM EXTERNALNETWORKS AS e JOIN NETWORKS AS n ON e.NETWORK_ID = n.ID JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r ON n.ID = r.OBJECT_ID WHERE e.IS_DEFAULT = 0 AND r.ACTION = 'access_as_external'
SELECT p.status, MAX(ps.binding_count) - MIN(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_range, VAR_SAMP(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_variance, STDDEV(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_stddev FROM ports AS p JOIN (SELECT p2.id AS port_id, COUNT(*) AS binding_count FROM ports AS p2 JOIN portsecuritybindings AS b ON p2.id = b.port_id WHERE p2.tenant_id IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p2.id) AS ps ON p.id = ps.port_id GROUP BY p.status ORDER BY binding_count_range DESC NULLS LAST
SELECT n.ACTION, AVG(s.IP_VERSION) AS avg_ip_version, VAR_SAMP(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_variance, STDDEV(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_stddev FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY n.ACTION ORDER BY ip_version_variance DESC NULLS LAST
SELECT s.NAME AS subnet_name, p.FIRST_IP, p.LAST_IP, SUM(s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY s.IP_VERSION ORDER BY s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN IPALLOCATIONPOOLS AS p ON s.ID = p.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.NAME = 'shift3' AND s.ID IN ('012993ae-5d73-4aad-834b-e87a31e99d48')
SELECT p.NAME, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (ORDER BY p.ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.NETWORK_ID IN ('0a1d0a27-cffa-4de3-92c5-9d3fd3f2e74d')
SELECT MIN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS min_standard_attr_id, SUM(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) AS total_admin_state_up FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')
SELECT s.name AS subnet_name, MAX(pool_count) - MIN(pool_count) AS pool_count_range, VAR_SAMP(pool_count) AS pool_count_variance, STDDEV(pool_count) AS pool_count_stddev FROM subnets AS s JOIN dnsnameservers AS d ON d.subnet_id = s.id JOIN (SELECT s2.id AS subnet_id, COUNT(*) AS pool_count FROM subnets AS s2 JOIN ipallocationpools AS p ON p.subnet_id = s2.id GROUP BY s2.id, p.id) AS pool_stats ON pool_stats.subnet_id = s.id JOIN networks AS n ON n.id = s.network_id WHERE NOT n.id IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY s.name ORDER BY pool_count_range DESC NULLS LAST
SELECT MIN(s.IP_VERSION) AS min_ip_version, SUM(CASE WHEN NOT p.ID IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS total_allocation_pools FROM SUBNETROUTES AS r JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON r.SUBNET_ID = s.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONPOOLS AS p ON s.ID = p.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.ENABLE_DHCP = 1 AND s.ID IN ('012993ae-5d73-4aad-834b-e87a31e99d48')
SELECT n.STATUS AS network_status, AVG(n.MTU) AS avg_mtu, STDDEV(n.MTU) AS mtu_stddev FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN NETWORKSECURITYBINDINGS AS nsb ON nsb.NETWORK_ID = n.ID JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS rbac ON n.ID = rbac.OBJECT_ID WHERE NOT n.MTU IS NULL AND rbac.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' AND NOT n.ID IN (SELECT r2.OBJECT_ID FROM NETWORKRBACS AS r2 WHERE r2.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' AND (r2.TARGET_TENANT = '*' OR r2.TARGET_TENANT = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY n.STATUS ORDER BY mtu_stddev DESC NULLS LAST
SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')
SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')

SELECT n.STATUS AS network_status, VAR_SAMP(nsb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED) AS port_security_enabled_variance, STDDEV(nsb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED) AS port_security_enabled_stddev FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN NETWORKSECURITYBINDINGS AS nsb ON n.ID = nsb.NETWORK_ID WHERE NOT n.ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY n.STATUS ORDER BY port_security_enabled_variance DESC NULLS LAST
SELECT n.STATUS AS network_status, AVG(n.MTU) AS avg_mtu, STDDEV(n.MTU) AS mtu_stddev FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN NETWORKSECURITYBINDINGS AS nsb ON nsb.NETWORK_ID = n.ID JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS rbac ON n.ID = rbac.OBJECT_ID WHERE NOT n.MTU IS NULL AND rbac.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' AND NOT n.ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY n.STATUS ORDER BY mtu_stddev DESC NULLS LAST
SELECT n.name AS network_name, r.action AS rbac_action, MAX(n.mtu) AS max_mtu FROM NETWORKSECURITYBINDINGS AS nsb JOIN NETWORKS AS n ON nsb.network_id = n.id JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r ON n.id = r.object_id WHERE n.status = 'ACTIVE' AND r.action IN ('access_as_shared', 'access_as_external') GROUP BY n.name, r.action HAVING MAX(n.mtu) > 8000
SELECT s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, EXP(AVG(LN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID))) AS geometric_mean_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID > 0 AND p.DEVICE_ID IN ('da811c75-0f9b-462c-b020-8ea55f6ca7c7') GROUP BY s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY geometric_mean_standard_attr_id DESC NULLS LAST
SELECT p.status, CAST(STDDEV(p.standard_attr_id) AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(AVG(p.standard_attr_id), 0), 0) AS coefficient_of_variation FROM securitygroupportbindings AS sgpb JOIN ports AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE NOT p.network_id IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY p.status HAVING AVG(p.standard_attr_id) > 0 ORDER BY coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST
SELECT n.NAME AS network_name, r.ACTION AS rbac_action, s.CIDR AS subnet_cidr, SUM(n.MTU) OVER (PARTITION BY r.ACTION ORDER BY n.MTU NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r ON n.ID = r.OBJECT_ID JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON r.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE' AND r.ACTION = 'access_as_shared'
SELECT s.name, MAX(s.ip_version) AS max_ip_version, s.gateway_ip FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.object_id = s.network_id WHERE n.action = 'access_as_shared' AND n.target_tenant = '*' GROUP BY s.name, s.gateway_ip HAVING MAX(s.ip_version) > 3

SELECT p.status, MAX(p.standard_attr_id) - MIN(p.standard_attr_id) AS standard_attr_id_range, VAR_SAMP(p.standard_attr_id) AS standard_attr_id_variance FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.port_id = p.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE b.host = 'drive-59' AND NOT p.network_id IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY p.status ORDER BY standard_attr_id_range DESC NULLS LAST

SELECT n.status, AVG(n.mtu) AS avg_mtu, VAR_SAMP(n.mtu) AS mtu_variance, STDDEV(n.mtu) AS mtu_stddev FROM EXTERNALNETWORKS AS e JOIN NETWORKS AS n ON e.network_id = n.id JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r ON n.id = r.object_id WHERE e.is_default = 0 AND r.action = 'access_as_shared' AND r.target_tenant <> '*' GROUP BY n.status ORDER BY mtu_variance DESC NULLS LAST
SELECT p.status, CAST(STDDEV(LENGTH(ia.ip_address)) AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(AVG(LENGTH(ia.ip_address)), 0), 0) AS coefficient_of_variation FROM portsecuritybindings AS psb JOIN ports AS p ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON p.id = ia.port_id WHERE psb.port_security_enabled = 1 AND ia.ip_address IN ('10.150.64.113/8') GROUP BY p.status HAVING AVG(LENGTH(ia.ip_address)) > 0 ORDER BY coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST

SELECT s.IP_VERSION, AVG(s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS avg_standard_attr_id, VAR_SAMP(s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS standard_attr_id_variance, STDDEV(s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS standard_attr_id_stddev FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN SUBNETROUTES AS r ON r.SUBNET_ID = s.ID JOIN NETWORKS AS n ON n.ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE NOT s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID IS NULL AND NOT n.ID IN (SELECT nr.object_id FROM networkrbacs AS nr WHERE nr.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (nr.target_tenant = '*' OR nr.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY s.IP_VERSION ORDER BY standard_attr_id_variance DESC NULLS LAST

SELECT s.NAME AS subnet_name, MAX(route_count) - MIN(route_count) AS route_count_range, VAR_SAMP(route_count) AS route_count_variance, STDDEV(route_count) AS route_count_stddev FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN (SELECT sr.SUBNET_ID, COUNT(*) AS route_count FROM SUBNETROUTES AS sr GROUP BY sr.SUBNET_ID, sr.NEXTHOP) AS route_stats ON s.ID = route_stats.SUBNET_ID JOIN NETWORKS AS n ON n.ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND NOT n.ID IN (SELECT r.object_id FROM NETWORKRBACS AS r WHERE r.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (r.target_tenant = '*' OR r.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY s.NAME ORDER BY route_count_range DESC NULLS LAST

SELECT nr.ACTION, AVG(s.IP_VERSION) AS avg_ip_version, VAR_SAMP(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_variance FROM NETWORKRBACS AS nr JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON nr.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE nr.ACTION IN ('access_as_shared', 'access_as_external') AND nr.TARGET_TENANT = '*' GROUP BY nr.ACTION ORDER BY ip_version_variance DESC NULLS LAST
SELECT n.id AS network_id, SUM(1) AS total_subnets FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r ON n.id = r.object_id JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON r.object_id = s.network_id WHERE n.status = 'ACTIVE' AND r.action = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY n.id HAVING SUM(1) > (SELECT AVG(subnet_cnt) + STDDEV(subnet_cnt) FROM (SELECT n2.id AS network_id, SUM(1) AS subnet_cnt FROM NETWORKS AS n2 JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r2 ON n2.id = r2.object_id JOIN SUBNETS AS s2 ON r2.object_id = s2.network_id WHERE n2.status = 'ACTIVE' AND r2.action = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY n2.id) AS per_network)
SELECT n.STATUS AS network_status, VAR_SAMP(n.MTU) AS mtu_variance, STDDEV(n.MTU) AS mtu_stddev FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r ON n.ID = r.OBJECT_ID WHERE r.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' AND r.TARGET_TENANT = '*' AND NOT n.MTU IS NULL GROUP BY n.STATUS ORDER BY mtu_variance DESC NULLS LAST

SELECT s.NAME AS subnet_name, MAX(route_count) - MIN(route_count) AS route_count_range, VAR_SAMP(route_count) AS route_count_variance, STDDEV(route_count) AS route_count_stddev FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN (SELECT sr.SUBNET_ID, COUNT(*) AS route_count FROM SUBNETROUTES AS sr GROUP BY sr.SUBNET_ID, sr.NEXTHOP) AS route_stats ON s.ID = route_stats.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND s.ID IN ('012993ae-5d73-4aad-834b-e87a31e99d48') GROUP BY s.NAME ORDER BY route_count_range DESC NULLS LAST

SELECT n.id AS network_id, SUM(1) AS total_subnets FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r ON n.id = r.object_id JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON r.object_id = s.network_id WHERE n.status = 'ACTIVE' AND NOT n.id IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY n.id HAVING SUM(1) > (SELECT AVG(subnet_cnt) + STDDEV(subnet_cnt) FROM (SELECT n2.id AS network_id, SUM(1) AS subnet_cnt FROM NETWORKS AS n2 JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r2 ON n2.id = r2.object_id JOIN SUBNETS AS s2 ON r2.object_id = s2.network_id WHERE n2.status = 'ACTIVE' AND NOT n2.id IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY n2.id) AS per_network)

SELECT n.ACTION AS rbac_action, AVG(s.IP_VERSION) AS avg_ip_version, MAX(s.IP_VERSION) - MIN(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_range, VAR_SAMP(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_variance FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION IN (4, 6) AND n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY n.ACTION ORDER BY ip_version_range DESC NULLS LAST
SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(ia.ip_address) AS ip_allocation_count, COUNT(DISTINCT ia.subnet_id) AS distinct_subnet_count FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON ia.port_id = p.id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND NOT ia.ip_address IS NULL AND p.tenant_id IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address
SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(sa.ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sa.RESOURCE_TYPE ORDER BY sa.ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7')
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH Subq1 AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.STATUS AS port_status, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY p.NETWORK_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID = '0008e629-5619-41e0-b86c-4eecfeaacb6c'), Subq2 AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY psb.port_security_enabled NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN'), PortNetwork AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.NETWORK_ID AS network_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID WHERE sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID = '0008e629-5619-41e0-b86c-4eecfeaacb6c') SELECT pn.network_id, COUNT(DISTINCT s1.port_id) AS total_ports_in_security_group, COUNT(DISTINCT s2.port_id) AS total_down_ports, ROUND(CAST(CAST(COUNT(DISTINCT s2.port_id) * 1.0 AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(COUNT(DISTINCT s1.port_id), 0), 0) AS DECIMAL), 2) AS down_port_ratio FROM PortNetwork AS pn LEFT JOIN Subq1 AS s1 ON s1.port_id = pn.port_id LEFT JOIN Subq2 AS s2 ON s2.port_id = pn.port_id GROUP BY pn.network_id) SELECT network_id, total_ports_in_security_group, total_down_ports, down_port_ratio FROM inner_cte WHERE total_ports_in_security_group > 0 AND down_port_ratio > 0.50
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH DownPortsWithIPs AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, b.HOST AS host, b.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ia.IP_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), PortStatusSecurityBindingStats AS (SELECT p.status, MAX(ps.binding_count) - MIN(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_range, VAR_SAMP(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_variance, STDDEV(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_stddev FROM ports AS p JOIN (SELECT p2.id AS port_id, COUNT(*) AS binding_count FROM ports AS p2 JOIN portsecuritybindings AS b ON p2.id = b.port_id GROUP BY p2.id) AS ps ON p.id = ps.port_id GROUP BY p.status ORDER BY binding_count_range DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT d.port_id, d.tenant_id, d.host, d.vif_type, d.ip_address, d.window_column, s.binding_count_range, s.binding_count_variance, s.binding_count_stddev FROM DownPortsWithIPs AS d JOIN PortStatusSecurityBindingStats AS s ON s.status = 'DOWN' ORDER BY d.ip_address NULLS FIRST), avg_down_ports AS (SELECT AVG(down_port_count) AS avg_down_port_count FROM (SELECT tenant_id, COUNT(*) AS down_port_count FROM ports WHERE status = 'DOWN' GROUP BY tenant_id) AS sub), tenant_down_counts AS (SELECT tenant_id, COUNT(*) AS total_down_ports FROM ports WHERE status = 'DOWN' GROUP BY tenant_id) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.host, i.vif_type, i.ip_address, i.window_column, i.binding_count_range, i.binding_count_variance, i.binding_count_stddev, tdc.total_down_ports, adp.avg_down_port_count FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN tenant_down_counts AS tdc ON tdc.tenant_id = i.tenant_id JOIN avg_down_ports AS adp ON 1 = 1 ORDER BY i.ip_address NULLS FIRST
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH DownPortsWithIPs AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, b.HOST AS host, b.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ia.IP_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), PortStatusSecurityBindingStats AS (SELECT p.status, MAX(ps.binding_count) - MIN(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_range, VAR_SAMP(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_variance, STDDEV(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_stddev FROM ports AS p JOIN (SELECT p2.id AS port_id, COUNT(*) AS binding_count FROM ports AS p2 JOIN portsecuritybindings AS b ON p2.id = b.port_id GROUP BY p2.id) AS ps ON p.id = ps.port_id GROUP BY p.status ORDER BY binding_count_range DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT d.port_id, d.tenant_id, d.host, d.vif_type, d.ip_address, d.window_column, s.binding_count_range, s.binding_count_variance, s.binding_count_stddev FROM DownPortsWithIPs AS d JOIN PortStatusSecurityBindingStats AS s ON s.status = 'DOWN' ORDER BY d.ip_address NULLS FIRST), TenantDownPortCounts AS (SELECT p.tenant_id, COUNT(*) AS down_port_count FROM ports AS p WHERE p.status = 'DOWN' GROUP BY p.tenant_id), AvgDownPortsPerTenant AS (SELECT AVG(down_port_count) AS avg_down_ports FROM TenantDownPortCounts) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.host, i.vif_type, i.ip_address, i.window_column, i.binding_count_range, i.binding_count_variance, i.binding_count_stddev, t.down_port_count FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN TenantDownPortCounts AS t ON t.tenant_id = i.tenant_id WHERE t.down_port_count > (SELECT avg_down_ports FROM AvgDownPortsPerTenant) ORDER BY i.ip_address NULLS FIRST
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH Subq1 AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), Subq2 AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.STATUS AS port_status, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY p.NETWORK_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID = '0008e629-5619-41e0-b86c-4eecfeaacb6c') SELECT s1.port_id, s1.tenant_id, s1.mac_address, s2.port_status, s2.ip_address, s1.window_column AS running_sum_standard_attr_id_within_security_group, s2.window_column AS running_sum_standard_attr_id_within_network FROM Subq1 AS s1 JOIN Subq2 AS s2 ON s1.port_id = s2.port_id ORDER BY s1.port_id NULLS FIRST) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.mac_address, i.port_status, i.ip_address, i.running_sum_standard_attr_id_within_security_group, i.running_sum_standard_attr_id_within_network, COUNT(p_all.id) AS total_ports_for_tenant, avg_ports.avg_port_count AS avg_ports_across_all_tenants FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN ports AS p_all ON p_all.tenant_id = i.tenant_id, (SELECT AVG(port_count) AS avg_port_count FROM (SELECT COUNT(id) AS port_count FROM ports GROUP BY tenant_id) AS sub) AS avg_ports GROUP BY i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.mac_address, i.port_status, i.ip_address, i.running_sum_standard_attr_id_within_security_group, i.running_sum_standard_attr_id_within_network, avg_ports.avg_port_count ORDER BY i.port_id NULLS FIRST
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH SubnetPoolWindowStats AS (SELECT s.NAME, s.TENANT_ID, s.NETWORK_ID, s.IP_VERSION, s.CIDR, s.GATEWAY_IP, s.ENABLE_DHCP, p.FIRST_IP, p.LAST_IP, MAX(s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (ORDER BY s.ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_standard_attr_id_next2 FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN IPALLOCATIONPOOLS AS p ON s.ID = p.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4), SubnetDnsOrderVariation AS (SELECT s.name AS subnet_name, CAST(STDDEV(d.ORDER) AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(AVG(d.ORDER), 0), 0) AS coefficient_of_variation FROM subnets AS s JOIN dnsnameservers AS d ON d.subnet_id = s.id JOIN ipallocationpools AS p ON p.subnet_id = s.id WHERE s.ip_version = 4 GROUP BY s.name HAVING AVG(d.ORDER) > 0 ORDER BY coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT spws.NAME AS subnet_name, spws.TENANT_ID, spws.NETWORK_ID, spws.IP_VERSION, spws.CIDR, spws.GATEWAY_IP, spws.ENABLE_DHCP, spws.FIRST_IP, spws.LAST_IP, spws.max_standard_attr_id_next2, sdov.coefficient_of_variation FROM SubnetPoolWindowStats AS spws JOIN SubnetDnsOrderVariation AS sdov ON spws.NAME = sdov.subnet_name ORDER BY sdov.coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT i.subnet_name, i.tenant_id, i.network_id, i.ip_version, i.cidr, i.gateway_ip, i.enable_dhcp, i.first_ip, i.last_ip, i.max_standard_attr_id_next2, i.coefficient_of_variation FROM inner_cte AS i WHERE i.coefficient_of_variation > (SELECT AVG(i2.coefficient_of_variation) FROM inner_cte AS i2) ORDER BY i.coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH SubnetOrderRangeVariance AS (SELECT s.name AS subnet_name, MAX(d.ORDER) - MIN(d.ORDER) AS order_range, VAR_SAMP(d.ORDER) AS order_variance FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN DNSNAMESERVERS AS d ON s.id = d.subnet_id WHERE s.ip_version = 4 GROUP BY s.name ORDER BY order_range DESC NULLS LAST), SubnetOrderStddevOver5 AS (SELECT s.name, STDDEV(d.ORDER) AS order_stddev FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN DNSNAMESERVERS AS d ON s.id = d.subnet_id WHERE s.ip_version = 4 GROUP BY s.name HAVING COUNT(*) > 5 ORDER BY order_stddev DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT sorv.subnet_name, sorv.order_range, sorv.order_variance, sos.order_stddev FROM SubnetOrderRangeVariance AS sorv JOIN SubnetOrderStddevOver5 AS sos ON sorv.subnet_name = sos.name), AverageOrderRange AS (SELECT AVG(order_range) AS avg_order_range FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.subnet_name, i.order_range, i.order_variance, i.order_stddev, (i.order_range - aor.avg_order_range) AS deviation_from_avg_order_range FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN AverageOrderRange AS aor
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH DownPortsWithIPs AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, b.HOST AS host, b.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ia.IP_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), PortStatusSecurityBindingStats AS (SELECT p.status, MAX(ps.binding_count) - MIN(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_range, VAR_SAMP(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_variance, STDDEV(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_stddev FROM ports AS p JOIN (SELECT p2.id AS port_id, COUNT(*) AS binding_count FROM ports AS p2 JOIN portsecuritybindings AS b ON p2.id = b.port_id GROUP BY p2.id) AS ps ON p.id = ps.port_id GROUP BY p.status ORDER BY binding_count_range DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT d.port_id, d.tenant_id, d.host, d.vif_type, d.ip_address, d.window_column, s.binding_count_range, s.binding_count_variance, s.binding_count_stddev FROM DownPortsWithIPs AS d JOIN PortStatusSecurityBindingStats AS s ON s.status = 'DOWN' ORDER BY d.ip_address NULLS FIRST) SELECT i.tenant_id, COUNT(DISTINCT i.port_id) AS total_down_ports_with_ip_and_psb, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT f.id) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_floating_ips, MAX(i.binding_count_range) AS down_binding_count_range, MAX(i.binding_count_variance) AS down_binding_count_variance, MAX(i.binding_count_stddev) AS down_binding_count_stddev FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON psb.port_id = i.port_id LEFT JOIN floatingips AS f ON f.fixed_port_id = i.port_id GROUP BY i.tenant_id ORDER BY total_down_ports_with_ip_and_psb DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH PortDownMacWindow AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, AVG(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST), PortDownIpRowNumber AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, b.HOST AS host, b.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ia.IP_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT p2.vif_type, COUNT(DISTINCT p2.port_id) AS Total_Down_Ports, AVG(p1.window_column) AS Avg_AdminStateUp_Window_Across_Ports, MAX(p2.window_column) AS Max_Ip_RowNumber_Window, COUNT(DISTINCT p2.tenant_id) AS Distinct_Tenants FROM PortDownIpRowNumber AS p2 JOIN PortDownMacWindow AS p1 ON p1.HOST = p2.host AND p1.VIF_TYPE = p2.vif_type GROUP BY p2.vif_type ORDER BY Total_Down_Ports DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT vif_type, Total_Down_Ports, Avg_AdminStateUp_Window_Across_Ports, Max_Ip_RowNumber_Window, Distinct_Tenants FROM inner_cte WHERE Total_Down_Ports > (SELECT AVG(Total_Down_Ports) FROM inner_cte) ORDER BY Total_Down_Ports DESC NULLS LAST
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH HapLoadBalancersByOpStatus AS (SELECT lb.OPERATING_STATUS, COUNT(*) AS total_loadbalancers FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb JOIN PROVIDERRESOURCEASSOCIATIONS AS pra ON lb.ID = pra.RESOURCE_ID WHERE pra.PROVIDER_NAME = 'haproxy' GROUP BY lb.OPERATING_STATUS HAVING COUNT(*) > 1), ActiveBytesInByOpStatus AS (SELECT lb.OPERATING_STATUS, SUM(s.BYTES_IN) AS total_bytes_in FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCER_STATISTICS AS s ON lb.ID = s.LOADBALANCER_ID WHERE lb.PROVISIONING_STATUS = 'ACTIVE' GROUP BY lb.OPERATING_STATUS HAVING SUM(s.BYTES_IN) > 1000000) SELECT h.OPERATING_STATUS, h.total_loadbalancers, a.total_bytes_in FROM HapLoadBalancersByOpStatus AS h JOIN ActiveBytesInByOpStatus AS a ON h.OPERATING_STATUS = a.OPERATING_STATUS), ActiveHaproxyBytesByOpStatus AS (SELECT lb.OPERATING_STATUS, SUM(s.BYTES_IN) AS haproxy_active_bytes_in FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb JOIN PROVIDERRESOURCEASSOCIATIONS AS pra ON lb.ID = pra.RESOURCE_ID JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCER_STATISTICS AS s ON lb.ID = s.LOADBALANCER_ID WHERE pra.PROVIDER_NAME = 'haproxy' AND lb.PROVISIONING_STATUS = 'ACTIVE' GROUP BY lb.OPERATING_STATUS) SELECT i.OPERATING_STATUS, i.total_loadbalancers, i.total_bytes_in, ah.haproxy_active_bytes_in, (CAST(ah.haproxy_active_bytes_in * 1.0 AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(i.total_bytes_in, 0), 0)) AS haproxy_active_bytes_in_pct FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN ActiveHaproxyBytesByOpStatus AS ah ON i.OPERATING_STATUS = ah.OPERATING_STATUS
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH AdminUpPorts AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1), ActiveNetworks AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE') SELECT an.network_id, an.network_name, an.tenant_id, an.admin_state_up, an.mtu, an.standard_attr_updated_at, an.window_column AS network_row_number_within_tenant, ap.PORT_ID, ap.HOST, ap.VIF_TYPE, ap.VNIC_TYPE, ap.MAC_ADDRESS, ap.STATUS AS port_status, ap.DEVICE_OWNER, ap.window_column AS avg_standard_attr_id_window_2_preceding_2_following FROM ActiveNetworks AS an JOIN AdminUpPorts AS ap ON ap.NETWORK_ID = an.network_id AND ap.TENANT_ID = an.tenant_id), VifTypePortCounts AS (SELECT network_id, tenant_id, VIF_TYPE, COUNT(DISTINCT PORT_ID) AS admin_up_ports_in_vif_type FROM inner_cte GROUP BY network_id, tenant_id, VIF_TYPE) SELECT network_id, tenant_id, MAX(admin_up_ports_in_vif_type) AS max_admin_up_ports_sharing_same_vif_type FROM VifTypePortCounts GROUP BY network_id, tenant_id
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH SecurityGroupBoundPorts AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.name AS port_name, COUNT(sgpb.security_group_id) AS security_group_binding_count, SUM(CASE WHEN pd.port_id IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END) AS dns_record_count FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id LEFT JOIN PORTDNSES AS pd ON p.id = pd.port_id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.name), DownPortBindings AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, b.HOST AS host, b.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ia.IP_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT sgp.tenant_id, sgp.port_id, sgp.port_name, sgp.security_group_binding_count, sgp.dns_record_count, dpb.host, dpb.vif_type, dpb.ip_address, dpb.window_column FROM SecurityGroupBoundPorts AS sgp JOIN DownPortBindings AS dpb ON sgp.port_id = dpb.port_id WHERE sgp.security_group_binding_count >= 1 ORDER BY sgp.security_group_binding_count DESC NULLS LAST, sgp.dns_record_count DESC NULLS LAST, dpb.ip_address NULLS FIRST) SELECT i.tenant_id AS qualifying_ports_tenant_id, COUNT(DISTINCT i.port_id) AS total_qualifying_ports, avg_q.avg_qualifying_port_count FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN (SELECT AVG(tenant_port_count) AS avg_qualifying_port_count FROM (SELECT tenant_id, COUNT(DISTINCT port_id) AS tenant_port_count FROM inner_cte GROUP BY tenant_id) AS per_tenant) AS avg_q ON 1 = 1 GROUP BY i.tenant_id, avg_q.avg_qualifying_port_count ORDER BY total_qualifying_ports DESC NULLS LAST, qualifying_ports_tenant_id NULLS FIRST
