WITH inner_cte AS (WITH subq1 AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(sa.ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sa.RESOURCE_TYPE ORDER BY sa.ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), subq2 AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, p.TENANT_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE b.HOST = 'drive-59') SELECT s2.PORT_ID AS port_id, s2.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, s2.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, s1.window_column AS down_ports_running_sum_by_resource_type, s2.window_column AS drive_59_ports_running_sum_by_vif_type FROM subq2 AS s2 JOIN subq1 AS s1 ON s1.port_id = s2.PORT_ID AND s1.tenant_id = s2.TENANT_ID AND s1.mac_address = s2.MAC_ADDRESS ORDER BY s2.PORT_ID NULLS FIRST), benchmark_cte AS (SELECT AVG(down_ports_running_sum_by_resource_type) AS avg_down_running_sum FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.mac_address, i.down_ports_running_sum_by_resource_type, i.drive_59_ports_running_sum_by_vif_type, b.avg_down_running_sum AS benchmark, CASE WHEN i.down_ports_running_sum_by_resource_type > b.avg_down_running_sum * 1.5 THEN 'ABOVE_BENCHMARK' WHEN i.down_ports_running_sum_by_resource_type < b.avg_down_running_sum * 0.5 THEN 'BELOW_BENCHMARK' ELSE 'AROUND_BENCHMARK' END AS benchmark_category FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN benchmark_cte AS b ORDER BY i.port_id NULLS FIRST
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH DownPortsWithBindings AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, AVG(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST), ActiveNetworksWithAttrs AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE'), DownPortsOnActiveNetworks AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.NETWORK_ID AS network_id, dpb.NAME AS port_name, dpb.MAC_ADDRESS, dpb.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS port_admin_state_up, dpb.HOST AS binding_host, dpb.VIF_TYPE, dpb.window_column AS port_admin_state_up_avg_window_2_pre_2_fol, an.network_name, an.tenant_id, an.admin_state_up AS network_admin_state_up, an.mtu, an.standard_attr_updated_at, an.window_column AS network_row_number_within_tenant FROM PORTS AS p JOIN DownPortsWithBindings AS dpb ON p.MAC_ADDRESS = dpb.MAC_ADDRESS AND p.NAME = dpb.NAME AND p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = dpb.ADMIN_STATE_UP JOIN ActiveNetworksWithAttrs AS an ON an.network_id = p.NETWORK_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT * FROM DownPortsOnActiveNetworks ORDER BY tenant_id NULLS FIRST, network_id NULLS FIRST, MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST), ActiveNetworkMtuAvg AS (SELECT AVG(n.MTU) AS avg_active_network_mtu FROM NETWORKS AS n WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE') SELECT i.* FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN ActiveNetworkMtuAvg AS a WHERE i.mtu > a.avg_active_network_mtu ORDER BY i.tenant_id NULLS FIRST, i.network_id NULLS FIRST, i.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH PortSecurityGroupAndDnsCounts AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.name AS port_name, COUNT(sgpb.security_group_id) AS security_group_binding_count, SUM(CASE WHEN pd.port_id IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END) AS dns_record_count FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id LEFT JOIN PORTDNSES AS pd ON p.id = pd.port_id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.name), DownPortBindingRanks AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.NAME AS port_name, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, b.HOST AS binding_host, b.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY p.STATUS ORDER BY p.ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT dpr.port_id, dpr.port_name, psc.tenant_id, dpr.mac_address, dpr.binding_host, dpr.vif_type, dpr.window_column AS down_status_rank, psc.security_group_binding_count, psc.dns_record_count FROM DownPortBindingRanks AS dpr JOIN PortSecurityGroupAndDnsCounts AS psc ON dpr.port_id = psc.port_id ORDER BY psc.security_group_binding_count DESC NULLS LAST, psc.dns_record_count DESC NULLS LAST, dpr.port_id NULLS FIRST) SELECT tenant_id, MAX(security_group_binding_count) AS max_security_group_bindings_on_a_down_port, MAX(dns_record_count) AS max_dns_records_on_a_down_port FROM inner_cte GROUP BY tenant_id
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH subnet_details AS (SELECT s.NAME, s.TENANT_ID, s.NETWORK_ID, s.IP_VERSION, s.CIDR, s.GATEWAY_IP, s.ENABLE_DHCP, p.FIRST_IP, p.LAST_IP, MAX(s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (ORDER BY s.ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_standard_attr_id_next2 FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN IPALLOCATIONPOOLS AS p ON s.ID = p.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4), top_shared_networks AS (SELECT n.OBJECT_ID AS network_id, COUNT(*) AS subnet_count FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY n.OBJECT_ID ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 3) SELECT tsn.network_id, tsn.subnet_count, sd.NAME AS subnet_name, sd.TENANT_ID AS subnet_tenant_id, sd.IP_VERSION, sd.CIDR, sd.GATEWAY_IP, sd.ENABLE_DHCP, sd.FIRST_IP AS allocation_pool_first_ip, sd.LAST_IP AS allocation_pool_last_ip, sd.max_standard_attr_id_next2 FROM top_shared_networks AS tsn JOIN subnet_details AS sd ON sd.NETWORK_ID = tsn.network_id ORDER BY tsn.subnet_count DESC NULLS LAST, tsn.network_id NULLS FIRST, sd.NAME NULLS FIRST) SELECT i.network_id, i.subnet_count, i.subnet_name, i.subnet_tenant_id, i.IP_VERSION, i.CIDR, i.GATEWAY_IP, i.ENABLE_DHCP, i.allocation_pool_first_ip, i.allocation_pool_last_ip, i.max_standard_attr_id_next2 FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN (SELECT AVG(max_standard_attr_id_next2) AS avg_max_standard_attr_id_next2 FROM inner_cte) AS a ON 1 = 1 WHERE i.max_standard_attr_id_next2 > a.avg_max_standard_attr_id_next2 ORDER BY i.subnet_count DESC NULLS LAST, i.network_id NULLS FIRST, i.subnet_name NULLS FIRST
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH OrderRangeVariance AS (SELECT s.name AS subnet_name, MAX(d.ORDER) - MIN(d.ORDER) AS order_range, VAR_SAMP(d.ORDER) AS order_variance FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN DNSNAMESERVERS AS d ON s.id = d.subnet_id WHERE s.ip_version = 4 GROUP BY s.name ORDER BY order_range DESC NULLS LAST), CoefficientOfVariation AS (SELECT s.name AS subnet_name, CAST(STDDEV(d.ORDER) AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(AVG(d.ORDER), 0), 0) AS coefficient_of_variation FROM subnets AS s JOIN dnsnameservers AS d ON d.subnet_id = s.id JOIN ipallocationpools AS p ON p.subnet_id = s.id WHERE s.ip_version = 4 GROUP BY s.name HAVING AVG(d.ORDER) > 0 ORDER BY coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT orv.subnet_name, orv.order_range, orv.order_variance, cov.coefficient_of_variation FROM OrderRangeVariance AS orv JOIN CoefficientOfVariation AS cov ON cov.subnet_name = orv.subnet_name) SELECT i.subnet_name, i.order_range, i.order_variance, i.coefficient_of_variation FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN subnets AS s ON s.name = i.subnet_name JOIN dnsnameservers AS d ON d.subnet_id = s.id WHERE i.order_range > (SELECT AVG(order_range) FROM inner_cte) GROUP BY i.subnet_name, i.order_range, i.order_variance, i.coefficient_of_variation
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH DownPortBindingsAndIPs AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, b.HOST AS host, b.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ia.IP_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), DownPortsWithSecurityGroups AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (ORDER BY p.ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT dpbip.tenant_id, COUNT(DISTINCT dpbip.port_id) AS down_port_count, MIN(dpbip.ip_address) AS min_allocated_ip_address, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT dpsg.SECURITY_GROUP_ID) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS Has_Bound_Security_Groups FROM DownPortBindingsAndIPs AS dpbip JOIN PORTS AS p ON p.ID = dpbip.port_id JOIN DownPortsWithSecurityGroups AS dpsg ON dpsg.NETWORK_ID = p.NETWORK_ID AND dpsg.MAC_ADDRESS = p.MAC_ADDRESS AND dpsg.STATUS = p.STATUS WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' GROUP BY dpbip.tenant_id HAVING COUNT(DISTINCT dpbip.port_id) >= 2 ORDER BY down_port_count DESC NULLS LAST, min_allocated_ip_address ASC NULLS FIRST) SELECT tenant_id, down_port_count, min_allocated_ip_address, Has_Bound_Security_Groups FROM inner_cte WHERE down_port_count > (SELECT AVG(down_port_count) FROM inner_cte) ORDER BY down_port_count DESC NULLS LAST, min_allocated_ip_address ASC NULLS FIRST
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH PortStatusOwnerStats AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), PortStatusVariation AS (SELECT p.status, CAST(STDDEV(p.standard_attr_id) AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(AVG(p.standard_attr_id), 0), 0) AS coefficient_of_variation FROM securitygroupportbindings AS sgpb JOIN ports AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id GROUP BY p.status HAVING AVG(p.standard_attr_id) > 0 ORDER BY coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST), CombinedPortStatusMetrics AS (SELECT psos.port_status AS port_status, psos.device_owner AS device_owner, psos.port_count AS port_count, psos.earliest_standard_attr_update AS earliest_standard_attr_update, psv.coefficient_of_variation AS coefficient_of_variation FROM PortStatusOwnerStats AS psos JOIN PortStatusVariation AS psv ON psos.port_status = psv.status) SELECT port_status, device_owner, port_count, earliest_standard_attr_update, coefficient_of_variation FROM CombinedPortStatusMetrics ORDER BY port_status NULLS FIRST, device_owner NULLS FIRST), StatusAverages AS (SELECT port_status, AVG(port_count) AS avg_ports_per_owner_in_status FROM inner_cte GROUP BY port_status) SELECT i.port_status, i.device_owner, i.port_count, i.earliest_standard_attr_update, i.coefficient_of_variation FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN StatusAverages AS sa ON sa.port_status = i.port_status WHERE i.port_count > sa.avg_ports_per_owner_in_status ORDER BY i.port_status NULLS FIRST, i.device_owner NULLS FIRST
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH DnsOrderStats AS (SELECT s.name AS subnet_name, MAX(d.ORDER) - MIN(d.ORDER) AS order_range, VAR_SAMP(d.ORDER) AS order_variance FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN DNSNAMESERVERS AS d ON s.id = d.subnet_id WHERE s.ip_version = 4 GROUP BY s.name ORDER BY order_range DESC NULLS LAST), SubnetRoutePoolDetails AS (SELECT r.DESTINATION, r.NEXTHOP, s.NAME AS subnet_name, s.CIDR AS subnet_cidr, p.FIRST_IP, p.LAST_IP, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY r.SUBNET_ID ORDER BY r.SUBNET_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SUBNETROUTES AS r JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON r.SUBNET_ID = s.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONPOOLS AS p ON s.ID = p.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4) SELECT srpd.subnet_name, srpd.subnet_cidr, srpd.DESTINATION, srpd.NEXTHOP, srpd.FIRST_IP, srpd.LAST_IP, srpd.window_column, dos.order_range, dos.order_variance FROM SubnetRoutePoolDetails AS srpd JOIN DnsOrderStats AS dos ON srpd.subnet_name = dos.subnet_name ORDER BY srpd.subnet_name NULLS FIRST, srpd.window_column NULLS FIRST, srpd.DESTINATION NULLS FIRST), AvgDnsOrderRange AS (SELECT AVG(order_range) AS avg_order_range FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.subnet_name, i.subnet_cidr, i.DESTINATION, i.NEXTHOP, i.FIRST_IP, i.LAST_IP, i.window_column, i.order_range, i.order_variance, (i.order_range - a.avg_order_range) AS deviation_from_avg_order_range FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN AvgDnsOrderRange AS a ORDER BY i.subnet_name NULLS FIRST, i.window_column NULLS FIRST, i.DESTINATION NULLS FIRST
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH ip_allocations_admin_up AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(ia.ip_address) AS ip_allocation_count, SUM(CASE WHEN NOT ia.subnet_id IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS subnet_id_rows FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON ia.port_id = p.id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND NOT ia.ip_address IS NULL GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address), down_ports_window AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT COUNT(*) AS admin_up_ports_with_ip_allocations_count, COUNT(DISTINCT dpw.port_id) AS down_ports_count FROM ip_allocations_admin_up AS iaau CROSS JOIN down_ports_window AS dpw), DownPortsPerSecurityGroup AS (SELECT sgpb.security_group_id, COUNT(DISTINCT dpw.port_id) AS down_ports_in_sg, MAX(inner_cte.admin_up_ports_with_ip_allocations_count) AS admin_up_ports_with_ip_allocations_count FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, sgpb2.SECURITY_GROUP_ID AS security_group_id, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb2.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb2 JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb2.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') AS dpw ON dpw.security_group_id = sgpb.security_group_id AND dpw.port_id = sgpb.port_id CROSS JOIN inner_cte GROUP BY sgpb.security_group_id) SELECT security_group_id, down_ports_in_sg, admin_up_ports_with_ip_allocations_count FROM DownPortsPerSecurityGroup WHERE down_ports_in_sg > (SELECT AVG(down_ports_in_sg) FROM DownPortsPerSecurityGroup)
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH AdminUpPorts AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1), DownPortsWithIPs AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, b.HOST AS host, b.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ia.IP_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT aup.TENANT_ID, aup.VIF_TYPE, COUNT(DISTINCT aup.PORT_ID) AS Admin_Up_Port_Count, COUNT(DISTINCT dpi.port_id) AS Down_Port_Count, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT dpi.port_id) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS Has_Down_Ports_With_IPs FROM AdminUpPorts AS aup LEFT JOIN DownPortsWithIPs AS dpi ON aup.TENANT_ID = dpi.tenant_id AND aup.VIF_TYPE = dpi.vif_type WHERE aup.window_column > 0 GROUP BY aup.TENANT_ID, aup.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY Admin_Up_Port_Count DESC NULLS LAST, Down_Port_Count DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT inner_cte.TENANT_ID, inner_cte.VIF_TYPE, inner_cte.Admin_Up_Port_Count, inner_cte.Down_Port_Count, inner_cte.Has_Down_Ports_With_IPs FROM inner_cte ORDER BY inner_cte.Admin_Up_Port_Count DESC NULLS LAST, inner_cte.Down_Port_Count DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH AllowedAddressPairsOnPort AS (SELECT aap.MAC_ADDRESS AS allowed_mac_address, aap.IP_ADDRESS AS allowed_ip_address, p.ID AS port_id, sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID AS security_group_id, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY p.ID ORDER BY aap.IP_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN ALLOWEDADDRESSPAIRS AS aap ON p.ID = aap.PORT_ID WHERE p.ID = '25d421c1-c76d-4c26-9550-9f871a1d5034' ORDER BY aap.IP_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST), AdminUpPortBindingDetails AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1), AdminUpDetailsForTargetPort AS (SELECT * FROM AdminUpPortBindingDetails WHERE PORT_ID = '25d421c1-c76d-4c26-9550-9f871a1d5034') SELECT aap.allowed_mac_address, aap.allowed_ip_address, aap.port_id, aap.security_group_id, aap.window_column AS allowed_address_pairs_running_avg_standard_attr_id, t.HOST, t.VIF_TYPE, t.VNIC_TYPE, t.TENANT_ID, t.NETWORK_ID, t.MAC_ADDRESS, t.STATUS, t.DEVICE_OWNER, t.window_column AS admin_up_ports_window_avg_standard_attr_id FROM AllowedAddressPairsOnPort AS aap LEFT JOIN AdminUpDetailsForTargetPort AS t ON aap.port_id = t.PORT_ID ORDER BY aap.allowed_ip_address NULLS FIRST), TargetPortNetwork AS (SELECT p.network_id FROM PORTS AS p WHERE p.id = '25d421c1-c76d-4c26-9550-9f871a1d5034'), NetworkSecurityGroups AS (SELECT p.network_id, p.id AS port_id, COUNT(DISTINCT spb.security_group_id) AS security_group_count FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS spb ON p.id = spb.port_id JOIN TargetPortNetwork AS tpn ON p.network_id = tpn.network_id GROUP BY p.network_id, p.id), NetworkMaxSecurityGroups AS (SELECT network_id, MAX(security_group_count) AS max_security_groups_on_any_port FROM NetworkSecurityGroups GROUP BY network_id) SELECT i.allowed_mac_address, i.allowed_ip_address, i.port_id, i.security_group_id, i.allowed_address_pairs_running_avg_standard_attr_id, i.HOST, i.VIF_TYPE, i.VNIC_TYPE, i.TENANT_ID, i.NETWORK_ID, i.MAC_ADDRESS, i.STATUS, i.DEVICE_OWNER, i.admin_up_ports_window_avg_standard_attr_id, nmsg.max_security_groups_on_any_port FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN NetworkMaxSecurityGroups AS nmsg ON i.NETWORK_ID = nmsg.network_id ORDER BY i.allowed_ip_address NULLS FIRST
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH subq1 AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, sgpb.security_group_id, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.security_group_id ORDER BY p.id NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN'), subq2 AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (ORDER BY p.ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT subq1.port_id, subq1.tenant_id, subq2.name AS port_name, subq2.network_id, subq1.mac_address, subq2.admin_state_up, subq2.status, subq2.device_owner, subq1.security_group_id, subq1.window_column AS port_rank_within_security_group_by_port_id, subq2.window_column AS min_admin_state_up_in_3_preceding_1_following_by_port_id FROM subq1 JOIN subq2 ON subq1.security_group_id = subq2.security_group_id AND subq1.mac_address = subq2.mac_address ORDER BY subq1.security_group_id NULLS FIRST, subq1.window_column NULLS FIRST, subq1.port_id NULLS FIRST) SELECT inner_cte.port_id, inner_cte.tenant_id, inner_cte.port_name, inner_cte.network_id, inner_cte.mac_address, inner_cte.admin_state_up, inner_cte.status, inner_cte.device_owner, inner_cte.security_group_id, inner_cte.port_rank_within_security_group_by_port_id, inner_cte.min_admin_state_up_in_3_preceding_1_following_by_port_id, tenant_stats.total_down_ports, avg_stats.avg_down_ports_across_tenants FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT tenant_id, COUNT(*) AS total_down_ports FROM PORTS WHERE status = 'DOWN' GROUP BY tenant_id) AS tenant_stats ON tenant_stats.tenant_id = inner_cte.tenant_id JOIN (SELECT AVG(down_port_count) AS avg_down_ports_across_tenants FROM (SELECT tenant_id, COUNT(*) AS down_port_count FROM PORTS WHERE status = 'DOWN' GROUP BY tenant_id) AS per_tenant) AS avg_stats ON 1 = 1 ORDER BY inner_cte.security_group_id NULLS FIRST, inner_cte.port_rank_within_security_group_by_port_id NULLS FIRST, inner_cte.port_id NULLS FIRST
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH PortStatusVariances AS (SELECT p.STATUS, VAR_SAMP(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS standard_attr_id_variance FROM PORTS AS p JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE NOT p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID IS NULL GROUP BY p.STATUS ORDER BY standard_attr_id_variance DESC NULLS LAST), DownPortHostVariances AS (SELECT b.HOST, VAR_SAMP(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS standard_attr_id_variance FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND NOT ia.PORT_ID IS NULL GROUP BY b.HOST ORDER BY standard_attr_id_variance DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT psv.STATUS, psv.standard_attr_id_variance AS status_standard_attr_id_variance, dhv.HOST, dhv.standard_attr_id_variance AS down_host_standard_attr_id_variance FROM PortStatusVariances AS psv JOIN DownPortHostVariances AS dhv ON psv.STATUS = 'DOWN' WHERE NOT psv.standard_attr_id_variance IS NULL ORDER BY psv.standard_attr_id_variance DESC NULLS LAST, dhv.standard_attr_id_variance DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT i.STATUS, i.status_standard_attr_id_variance, i.HOST, i.down_host_standard_attr_id_variance, host_counts.down_ports_with_ip_count, avg_host_counts.avg_down_ports_with_ip_count FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN (SELECT b.HOST, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS down_ports_with_ip_count FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND NOT ia.PORT_ID IS NULL GROUP BY b.HOST) AS host_counts ON i.HOST = host_counts.HOST, (SELECT AVG(down_ports_with_ip_count) AS avg_down_ports_with_ip_count FROM (SELECT b.HOST, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS down_ports_with_ip_count FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND NOT ia.PORT_ID IS NULL GROUP BY b.HOST) AS sub) AS avg_host_counts ORDER BY i.status_standard_attr_id_variance DESC NULLS LAST, i.down_host_standard_attr_id_variance DESC NULLS LAST, host_counts.down_ports_with_ip_count DESC NULLS LAST
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH sq1 AS (SELECT p.status, CAST(STDDEV(p.standard_attr_id) AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(AVG(p.standard_attr_id), 0), 0) AS coefficient_of_variation FROM securitygroupportbindings AS sgpb JOIN ports AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id GROUP BY p.status HAVING AVG(p.standard_attr_id) > 0 ORDER BY coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST), sq2 AS (SELECT p.STATUS, VAR_SAMP(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS standard_attr_id_variance FROM PORTS AS p JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE NOT p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID IS NULL GROUP BY p.STATUS ORDER BY standard_attr_id_variance DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT sq1.status, sq1.coefficient_of_variation, sq2.standard_attr_id_variance FROM sq1 JOIN sq2 ON sq1.status = sq2.status ORDER BY sq1.coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST), avg_stats AS (SELECT AVG(coefficient_of_variation) AS avg_coefficient_of_variation FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.status, i.coefficient_of_variation, i.standard_attr_id_variance, a.avg_coefficient_of_variation, (i.coefficient_of_variation - a.avg_coefficient_of_variation) AS deviation_from_avg_coefficient_of_variation FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_stats AS a ORDER BY i.coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH RbacSharedIpVersionVariance AS (SELECT n.ACTION, VAR_SAMP(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_variance FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY n.ACTION ORDER BY ip_version_variance DESC NULLS LAST), RbacIpVersionStats AS (SELECT n.ACTION AS rbac_action, AVG(s.IP_VERSION) AS avg_ip_version, MAX(s.IP_VERSION) - MIN(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_range, VAR_SAMP(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_variance FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION IN (4, 6) GROUP BY n.ACTION ORDER BY ip_version_range DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT rs.ACTION AS rbac_action, rs.ip_version_variance AS shared_action_ip_version_variance, ris.avg_ip_version, ris.ip_version_range, ris.ip_version_variance AS overall_ip_version_variance FROM RbacSharedIpVersionVariance AS rs JOIN RbacIpVersionStats AS ris ON rs.ACTION = ris.rbac_action ORDER BY rs.ip_version_variance DESC NULLS LAST), AllActionIpVersionVariance AS (SELECT n.ACTION, VAR_SAMP(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_variance FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION IN (4, 6) GROUP BY n.ACTION), VarianceBenchmark AS (SELECT AVG(ip_version_variance) AS avg_action_ip_version_variance FROM AllActionIpVersionVariance) SELECT i.rbac_action, i.shared_action_ip_version_variance, i.avg_ip_version, i.ip_version_range, i.overall_ip_version_variance, vb.avg_action_ip_version_variance AS variance_benchmark, CASE WHEN i.shared_action_ip_version_variance > vb.avg_action_ip_version_variance * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_VARIANCE' WHEN i.shared_action_ip_version_variance < vb.avg_action_ip_version_variance * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_VARIANCE' ELSE 'AVERAGE_VARIANCE' END AS variance_category FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN VarianceBenchmark AS vb
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH DownPortsWindow AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, AVG(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST), Drive59PortsRunningSum AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, p.TENANT_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE b.HOST = 'drive-59'), DownDrive59PortDetails AS (SELECT dpw.NAME, dpw.MAC_ADDRESS, dpw.ADMIN_STATE_UP, dpw.HOST, dpw.VIF_TYPE, dpw.window_column AS down_ports_avg_admin_state_up_window, d59.PORT_ID, d59.TENANT_ID, d59.window_column AS drive59_running_sum_standard_attr_id FROM DownPortsWindow AS dpw JOIN Drive59PortsRunningSum AS d59 ON dpw.MAC_ADDRESS = d59.MAC_ADDRESS WHERE dpw.HOST = 'drive-59') SELECT * FROM DownDrive59PortDetails ORDER BY VIF_TYPE NULLS FIRST, MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST, PORT_ID NULLS FIRST), avg_ports AS (SELECT AVG(port_count) AS avg_port_count FROM (SELECT COUNT(p2.ID) AS port_count FROM PORTS AS p2 GROUP BY p2.TENANT_ID) AS sub), tenant_port_counts AS (SELECT p3.TENANT_ID, COUNT(p3.ID) AS total_ports FROM PORTS AS p3 GROUP BY p3.TENANT_ID) SELECT i.*, tpc.total_ports, ap.avg_port_count FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN tenant_port_counts AS tpc ON i.TENANT_ID = tpc.TENANT_ID, avg_ports AS ap ORDER BY i.VIF_TYPE NULLS FIRST, i.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST, i.PORT_ID NULLS FIRST
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH q1 AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, sgpb.security_group_id, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.security_group_id ORDER BY p.id NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN'), q2 AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE') SELECT q2.network_id, q2.network_name, q2.tenant_id, q2.admin_state_up, q2.mtu, q2.standard_attr_updated_at, q1.port_id, q1.mac_address, q1.security_group_id, q1.window_column AS port_rank_within_security_group, q2.window_column AS network_row_number_within_tenant FROM q2 JOIN PORTS AS p ON p.network_id = q2.network_id JOIN q1 ON q1.port_id = p.id AND q1.tenant_id = q2.tenant_id ORDER BY q2.tenant_id NULLS FIRST, q2.network_name NULLS FIRST, q1.security_group_id NULLS FIRST, q1.window_column NULLS FIRST, q1.port_id NULLS FIRST), avg_active_mtu AS (SELECT AVG(n.mtu) AS avg_mtu FROM NETWORKS AS n WHERE n.status = 'ACTIVE') SELECT i.network_id, i.network_name, i.tenant_id, i.admin_state_up, i.mtu, i.standard_attr_updated_at, i.port_id, i.mac_address, i.security_group_id, i.port_rank_within_security_group, i.network_row_number_within_tenant, (i.mtu - a.avg_mtu) AS deviation_from_avg_active_network_mtu FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_active_mtu AS a
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH DownPortsWithIPs AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.NAME AS port_name, p.NETWORK_ID AS network_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY p.NETWORK_ID ORDER BY p.ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), PortSecurityBindingStatsByStatus AS (SELECT p.status, MAX(ps.binding_count) - MIN(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_range, VAR_SAMP(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_variance, STDDEV(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_stddev FROM ports AS p JOIN (SELECT p2.id AS port_id, COUNT(*) AS binding_count FROM ports AS p2 JOIN portsecuritybindings AS b ON p2.id = b.port_id GROUP BY p2.id) AS ps ON p.id = ps.port_id GROUP BY p.status ORDER BY binding_count_range DESC NULLS LAST), DownPortCountsByNetwork AS (SELECT network_id, COUNT(DISTINCT port_id) AS down_port_count FROM DownPortsWithIPs GROUP BY network_id) SELECT d.port_id, d.tenant_id, d.port_name, d.network_id, d.mac_address, d.device_owner, d.ip_address, d.window_column, ps.status, ps.binding_count_range, ps.binding_count_variance, ps.binding_count_stddev, c.down_port_count FROM DownPortsWithIPs AS d JOIN PortSecurityBindingStatsByStatus AS ps ON ps.status = 'DOWN' JOIN DownPortCountsByNetwork AS c ON d.network_id = c.network_id ORDER BY d.network_id NULLS FIRST, d.window_column NULLS FIRST), SubnetAllocations AS (SELECT ia.subnet_id, ia.network_id, COUNT(DISTINCT ia.ip_address) AS allocated_ips FROM ipallocations AS ia GROUP BY ia.subnet_id, ia.network_id), AverageAllocations AS (SELECT AVG(allocated_ips) AS avg_ips FROM SubnetAllocations) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.port_name, i.network_id, i.mac_address, i.device_owner, i.ip_address, i.window_column, i.status, i.binding_count_range, i.binding_count_variance, i.binding_count_stddev, i.down_port_count, ia.subnet_id, sa.allocated_ips AS subnet_allocated_ips, (sa.allocated_ips - aa.avg_ips) AS subnet_deviation_from_avg_allocated_ips FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON ia.port_id = i.port_id AND ia.ip_address = i.ip_address JOIN SubnetAllocations AS sa ON sa.subnet_id = ia.subnet_id AND sa.network_id = ia.network_id CROSS JOIN AverageAllocations AS aa ORDER BY i.network_id NULLS FIRST, i.window_column NULLS FIRST
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH Ipv4SubnetDnsOrderStats AS (SELECT s.name AS subnet_name, MAX(d.ORDER) - MIN(d.ORDER) AS order_range, VAR_SAMP(d.ORDER) AS order_variance FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN DNSNAMESERVERS AS d ON s.id = d.subnet_id WHERE s.ip_version = 4 GROUP BY s.name ORDER BY order_range DESC NULLS LAST), SharedNetworkSubnets AS (SELECT s.NAME AS subnet_name, s.CIDR AS subnet_cidr, n.TARGET_TENANT AS rbac_target_tenant, SUM(s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY s.IP_VERSION ORDER BY s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS n ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared'), CombinedSubnetInsights AS (SELECT sns.subnet_name, sns.subnet_cidr, sns.rbac_target_tenant, sns.window_column, ids.order_range, ids.order_variance FROM SharedNetworkSubnets AS sns JOIN Ipv4SubnetDnsOrderStats AS ids ON ids.subnet_name = sns.subnet_name) SELECT subnet_name, subnet_cidr, rbac_target_tenant, window_column, order_range, order_variance FROM CombinedSubnetInsights ORDER BY subnet_name NULLS FIRST) SELECT i.rbac_target_tenant, COUNT(DISTINCT i.subnet_name) AS total_shared_subnets, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT d.subnet_id) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_any_dns_nameservers FROM inner_cte AS i LEFT JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON s.name = i.subnet_name AND s.cidr = i.subnet_cidr LEFT JOIN DNSNAMESERVERS AS d ON d.subnet_id = s.id GROUP BY i.rbac_target_tenant ORDER BY total_shared_subnets DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH DownPortsWindow AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, AVG(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST), HostStandardAttrVariance AS (SELECT b.HOST, VAR_SAMP(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS standard_attr_id_variance FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND NOT ia.PORT_ID IS NULL GROUP BY b.HOST ORDER BY standard_attr_id_variance DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT dpw.VIF_TYPE, COUNT(*) AS down_ports_count, AVG(dpw.window_column) AS avg_window_admin_state_up, AVG(hsav.standard_attr_id_variance) AS avg_standard_attr_id_variance_across_hosts FROM DownPortsWindow AS dpw JOIN HostStandardAttrVariance AS hsav ON dpw.HOST = hsav.HOST GROUP BY dpw.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY down_ports_count DESC NULLS LAST), AverageDownPorts AS (SELECT AVG(down_ports_count) AS avg_down_ports_count FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.VIF_TYPE, i.down_ports_count, i.avg_window_admin_state_up, i.avg_standard_attr_id_variance_across_hosts, adp.avg_down_ports_count AS benchmark, CASE WHEN i.down_ports_count > adp.avg_down_ports_count * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_DOWN' WHEN i.down_ports_count < adp.avg_down_ports_count * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_DOWN' ELSE 'AVERAGE_DOWN' END AS down_port_category FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN AverageDownPorts AS adp
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH default_external_network_rbac_rank AS (SELECT n.NAME, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY r.ACTION NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM EXTERNALNETWORKS AS e JOIN NETWORKS AS n ON e.NETWORK_ID = n.ID JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r ON n.ID = r.OBJECT_ID WHERE e.IS_DEFAULT = 0), default_external_network_status_admin_avg AS (SELECT e.NETWORK_ID, n.NAME, n.STATUS, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP, AVG(n.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (PARTITION BY n.STATUS ORDER BY n.NAME NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM EXTERNALNETWORKS AS e JOIN NETWORKS AS n ON e.NETWORK_ID = n.ID WHERE e.IS_DEFAULT = 0) SELECT s.NETWORK_ID, s.NAME, s.STATUS, s.ADMIN_STATE_UP, s.window_column AS avg_admin_state_up_within_status_by_name, r.window_column AS dense_rank_by_rbac_action_with_2_preceding_frame FROM default_external_network_status_admin_avg AS s JOIN default_external_network_rbac_rank AS r ON s.NAME = r.NAME) SELECT i.NETWORK_ID, i.NAME, i.STATUS, i.ADMIN_STATE_UP, i.avg_admin_state_up_within_status_by_name, i.dense_rank_by_rbac_action_with_2_preceding_frame, overall_avg.overall_avg_of_status_group_avgs FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN (SELECT AVG(avg_admin_state_up_within_status_by_name) AS overall_avg_of_status_group_avgs FROM inner_cte) AS overall_avg ON 1 = 1
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH subnet_shared_rbac AS (SELECT s.NAME AS subnet_name, s.CIDR AS subnet_cidr, n.TARGET_TENANT AS rbac_target_tenant, SUM(s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY s.IP_VERSION ORDER BY s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS n ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared'), active_network_rbac AS (SELECT n.name, n.status, n.admin_state_up, r.action, r.target_tenant, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY n.id ORDER BY n.id NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM networksecuritybindings AS b JOIN networks AS n ON b.network_id = n.id JOIN networkrbacs AS r ON n.id = r.object_id WHERE n.status = 'ACTIVE') SELECT anr.name AS network_name, anr.status AS network_status, anr.admin_state_up, anr.action AS rbac_action, anr.target_tenant AS rbac_target_tenant, anr.window_column AS network_dense_rank, ssr.subnet_name, ssr.subnet_cidr, ssr.window_column AS subnet_standard_attr_running_sum FROM active_network_rbac AS anr JOIN subnet_shared_rbac AS ssr ON ssr.rbac_target_tenant = anr.target_tenant AND anr.action = 'access_as_shared'), avg_running_sum AS (SELECT AVG(subnet_standard_attr_running_sum) AS avg_subnet_running_sum FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.network_name, i.network_status, i.admin_state_up, i.rbac_action, i.rbac_target_tenant, i.network_dense_rank, i.subnet_name, i.subnet_cidr, i.subnet_standard_attr_running_sum, (i.subnet_standard_attr_running_sum - a.avg_subnet_running_sum) AS deviation_from_avg_running_sum FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_running_sum AS a
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH port_status_owner_stats AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), bound_admin_ports AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.name AS port_name, COUNT(sgpb.security_group_id) AS security_group_binding_count, SUM(CASE WHEN pd.port_id IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END) AS dns_record_count FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id LEFT JOIN PORTDNSES AS pd ON p.id = pd.port_id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.name), top10_status_owner_by_bound_ports AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS bound_admin_port_count FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb ON sgpb.port_id = p.id WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' AND p.admin_state_up = 1 GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER ORDER BY bound_admin_port_count DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10) SELECT t.port_status, t.device_owner, t.bound_admin_port_count, psos.port_count AS total_ports_with_standard_attr_resource_type_ports, psos.earliest_standard_attr_update, SUM(bap.security_group_binding_count) AS total_security_group_bindings_across_bound_admin_ports, SUM(bap.dns_record_count) AS total_dns_records_across_bound_admin_ports FROM top10_status_owner_by_bound_ports AS t JOIN port_status_owner_stats AS psos ON psos.port_status = t.port_status AND ((psos.device_owner = t.device_owner) OR (psos.device_owner IS NULL AND t.device_owner IS NULL)) JOIN PORTS AS p ON p.STATUS = t.port_status AND ((p.DEVICE_OWNER = t.device_owner) OR (p.DEVICE_OWNER IS NULL AND t.device_owner IS NULL)) JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID AND sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' JOIN bound_admin_ports AS bap ON bap.port_id = p.id GROUP BY t.port_status, t.device_owner, t.bound_admin_port_count, psos.port_count, psos.earliest_standard_attr_update ORDER BY t.bound_admin_port_count DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT i.port_status, i.device_owner, i.bound_admin_port_count, i.total_ports_with_standard_attr_resource_type_ports, i.earliest_standard_attr_update, i.total_security_group_bindings_across_bound_admin_ports, i.total_dns_records_across_bound_admin_ports, avg_stats.avg_bound_admin_port_count_across_top10 FROM inner_cte AS i, (SELECT AVG(bound_admin_port_count) AS avg_bound_admin_port_count_across_top10 FROM inner_cte) AS avg_stats
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH route_metrics AS (SELECT s.NAME AS subnet_name, MAX(route_count) - MIN(route_count) AS route_count_range, VAR_SAMP(route_count) AS route_count_variance, STDDEV(route_count) AS route_count_stddev FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN (SELECT sr.SUBNET_ID, COUNT(*) AS route_count FROM SUBNETROUTES AS sr GROUP BY sr.SUBNET_ID, sr.NEXTHOP) AS route_stats ON s.ID = route_stats.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 GROUP BY s.NAME ORDER BY route_count_range DESC NULLS LAST), shared_rbac_max_ip AS (SELECT s.name, MAX(s.ip_version) AS max_ip_version, s.gateway_ip FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.object_id = s.network_id WHERE n.action = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY s.name, s.gateway_ip HAVING MAX(s.ip_version) > 3) SELECT rm.subnet_name, srm.gateway_ip, srm.max_ip_version, rm.route_count_range, rm.route_count_variance, rm.route_count_stddev FROM route_metrics AS rm JOIN shared_rbac_max_ip AS srm ON rm.subnet_name = srm.name ORDER BY rm.route_count_range DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT s.network_id, MAX(inner_cte.route_count_range) AS max_route_count_range FROM inner_cte JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON s.name = inner_cte.subnet_name AND s.gateway_ip = inner_cte.gateway_ip GROUP BY s.network_id
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH PortSecurityWindow AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY psb.port_security_enabled NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN'), PortIpBindingWindow AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, b.HOST AS host, b.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ia.IP_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT pibw.tenant_id, COUNT(DISTINCT pibw.port_id) AS down_port_count, AVG(pibw.window_column) AS avg_ip_row_number_window, AVG(psw.window_column) AS avg_port_security_dense_rank_window FROM PortIpBindingWindow AS pibw JOIN PortSecurityWindow AS psw ON pibw.port_id = psw.port_id WHERE NOT pibw.tenant_id IS NULL GROUP BY pibw.tenant_id HAVING COUNT(DISTINCT pibw.port_id) >= 2 ORDER BY down_port_count DESC NULLS LAST, pibw.tenant_id NULLS FIRST), Benchmark AS (SELECT AVG(down_port_count) AS avg_down_ports FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.tenant_id, i.down_port_count, i.avg_ip_row_number_window, i.avg_port_security_dense_rank_window, b.avg_down_ports AS benchmark, CASE WHEN i.down_port_count > b.avg_down_ports * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_DOWN' WHEN i.down_port_count < b.avg_down_ports * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_DOWN' ELSE 'AVERAGE_DOWN' END AS down_category FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN Benchmark AS b
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH DnsOrderStats AS (SELECT s.NAME AS subnet_name, AVG(d.ORDER) AS avg_dns_order, VAR_SAMP(d.ORDER) AS dns_order_variance FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN DNSNAMESERVERS AS d ON s.ID = d.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 GROUP BY s.NAME ORDER BY dns_order_variance DESC NULLS LAST), RoutePoolDetails AS (SELECT r.DESTINATION, r.NEXTHOP, s.NAME AS subnet_name, s.CIDR AS subnet_cidr, p.FIRST_IP, p.LAST_IP, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY r.SUBNET_ID ORDER BY r.SUBNET_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SUBNETROUTES AS r JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON r.SUBNET_ID = s.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONPOOLS AS p ON s.ID = p.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4) SELECT rpd.subnet_name, rpd.subnet_cidr, rpd.DESTINATION, rpd.NEXTHOP, rpd.FIRST_IP, rpd.LAST_IP, rpd.window_column, dos.avg_dns_order, dos.dns_order_variance FROM RoutePoolDetails AS rpd JOIN DnsOrderStats AS dos ON rpd.subnet_name = dos.subnet_name ORDER BY dos.dns_order_variance DESC NULLS LAST, rpd.subnet_name NULLS FIRST, rpd.window_column NULLS FIRST), VarianceBenchmark AS (SELECT AVG(dns_order_variance) AS avg_dns_order_variance FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.subnet_name, i.subnet_cidr, i.DESTINATION, i.NEXTHOP, i.FIRST_IP, i.LAST_IP, i.window_column, i.avg_dns_order, i.dns_order_variance, vb.avg_dns_order_variance AS benchmark_dns_order_variance, CASE WHEN i.dns_order_variance > vb.avg_dns_order_variance * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_VARIANCE' WHEN i.dns_order_variance < vb.avg_dns_order_variance * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_VARIANCE' ELSE 'AVERAGE_VARIANCE' END AS variance_category FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN VarianceBenchmark AS vb ORDER BY i.dns_order_variance DESC NULLS LAST, i.subnet_name NULLS FIRST, i.window_column NULLS FIRST
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH DownPortsWithBindings AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, AVG(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST), AdminUpPortsWithSecurity AS (SELECT p.NAME AS port_name, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, psb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED AS port_security_enabled, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1) SELECT dp.NAME AS down_port_name, dp.MAC_ADDRESS AS down_mac_address, dp.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS down_admin_state_up, dp.HOST AS binding_host, dp.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, dp.window_column AS avg_admin_state_up_window_2p_2f, au.port_name AS admin_up_port_name, au.mac_address AS admin_up_mac_address, au.port_security_enabled, au.window_column AS row_number_window_2p_1f FROM DownPortsWithBindings AS dp JOIN AdminUpPortsWithSecurity AS au ON dp.MAC_ADDRESS = au.mac_address ORDER BY dp.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST), overall_avg AS (SELECT AVG(avg_admin_state_up_window_2p_2f) AS overall_avg_admin_state_up_window FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.down_port_name, i.down_mac_address, i.down_admin_state_up, i.binding_host, i.vif_type, i.avg_admin_state_up_window_2p_2f, i.admin_up_port_name, i.admin_up_mac_address, i.port_security_enabled, i.row_number_window_2p_1f, (i.avg_admin_state_up_window_2p_2f - oa.overall_avg_admin_state_up_window) AS deviation_from_overall_avg_windowed_admin_state_up FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN overall_avg AS oa ORDER BY i.down_mac_address NULLS FIRST
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH AdminUpPorts AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1), ActiveNetworks AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE') SELECT an.network_id, an.network_name, an.tenant_id, an.admin_state_up, an.mtu, an.standard_attr_updated_at, an.window_column AS network_row_number_within_tenant, ap.PORT_ID, ap.HOST, ap.VIF_TYPE, ap.VNIC_TYPE, ap.MAC_ADDRESS, ap.STATUS AS port_status, ap.DEVICE_OWNER, ap.window_column AS avg_standard_attr_id_window_2_preceding_2_following FROM ActiveNetworks AS an JOIN AdminUpPorts AS ap ON ap.NETWORK_ID = an.network_id AND ap.TENANT_ID = an.tenant_id), TenantActiveAdminUpPortCounts AS (SELECT n.tenant_id, n.id AS network_id, COUNT(p.id) AS admin_up_port_count FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN PORTS AS p ON p.network_id = n.id AND p.tenant_id = n.tenant_id WHERE n.status = 'ACTIVE' AND p.admin_state_up = 1 GROUP BY n.tenant_id, n.id), TenantActiveAdminUpAverages AS (SELECT tenant_id, AVG(admin_up_port_count) AS avg_admin_up_ports_per_active_network FROM TenantActiveAdminUpPortCounts GROUP BY tenant_id) SELECT i.network_id, i.network_name, i.tenant_id, i.admin_state_up, i.mtu, i.standard_attr_updated_at, i.network_row_number_within_tenant, i.port_id, i.host, i.vif_type, i.vnic_type, i.mac_address, i.port_status, i.device_owner, i.avg_standard_attr_id_window_2_preceding_2_following FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN TenantActiveAdminUpPortCounts AS c ON c.tenant_id = i.tenant_id AND c.network_id = i.network_id JOIN TenantActiveAdminUpAverages AS a ON a.tenant_id = i.tenant_id WHERE c.admin_up_port_count > a.avg_admin_up_ports_per_active_network
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH PortDetailsForSecurityGroup AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.STATUS AS port_status, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY p.NETWORK_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID = '0008e629-5619-41e0-b86c-4eecfeaacb6c'), PortSecurityBindingStatsByStatus AS (SELECT p.status, MAX(ps.binding_count) - MIN(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_range, VAR_SAMP(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_variance, STDDEV(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_stddev FROM ports AS p JOIN (SELECT p2.id AS port_id, COUNT(*) AS binding_count FROM ports AS p2 JOIN portsecuritybindings AS b ON p2.id = b.port_id GROUP BY p2.id) AS ps ON p.id = ps.port_id GROUP BY p.status ORDER BY binding_count_range DESC NULLS LAST), SecurityGroupPortStatusCounts AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS ports_in_security_group FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID WHERE sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID = '0008e629-5619-41e0-b86c-4eecfeaacb6c' GROUP BY p.STATUS) SELECT pd.port_id, pd.port_status, pd.ip_address, pd.window_column, pss.binding_count_range, pss.binding_count_variance, pss.binding_count_stddev, sgc.ports_in_security_group FROM PortDetailsForSecurityGroup AS pd JOIN PortSecurityBindingStatsByStatus AS pss ON pd.port_status = pss.status JOIN SecurityGroupPortStatusCounts AS sgc ON pd.port_status = sgc.port_status ORDER BY pd.port_status NULLS FIRST, pd.window_column NULLS FIRST, pd.port_id NULLS FIRST), StatusBenchmark AS (SELECT AVG(ports_in_security_group) AS avg_ports_per_status FROM (SELECT port_status, MAX(ports_in_security_group) AS ports_in_security_group FROM inner_cte GROUP BY port_status) AS s) SELECT i.port_id, i.port_status, i.ip_address, i.window_column, i.binding_count_range, i.binding_count_variance, i.binding_count_stddev, i.ports_in_security_group, sb.avg_ports_per_status AS benchmark, CASE WHEN i.ports_in_security_group > sb.avg_ports_per_status * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_STATUS' WHEN i.ports_in_security_group < sb.avg_ports_per_status * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_STATUS' ELSE 'AVERAGE_STATUS' END AS status_category FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN StatusBenchmark AS sb ORDER BY i.port_status NULLS FIRST, i.window_column NULLS FIRST, i.port_id NULLS FIRST
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH SubnetRouteStats AS (SELECT s.NAME AS subnet_name, MAX(route_count) - MIN(route_count) AS route_count_range, VAR_SAMP(route_count) AS route_count_variance, STDDEV(route_count) AS route_count_stddev FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN (SELECT sr.SUBNET_ID, COUNT(*) AS route_count FROM SUBNETROUTES AS sr GROUP BY sr.SUBNET_ID, sr.NEXTHOP) AS route_stats ON s.ID = route_stats.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 GROUP BY s.NAME ORDER BY route_count_range DESC NULLS LAST), ActiveNetworks AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE') SELECT an.tenant_id, an.network_id, an.network_name, an.admin_state_up, an.mtu, an.standard_attr_updated_at, an.window_column, srs.subnet_name, srs.route_count_range, srs.route_count_variance, srs.route_count_stddev FROM ActiveNetworks AS an JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON s.NETWORK_ID = an.network_id AND s.IP_VERSION = 4 JOIN SubnetRouteStats AS srs ON srs.subnet_name = s.NAME) SELECT i.tenant_id, COUNT(DISTINCT i.network_id) AS total_active_networks, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN i.route_count_range > 0 THEN i.subnet_name END) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_ipv4_subnet_with_nonzero_route_range FROM inner_cte AS i GROUP BY i.tenant_id ORDER BY total_active_networks DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH HostDrive59Ports AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, p.TENANT_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE b.HOST = 'drive-59'), AdminUpPorts AS (SELECT p.NAME AS port_name, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, psb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED AS port_security_enabled, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1), Drive59AdminUpSummary AS (SELECT h.TENANT_ID, COUNT(DISTINCT h.PORT_ID) AS drive59_port_count, COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN a.port_security_enabled = 1 THEN h.PORT_ID END) AS drive59_secured_port_count, ROUND(CAST(CAST(COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN a.port_security_enabled = 1 THEN h.PORT_ID END) * 1.0 AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(COUNT(DISTINCT h.PORT_ID), 0), 0) AS DECIMAL), 2) AS secured_port_ratio FROM HostDrive59Ports AS h JOIN AdminUpPorts AS a ON a.mac_address = h.MAC_ADDRESS GROUP BY h.TENANT_ID) SELECT s.TENANT_ID, s.drive59_port_count, s.drive59_secured_port_count, s.secured_port_ratio FROM Drive59AdminUpSummary AS s ORDER BY s.secured_port_ratio DESC NULLS LAST, s.drive59_port_count DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT inner_cte.TENANT_ID, inner_cte.drive59_port_count, inner_cte.drive59_secured_port_count, inner_cte.secured_port_ratio, avg_stats.avg_secured_port_ratio FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT AVG(secured_port_ratio) AS avg_secured_port_ratio FROM inner_cte) AS avg_stats ON 1 = 1 ORDER BY inner_cte.secured_port_ratio DESC NULLS LAST, inner_cte.drive59_port_count DESC NULLS LAST
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH DownPorts AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.NAME AS port_name, p.NETWORK_ID AS network_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY p.NETWORK_ID ORDER BY p.ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), ActiveNetworks AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE') SELECT an.tenant_id, an.network_id, an.network_name, an.admin_state_up, an.mtu, an.standard_attr_updated_at, COUNT(DISTINCT dp.port_id) AS down_port_count FROM ActiveNetworks AS an LEFT JOIN DownPorts AS dp ON dp.network_id = an.network_id AND dp.tenant_id = an.tenant_id WHERE an.admin_state_up = 1 GROUP BY an.tenant_id, an.network_id, an.network_name, an.admin_state_up, an.mtu, an.standard_attr_updated_at HAVING COUNT(DISTINCT dp.port_id) > 0 ORDER BY down_port_count DESC NULLS LAST, an.network_name NULLS FIRST), DownPortAverages AS (SELECT AVG(down_port_count) AS avg_down_ports FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.tenant_id, i.network_id, i.network_name, i.admin_state_up, i.mtu, i.standard_attr_updated_at, i.down_port_count FROM inner_cte AS i WHERE i.down_port_count > (SELECT avg_down_ports FROM DownPortAverages) ORDER BY i.down_port_count DESC NULLS LAST, i.network_name NULLS FIRST
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH HostDrive59Ports AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, p.TENANT_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE b.HOST = 'drive-59'), AdminUpPorts AS (SELECT p.NAME AS port_name, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, psb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED AS port_security_enabled, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1), Drive59AdminUpSummary AS (SELECT h.TENANT_ID, COUNT(DISTINCT h.PORT_ID) AS drive59_port_count, COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN a.port_security_enabled = 1 THEN h.PORT_ID END) AS drive59_secured_port_count, ROUND(CAST(CAST(COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN a.port_security_enabled = 1 THEN h.PORT_ID END) * 1.0 AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(COUNT(DISTINCT h.PORT_ID), 0), 0) AS DECIMAL), 2) AS secured_port_ratio FROM HostDrive59Ports AS h JOIN AdminUpPorts AS a ON a.mac_address = h.MAC_ADDRESS GROUP BY h.TENANT_ID) SELECT s.TENANT_ID, s.drive59_port_count, s.drive59_secured_port_count, s.secured_port_ratio FROM Drive59AdminUpSummary AS s ORDER BY s.secured_port_ratio DESC NULLS LAST, s.drive59_port_count DESC NULLS LAST), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(secured_port_ratio) AS avg_secured_port_ratio FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.TENANT_ID, i.drive59_port_count, i.drive59_secured_port_count, i.secured_port_ratio, (i.secured_port_ratio - a.avg_secured_port_ratio) AS deviation_from_avg_secured_port_ratio FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS a ORDER BY deviation_from_avg_secured_port_ratio DESC NULLS LAST, i.secured_port_ratio DESC NULLS LAST, i.drive59_port_count DESC NULLS LAST
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH DnsOrderStats AS (SELECT s.name AS subnet_name, MAX(d.ORDER) - MIN(d.ORDER) AS order_range, VAR_SAMP(d.ORDER) AS order_variance FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN DNSNAMESERVERS AS d ON s.id = d.subnet_id WHERE s.ip_version = 4 GROUP BY s.name ORDER BY order_range DESC NULLS LAST), RouteCountStats AS (SELECT s.NAME AS subnet_name, MAX(route_count) - MIN(route_count) AS route_count_range, VAR_SAMP(route_count) AS route_count_variance, STDDEV(route_count) AS route_count_stddev FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN (SELECT sr.SUBNET_ID, COUNT(*) AS route_count FROM SUBNETROUTES AS sr GROUP BY sr.SUBNET_ID, sr.NEXTHOP) AS route_stats ON s.ID = route_stats.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 GROUP BY s.NAME ORDER BY route_count_range DESC NULLS LAST), CombinedSubnetStats AS (SELECT subnet_name, order_range, order_variance, NULL AS route_count_range, NULL AS route_count_variance, NULL AS route_count_stddev, 'DNS Nameserver Order Stats' AS reason FROM DnsOrderStats UNION ALL SELECT subnet_name, NULL AS order_range, NULL AS order_variance, route_count_range, route_count_variance, route_count_stddev, 'Route Count Stats' AS reason FROM RouteCountStats) SELECT * FROM CombinedSubnetStats ORDER BY subnet_name NULLS FIRST, reason NULLS FIRST), Enriched AS (SELECT i.subnet_name, s.network_id, i.order_range, i.order_variance, i.route_count_range, i.route_count_variance, i.route_count_stddev, i.reason, CASE WHEN i.reason = 'DNS Nameserver Order Stats' THEN i.order_range ELSE i.route_count_range END AS range_value FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN subnets AS s ON s.name = i.subnet_name AND s.ip_version = 4), AvgByReason AS (SELECT reason, AVG(range_value) AS avg_range_value FROM Enriched GROUP BY reason) SELECT e.subnet_name, e.network_id, e.order_range, e.order_variance, e.route_count_range, e.route_count_variance, e.route_count_stddev, e.reason, (e.range_value - a.avg_range_value) AS deviation_from_avg_range_within_reason FROM Enriched AS e JOIN AvgByReason AS a ON e.reason = a.reason ORDER BY e.subnet_name NULLS FIRST, e.reason NULLS FIRST
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH DownPortBindingIPs AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, b.HOST AS host, b.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ia.IP_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), DownPortMacAttrSums AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(sa.ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sa.RESOURCE_TYPE ORDER BY sa.ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT d1.port_id, d1.tenant_id, d1.host, d1.vif_type, d1.ip_address, d1.window_column AS ip_row_number_window, d2.mac_address, d2.window_column AS standard_attr_running_sum_window FROM DownPortBindingIPs AS d1 JOIN DownPortMacAttrSums AS d2 ON d1.port_id = d2.port_id ORDER BY d1.tenant_id NULLS FIRST, d1.port_id NULLS FIRST, d1.ip_address NULLS FIRST), TenantDownPortCounts AS (SELECT p.tenant_id, COUNT(DISTINCT p.id) AS down_ports FROM PORTS AS p WHERE p.status = 'DOWN' GROUP BY p.tenant_id), AverageTenantDownPortCounts AS (SELECT AVG(down_ports) AS avg_down_ports FROM TenantDownPortCounts) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.host, i.vif_type, i.ip_address, i.ip_row_number_window, i.mac_address, i.standard_attr_running_sum_window, t.down_ports AS tenant_down_port_count, (t.down_ports - a.avg_down_ports) AS deviation_from_avg_tenant_down_port_count FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN TenantDownPortCounts AS t ON t.tenant_id = i.tenant_id CROSS JOIN AverageTenantDownPortCounts AS a ORDER BY i.tenant_id NULLS FIRST, i.port_id NULLS FIRST, i.ip_address NULLS FIRST
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH PortSecurityWindow AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY psb.port_security_enabled NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN'), PortIpBindingWindow AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, b.HOST AS host, b.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ia.IP_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT pibw.tenant_id, COUNT(DISTINCT pibw.port_id) AS down_port_count, AVG(pibw.window_column) AS avg_ip_row_number_window, AVG(psw.window_column) AS avg_port_security_dense_rank_window FROM PortIpBindingWindow AS pibw JOIN PortSecurityWindow AS psw ON pibw.port_id = psw.port_id WHERE NOT pibw.tenant_id IS NULL GROUP BY pibw.tenant_id HAVING COUNT(DISTINCT pibw.port_id) >= 2 ORDER BY down_port_count DESC NULLS LAST, pibw.tenant_id NULLS FIRST) SELECT i.tenant_id, i.down_port_count, i.avg_ip_row_number_window, i.avg_port_security_dense_rank_window, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT b.port_id) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_any_port_binding FROM inner_cte AS i LEFT JOIN ports AS p ON p.tenant_id = i.tenant_id LEFT JOIN ml2_port_bindings AS b ON b.port_id = p.id GROUP BY i.tenant_id, i.down_port_count, i.avg_ip_row_number_window, i.avg_port_security_dense_rank_window ORDER BY i.down_port_count DESC NULLS LAST, i.tenant_id NULLS FIRST LIMIT 10
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH DownPortsStdAttrWindow AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(sa.ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sa.RESOURCE_TYPE ORDER BY sa.ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), DownPortsBindingRank AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.NAME AS port_name, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, b.HOST AS binding_host, b.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY p.STATUS ORDER BY p.ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT s.port_id, s.tenant_id, s.mac_address, s.window_column AS running_sum_standard_attr_ids_within_resource_type, b.port_name, b.binding_host, b.vif_type, b.window_column AS rank_within_down_status_by_port_id FROM DownPortsStdAttrWindow AS s JOIN DownPortsBindingRank AS b ON s.port_id = b.port_id AND s.mac_address = b.mac_address) SELECT port_id, tenant_id, mac_address, running_sum_standard_attr_ids_within_resource_type, port_name, binding_host, vif_type, rank_within_down_status_by_port_id FROM inner_cte WHERE rank_within_down_status_by_port_id > (SELECT AVG(rank_within_down_status_by_port_id) FROM inner_cte)
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH PortSecurityGroupStats AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS min_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS spb ON p.ID = spb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), PortStatusStdAttrVariance AS (SELECT p.STATUS, VAR_SAMP(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS standard_attr_id_variance FROM PORTS AS p JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE NOT p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID IS NULL GROUP BY p.STATUS ORDER BY standard_attr_id_variance DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT psgs.port_status, psgs.device_owner, psgs.port_count, psgs.min_standard_attr_id, pssav.standard_attr_id_variance FROM PortSecurityGroupStats AS psgs JOIN PortStatusStdAttrVariance AS pssav ON psgs.port_status = pssav.STATUS ORDER BY psgs.port_status NULLS FIRST, psgs.device_owner NULLS FIRST) SELECT i.device_owner, i.port_count, i.min_standard_attr_id, i.standard_attr_id_variance, COUNT(DISTINCT p.NETWORK_ID) AS distinct_networks FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN PORTS AS p ON p.DEVICE_OWNER = i.device_owner AND p.STATUS = i.port_status JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS spb ON p.ID = spb.PORT_ID GROUP BY i.device_owner, i.port_count, i.min_standard_attr_id, i.standard_attr_id_variance ORDER BY i.port_count DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH port_status_owner_sg_summary AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS min_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS spb ON p.ID = spb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), down_ports_with_ip_window AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, b.HOST AS host, b.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ia.IP_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT d.port_id, d.tenant_id, d.host, d.vif_type, d.ip_address, d.window_column, s.port_status, s.device_owner, s.port_count, s.min_standard_attr_id FROM down_ports_with_ip_window AS d JOIN PORTS AS p ON p.ID = d.port_id JOIN port_status_owner_sg_summary AS s ON s.port_status = p.STATUS AND s.device_owner = p.DEVICE_OWNER) SELECT p.NETWORK_ID AS network_id, MAX(i.window_column) AS max_ip_row_number FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN PORTS AS p ON p.ID = i.port_id JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS spb ON spb.PORT_ID = p.ID GROUP BY p.NETWORK_ID
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH PortStatusOwnerStats AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), ActiveTenantNetworks AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE'), ActiveTenantPortStatusOwnerStats AS (SELECT atn.tenant_id, atn.network_id, atn.network_name, atn.admin_state_up, atn.mtu, atn.standard_attr_updated_at, atn.window_column, psos.port_status, psos.device_owner, psos.port_count, psos.earliest_standard_attr_update FROM ActiveTenantNetworks AS atn JOIN PORTS AS p ON p.NETWORK_ID = atn.network_id JOIN PortStatusOwnerStats AS psos ON psos.port_status = p.STATUS AND psos.device_owner = p.DEVICE_OWNER JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa_p ON sa_p.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa_p.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' GROUP BY atn.tenant_id, atn.network_id, atn.network_name, atn.admin_state_up, atn.mtu, atn.standard_attr_updated_at, atn.window_column, psos.port_status, psos.device_owner, psos.port_count, psos.earliest_standard_attr_update) SELECT * FROM ActiveTenantPortStatusOwnerStats ORDER BY tenant_id NULLS FIRST, window_column NULLS FIRST, network_id NULLS FIRST, port_status NULLS FIRST, device_owner NULLS FIRST), StatusAverages AS (SELECT port_status, AVG(port_count) AS avg_port_count FROM (SELECT psos.port_status, psos.device_owner, psos.port_count FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID JOIN (SELECT p2.STATUS AS port_status, p2.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p2.ID) AS port_count FROM PORTS AS p2 JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa2 ON sa2.ID = p2.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa2.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' GROUP BY p2.STATUS, p2.DEVICE_OWNER) AS psos ON psos.port_status = p.STATUS AND psos.device_owner = p.DEVICE_OWNER WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' GROUP BY psos.port_status, psos.device_owner, psos.port_count) AS grouped_counts GROUP BY port_status) SELECT i.* FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN StatusAverages AS sa ON sa.port_status = i.port_status WHERE i.port_count > sa.avg_port_count ORDER BY i.tenant_id NULLS FIRST, i.window_column NULLS FIRST, i.network_id NULLS FIRST, i.port_status NULLS FIRST, i.device_owner NULLS FIRST
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH DownPortBindingsAndIPs AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, b.HOST AS host, b.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ia.IP_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), DownPortsMacAndStdAttrRunningSum AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(sa.ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sa.RESOURCE_TYPE ORDER BY sa.ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT d1.port_id, d1.tenant_id, d1.host, d1.vif_type, d1.ip_address, d1.window_column AS ip_row_number_window, d2.mac_address, d2.window_column AS stdattr_id_running_sum_window FROM DownPortBindingsAndIPs AS d1 JOIN DownPortsMacAndStdAttrRunningSum AS d2 ON d1.port_id = d2.port_id AND d1.tenant_id = d2.tenant_id), VifTypeAverages AS (SELECT b.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, AVG(sa.ID) AS avg_stdattr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' GROUP BY b.VIF_TYPE) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.host, i.vif_type, i.ip_address, i.ip_row_number_window, i.mac_address, i.stdattr_id_running_sum_window FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN PORTS AS p ON p.ID = i.port_id JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.ID > (SELECT v.avg_stdattr_id FROM VifTypeAverages AS v WHERE v.vif_type = i.vif_type)
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH SharedSubnets AS (SELECT s.NAME AS subnet_name, s.CIDR AS subnet_cidr, n.TARGET_TENANT AS rbac_target_tenant, SUM(s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY s.IP_VERSION ORDER BY s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS n ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared'), ActiveNetworkRBACs AS (SELECT n.name, n.status, n.admin_state_up, r.action, r.target_tenant, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY n.id ORDER BY n.id NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM networksecuritybindings AS b JOIN networks AS n ON b.network_id = n.id JOIN networkrbacs AS r ON n.id = r.object_id WHERE n.status = 'ACTIVE') SELECT anr.name AS network_name, anr.status AS network_status, anr.admin_state_up, anr.action AS rbac_action, anr.target_tenant AS rbac_target_tenant, ss.subnet_name, ss.subnet_cidr, ss.window_column AS subnet_std_attr_running_sum_by_ip_version, anr.window_column AS network_dense_rank_within_network FROM ActiveNetworkRBACs AS anr JOIN SharedSubnets AS ss ON ss.rbac_target_tenant = anr.target_tenant AND anr.action = 'access_as_shared' ORDER BY anr.name NULLS FIRST, ss.subnet_name NULLS FIRST) SELECT i.network_name, i.network_status, i.admin_state_up, i.rbac_action, i.rbac_target_tenant, i.subnet_name, i.subnet_cidr, i.subnet_std_attr_running_sum_by_ip_version, i.network_dense_rank_within_network, n.standard_attr_id AS network_standard_attr_id FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN networks AS n ON n.name = i.network_name WHERE i.subnet_std_attr_running_sum_by_ip_version > 50000 ORDER BY i.network_name NULLS FIRST, i.subnet_name NULLS FIRST
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH PortStatusOwnerStats AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), DefaultExternalNetworks AS (SELECT e.NETWORK_ID, n.NAME, n.STATUS, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP, AVG(n.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (PARTITION BY n.STATUS ORDER BY n.NAME NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM EXTERNALNETWORKS AS e JOIN NETWORKS AS n ON e.NETWORK_ID = n.ID WHERE e.IS_DEFAULT = 0), CombinedResults AS (SELECT den.NETWORK_ID AS entity_id, den.NAME AS entity_name, den.STATUS AS status, den.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, den.window_column AS metric_value, 'Default External Network' AS record_type FROM DefaultExternalNetworks AS den UNION ALL SELECT NULL AS entity_id, NULL AS entity_name, psos.port_status AS status, NULL AS admin_state_up, psos.port_count AS metric_value, 'Port Count by Status/Owner (ports resource_type)' AS record_type FROM PortStatusOwnerStats AS psos) SELECT record_type, entity_id, entity_name, status, admin_state_up, metric_value FROM CombinedResults ORDER BY record_type NULLS FIRST, status NULLS FIRST, entity_name NULLS FIRST, entity_id NULLS FIRST), avg_metrics AS (SELECT AVG(metric_value) AS avg_metric_value FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.record_type, i.entity_id, i.entity_name, i.status, i.admin_state_up, i.metric_value, (i.metric_value - a.avg_metric_value) AS deviation_from_overall_avg_metric_value FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_metrics AS a ORDER BY i.record_type NULLS FIRST, i.status NULLS FIRST, i.entity_name NULLS FIRST, i.entity_id NULLS FIRST
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH RbacSharedIpVersionVariance AS (SELECT n.ACTION, VAR_SAMP(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_variance FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY n.ACTION ORDER BY ip_version_variance DESC NULLS LAST), RbacIpVersionStats AS (SELECT n.ACTION AS rbac_action, AVG(s.IP_VERSION) AS avg_ip_version, MAX(s.IP_VERSION) - MIN(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_range, VAR_SAMP(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_variance FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION IN (4, 6) GROUP BY n.ACTION ORDER BY ip_version_range DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT rs.ACTION AS rbac_action, rs.ip_version_variance AS shared_action_ip_version_variance, ris.avg_ip_version, ris.ip_version_range, ris.ip_version_variance AS overall_ip_version_variance FROM RbacSharedIpVersionVariance AS rs JOIN RbacIpVersionStats AS ris ON rs.ACTION = ris.rbac_action ORDER BY rs.ip_version_variance DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT i.rbac_action, i.shared_action_ip_version_variance, i.avg_ip_version, i.ip_version_range, i.overall_ip_version_variance, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN s2.IP_VERSION = 6 THEN s2.ID END) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_any_ipv6_subnet_on_referenced_networks FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS n2 ON n2.ACTION = i.rbac_action JOIN SUBNETS AS s2 ON s2.NETWORK_ID = n2.OBJECT_ID GROUP BY i.rbac_action, i.shared_action_ip_version_variance, i.avg_ip_version, i.ip_version_range, i.overall_ip_version_variance ORDER BY i.shared_action_ip_version_variance DESC NULLS LAST
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH PortStatusIPLenCV AS (SELECT p.status, CAST(STDDEV(LENGTH(ia.ip_address)) AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(AVG(LENGTH(ia.ip_address)), 0), 0) AS coefficient_of_variation FROM portsecuritybindings AS psb JOIN ports AS p ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON p.id = ia.port_id WHERE psb.port_security_enabled = 1 GROUP BY p.status HAVING AVG(LENGTH(ia.ip_address)) > 0 ORDER BY coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST), DownPortsWithSecurityGroup AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (ORDER BY p.ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT d.NAME, d.NETWORK_ID, d.MAC_ADDRESS, d.ADMIN_STATE_UP, d.STATUS, d.DEVICE_OWNER, d.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, d.window_column, cv.coefficient_of_variation AS status_ip_length_coefficient_of_variation FROM DownPortsWithSecurityGroup AS d JOIN PortStatusIPLenCV AS cv ON d.STATUS = cv.status ORDER BY cv.coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST, d.NAME NULLS FIRST), DownPortIpCounts AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, COUNT(DISTINCT ia.ip_address) AS allocated_ip_count FROM ports AS p JOIN inner_cte AS i ON p.name = i.name AND p.network_id = i.network_id AND p.mac_address = i.mac_address AND p.admin_state_up = i.admin_state_up AND p.status = i.status AND p.device_owner = i.device_owner LEFT JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON ia.port_id = p.id GROUP BY p.id), AvgDownPortIpCounts AS (SELECT AVG(allocated_ip_count) AS avg_allocated_ip_count FROM DownPortIpCounts) SELECT i.NAME, i.NETWORK_ID, i.MAC_ADDRESS, i.ADMIN_STATE_UP, i.STATUS, i.DEVICE_OWNER, i.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, i.window_column, i.status_ip_length_coefficient_of_variation, dpc.allocated_ip_count, (dpc.allocated_ip_count - adpc.avg_allocated_ip_count) AS deviation_from_avg_allocated_ip_count FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN ports AS p ON p.name = i.name AND p.network_id = i.network_id AND p.mac_address = i.mac_address AND p.admin_state_up = i.admin_state_up AND p.status = i.status AND p.device_owner = i.device_owner JOIN DownPortIpCounts AS dpc ON dpc.port_id = p.id CROSS JOIN AvgDownPortIpCounts AS adpc ORDER BY i.status_ip_length_coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST, i.NAME NULLS FIRST
