WITH inner_cte AS (WITH PortIpAllocSummary AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(ia.ip_address) AS ip_allocation_count, SUM(CASE WHEN NOT ia.subnet_id IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS subnet_id_rows FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON ia.port_id = p.id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND NOT ia.ip_address IS NULL GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address), PortSecurityWindow AS (SELECT p.NAME AS port_name, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, psb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED AS port_security_enabled, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1), CombinedPortDetails AS (SELECT s.port_id, s.tenant_id, s.mac_address, w.port_name, w.port_security_enabled, w.window_column, s.ip_allocation_count, s.subnet_id_rows FROM PortIpAllocSummary AS s JOIN PortSecurityWindow AS w ON w.mac_address = s.mac_address) SELECT * FROM CombinedPortDetails ORDER BY tenant_id NULLS FIRST, mac_address NULLS FIRST, port_id NULLS FIRST), AvgIpAlloc AS (SELECT AVG(ip_allocation_count) AS avg_ip_allocation_count FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.mac_address, i.port_name, i.port_security_enabled, i.window_column, i.ip_allocation_count, i.subnet_id_rows, a.avg_ip_allocation_count AS benchmark, CASE WHEN i.ip_allocation_count > a.avg_ip_allocation_count * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_IP_ALLOC' WHEN i.ip_allocation_count < a.avg_ip_allocation_count * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_IP_ALLOC' ELSE 'AVERAGE_IP_ALLOC' END AS ip_alloc_category FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN AvgIpAlloc AS a ORDER BY i.tenant_id NULLS FIRST, i.mac_address NULLS FIRST, i.port_id NULLS FIRST
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH SubnetPoolStatsByName AS (SELECT s.name AS subnet_name, MAX(pool_count) - MIN(pool_count) AS pool_count_range, VAR_SAMP(pool_count) AS pool_count_variance, STDDEV(pool_count) AS pool_count_stddev FROM subnets AS s JOIN dnsnameservers AS d ON d.subnet_id = s.id JOIN (SELECT s2.id AS subnet_id, COUNT(*) AS pool_count FROM subnets AS s2 JOIN ipallocationpools AS p ON AS max_standard_attr_id_next2 FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN IPALLOCATIONPOOLS AS p ON s.ID = p.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4), DnsSubnetNames AS (SELECT DISTINCT s.name AS subnet_name FROM subnets AS s JOIN dnsnameservers AS d ON d.subnet_id = s.id) SELECT sd.NAME AS subnet_name, sd.TENANT_ID, sd.NETWORK_ID, sd.IP_VERSION, sd.CIDR, sd.GATEWAY_IP, sd.ENABLE_DHCP, sd.FIRST_IP, sd.LAST_IP, sd.max_standard_attr_id_next2, sp.pool_count_range, sp.pool_count_variance, sp.pool_count_stddev FROM SubnetDetailsWithWindowMax AS sd JOIN SubnetPoolStatsByName AS sp ON sd.NAME = sp.subnet_name JOIN max_pool_count_range_in_network FROM inner_cte AS ic JOIN subnets AS s ON s.name = ic.subnet_name GROUP BY s.network_id) SELECT ic.subnet_name, ic.tenant_id, ic.network_id, ic.ip_version, ic.cidr, inner_cte AS ic JOIN NetworkMaxPoolRange AS nmr ON nmr.network_id = ic.network_id ORDER BY nmr.max_pool_count_range_in_network DESC NULLS LAST, ic.pool_count_range DESC NULLS LAST, ic.subnet_name NULLS FIRST
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH DownPortStandardAttrWindow AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(sa.ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sa.RESOURCE_TYPE ORDER BY sa.ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), DownPortBindingRank AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.NAME AS port_name, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, b.HOST AS binding_host, b.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY p.STATUS ORDER BY p.ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT dpsaw.port_id, dpsaw.tenant_id, dpbr.port_name, dpsaw.mac_address, dpbr.binding_host, dpbr.vif_type, dpsaw.window_column AS standard_attr_id_running_sum_within_resource_type, dpbr.window_column AS port_rank_within_down_status FROM DownPortStandardAttrWindow AS dpsaw JOIN DownPortBindingRank AS dpbr ON dpsaw.port_id = dpbr.port_id AND dpsaw.mac_address = dpbr.mac_address ORDER BY dpsaw.port_id NULLS FIRST) SELECT i.tenant_id, COUNT(DISTINCT i.port_id) AS total_down_ports, CASE WHEN SUM(CASE WHEN i.vif_type = 'ovs' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_ovs_vif_type_on_any_down_port FROM inner_cte AS i GROUP BY i.tenant_id ORDER BY total_down_ports DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH PortStatusOwnerAgg AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS min_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS spb ON p.ID = spb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), DownPortsWindow AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, AVG(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST) SELECT psa.port_status, psa.device_owner, psa.port_count, psa.min_standard_attr_id, dpw.VIF_TYPE, COUNT(*) AS down_ports_in_vif_type, AVG(dpw.window_column) AS avg_window_admin_state_up FROM PortStatusOwnerAgg AS psa CROSS JOIN DownPortsWindow AS dpw GROUP BY psa.port_status, psa.device_owner, psa.port_count, psa.min_standard_attr_id, dpw.VIF_TYPE) SELECT VIF_TYPE, MAX(distinct_device_owners) AS max_distinct_device_owners FROM (SELECT VIF_TYPE, COUNT(DISTINCT device_owner) AS distinct_device_owners FROM inner_cte GROUP BY VIF_TYPE) AS t GROUP BY VIF_TYPE
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH StdAttrIdCV_ByStatus AS (SELECT p.status, CAST(STDDEV(p.standard_attr_id) AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(AVG(p.standard_attr_id), 0), 0) AS coefficient_of_variation FROM securitygroupportbindings AS sgpb JOIN ports AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id GROUP BY p.status HAVING AVG(p.standard_attr_id) > 0 ORDER BY coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST), IpAddressLenCV_ByStatus AS (SELECT p.status, CAST(STDDEV(LENGTH(ia.ip_address)) AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(AVG(LENGTH(ia.ip_address)), 0), 0) AS coefficient_of_variation FROM portsecuritybindings AS psb JOIN ports AS p ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON p.id = ia.port_id WHERE psb.port_security_enabled = 1 GROUP BY p.status HAVING AVG(LENGTH(ia.ip_address)) > 0 ORDER BY coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT s1.status, s1.coefficient_of_variation AS std_attr_id_coefficient_of_variation, s2.coefficient_of_variation AS ip_address_length_coefficient_of_variation FROM StdAttrIdCV_ByStatus AS s1 JOIN IpAddressLenCV_ByStatus AS s2 ON s1.status = s2.status) SELECT i.status, i.std_attr_id_coefficient_of_variation, i.ip_address_length_coefficient_of_variation FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN ports AS p ON p.status = i.status LEFT JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON ia.port_id = p.id GROUP BY i.status, i.std_attr_id_coefficient_of_variation, i.ip_address_length_coefficient_of_variation HAVING CAST(COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN NOT ia.ip_address IS NULL THEN p.id END) * 1.0 AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(COUNT(DISTINCT p.id), 0), 0) > 0.75
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH port_status_owner_sg_summary AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS min_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS spb ON p.ID = spb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), down_ports_with_ip_window AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, b.HOST AS host, b.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ia.IP_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT d.port_id, d.tenant_id, d.host, d.vif_type, d.ip_address, d.window_column, s.port_status, s.device_owner, s.port_count, s.min_standard_attr_id FROM down_ports_with_ip_window AS d JOIN PORTS AS p ON p.ID = d.port_id JOIN port_status_owner_sg_summary AS s ON s.port_status = p.STATUS AND s.device_owner = p.DEVICE_OWNER) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.host, i.vif_type, i.ip_address, i.window_column, i.port_status, i.device_owner, i.port_count, i.min_standard_attr_id, avg_counts.avg_port_count_across_down_status_owner_groups FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN (SELECT AVG(port_count) AS avg_port_count_across_down_status_owner_groups FROM (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS port_count FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS spb ON p.ID = spb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER) AS sub) AS avg_counts ON 1 = 1
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH RbacSharedIpVersionVariance AS (SELECT n.ACTION, VAR_SAMP(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_variance FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY n.ACTION ORDER BY ip_version_variance DESC NULLS LAST), RbacIpVersionStats AS (SELECT n.ACTION AS rbac_action, AVG(s.IP_VERSION) AS avg_ip_version, MAX(s.IP_VERSION) - MIN(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_range, VAR_SAMP(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_variance FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION IN (4, 6) GROUP BY n.ACTION ORDER BY ip_version_range DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT rs.ACTION AS rbac_action, rs.ip_version_variance AS shared_action_ip_version_variance, ris.avg_ip_version, ris.ip_version_range, ris.ip_version_variance AS overall_ip_version_variance FROM RbacSharedIpVersionVariance AS rs JOIN RbacIpVersionStats AS ris ON rs.ACTION = ris.rbac_action ORDER BY rs.ip_version_variance DESC NULLS LAST), dhcp_stats AS (SELECT n.ACTION AS rbac_action, AVG(CAST(s.ENABLE_DHCP AS REAL)) AS avg_enable_dhcp FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY n.ACTION) SELECT i.rbac_action, i.shared_action_ip_version_variance, i.avg_ip_version, i.ip_version_range, i.overall_ip_version_variance, d.avg_enable_dhcp FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN dhcp_stats AS d ON i.rbac_action = d.rbac_action ORDER BY i.shared_action_ip_version_variance DESC NULLS LAST
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH DownPortsWindow AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, AVG(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST), Drive59PortsRunningSum AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, p.TENANT_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE b.HOST = 'drive-59'), DownDrive59PortDetails AS (SELECT dpw.NAME, dpw.MAC_ADDRESS, dpw.ADMIN_STATE_UP, dpw.HOST, dpw.VIF_TYPE, dpw.window_column AS down_ports_avg_admin_state_up_window, d59.PORT_ID, d59.TENANT_ID, d59.window_column AS drive59_running_sum_standard_attr_id FROM DownPortsWindow AS dpw JOIN Drive59PortsRunningSum AS d59 ON dpw.MAC_ADDRESS = d59.MAC_ADDRESS WHERE dpw.HOST = 'drive-59') SELECT * FROM DownDrive59PortDetails ORDER BY VIF_TYPE NULLS FIRST, MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST, PORT_ID NULLS FIRST) SELECT i.tenant_id, COUNT(DISTINCT i.port_id) AS total_ports, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT psb.port_id) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_port_security_binding FROM inner_cte AS i LEFT JOIN PORTS AS p ON p.id = i.port_id LEFT JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON psb.port_id = p.id GROUP BY i.tenant_id ORDER BY total_ports DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH HostPortMaxAttr AS (SELECT b.HOST, MAX(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS max_standard_attr_id, p.STATUS FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN ALLOWEDADDRESSPAIRS AS a ON p.ID = a.PORT_ID WHERE a.IP_ADDRESS = '10.232.210.41/8' GROUP BY b.HOST, p.STATUS HAVING MAX(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) > 1), StatusAttrVariance AS (SELECT p.STATUS, VAR_SAMP(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS standard_attr_id_variance FROM PORTS AS p JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE NOT p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID IS NULL GROUP BY p.STATUS ORDER BY standard_attr_id_variance DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT hpma.HOST, hpma.STATUS, hpma.max_standard_attr_id, sav.standard_attr_id_variance FROM HostPortMaxAttr AS hpma JOIN StatusAttrVariance AS sav ON hpma.STATUS = sav.STATUS) SELECT i.HOST, i.STATUS, i.max_standard_attr_id, i.standard_attr_id_variance, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT ia.PORT_ID) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_ip_allocation_in_group FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON i.HOST = b.HOST JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID AND p.STATUS = i.STATUS LEFT JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID GROUP BY i.HOST, i.STATUS, i.max_standard_attr_id, i.standard_attr_id_variance
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH DownPortsBySecurityGroup AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), Drive59PortsByVifType AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, p.TENANT_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE b.HOST = 'drive-59'), CombinedPorts AS (SELECT port_id, tenant_id, mac_address, window_column, 'DOWN (by Security Group)' AS reason FROM DownPortsBySecurityGroup UNION ALL SELECT PORT_ID AS port_id, TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, window_column, 'Host drive-59 (by VIF Type)' AS reason FROM Drive59PortsByVifType) SELECT port_id, tenant_id, mac_address, window_column, reason FROM CombinedPorts ORDER BY tenant_id NULLS FIRST, port_id NULLS FIRST) SELECT inner_cte.tenant_id, COUNT(DISTINCT inner_cte.port_id) AS total_ports_in_combined_set, CASE WHEN SUM(CASE WHEN inner_cte.reason = 'Host drive-59 (by VIF Type)' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_drive59_ports FROM inner_cte GROUP BY inner_cte.tenant_id ORDER BY total_ports_in_combined_set DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH DownPortsWithBindings AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.NAME AS port_name, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, b.HOST AS binding_host, b.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY p.STATUS ORDER BY p.ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), AdminUpPortsWithSecurity AS (SELECT p.NAME AS port_name, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, psb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED AS port_security_enabled, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1) SELECT dpwb.port_id, dpwb.port_name, dpwb.mac_address, dpwb.binding_host, dpwb.vif_type, dpwb.window_column AS down_status_rank, aupws.port_security_enabled, aupws.window_column AS admin_up_row_number FROM DownPortsWithBindings AS dpwb JOIN AdminUpPortsWithSecurity AS aupws ON dpwb.mac_address = aupws.mac_address WHERE aupws.port_security_enabled = 1 ORDER BY dpwb.port_id NULLS FIRST) SELECT binding_host, MAX(down_status_rank) AS max_down_status_rank FROM inner_cte GROUP BY binding_host
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH DownPortsBySecurityGroup AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), AdminUpPortsWithRowNumbers AS (SELECT p.NAME AS port_name, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, psb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED AS port_security_enabled, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1) SELECT dpsg.port_id, dpsg.tenant_id, dpsg.mac_address, dpsg.window_column AS down_ports_running_sum_standard_attr_id, aup.port_name, aup.port_security_enabled, aup.window_column AS admin_up_ports_row_number FROM DownPortsBySecurityGroup AS dpsg JOIN AdminUpPortsWithRowNumbers AS aup ON dpsg.mac_address = aup.mac_address WHERE dpsg.window_column > 0 ORDER BY dpsg.window_column DESC NULLS LAST, dpsg.mac_address NULLS FIRST), AverageRunningSum AS (SELECT AVG(down_ports_running_sum_standard_attr_id) AS avg_running_sum FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.mac_address, i.down_ports_running_sum_standard_attr_id, i.port_name, i.port_security_enabled, i.admin_up_ports_row_number, (i.down_ports_running_sum_standard_attr_id - a.avg_running_sum) AS deviation_from_avg_running_sum FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN AverageRunningSum AS a ORDER BY i.down_ports_running_sum_standard_attr_id DESC NULLS LAST, i.mac_address NULLS FIRST
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH administratively_up_ports AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1), port_status_binding_stats AS (SELECT p.status, MAX(ps.binding_count) - MIN(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_range, VAR_SAMP(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_variance, STDDEV(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_stddev FROM ports AS p JOIN (SELECT p2.id AS port_id, COUNT(*) AS binding_count FROM ports AS p2 JOIN portsecuritybindings AS b ON p2.id = b.port_id GROUP BY p2.id) AS ps ON p.id = ps.port_id GROUP BY p.status ORDER BY binding_count_range DESC NULLS LAST), up_port_counts_by_status AS (SELECT aup.STATUS, COUNT(*) AS administratively_up_port_count FROM administratively_up_ports AS aup GROUP BY aup.STATUS) SELECT psbs.status, ucs.administratively_up_port_count, psbs.binding_count_range, psbs.binding_count_variance, psbs.binding_count_stddev FROM port_status_binding_stats AS psbs JOIN up_port_counts_by_status AS ucs ON psbs.status = ucs.STATUS ORDER BY psbs.binding_count_range DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT status, administratively_up_port_count, binding_count_range, binding_count_variance, binding_count_stddev FROM inner_cte WHERE administratively_up_port_count > (SELECT AVG(administratively_up_port_count) FROM inner_cte) ORDER BY administratively_up_port_count DESC NULLS LAST
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH RbacSharedIpVersionStats AS (SELECT n.ACTION, AVG(s.IP_VERSION) AS avg_ip_version, VAR_SAMP(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_variance, STDDEV(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_stddev FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY n.ACTION ORDER BY ip_version_variance DESC NULLS LAST), RbacDhcpNonSharedStddev AS (SELECT n.ACTION, STDDEV(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_stddev FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.ENABLE_DHCP = 1 AND NOT s.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY n.ACTION HAVING COUNT(*) > 5 ORDER BY ip_version_stddev DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT rs.ACTION, rs.avg_ip_version, rs.ip_version_variance, rs.ip_version_stddev AS shared_ip_version_stddev, rd.ip_version_stddev AS dhcp_nonshared_ip_version_stddev FROM RbacSharedIpVersionStats AS rs JOIN RbacDhcpNonSharedStddev AS rd ON rs.ACTION = rd.ACTION) SELECT i.ACTION, i.avg_ip_version, i.ip_version_variance, i.shared_ip_version_stddev, i.dhcp_nonshared_ip_version_stddev, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT s2.ID) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS shared_ipv4_has_any_dhcp_enabled_subnet FROM inner_cte AS i LEFT JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS n2 ON n2.ACTION = i.ACTION AND n2.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' LEFT JOIN SUBNETS AS s2 ON s2.NETWORK_ID = n2.OBJECT_ID AND s2.IP_VERSION = 4 AND s2.ENABLE_DHCP = 1 GROUP BY i.ACTION, i.avg_ip_version, i.ip_version_variance, i.shared_ip_version_stddev, i.dhcp_nonshared_ip_version_stddev ORDER BY i.ip_version_variance DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH PortStatusOwnerSecurityGroupStats AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS min_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS spb ON p.ID = spb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), PortStatusBindingCountDispersion AS (SELECT p.status, MAX(ps.binding_count) - MIN(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_range, VAR_SAMP(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_variance, STDDEV(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_stddev FROM ports AS p JOIN (SELECT p2.id AS port_id, COUNT(*) AS binding_count FROM ports AS p2 JOIN portsecuritybindings AS b ON p2.id = b.port_id WHERE p2.tenant_id IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p2.id) AS ps ON p.id = ps.port_id GROUP BY p.status ORDER BY binding_count_range DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT psosgs.port_status, psosgs.device_owner, psosgs.port_count, psosgs.min_standard_attr_id, psbcd.binding_count_range, psbcd.binding_count_variance, psbcd.binding_count_stddev FROM PortStatusOwnerSecurityGroupStats AS psosgs JOIN PortStatusBindingCountDispersion AS psbcd ON psosgs.port_status = psbcd.status ORDER BY psosgs.port_count DESC NULLS LAST), OwnerPortSecurityGroupCounts AS (SELECT p.device_owner, p.id AS port_id, COUNT(DISTINCT spb.security_group_id) AS security_group_count FROM ports AS p JOIN securitygroupportbindings AS spb ON p.id = spb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN' AND p.tenant_id IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p.device_owner, p.id) SELECT i.port_status, i.device_owner, i.port_count, i.min_standard_attr_id, i.binding_count_range, i.binding_count_variance, i.binding_count_stddev, MAX(opsgc.security_group_count) AS max_security_groups_on_any_port_for_owner FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN OwnerPortSecurityGroupCounts AS opsgc ON i.device_owner = opsgc.device_owner GROUP BY i.port_status, i.device_owner, i.port_count, i.min_standard_attr_id, i.binding_count_range, i.binding_count_variance, i.binding_count_stddev ORDER BY i.port_count DESC NULLS LAST
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH SecurityGroupBoundPorts AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS min_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS spb ON p.ID = spb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), OvsBoundPorts AS (SELECT p.STATUS, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) AS min_admin_state_up, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS avg_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE b.VIF_TYPE = 'ovs' AND p.DEVICE_ID IN ('da811c75-0f9b-462c-b020-8ea55f6ca7c7') GROUP BY p.STATUS), CombinedPortStatusMetrics AS (SELECT sg.port_status AS status, sg.device_owner AS device_owner, sg.port_count AS port_count, sg.min_standard_attr_id AS min_standard_attr_id, NULL AS min_admin_state_up, NULL AS avg_standard_attr_id, 'Security Group Bound Ports' AS reason FROM SecurityGroupBoundPorts AS sg UNION ALL SELECT ov.STATUS AS status, NULL AS device_owner, NULL AS port_count, NULL AS min_standard_attr_id, ov.min_admin_state_up AS min_admin_state_up, ov.avg_standard_attr_id AS avg_standard_attr_id, 'OVS VIF Bound Ports' AS reason FROM OvsBoundPorts AS ov) SELECT * FROM CombinedPortStatusMetrics ORDER BY status NULLS FIRST, device_owner NULLS FIRST, reason NULLS FIRST) SELECT i.status, i.reason, i.device_owner, i.port_count, i.min_standard_attr_id, i.min_admin_state_up, i.avg_standard_attr_id, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT b.port_id) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_ovs_binding, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT spb.port_id) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_security_group_binding FROM inner_cte AS i LEFT JOIN ports AS p ON p.status = i.status AND (i.device_owner IS NULL OR p.device_owner = i.device_owner) LEFT JOIN ml2_port_bindings AS b ON p.id = b.port_id AND b.vif_type = 'ovs' LEFT JOIN securitygroupportbindings AS spb ON p.id = spb.port_id GROUP BY i.status, i.reason, i.device_owner, i.port_count, i.min_standard_attr_id, i.min_admin_state_up, i.avg_standard_attr_id ORDER BY i.status NULLS FIRST, i.device_owner NULLS FIRST, i.reason NULLS FIRST
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH NetworkStatusSecurityStats AS (SELECT n.STATUS AS network_status, VAR_SAMP(nsb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED) AS port_security_enabled_variance, STDDEV(nsb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED) AS port_security_enabled_stddev FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN NETWORKSECURITYBINDINGS AS nsb ON n.ID = nsb.NETWORK_ID WHERE NOT n.ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY n.STATUS ORDER BY port_security_enabled_variance DESC NULLS LAST), PortStatusOwnerStats AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' AND NOT p.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER) SELECT nsss.network_status, nsss.port_security_enabled_variance, nsss.port_security_enabled_stddev, pss.port_status, pss.device_owner, pss.port_count, pss.earliest_standard_attr_update FROM NetworkStatusSecurityStats AS nsss CROSS JOIN PortStatusOwnerStats AS pss) SELECT network_status, MAX(port_count) AS max_port_count_across_port_groups FROM inner_cte GROUP BY network_status
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH q1 AS (SELECT s.NAME, s.TENANT_ID, s.NETWORK_ID, s.IP_VERSION, s.CIDR, s.GATEWAY_IP, s.ENABLE_DHCP, p.FIRST_IP, p.LAST_IP, MAX(s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (ORDER BY s.ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_standard_attr_id_next2 FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN IPALLOCATIONPOOLS AS p ON s.ID = p.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND s.ID IN ('012993ae-5d73-4aad-834b-e87a31e99d48')), q2 AS (SELECT r.DESTINATION, r.NEXTHOP, s.NAME AS subnet_name, s.CIDR AS subnet_cidr, p.FIRST_IP, p.LAST_IP, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY r.SUBNET_ID ORDER BY r.SUBNET_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SUBNETROUTES AS r JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON r.SUBNET_ID = s.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONPOOLS AS p ON s.ID = p.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND s.ID IN ('012993ae-5d73-4aad-834b-e87a31e99d48')) SELECT q1.NAME AS subnet_name, q1.TENANT_ID, q1.NETWORK_ID, q1.IP_VERSION, q1.CIDR, q1.GATEWAY_IP, q1.ENABLE_DHCP, q1.FIRST_IP AS allocation_first_ip, q1.LAST_IP AS allocation_last_ip, q1.max_standard_attr_id_next2, q2.DESTINATION AS route_destination, q2.NEXTHOP AS route_nexthop, q2.window_column AS route_dense_rank_within_subnet FROM q1 JOIN q2 ON q1.NAME = q2.subnet_name AND q1.CIDR = q2.subnet_cidr AND q1.FIRST_IP = q2.FIRST_IP AND q1.LAST_IP = q2.LAST_IP ORDER BY q1.NAME NULLS FIRST, q2.window_column NULLS FIRST, q2.DESTINATION NULLS FIRST) SELECT CASE WHEN LAG(subnet_name) OVER (ORDER BY subnet_name NULLS FIRST, route_dense_rank_within_subnet NULLS FIRST, route_destination NULLS FIRST) = subnet_name THEN NULL ELSE subnet_name END AS subnet_name, tenant_id, network_id, ip_version, cidr, gateway_ip, enable_dhcp, allocation_first_ip, allocation_last_ip, max_standard_attr_id_next2, route_destination, route_nexthop, route_dense_rank_within_subnet FROM inner_cte ORDER BY subnet_name NULLS FIRST, route_dense_rank_within_subnet NULLS FIRST, route_destination NULLS FIRST
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH sq1 AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' AND NOT p.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), sq2 AS (SELECT p.STATUS, VAR_SAMP(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS standard_attr_id_variance FROM PORTS AS p JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE NOT p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID IS NULL AND ia.IP_ADDRESS IN ('10.150.64.113/8') GROUP BY p.STATUS ORDER BY standard_attr_id_variance DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT sq1.port_status, sq1.device_owner, sq1.port_count, sq1.earliest_standard_attr_update, sq2.standard_attr_id_variance FROM sq1 JOIN sq2 ON sq1.port_status = sq2.STATUS ORDER BY sq1.port_count DESC NULLS LAST, sq2.standard_attr_id_variance DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT port_status, device_owner, port_count, earliest_standard_attr_update, standard_attr_id_variance FROM inner_cte WHERE port_count > (SELECT AVG(port_count) FROM inner_cte)
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH NetworkStatusSecurityStats AS (SELECT n.STATUS AS network_status, VAR_SAMP(nsb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED) AS port_security_enabled_variance, STDDEV(nsb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED) AS port_security_enabled_stddev FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN NETWORKSECURITYBINDINGS AS nsb ON n.ID = nsb.NETWORK_ID WHERE NOT n.ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY n.STATUS ORDER BY port_security_enabled_variance DESC NULLS LAST), ActiveNetworkRbacDetails AS (SELECT n.name, n.status, n.admin_state_up, r.action, r.target_tenant, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY n.id ORDER BY n.id NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM networksecuritybindings AS b JOIN networks AS n ON b.network_id = n.id JOIN networkrbacs AS r ON n.id = r.object_id WHERE n.status = 'ACTIVE' AND NOT n.id IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b'))), ActiveNetworkCountsByStatus AS (SELECT status AS network_status, COUNT(DISTINCT name) AS active_network_count FROM ActiveNetworkRbacDetails GROUP BY status) SELECT nsss.network_status, anc.active_network_count, nsss.port_security_enabled_variance, nsss.port_security_enabled_stddev FROM NetworkStatusSecurityStats AS nsss JOIN ActiveNetworkCountsByStatus AS anc ON nsss.network_status = anc.network_status ORDER BY anc.active_network_count DESC NULLS LAST, nsss.port_security_enabled_variance DESC NULLS LAST), VarianceBenchmark AS (SELECT AVG(port_security_enabled_variance) AS avg_variance_benchmark FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.network_status, i.active_network_count, i.port_security_enabled_variance, i.port_security_enabled_stddev, vb.avg_variance_benchmark AS benchmark, CASE WHEN i.port_security_enabled_variance > vb.avg_variance_benchmark * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_VARIANCE' WHEN i.port_security_enabled_variance < vb.avg_variance_benchmark * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_VARIANCE' ELSE 'AVERAGE_VARIANCE' END AS variance_category FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN VarianceBenchmark AS vb ORDER BY i.active_network_count DESC NULLS LAST, i.port_security_enabled_variance DESC NULLS LAST

standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE' AND n.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), AdminUpTenantPorts AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, TenantPortCounts AS (SELECT ap.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, COUNT(DISTINCT ap.PORT_ID) AS admin_up_port_count FROM AdminUpTenantPorts AS ap GROUP BY ap.TENANT_ID) SELECT an.tenant_id, tpc.admin_up_port_count, COUNT(DISTINCT an.network_id) AS active_network_count, MAX(an.standard_attr_updated_at) AS latest_network_standard_attr_updated_at FROM ActiveTenantNetworks AS an JOIN TenantPortCounts AS tpc ON an.tenant_id = tpc.tenant_id WHERE tpc.admin_up_port_count >= 2 GROUP BY an.tenant_id, tpc.admin_up_port_count ORDER BY tpc.admin_up_port_count DESC NULLS LAST, active_network_count DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT i.tenant_id, i.admin_up_port_count, i.active_network_count, i.latest_network_standard_attr_updated_at, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT p_down.id) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_admin_up_down_ports FROM inner_cte AS i LEFT JOIN ports AS p_down ON p_down.tenant_id = i.tenant_id AND p_down.admin_state_up = 1 AND p_down.status = 'DOWN' GROUP BY i.tenant_id, i.admin_up_port_count, i.active_network_count, i.latest_network_standard_attr_updated_at ORDER BY i.admin_up_port_count DESC NULLS LAST, i.active_network_count DESC NULLS LAST

WITH inner_cte AS (WITH subq1 AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, sgpb.security_group_id, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.security_group_id ORDER BY p.id NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN' AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), subq2 AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT subq2.NETWORK_ID, subq2.VIF_TYPE, COUNT(DISTINCT subq2.PORT_ID) AS total_admin_up_ports, COUNT(DISTINCT subq1.port_id) AS total_down_ports_with_security_group_binding, AVG(subq2.window_column) AS avg_standard_attr_id_window_avg, MIN(subq1.window_column) AS best_rank_within_security_group_for_down_ports FROM subq2 LEFT JOIN subq1 ON subq2.PORT_ID = subq1.port_id AND subq2.TENANT_ID = subq1.tenant_id GROUP BY subq2.NETWORK_ID, subq2.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY total_admin_up_ports DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT network_id, vif_type, total_admin_up_ports, total_down_ports_with_security_group_binding, avg_standard_attr_id_window_avg, best_rank_within_security_group_for_down_ports FROM inner_cte WHERE total_admin_up_ports > (SELECT AVG(total_admin_up_ports) FROM inner_cte) ORDER BY total_admin_up_ports DESC NULLS LAST
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH PortStatusOwnerSecurityGroupStats AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS min_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS spb ON p.ID = spb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), PortStatusBindingCountDispersion AS (SELECT p.status, MAX(ps.binding_count) - MIN(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_range, VAR_SAMP(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_variance, STDDEV(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_stddev FROM ports AS p JOIN (SELECT p2.id AS port_id, COUNT(*) AS binding_count FROM ports AS p2 JOIN portsecuritybindings AS b ON p2.id = b.port_id WHERE p2.tenant_id IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p2.id) AS ps ON p.id = ps.port_id GROUP BY p.status ORDER BY binding_count_range DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT psosgs.port_status, psosgs.device_owner, psosgs.port_count, psosgs.min_standard_attr_id, psbcd.binding_count_range, psbcd.binding_count_variance, psbcd.binding_count_stddev FROM PortStatusOwnerSecurityGroupStats AS psosgs JOIN PortStatusBindingCountDispersion AS psbcd ON psosgs.port_status = psbcd.status ORDER BY psosgs.port_status NULLS FIRST, psosgs.device_owner NULLS FIRST) SELECT i.port_status, i.device_owner, i.port_count, i.min_standard_attr_id, i.binding_count_range, i.binding_count_variance, i.binding_count_stddev, COUNT(spb2.security_group_id) AS total_securitygroup_port_bindings, CAST((COUNT(spb2.security_group_id) * 1.0) AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(i.port_count, 0), 0) AS avg_securitygroup_bindings_per_port FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN ports AS p2 ON p2.status = i.port_status AND p2.device_owner = i.device_owner AND p2.tenant_id = '17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7' JOIN securitygroupportbindings AS spb2 ON spb2.port_id = p2.id WHERE p2.status = 'DOWN' GROUP BY i.port_status, i.device_owner, i.port_count, i.min_standard_attr_id, i.binding_count_range, i.binding_count_variance, i.binding_count_stddev ORDER BY i.port_status NULLS FIRST, i.device_owner NULLS FIRST

s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.NETWORK_ID IN ('0a1d0a27-cffa-4de3-92c5-9d3fd3f2e74d')), TenantUpPortsWithIPsOnNetwork AS (SELECT t.port_id, t.tenant_id, t.mac_address, t.mac_address = d.MAC_ADDRESS GROUP BY d.NETWORK_ID) SELECT network_id, down_port_name_count, down_port_security_group_count, tenant_up_port_count_with_ips, tenant_up_total_ip_allocations, psb ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON ia.port_id = p.id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND NOT ia.ip_address IS NULL AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY WITH inner_cte AS (WITH TenantAdminUpPorts AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), OvsDeviceStatusStats AS (SELECT p.STATUS, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) AS min_admin_state_up, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS avg_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE b.VIF_TYPE = 'ovs' AND p.DEVICE_ID IN ('da811c75-0f9b-462c-b020-8ea55f6ca7c7') GROUP BY p.STATUS) SELECT tap.STATUS, COUNT(DISTINCT tap.PORT_ID) AS ports_for_tenant_admin_up, AVG(tap.window_column) AS avg_window_standard_attr_id, ods.min_admin_state_up, ods.avg_standard_attr_id FROM TenantAdminUpPorts AS tap JOIN OvsDeviceStatusStats AS ods ON tap.STATUS = ods.STATUS GROUP BY tap.STATUS, ods.min_admin_state_up, ods.avg_standard_attr_id ORDER BY ports_for_tenant_admin_up DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT i.STATUS, i.ports_for_tenant_admin_up, i.avg_window_standard_attr_id, i.min_admin_state_up, i.avg_standard_attr_id FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN (SELECT p.STATUS, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS ovs_device_ports_for_status FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE b.VIF_TYPE = 'ovs' AND p.DEVICE_ID IN ('da811c75-0f9b-462c-b020-8ea55f6ca7c7') GROUP BY p.STATUS) AS d ON d.STATUS = i.STATUS WHERE d.ovs_device_ports_for_status > 0 AND (CAST(i.ports_for_tenant_admin_up * 1.0 AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(d.ovs_device_ports_for_status, 0)) > 0.75 ORDER BY i.ports_for_tenant_admin_up DESC NULLS LAST
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH Subquery1 AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), Subquery2 AS (SELECT p.STATUS, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) AS min_admin_state_up, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS avg_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE b.VIF_TYPE = 'ovs' AND p.DEVICE_ID IN ('da811c75-0f9b-462c-b020-8ea55f6ca7c7') GROUP BY p.STATUS) SELECT s1.PORT_ID, s1.HOST, s1.VIF_TYPE, s1.VNIC_TYPE, s1.TENANT_ID, s1.NETWORK_ID, s1.MAC_ADDRESS, s1.STATUS, s1.DEVICE_OWNER, s1.window_column, s2.min_admin_state_up, s2.avg_standard_attr_id FROM Subquery1 AS s1 JOIN Subquery2 AS s2 ON s1.STATUS = s2.STATUS) SELECT i.PORT_ID, i.HOST, i.VIF_TYPE, i.VNIC_TYPE, i.TENANT_ID, i.NETWORK_ID, i.MAC_ADDRESS, i.STATUS, i.DEVICE_OWNER, i.window_column, i.min_admin_state_up, i.avg_standard_attr_id, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT f.ID) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_floating_ip FROM inner_cte AS i LEFT JOIN FLOATINGIPS AS f ON i.PORT_ID = f.FIXED_PORT_ID GROUP BY i.PORT_ID, i.HOST, i.VIF_TYPE, i.VNIC_TYPE, i.TENANT_ID, i.NETWORK_ID, i.MAC_ADDRESS, i.STATUS, i.DEVICE_OWNER, i.window_column, i.min_admin_state_up, i.avg_standard_attr_id
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH sq1 AS (SELECT p.status, CAST(STDDEV(LENGTH(ia.ip_address)) AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(AVG(LENGTH(ia.ip_address)), 0), 0) AS coefficient_of_variation FROM portsecuritybindings AS psb JOIN ports AS p ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON p.id = ia.port_id WHERE psb.port_security_enabled = 1 AND ia.ip_address IN ('10.150.64.113/8') GROUP BY p.status HAVING AVG(LENGTH(ia.ip_address)) > 0 ORDER BY coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST), sq2 AS (SELECT p.STATUS, VAR_SAMP(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS standard_attr_id_variance FROM PORTS AS p JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE NOT p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID IS NULL AND ia.IP_ADDRESS IN ('10.150.64.113/8') GROUP BY p.STATUS ORDER BY standard_attr_id_variance DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT sq1.status, sq1.coefficient_of_variation, sq2.standard_attr_id_variance FROM sq1 JOIN sq2 ON sq1.status = sq2.status ORDER BY sq1.coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT MAX(coefficient_of_variation) AS max_coefficient_of_variation FROM inner_cte
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH PortStatusOwnerStats AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS min_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS spb ON p.ID = spb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), AdminUpPortWindowStats AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT psos.port_status, psos.device_owner, psos.port_count, psos.min_standard_attr_id, aupws.VIF_TYPE, COUNT(DISTINCT aupws.PORT_ID) AS admin_up_ports, AVG(aupws.window_column) AS avg_window_standard_attr_id FROM PortStatusOwnerStats AS psos JOIN AdminUpPortWindowStats AS aupws ON psos.port_status = aupws.STATUS AND psos.device_owner = aupws.DEVICE_OWNER GROUP BY psos.port_status, psos.device_owner, psos.port_count, psos.min_standard_attr_id, aupws.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY psos.port_count DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT port_status, device_owner, MAX(avg_window_standard_attr_id) AS max_avg_window_standard_attr_id_across_vif_types FROM inner_cte GROUP BY port_status, device_owner
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH Subq1 AS (SELECT n.ACTION, STDDEV(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_stddev FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.ENABLE_DHCP = 1 AND NOT s.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY n.ACTION HAVING COUNT(*) > 5 ORDER BY ip_version_stddev DESC NULLS LAST), Subq2 AS (SELECT n.name, n.status, n.admin_state_up, r.action, r.target_tenant, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY n.id ORDER BY n.id NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM networksecuritybindings AS b JOIN networks AS n ON b.network_id = n.id JOIN networkrbacs AS r ON n.id = r.object_id WHERE n.status = 'ACTIVE' AND NOT n.id IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b'))) SELECT s2.name, s2.status, s2.admin_state_up, s2.action, s2.target_tenant, s2.window_column, s1.ip_version_stddev FROM Subq2 AS s2 JOIN Subq1 AS s1 ON s2.action = s1.ACTION), PerNetworkSubnetCounts AS (SELECT r.action, s.network_id, COUNT(*) AS dhcp_subnet_count FROM networkrbacs AS r JOIN subnets AS s ON r.object_id = s.network_id WHERE s.enable_dhcp = 1 AND NOT s.network_id IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY r.action, s.network_id), MaxSubnetCountsByAction AS (SELECT action, MAX(dhcp_subnet_count) AS max_dhcp_subnet_count_per_network FROM PerNetworkSubnetCounts GROUP BY action) SELECT i.name, i.status, i.admin_state_up, i.action, i.target_tenant, i.window_column, i.ip_version_stddev, m.max_dhcp_subnet_count_per_network FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN MaxSubnetCountsByAction AS m ON i.action = m.action

WITH inner_cte AS (WITH PortStatusOwnerStats AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS min_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS spb ON p.ID = spb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), PortSecurityBindingDispersion AS (SELECT p.status, MAX(ps.binding_count) - MIN(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_range, VAR_SAMP(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_variance, STDDEV(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_stddev FROM ports AS p JOIN (SELECT p2.id AS port_id, COUNT(*) AS binding_count FROM ports AS p2 JOIN portsecuritybindings AS b ON p2.id = b.port_id WHERE p2.tenant_id IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p2.id) AS ps ON p.id = ps.port_id GROUP BY p.status ORDER BY binding_count_range DESC NULLS LAST), CombinedPortStats AS (SELECT pso.port_status AS status, pso.device_owner AS device_owner, pso.port_count AS port_count, pso.min_standard_attr_id AS min_standard_attr_id, NULL AS binding_count_range, NULL AS binding_count_variance, NULL AS binding_count_stddev, 'Security Group Bound Port Summary' AS reason FROM PortStatusOwnerStats AS pso UNION ALL SELECT psbd.status AS status, NULL AS device_owner, NULL AS port_count, NULL AS min_standard_attr_id, psbd.binding_count_range AS binding_count_range, psbd.binding_count_variance AS binding_count_variance, psbd.binding_count_stddev AS binding_count_stddev, 'Port Security Binding Dispersion' AS reason FROM PortSecurityBindingDispersion AS psbd) SELECT * FROM CombinedPortStats ORDER BY status NULLS FIRST, reason NULLS FIRST, device_owner NULLS FIRST) SELECT inner_cte.*, tenant_totals.total_ports_for_tenant, avg_ports.avg_port_count_across_tenants FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT p.tenant_id, COUNT(p.id) AS total_ports_for_tenant FROM ports AS p GROUP BY p.tenant_id) AS tenant_totals ON tenant_totals.tenant_id = '17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7' JOIN (SELECT AVG(port_count) AS avg_port_count_across_tenants FROM (SELECT COUNT(id) AS port_count FROM ports GROUP BY tenant_id) AS sub) AS avg_ports ON 1 = 1 ORDER BY inner_cte.status NULLS FIRST, inner_cte.reason NULLS FIRST, inner_cte.device_owner NULLS FIRST

WITH inner_cte AS (WITH RbacActionIpVersionStddev AS (SELECT n.ACTION, STDDEV(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_stddev FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.ENABLE_DHCP = 1 AND NOT s.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY n.ACTION HAVING COUNT(*) > 5 ORDER BY ip_version_stddev DESC NULLS LAST), ActiveHighMtuNetworksByRbacAction AS (SELECT n.name AS network_name, r.action AS rbac_action, MAX(n.mtu) AS max_mtu FROM NETWORKSECURITYBINDINGS AS nsb JOIN NETWORKS AS n ON nsb.network_id = n.id JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r ON n.id = r.object_id WHERE n.status = 'ACTIVE' AND r.action IN ('access_as_shared', 'access_as_external') GROUP BY n.name, r.action HAVING MAX(n.mtu) > 8000) SELECT ahmn.network_name, ahmn.rbac_action, ahmn.max_mtu, raivs.ip_version_stddev FROM ActiveHighMtuNetworksByRbacAction AS ahmn JOIN RbacActionIpVersionStddev AS raivs ON ahmn.rbac_action = raivs.ACTION ORDER BY ahmn.max_mtu DESC NULLS LAST, raivs.ip_version_stddev DESC NULLS LAST), AvgMaxMtu AS (SELECT AVG(max_mtu) AS avg_max_mtu FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.network_name, i.rbac_action, i.max_mtu, i.ip_version_stddev, (i.max_mtu - a.avg_max_mtu) AS deviation_from_avg_max_mtu FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN AvgMaxMtu AS a ORDER BY i.max_mtu DESC NULLS LAST, i.ip_version_stddev DESC NULLS LAST
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH SubnetWindowRanks AS (SELECT s.NAME, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY sa.UPDATED_AT NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND s.ID IN ('012993ae-5d73-4aad-834b-e87a31e99d48')), SubnetRouteStats AS (SELECT s.NAME AS subnet_name, MAX(route_count) - MIN(route_count) AS route_count_range, VAR_SAMP(route_count) AS route_count_variance, STDDEV(route_count) AS route_count_stddev FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN (SELECT sr.SUBNET_ID, COUNT(*) AS route_count FROM SUBNETROUTES AS sr GROUP BY sr.SUBNET_ID, sr.NEXTHOP) AS route_stats ON s.ID = route_stats.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND s.ID IN ('012993ae-5d73-4aad-834b-e87a31e99d48') GROUP BY s.NAME ORDER BY route_count_range DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT swr.NAME AS subnet_name, swr.window_column, srs.route_count_range, srs.route_count_variance, srs.route_count_stddev FROM SubnetWindowRanks AS swr JOIN SubnetRouteStats AS srs ON srs.subnet_name = swr.NAME ORDER BY swr.NAME NULLS FIRST, swr.window_column NULLS FIRST) SELECT i.subnet_name, i.window_column, i.route_count_range, i.route_count_variance, i.route_count_stddev, CASE WHEN COALESCE(sa.DESCRIPTION, '') <> '' THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_standardattr_description FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON s.NAME = i.subnet_name AND s.IP_VERSION = 4 JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID ORDER BY i.subnet_name NULLS FIRST, i.window_column NULLS FIRST

WITH inner_cte AS (WITH Subq1 AS (SELECT n.ACTION, STDDEV(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_stddev FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.ENABLE_DHCP = 1 AND NOT s.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY n.ACTION HAVING COUNT(*) > 5 ORDER BY ip_version_stddev DESC NULLS LAST), Subq2 AS (SELECT n.ACTION AS rbac_action, AVG(s.IP_VERSION) AS avg_ip_version, MAX(s.IP_VERSION) - MIN(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_range, VAR_SAMP(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_variance FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION IN (4, 6) AND n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY n.ACTION ORDER BY ip_version_range DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT s2.rbac_action, s2.avg_ip_version, s2.ip_version_range, s2.ip_version_variance, s1.ip_version_stddev FROM Subq2 AS s2 LEFT JOIN Subq1 AS s1 ON s1.ACTION = s2.rbac_action), TargetTenantCountsPerNetwork AS (SELECT n.action, n.object_id AS network_id, COUNT(DISTINCT n.target_tenant) AS distinct_target_tenants FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON s.network_id = n.object_id WHERE n.action = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY n.action, n.object_id), MaxTargetTenantsByAction AS (SELECT action, MAX(distinct_target_tenants) AS max_distinct_target_tenants_on_any_network FROM TargetTenantCountsPerNetwork GROUP BY action) SELECT i.rbac_action, i.avg_ip_version, i.ip_version_range, i.ip_version_variance, i.ip_version_stddev, m.max_distinct_target_tenants_on_any_network FROM inner_cte AS i LEFT JOIN MaxTargetTenantsByAction AS m ON m.action = i.rbac_action
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH PortStatusOwnerSecurityGroupStats AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS min_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS spb ON p.ID = spb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), PortStatusBindingCountDispersion AS (SELECT p.status, MAX(ps.binding_count) - MIN(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_range, VAR_SAMP(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_variance, STDDEV(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_stddev FROM ports AS p JOIN (SELECT p2.id AS port_id, COUNT(*) AS binding_count FROM ports AS p2 JOIN portsecuritybindings AS b ON p2.id = b.port_id WHERE p2.tenant_id IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p2.id) AS ps ON p.id = ps.port_id GROUP BY p.status ORDER BY binding_count_range DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT psosgs.port_status, psosgs.device_owner, psosgs.port_count, psosgs.min_standard_attr_id, psbcd.binding_count_range, psbcd.binding_count_variance, psbcd.binding_count_stddev FROM PortStatusOwnerSecurityGroupStats AS psosgs JOIN PortStatusBindingCountDispersion AS psbcd ON psosgs.port_status = psbcd.status ORDER BY psosgs.port_count DESC NULLS LAST), StatusAverages AS (SELECT port_status, AVG(port_count) AS avg_port_count FROM inner_cte GROUP BY port_status) SELECT i.port_status, i.device_owner, i.port_count, i.min_standard_attr_id, i.binding_count_range, i.binding_count_variance, i.binding_count_stddev FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN StatusAverages AS sa ON sa.port_status = i.port_status WHERE i.port_count > (SELECT avg_port_count FROM StatusAverages WHERE StatusAverages.port_status = i.port_status) ORDER BY i.port_count DESC NULLS LAST
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH PortStatusOwnerAgg AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS min_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS spb ON p.ID = spb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), DownNetworkPortDetails AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (ORDER BY p.ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.NETWORK_ID IN ('0a1d0a27-cffa-4de3-92c5-9d3fd3f2e74d')) SELECT dnpd.DEVICE_OWNER, dnpd.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, psa.port_count, psa.min_standard_attr_id, COUNT(*) AS matching_down_ports_in_network FROM DownNetworkPortDetails AS dnpd JOIN PORTS AS p ON p.MAC_ADDRESS = dnpd.MAC_ADDRESS AND p.NETWORK_ID = dnpd.NETWORK_ID AND p.DEVICE_OWNER = dnpd.DEVICE_OWNER AND p.STATUS = dnpd.STATUS JOIN PortStatusOwnerAgg AS psa ON psa.port_status = dnpd.STATUS AND psa.device_owner = dnpd.DEVICE_OWNER WHERE p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY dnpd.DEVICE_OWNER, dnpd.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, psa.port_count, psa.min_standard_attr_id ORDER BY matching_down_ports_in_network DESC NULLS LAST, dnpd.DEVICE_OWNER NULLS FIRST, dnpd.SECURITY_GROUP_ID NULLS FIRST) SELECT inner_cte.DEVICE_OWNER, inner_cte.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, inner_cte.port_count, inner_cte.min_standard_attr_id, inner_cte.matching_down_ports_in_network, avg_stats.avg_matching_down_ports_in_network FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT AVG(matching_down_ports_in_network) AS avg_matching_down_ports_in_network FROM inner_cte) AS avg_stats ON 1 = 1
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH PortBindingWindow AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), PortStatusVariance AS (SELECT p.STATUS, VAR_SAMP(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS standard_attr_id_variance FROM PORTS AS p JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE NOT p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID IS NULL AND ia.IP_ADDRESS IN ('10.150.64.113/8') GROUP BY p.STATUS ORDER BY standard_attr_id_variance DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT pbw.PORT_ID, pbw.HOST, pbw.VIF_TYPE, pbw.VNIC_TYPE, pbw.TENANT_ID, pbw.NETWORK_ID, pbw.MAC_ADDRESS, pbw.STATUS, pbw.DEVICE_OWNER, pbw.window_column, psv.standard_attr_id_variance FROM PortBindingWindow AS pbw JOIN PortStatusVariance AS psv ON pbw.STATUS = psv.STATUS ORDER BY pbw.VIF_TYPE NULLS FIRST, pbw.PORT_ID NULLS FIRST), NetworkAllocationCounts AS (SELECT ia.NETWORK_ID, COUNT(DISTINCT ia.IP_ADDRESS) AS allocated_ip_count FROM IPALLOCATIONS AS ia GROUP BY ia.NETWORK_ID) SELECT i.PORT_ID, i.HOST, i.VIF_TYPE, i.VNIC_TYPE, i.TENANT_ID, i.NETWORK_ID, i.MAC_ADDRESS, i.STATUS, i.DEVICE_OWNER, i.window_column, i.standard_attr_id_variance, nac.allocated_ip_count FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN NetworkAllocationCounts AS nac ON i.NETWORK_ID = nac.NETWORK_ID WHERE nac.allocated_ip_count > 1 ORDER BY nac.allocated_ip_count DESC NULLS LAST, i.VIF_TYPE NULLS FIRST, i.PORT_ID NULLS FIRST
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH port_sg_dns_summary AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.name AS port_name, COUNT(sgpb.security_group_id) AS security_group_binding_count, SUM(CASE WHEN pd.port_id IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END) AS dns_record_count FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id LEFT JOIN PORTDNSES AS pd ON p.id = pd.port_id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.name), port_binding_window_details AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT pbwd.PORT_ID, pbwd.TENANT_ID, psds.port_name, pbwd.HOST, pbwd.VIF_TYPE, pbwd.VNIC_TYPE, pbwd.NETWORK_ID, pbwd.MAC_ADDRESS, pbwd.STATUS, pbwd.DEVICE_OWNER, pbwd.window_column, psds.security_group_binding_count, psds.dns_record_count FROM port_binding_window_details AS pbwd JOIN port_sg_dns_summary AS psds ON psds.port_id = pbwd.PORT_ID AND psds.tenant_id = pbwd.TENANT_ID), avg_sg_bindings AS (SELECT AVG(security_group_binding_count) AS avg_security_group_binding_count FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.PORT_ID, i.TENANT_ID, i.port_name, i.HOST, i.VIF_TYPE, i.VNIC_TYPE, i.NETWORK_ID, i.MAC_ADDRESS, i.STATUS, i.DEVICE_OWNER, i.window_column, i.security_group_binding_count, i.dns_record_count, (i.security_group_binding_count - a.avg_security_group_binding_count) AS deviation_from_avg_security_group_binding_count FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_sg_bindings AS a
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH rbac_ip_version_stats_shared AS (SELECT n.ACTION, AVG(s.IP_VERSION) AS avg_ip_version, VAR_SAMP(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_variance, STDDEV(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_stddev FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY n.ACTION ORDER BY ip_version_variance DESC NULLS LAST), rbac_ip_version_stddev_dhcp_not_shared AS (SELECT n.ACTION, STDDEV(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_stddev FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.ENABLE_DHCP = 1 AND NOT s.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY n.ACTION HAVING COUNT(*) > 5 ORDER BY ip_version_stddev DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT s1.ACTION, s1.avg_ip_version, s1.ip_version_variance, s1.ip_version_stddev AS shared_ip_version_stddev, s2.ip_version_stddev AS dhcp_not_shared_ip_version_stddev FROM rbac_ip_version_stats_shared AS s1 JOIN rbac_ip_version_stddev_dhcp_not_shared AS s2 ON s1.ACTION = s2.ACTION) SELECT i.ACTION, i.avg_ip_version, i.ip_version_variance, i.shared_ip_version_stddev, i.dhcp_not_shared_ip_version_stddev, overall_avg.avg_of_avg_ip_version AS overall_shared_ipv4_avg_ip_version_across_actions FROM inner_cte AS i, (SELECT AVG(avg_ip_version) AS avg_of_avg_ip_version FROM inner_cte) AS overall_avg
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH PortStatusOwnerAgg AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' AND NOT p.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), DownPortsWithSG AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (ORDER BY p.ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.NETWORK_ID IN ('0a1d0a27-cffa-4de3-92c5-9d3fd3f2e74d')), DownPortsSGCounts AS (SELECT dp.STATUS AS port_status, dp.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(*) AS down_ports_in_network_with_sg FROM DownPortsWithSG AS dp GROUP BY dp.STATUS, dp.DEVICE_OWNER) SELECT psoa.port_status, psoa.device_owner, psoa.port_count, psoa.earliest_standard_attr_update, dpsc.down_ports_in_network_with_sg FROM PortStatusOwnerAgg AS psoa JOIN DownPortsSGCounts AS dpsc ON psoa.port_status = dpsc.port_status AND psoa.device_owner = dpsc.device_owner ORDER BY psoa.port_count DESC NULLS LAST), AvgPortCount AS (SELECT AVG(port_count) AS avg_port_count FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.port_status, i.device_owner, i.port_count, i.earliest_standard_attr_update, i.down_ports_in_network_with_sg, (i.port_count - a.avg_port_count) AS deviation_from_avg_port_count FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN AvgPortCount AS a ORDER BY i.port_count DESC NULLS LAST
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH subq1 AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, sgpb.security_group_id, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.security_group_id ORDER BY p.id NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN' AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), subq2 AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT subq2.NETWORK_ID, subq2.VIF_TYPE, COUNT(DISTINCT subq2.PORT_ID) AS total_admin_up_ports, COUNT(DISTINCT subq1.port_id) AS total_down_ports_with_security_group_binding, AVG(subq2.window_column) AS avg_standard_attr_id_window_avg, MIN(subq1.window_column) AS best_rank_within_security_group_for_down_ports FROM subq2 LEFT JOIN subq1 ON subq2.PORT_ID = subq1.port_id AND subq2.TENANT_ID = subq1.tenant_id GROUP BY subq2.NETWORK_ID, subq2.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY total_admin_up_ports DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT network_id, MAX(total_admin_up_ports) AS max_admin_up_ports_across_vif_types, AVG(avg_standard_attr_id_window_avg) AS avg_of_viftype_window_avgs, MIN(best_rank_within_security_group_for_down_ports) AS best_rank_among_down_ports_in_network FROM inner_cte GROUP BY network_id ORDER BY max_admin_up_ports_across_vif_types DESC NULLS LAST
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH port_security_binding_stats_by_status AS (SELECT p.status, MAX(ps.binding_count) - MIN(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_range, VAR_SAMP(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_variance, STDDEV(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_stddev FROM ports AS p JOIN (SELECT p2.id AS port_id, COUNT(*) AS binding_count FROM ports AS p2 JOIN portsecuritybindings AS b ON p2.id = b.port_id WHERE p2.tenant_id IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p2.id) AS ps ON p.id = ps.port_id GROUP BY p.status ORDER BY binding_count_range DESC NULLS LAST), down_ports_with_security_groups AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (ORDER BY p.ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.NETWORK_ID IN ('0a1d0a27-cffa-4de3-92c5-9d3fd3f2e74d')), down_ports_with_security_groups_tenant_filtered AS (SELECT dpsg.NAME, dpsg.NETWORK_ID, dpsg.MAC_ADDRESS, dpsg.ADMIN_STATE_UP, dpsg.STATUS, dpsg.DEVICE_OWNER, dpsg.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, dpsg.window_column FROM down_ports_with_security_groups AS dpsg JOIN ports AS p ON p.network_id = dpsg.NETWORK_ID AND p.mac_address = dpsg.MAC_ADDRESS WHERE p.tenant_id IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7')) SELECT psbs.status, psbs.binding_count_range, psbs.binding_count_variance, psbs.binding_count_stddev, COUNT(*) AS down_port_rows_for_tenant_and_network FROM port_security_binding_stats_by_status AS psbs CROSS JOIN down_ports_with_security_groups_tenant_filtered AS dptf GROUP BY psbs.status, psbs.binding_count_range, psbs.binding_count_variance, psbs.binding_count_stddev ORDER BY psbs.binding_count_range DESC NULLS LAST), DownPortSecurityGroups AS (SELECT p.network_id, p.id AS port_id, COUNT(DISTINCT spb.security_group_id) AS security_group_count FROM inner_cte AS ic JOIN ports AS p ON p.status = ic.status JOIN securitygroupportbindings AS spb ON p.id = spb.port_id WHERE p.tenant_id IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') AND p.status = 'DOWN' AND p.network_id IN ('0a1d0a27-cffa-4de3-92c5-9d3fd3f2e74d') GROUP BY p.network_id, p.id) SELECT ic.status, ic.binding_count_range, ic.binding_count_variance, ic.binding_count_stddev, ic.down_port_rows_for_tenant_and_network, MAX(dpsg.security_group_count) AS max_security_groups_on_any_down_port FROM inner_cte AS ic JOIN DownPortSecurityGroups AS dpsg ON dpsg.network_id IN ('0a1d0a27-cffa-4de3-92c5-9d3fd3f2e74d') GROUP BY ic.status, ic.binding_count_range, ic.binding_count_variance, ic.binding_count_stddev, ic.down_port_rows_for_tenant_and_network ORDER BY ic.binding_count_range DESC NULLS LAST
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH PortStatusOwnerAgg AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' AND NOT p.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), DownPortsWithSG AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (ORDER BY p.ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.NETWORK_ID IN ('0a1d0a27-cffa-4de3-92c5-9d3fd3f2e74d')), DownPortsSGCounts AS (SELECT dp.STATUS AS port_status, dp.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(*) AS down_ports_in_network_with_sg FROM DownPortsWithSG AS dp GROUP BY dp.STATUS, dp.DEVICE_OWNER) SELECT psoa.port_status, psoa.device_owner, psoa.port_count, psoa.earliest_standard_attr_update, dpsc.down_ports_in_network_with_sg FROM PortStatusOwnerAgg AS psoa JOIN DownPortsSGCounts AS dpsc ON psoa.port_status = dpsc.port_status AND psoa.device_owner = dpsc.device_owner ORDER BY psoa.port_count DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT inner_cte.port_status, inner_cte.device_owner, inner_cte.port_count, inner_cte.earliest_standard_attr_update, inner_cte.down_ports_in_network_with_sg, (CAST(inner_cte.down_ports_in_network_with_sg * 1.0 AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(inner_cte.port_count, 0), 0)) AS down_sg_ratio FROM inner_cte JOIN PORTS AS p2 ON p2.DEVICE_OWNER = inner_cte.device_owner AND p2.STATUS = inner_cte.port_status WHERE (CAST(inner_cte.down_ports_in_network_with_sg * 1.0 AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(inner_cte.port_count, 0), 0)) > 0.25 GROUP BY inner_cte.port_status, inner_cte.device_owner, inner_cte.port_count, inner_cte.earliest_standard_attr_update, inner_cte.down_ports_in_network_with_sg ORDER BY down_sg_ratio DESC NULLS LAST, inner_cte.port_count DESC NULLS LAST
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH q1 AS (SELECT n.ACTION, VAR_SAMP(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_variance FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' AND n.TARGET_TENANT = '*' GROUP BY n.ACTION ORDER BY ip_version_variance DESC NULLS LAST), q2 AS (SELECT n.ACTION AS rbac_action, AVG(s.IP_VERSION) AS avg_ip_version, MAX(s.IP_VERSION) - MIN(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_range, VAR_SAMP(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_variance FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION IN (4, 6) AND n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY n.ACTION ORDER BY ip_version_range DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT q1.ACTION AS rbac_action, q2.avg_ip_version, q2.ip_version_range, q1.ip_version_variance AS public_access_ip_version_variance, q2.ip_version_variance AS overall_ip_version_variance FROM q1 JOIN q2 ON q1.ACTION = q2.rbac_action ORDER BY q2.ip_version_range DESC NULLS LAST), tenant_stats AS (SELECT n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, COUNT(DISTINCT n.OBJECT_ID) AS distinct_networks_referenced, AVG(s.IP_VERSION) AS avg_ip_version, MAX(s.IP_VERSION) - MIN(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_range, VAR_SAMP(CASE WHEN n.TARGET_TENANT = '*' THEN s.IP_VERSION END) AS public_access_ip_version_variance, VAR_SAMP(s.IP_VERSION) AS overall_ip_version_variance FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' AND s.IP_VERSION IN (4, 6) GROUP BY n.TENANT_ID) SELECT t.tenant_id, t.distinct_networks_referenced, t.avg_ip_version, t.ip_version_range, t.public_access_ip_version_variance, t.overall_ip_version_variance, i.rbac_action AS action_name, i.public_access_ip_version_variance AS action_level_public_access_ip_version_variance, i.overall_ip_version_variance AS action_level_overall_ip_version_variance FROM tenant_stats AS t CROSS JOIN inner_cte AS i ORDER BY t.distinct_networks_referenced DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH ActiveTenantNetworks AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE' AND n.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), AdminUpTenantPorts AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT n.network_id, n.network_name, n.tenant_id, n.admin_state_up AS network_admin_state_up, n.mtu, n.standard_attr_updated_at AS network_standard_attr_updated_at, n.window_column AS network_row_number_within_tenant, p.PORT_ID AS port_id, p.HOST AS port_host, p.VIF_TYPE AS port_vif_type, p.VNIC_TYPE AS port_vnic_type, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS port_mac_address, p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS port_device_owner, p.window_column AS port_avg_standard_attr_id_window FROM ActiveTenantNetworks AS n JOIN AdminUpTenantPorts AS p ON p.NETWORK_ID = n.network_id AND p.TENANT_ID = n.tenant_id ORDER BY n.network_name NULLS FIRST, p.PORT_ID NULLS FIRST) SELECT i.tenant_id, COUNT(DISTINCT i.network_id) AS total_networks, COUNT(DISTINCT i.port_id) AS total_ports, CASE WHEN SUM(CASE WHEN i.port_vif_type = 'ovs' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_ovs_vif_type FROM inner_cte AS i GROUP BY i.tenant_id ORDER BY total_ports DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH sq1 AS (SELECT n.ACTION, AVG(s.IP_VERSION) AS avg_ip_version, VAR_SAMP(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_variance, STDDEV(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_stddev FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY n.ACTION ORDER BY ip_version_variance DESC NULLS LAST), sq2 AS (SELECT n.ACTION AS rbac_action, AVG(s.IP_VERSION) AS avg_ip_version, MAX(s.IP_VERSION) - MIN(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_range, VAR_SAMP(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_variance FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION IN (4, 6) AND n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY n.ACTION ORDER BY ip_version_range DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT sq2.rbac_action, sq1.avg_ip_version AS avg_ip_version_ipv4_only, sq1.ip_version_variance AS ip_version_variance_ipv4_only, sq1.ip_version_stddev AS ip_version_stddev_ipv4_only, sq2.avg_ip_version AS avg_ip_version_ipv4_or_ipv6, sq2.ip_version_range, sq2.ip_version_variance AS ip_version_variance_ipv4_or_ipv6 FROM sq2 JOIN sq1 ON sq2.rbac_action = sq1.ACTION ORDER BY sq2.ip_version_range DESC NULLS LAST), overall_avg AS (SELECT AVG(avg_ip_version_ipv4_or_ipv6) AS overall_avg_ip_version_ipv4_or_ipv6 FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.rbac_action, i.avg_ip_version_ipv4_only, i.ip_version_variance_ipv4_only, i.ip_version_stddev_ipv4_only, i.avg_ip_version_ipv4_or_ipv6, i.ip_version_range, i.ip_version_variance_ipv4_or_ipv6, (i.avg_ip_version_ipv4_or_ipv6 - oa.overall_avg_ip_version_ipv4_or_ipv6) AS deviation_from_overall_avg_ipv4_or_ipv6 FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN overall_avg AS oa ORDER BY i.ip_version_range DESC NULLS LAST
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH SecurityGroupAndDnsByPort AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.name AS port_name, COUNT(sgpb.security_group_id) AS security_group_binding_count, SUM(CASE WHEN pd.port_id IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END) AS dns_record_count FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id LEFT JOIN PORTDNSES AS pd ON p.id = pd.port_id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.name), IpAllocationsByPort AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(ia.ip_address) AS ip_allocation_count, SUM(CASE WHEN NOT ia.subnet_id IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS subnet_id_rows FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON ia.port_id = p.id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND NOT ia.ip_address IS NULL AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address) SELECT sgd.port_id, sgd.tenant_id, sgd.port_name, ipa.mac_address, sgd.security_group_binding_count, sgd.dns_record_count, ipa.ip_allocation_count, ipa.subnet_id_rows FROM SecurityGroupAndDnsByPort AS sgd JOIN IpAllocationsByPort AS ipa ON ipa.port_id = sgd.port_id AND ipa.tenant_id = sgd.tenant_id) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.port_name, i.mac_address, i.security_group_binding_count, i.dns_record_count, i.ip_allocation_count, i.subnet_id_rows, avg_ip.avg_ip_allocations_per_port FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN (SELECT tenant_id, AVG(ip_allocation_count) AS avg_ip_allocations_per_port FROM inner_cte GROUP BY tenant_id) AS avg_ip ON avg_ip.tenant_id = i.tenant_id
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH RbacActionIpVersionStddev AS (SELECT n.ACTION, STDDEV(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_stddev FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.ENABLE_DHCP = 1 AND NOT s.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY n.ACTION HAVING COUNT(*) > 5 ORDER BY ip_version_stddev DESC NULLS LAST), ActiveNetworkRbacDetails AS (SELECT n.name,
