b.network_id = n.id JOIN networkrbacs AS r ON n.id = r.object_id WHERE n.status = 'ACTIVE' AND NOT n.id IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b'))) SELECT anrd.name, anrd.status, anrd.admin_state_up, anrd.action, anrd.target_tenant, anrd.window_column, rais.ip_version_stddev FROM ActiveNetworkRbacDetails AS anrd JOIN RbacActionIpVersionStddev AS rais ON anrd.action = rais.action ORDER BY rais.ip_version_stddev DESC NULLS LAST, anrd.name NULLS FIRST), NetworkDhcpSubnetCounts AS (SELECT n.id AS network_id, COUNT(DISTINCT s.id) AS dhcp_subnet_count FROM networks AS n JOIN subnets AS s ON s.network_id = n.id WHERE s.enable_dhcp = 1 AND NOT n.id IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY n.id), AvgDhcpSubnetCount AS (SELECT AVG(dhcp_subnet_count * 1.0) AS avg_dhcp_subnet_count FROM NetworkDhcpSubnetCounts) SELECT i.name, i.status, i.admin_state_up, i.action, i.target_tenant, i.window_column, i.ip_version_stddev, ndsc.dhcp_subnet_count FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN networks AS n ON n.name = i.name AND n.status = i.status AND n.admin_state_up = i.admin_state_up JOIN NetworkDhcpSubnetCounts AS ndsc ON ndsc.network_id = n.id CROSS JOIN AvgDhcpSubnetCount AS adc WHERE ndsc.dhcp_subnet_count > adc.avg_dhcp_subnet_count ORDER BY i.ip_version_stddev DESC NULLS LAST, ndsc.dhcp_subnet_count DESC NULLS LAST, i.name NULLS FIRST
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH active_networks AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE' AND n.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), admin_up_ports_with_ip_allocs AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(ia.ip_address) AS ia.port_id = p.id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND NOT ia.ip_address IS NULL AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address), tenant_port_ip_summary AS p.tenant_id) SELECT an.tenant_id, COUNT(an.network_id) AS active_network_count, MAX(an.standard_attr_updated_at) AS latest_network_standard_attr_updated_at, tps.admin_up_port_count, tps.total_ip_allocations, tps.total_subnet_id_rows FROM active_networks AS an JOIN tenant_port_ip_summary AS tps ON tps.tenant_id = an.tenant_id WHERE an.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY an.tenant_id, tps.admin_up_port_count, tps.total_ip_allocations, tps.total_subnet_id_rows) SELECT tenant_id, active_network_count, 0), 0)) AS subnet_id_row_utilization_pct FROM inner_cte WHERE total_ip_allocations > 0 AND (CAST(total_subnet_id_rows * 1.0 AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(total_ip_allocations, 0)) > 0.75
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH PortCountsAndEarliestUpdate AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' AND NOT p.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), PortStatusIpLengthVariation AS (SELECT p.status, CAST(STDDEV(LENGTH(ia.ip_address)) AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(AVG(LENGTH(ia.ip_address)), 0), 0) AS coefficient_of_variation FROM portsecuritybindings AS psb JOIN ports AS p ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON p.id = ia.port_id WHERE psb.port_security_enabled = 1 AND ia.ip_address IN ('10.150.64.113/8') GROUP BY p.status HAVING AVG(LENGTH(ia.ip_address)) > 0 ORDER BY coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST), CombinedPortStatusMetrics AS (SELECT pc.port_status AS port_status, pc.device_owner AS device_owner, pc.port_count AS port_count, pc.earliest_standard_attr_update AS earliest_standard_attr_update, pv.coefficient_of_variation AS coefficient_of_variation FROM PortCountsAndEarliestUpdate AS pc JOIN PortStatusIpLengthVariation AS pv ON pv.status = pc.port_status) SELECT port_status, device_owner, port_count, earliest_standard_attr_update, coefficient_of_variation FROM CombinedPortStatusMetrics ORDER BY port_status NULLS FIRST, device_owner NULLS FIRST) SELECT port_status, MAX(coefficient_of_variation) AS max_coefficient_of_variation_across_device_owners FROM inner_cte GROUP BY port_status ORDER BY port_status NULLS FIRST

NULLIF(COUNT(*), 0)) > 0.25) SELECT i.VIF_TYPE, i.admin_up_port_count, i.down_port_count, i.down_share_of_admin_up_ports, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT sgpb.port_id) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_any_security_group_binding_among_admin_up_ports FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON b.VIF_TYPE = i.VIF_TYPE JOIN PORTS AS p ON p.id = b.port_id AND p.tenant_id = '98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57' AND p.admin_state_up = 1 LEFT JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb ON sgpb.port_id = p.id GROUP BY i.VIF_TYPE, i.admin_up_port_count, i.down_port_count, i.down_share_of_admin_up_ports ORDER BY i.down_share_of_admin_up_ports DESC NULLS LAST
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH port_security_binding_stats_by_status AS (SELECT p.status, MAX(ps.binding_count) - MIN(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_range, VAR_SAMP(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_variance, STDDEV(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_stddev FROM ports AS p JOIN (SELECT p2.id AS port_id, COUNT(*) AS binding_count FROM ports AS p2 JOIN portsecuritybindings AS b ON p2.id = b.port_id WHERE p2.tenant_id IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p2.id) AS ps ON p.id = ps.port_id GROUP BY p.status ORDER BY binding_count_range DESC NULLS LAST), admin_up_port_windowed_standard_attr AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT psbs.status, psbs.binding_count_range, psbs.binding_count_variance, psbs.binding_count_stddev, COUNT(aw.PORT_ID) AS admin_up_ports_count, AVG(aw.window_column) AS avg_window_column FROM port_security_binding_stats_by_status AS psbs CROSS JOIN admin_up_port_windowed_standard_attr AS aw GROUP BY psbs.status, psbs.binding_count_range, psbs.binding_count_variance, psbs.binding_count_stddev) SELECT i.status, i.binding_count_range, i.binding_count_variance, i.binding_count_stddev, i.admin_up_ports_count, i.avg_window_column, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT mb.PORT_ID) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_any_ovs_binding FROM inner_cte AS i LEFT JOIN PORTS AS p_admin ON p_admin.TENANT_ID = '98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57' AND p_admin.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 LEFT JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS mb ON mb.PORT_ID = p_admin.ID AND mb.VIF_TYPE = 'ovs' GROUP BY i.status, i.binding_count_range, i.binding_count_variance, i.binding_count_stddev, i.admin_up_ports_count, i.avg_window_column
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH TenantAdminUpBoundPorts AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.name AS port_name, COUNT(sgpb.security_group_id) AS security_group_binding_count, SUM(CASE WHEN pd.port_id IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END) AS dns_record_count FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id LEFT JOIN PORTDNSES AS pd ON p.id = pd.port_id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.name), DownNetworkPortsWithWindow AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (ORDER BY p.ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.NETWORK_ID IN ('0a1d0a27-cffa-4de3-92c5-9d3fd3f2e74d')), DownNetworkPortsWithWindowAndId AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, q.NAME, q.NETWORK_ID, q.MAC_ADDRESS, q.ADMIN_STATE_UP, q.STATUS, q.DEVICE_OWNER, q.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, q.window_column FROM DownNetworkPortsWithWindow AS q JOIN PORTS AS p ON p.NAME = q.NAME AND p.NETWORK_ID = q.NETWORK_ID AND p.MAC_ADDRESS = q.MAC_ADDRESS AND p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = q.ADMIN_STATE_UP AND p.STATUS = q.STATUS AND p.DEVICE_OWNER = q.DEVICE_OWNER) SELECT d.port_id, t.tenant_id, t.port_name, d.network_id, d.mac_address, d.admin_state_up, d.status, d.device_owner, d.security_group_id, d.window_column, t.security_group_binding_count, t.dns_record_count FROM DownNetworkPortsWithWindowAndId AS d JOIN TenantAdminUpBoundPorts AS t ON d.port_id = t.port_id ORDER BY d.network_id NULLS FIRST, d.port_id NULLS FIRST, d.security_group_id NULLS FIRST) SELECT network_id, MAX(security_group_binding_count) AS max_security_group_bindings_on_any_port FROM inner_cte GROUP BY network_id
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH PortStatusIPLenCV AS (SELECT p.status, CAST(STDDEV(LENGTH(ia.ip_address)) AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(AVG(LENGTH(ia.ip_address)), 0), 0) AS coefficient_of_variation FROM portsecuritybindings AS psb JOIN ports AS p ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON p.id = ia.port_id WHERE psb.port_security_enabled = 1 AND ia.ip_address IN ('10.150.64.113/8') GROUP BY p.status HAVING AVG(LENGTH(ia.ip_address)) > 0 ORDER BY coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST), PortStatusBindingDispersion AS (SELECT p.status, MAX(ps.binding_count) - MIN(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_range, VAR_SAMP(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_variance, STDDEV(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_stddev FROM ports AS p JOIN (SELECT p2.id AS port_id, COUNT(*) AS binding_count FROM ports AS p2 JOIN portsecuritybindings AS b ON p2.id = b.port_id WHERE p2.tenant_id IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p2.id) AS ps ON p.id = ps.port_id GROUP BY p.status ORDER BY binding_count_range DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT pilcv.status, pilcv.coefficient_of_variation, pbd.binding_count_range, pbd.binding_count_variance, pbd.binding_count_stddev FROM PortStatusIPLenCV AS pilcv JOIN PortStatusBindingDispersion AS pbd ON pilcv.status = pbd.status ORDER BY pilcv.coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT i.status, i.coefficient_of_variation, i.binding_count_range, i.binding_count_variance, i.binding_count_stddev, sp.status_port_count, avg_sp.avg_status_port_count FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN (SELECT p.status, COUNT(DISTINCT p.id) AS status_port_count FROM ports AS p JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON ia.port_id = p.id JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON psb.port_id = p.id WHERE psb.port_security_enabled = 1 AND ia.ip_address IN ('10.150.64.113/8') AND p.tenant_id IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p.status) AS sp ON sp.status = i.status, (SELECT AVG(status_port_count) AS avg_status_port_count FROM (SELECT p2.status, COUNT(DISTINCT p2.id) AS status_port_count FROM ports AS p2 JOIN ipallocations AS ia2 ON ia2.port_id = p2.id JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb2 ON psb2.port_id = p2.id WHERE psb2.port_security_enabled = 1 AND ia2.ip_address IN ('10.150.64.113/8') AND p2.tenant_id IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p2.status) AS sub) AS avg_sp ORDER BY i.coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH SubnetPoolAndMaxAttrNext2 AS (SELECT s.NAME, s.TENANT_ID, s.NETWORK_ID, s.IP_VERSION, s.CIDR, s.GATEWAY_IP, s.ENABLE_DHCP, p.FIRST_IP, p.LAST_IP, MAX(s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (ORDER BY s.ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_standard_attr_id_next2 FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN IPALLOCATIONPOOLS AS p ON s.ID = p.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND s.ID IN ('012993ae-5d73-4aad-834b-e87a31e99d48')), SubnetAttrDenseRankWindow AS (SELECT s.NAME, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY sa.UPDATED_AT NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND s.ID IN ('012993ae-5d73-4aad-834b-e87a31e99d48')) SELECT sp.NAME AS subnet_name, sp.TENANT_ID, sp.NETWORK_ID, sp.IP_VERSION, sp.CIDR, sp.GATEWAY_IP, sp.ENABLE_DHCP, sp.FIRST_IP, sp.LAST_IP, sp.max_standard_attr_id_next2, sadr.window_column AS standard_attr_dense_rank_window FROM SubnetPoolAndMaxAttrNext2 AS sp JOIN SubnetAttrDenseRankWindow AS sadr ON sp.NAME = sadr.NAME) SELECT i.subnet_name, i.tenant_id, i.network_id, i.ip_version, i.cidr, i.gateway_ip, i.enable_dhcp, i.first_ip, i.last_ip, i.max_standard_attr_id_next2, i.standard_attr_dense_rank_window, tenant_subnets.total_ipv4_subnets, avg_subnets.avg_ipv4_subnets_per_tenant FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN (SELECT tenant_id, COUNT(id) AS total_ipv4_subnets FROM subnets WHERE ip_version = 4 GROUP BY tenant_id) AS tenant_subnets ON tenant_subnets.tenant_id = i.tenant_id JOIN (SELECT AVG(subnet_count) AS avg_ipv4_subnets_per_tenant FROM (SELECT COUNT(id) AS subnet_count FROM subnets WHERE ip_version = 4 GROUP BY tenant_id) AS per_tenant) AS avg_subnets ON 1 = 1

WITH inner_cte AS (WITH PortStatusOwnerCounts AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' AND NOT p.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), PortStatusIpLenCV AS (SELECT p.status, CAST(STDDEV(LENGTH(ia.ip_address)) AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(AVG(LENGTH(ia.ip_address)), 0), 0) AS coefficient_of_variation FROM portsecuritybindings AS psb JOIN ports AS p ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON p.id = ia.port_id WHERE psb.port_security_enabled = 1 AND ia.ip_address IN ('10.150.64.113/8') GROUP BY p.status HAVING AVG(LENGTH(ia.ip_address)) > 0 ORDER BY coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT psoc.port_status, psoc.device_owner, psoc.port_count, psoc.earliest_standard_attr_update, psicv.coefficient_of_variation FROM PortStatusOwnerCounts AS psoc JOIN PortStatusIpLenCV AS psicv ON psoc.port_status = psicv.status WHERE psoc.port_count > 0 ORDER BY psicv.coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST, psoc.port_count DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT port_status, device_owner, port_count, earliest_standard_attr_update, coefficient_of_variation FROM inner_cte WHERE port_count > (SELECT AVG(port_count) FROM inner_cte) ORDER BY coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST, port_count DESC NULLS LAST
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH RbacSharedIpVersionStats AS (SELECT n.ACTION, AVG(s.IP_VERSION) AS avg_ip_version, VAR_SAMP(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_variance, STDDEV(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_stddev FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY n.ACTION ORDER BY ip_version_variance DESC NULLS LAST), RbacDhcpNonSharedStddev AS (SELECT n.ACTION, STDDEV(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_stddev FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.ENABLE_DHCP = 1 AND NOT s.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY n.ACTION HAVING COUNT(*) > 5 ORDER BY ip_version_stddev DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT rs.ACTION, rs.avg_ip_version, rs.ip_version_variance, rs.ip_version_stddev AS shared_ip_version_stddev, rd.ip_version_stddev AS dhcp_nonshared_ip_version_stddev FROM RbacSharedIpVersionStats AS rs JOIN RbacDhcpNonSharedStddev AS rd ON rs.ACTION = rd.ACTION), SharedStddevBenchmark AS (SELECT AVG(shared_ip_version_stddev) AS avg_shared_stddev FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.ACTION, i.avg_ip_version, i.ip_version_variance, i.shared_ip_version_stddev, i.dhcp_nonshared_ip_version_stddev, b.avg_shared_stddev AS benchmark_shared_stddev, CASE WHEN i.shared_ip_version_stddev > b.avg_shared_stddev * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_VOLATILITY' WHEN i.shared_ip_version_stddev < b.avg_shared_stddev * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_VOLATILITY' ELSE 'AVERAGE_VOLATILITY' END AS volatility_category FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN SharedStddevBenchmark AS b

psos.earliest_standard_attr_update FROM ActiveNetworkRbacSummary AS anrs CROSS JOIN PortStatusOwnerStats AS psos ORDER BY anrs.name NULLS FIRST, anrs.action NULLS FIRST, anrs.target_tenant NULLS FIRST, psos.port_status NULLS FIRST, psos.device_owner NULLS FIRST), AvgPortCount AS (SELECT AVG(port_count) AS avg_port_count FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.network_name, i.network_status, a.avg_port_count * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_USER' WHEN i.port_count < a.avg_port_count * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_USER' ELSE 'AVERAGE_USER' END AS user_category FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN AvgPortCount AS a

WITH inner_cte AS (WITH NetworkStatusSecurityStats AS (SELECT n.STATUS AS network_status, VAR_SAMP(nsb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED) AS port_security_enabled_variance, STDDEV(nsb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED) AS port_security_enabled_stddev FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN NETWORKSECURITYBINDINGS AS nsb ON n.ID = nsb.NETWORK_ID WHERE NOT n.ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY n.STATUS ORDER BY port_security_enabled_variance DESC NULLS LAST), ActiveNetworkRbacDetails AS (SELECT n.name, n.status, n.admin_state_up, r.action, r.target_tenant, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY n.id ORDER BY n.id NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM networksecuritybindings AS b JOIN networks AS n ON b.network_id = n.id JOIN networkrbacs AS r ON n.id = r.object_id WHERE n.status = 'ACTIVE' AND NOT n.id IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b'))), ActiveNetworkCountsByStatus AS (SELECT status AS network_status, COUNT(DISTINCT name) AS active_network_count FROM ActiveNetworkRbacDetails GROUP BY status) SELECT nsss.network_status, anc.active_network_count, nsss.port_security_enabled_variance, nsss.port_security_enabled_stddev FROM NetworkStatusSecurityStats AS nsss JOIN ActiveNetworkCountsByStatus AS anc ON nsss.network_status = anc.network_status ORDER BY anc.active_network_count DESC NULLS LAST, nsss.port_security_enabled_variance DESC NULLS LAST), avg_active AS (SELECT AVG(active_network_count) AS avg_active_network_count FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.network_status, i.active_network_count, i.port_security_enabled_variance, i.port_security_enabled_stddev, (i.active_network_count - a.avg_active_network_count) AS deviation_from_avg_active_network_count FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_active AS a ORDER BY i.active_network_count DESC NULLS LAST, i.port_security_enabled_variance DESC NULLS LAST
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH sq1 AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' AND NOT p.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), sq2 AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (ORDER BY p.ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.NETWORK_ID IN ('0a1d0a27-cffa-4de3-92c5-9d3fd3f2e74d')) SELECT sq2.NETWORK_ID, sq2.NAME AS port_name, sq2.MAC_ADDRESS, sq2.ADMIN_STATE_UP, sq2.STATUS AS port_status, sq2.DEVICE_OWNER, sq2.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, sq2.window_column AS min_admin_state_up_window, sq1.port_count, sq1.earliest_standard_attr_update FROM sq2 JOIN sq1 ON sq1.port_status = sq2.STATUS AND sq1.device_owner = sq2.DEVICE_OWNER ORDER BY sq1.port_count DESC NULLS LAST, sq2.NAME NULLS FIRST), AvgCounts AS (SELECT AVG(port_count) AS avg_port_count FROM (SELECT port_status, device_owner, MAX(port_count) AS port_count FROM inner_cte GROUP BY port_status, device_owner) AS grouped_counts) SELECT inner_cte.NETWORK_ID, inner_cte.port_name, inner_cte.MAC_ADDRESS, inner_cte.ADMIN_STATE_UP, inner_cte.port_status, inner_cte.DEVICE_OWNER, inner_cte.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, inner_cte.min_admin_state_up_window, inner_cte.port_count, inner_cte.earliest_standard_attr_update FROM inner_cte JOIN AvgCounts ON 1 = 1 WHERE inner_cte.port_count > (SELECT avg_port_count FROM AvgCounts) ORDER BY inner_cte.port_count DESC NULLS LAST, inner_cte.port_name NULLS FIRST
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH rbac_ip_version_stats_shared AS (SELECT n.ACTION, AVG(s.IP_VERSION) AS avg_ip_version, VAR_SAMP(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_variance, STDDEV(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_stddev FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY n.ACTION ORDER BY ip_version_variance DESC NULLS LAST), rbac_ip_version_stddev_dhcp_not_shared AS (SELECT n.ACTION, STDDEV(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_stddev FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.ENABLE_DHCP = 1 AND NOT s.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY n.ACTION HAVING COUNT(*) > 5 ORDER BY ip_version_stddev DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT s1.ACTION, s1.avg_ip_version, s1.ip_version_variance, s1.ip_version_stddev AS shared_ip_version_stddev, s2.ip_version_stddev AS dhcp_not_shared_ip_version_stddev FROM rbac_ip_version_stats_shared AS s1 JOIN rbac_ip_version_stddev_dhcp_not_shared AS s2 ON s1.ACTION = s2.ACTION) SELECT i.ACTION, i.avg_ip_version, i.ip_version_variance, i.shared_ip_version_stddev, i.dhcp_not_shared_ip_version_stddev, (CAST(SUM(CASE WHEN s_all.ENABLE_DHCP = 1 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) * 1.0 AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(COUNT(*), 0), 0)) AS dhcp_enabled_subnet_pct FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS n_all ON n_all.ACTION = i.ACTION JOIN SUBNETS AS s_all ON s_all.NETWORK_ID = n_all.OBJECT_ID GROUP BY i.ACTION, i.avg_ip_version, i.ip_version_variance, i.shared_ip_version_stddev, i.dhcp_not_shared_ip_version_stddev
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH ActiveTenantNetworks AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE' AND n.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), AdminUpTenantPorts AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), NetworkPortRollup AS (SELECT atn.tenant_id, atn.network_id, atn.network_name, atn.admin_state_up, atn.mtu, atn.standard_attr_updated_at, atn.window_column AS network_row_number_within_tenant, COUNT(DISTINCT autp.PORT_ID) AS total_admin_up_ports, COUNT(DISTINCT autp.VIF_TYPE) AS distinct_vif_types, AVG(autp.window_column) AS avg_port_window_avg_standard_attr_id FROM ActiveTenantNetworks AS atn LEFT JOIN AdminUpTenantPorts AS autp ON autp.NETWORK_ID = atn.network_id AND autp.TENANT_ID = atn.tenant_id GROUP BY atn.tenant_id, atn.network_id, atn.network_name, atn.admin_state_up, atn.mtu, atn.standard_attr_updated_at, atn.window_column) SELECT tenant_id, network_id, network_name, admin_state_up, mtu, standard_attr_updated_at, network_row_number_within_tenant, total_admin_up_ports, distinct_vif_types, avg_port_window_avg_standard_attr_id FROM NetworkPortRollup ORDER BY total_admin_up_ports DESC NULLS LAST, network_name ASC NULLS FIRST), TenantActiveAvg AS (SELECT tenant_id, AVG(total_admin_up_ports) AS avg_admin_up_ports_per_active_network FROM inner_cte GROUP BY tenant_id) SELECT i.tenant_id, i.network_id, i.network_name, i.admin_state_up, i.mtu, i.standard_attr_updated_at, i.network_row_number_within_tenant, i.total_admin_up_ports, i.distinct_vif_types, i.avg_port_window_avg_standard_attr_id FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN TenantActiveAvg AS taa ON taa.tenant_id = i.tenant_id WHERE i.total_admin_up_ports > taa.avg_admin_up_ports_per_active_network ORDER BY i.total_admin_up_ports DESC NULLS LAST, i.network_name ASC NULLS FIRST
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH sq1 AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' AND NOT p.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), sq2 AS (SELECT p.STATUS, VAR_SAMP(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS standard_attr_id_variance FROM PORTS AS p JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE NOT p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID IS NULL AND ia.IP_ADDRESS IN ('10.150.64.113/8') GROUP BY p.STATUS ORDER BY standard_attr_id_variance DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT sq1.port_status, sq1.device_owner, sq1.port_count, sq1.earliest_standard_attr_update, sq2.standard_attr_id_variance FROM sq1 JOIN sq2 ON sq1.port_status = sq2.STATUS ORDER BY sq1.port_count DESC NULLS LAST, sq2.standard_attr_id_variance DESC NULLS LAST), StatusAverages AS (SELECT port_status, AVG(port_count) AS avg_port_count FROM inner_cte GROUP BY port_status) SELECT i.port_status, i.device_owner, i.port_count, i.earliest_standard_attr_update, i.standard_attr_id_variance FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN StatusAverages AS sa ON sa.port_status = i.port_status WHERE i.port_count > sa.avg_port_count ORDER BY i.port_status NULLS FIRST, i.port_count DESC NULLS LAST, i.standard_attr_id_variance DESC NULLS LAST
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH Subq1 AS (SELECT n.ACTION, AVG(s.IP_VERSION) AS avg_ip_version, VAR_SAMP(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_variance, STDDEV(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_stddev FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY n.ACTION ORDER BY ip_version_variance DESC NULLS LAST), Subq2 AS (SELECT n.name, n.status, n.admin_state_up, r.action, r.target_tenant, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY n.id ORDER BY n.id NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM networksecuritybindings AS b JOIN networks AS n ON b.network_id = n.id JOIN networkrbacs AS r ON n.id = r.object_id WHERE n.status = 'ACTIVE' AND NOT n.id IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b'))) SELECT s2.name, s2.status, s2.admin_state_up, s2.action, s2.target_tenant, s2.window_column, s1.avg_ip_version, s1.ip_version_variance, s1.ip_version_stddev FROM Subq2 AS s2 CROSS JOIN Subq1 AS s1 WHERE s1.action = 'access_as_shared'), SubnetCounts AS (SELECT n.id AS network_id, COUNT(s.id) AS total_subnets, SUM(CASE WHEN s.ip_version = 4 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS ipv4_subnets FROM networks AS n JOIN subnets AS s ON s.network_id = n.id GROUP BY n.id) SELECT i.name, i.status, i.admin_state_up, i.action, i.target_tenant, i.window_column, i.avg_ip_version, i.ip_version_variance, i.ip_version_stddev, sc.total_subnets, sc.ipv4_subnets FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN networks AS n ON n.name = i.name JOIN SubnetCounts AS sc ON sc.network_id = n.id WHERE sc.total_subnets > 0 AND (CAST(sc.ipv4_subnets * 1.0 AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(sc.total_subnets, 0)) > 0.75
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH PortStatusOwnerAgg AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' AND NOT p.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), TenantAdminUpPortWindow AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT t.PORT_ID, t.HOST, t.VIF_TYPE, t.VNIC_TYPE, t.TENANT_ID, t.NETWORK_ID, t.MAC_ADDRESS, t.STATUS, t.DEVICE_OWNER, t.window_column, a.port_count, a.earliest_standard_attr_update FROM TenantAdminUpPortWindow AS t JOIN PortStatusOwnerAgg AS a ON a.port_status = t.STATUS AND a.device_owner = t.DEVICE_OWNER ORDER BY t.NETWORK_ID NULLS FIRST, t.PORT_ID NULLS FIRST) SELECT inner_cte.PORT_ID, inner_cte.HOST, inner_cte.VIF_TYPE, inner_cte.VNIC_TYPE, inner_cte.TENANT_ID, inner_cte.NETWORK_ID, inner_cte.MAC_ADDRESS, inner_cte.STATUS, inner_cte.DEVICE_OWNER, inner_cte.window_column, inner_cte.port_count, inner_cte.earliest_standard_attr_update FROM inner_cte JOIN PORTS AS p2 ON p2.ID = inner_cte.PORT_ID WHERE inner_cte.port_count > 10 ORDER BY inner_cte.NETWORK_ID NULLS FIRST, inner_cte.PORT_ID NULLS FIRST
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH DownPortsWithWindow AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (ORDER BY p.ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.NETWORK_ID IN ('0a1d0a27-cffa-4de3-92c5-9d3fd3f2e74d')), SecurityGroupGeomMean AS (SELECT s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, EXP(AVG(LN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID))) AS geometric_mean_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID > 0 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY geometric_mean_standard_attr_id DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT dpw.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, COUNT(*) AS down_port_count_in_network, AVG(dpw.window_column) AS avg_window_min_admin_state_up, MAX(sgg.geometric_mean_standard_attr_id) AS geometric_mean_standard_attr_id_for_tenant FROM DownPortsWithWindow AS dpw LEFT JOIN SecurityGroupGeomMean AS sgg ON dpw.SECURITY_GROUP_ID = sgg.SECURITY_GROUP_ID GROUP BY dpw.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY down_port_count_in_network DESC NULLS LAST), tenant_down_presence AS (SELECT s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS tenant_down_ports FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.NETWORK_ID IN ('0a1d0a27-cffa-4de3-92c5-9d3fd3f2e74d') AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID) SELECT i.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, i.down_port_count_in_network, i.avg_window_min_admin_state_up, i.geometric_mean_standard_attr_id_for_tenant, CASE WHEN COALESCE(tdp.tenant_down_ports, 0) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_any_down_ports_in_tenant FROM inner_cte AS i LEFT JOIN tenant_down_presence AS tdp ON i.SECURITY_GROUP_ID = tdp.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY i.down_port_count_in_network DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH PortIpAllocStats AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(ia.ip_address) AS ip_allocation_count, SUM(CASE WHEN NOT ia.subnet_id IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS subnet_id_rows FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON ia.port_id = p.id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND NOT ia.ip_address IS NULL AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address), PortBindingWindowStats AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT pbws.PORT_ID AS port_id, pbws.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, pbws.NETWORK_ID AS network_id, pbws.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, pbws.HOST AS host, pbws.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, pbws.VNIC_TYPE AS vnic_type, pbws.STATUS AS status, pbws.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, pbws.window_column AS avg_standard_attr_id_window, pias.ip_allocation_count AS ip_allocation_count, pias.subnet_id_rows AS subnet_id_rows FROM PortBindingWindowStats AS pbws JOIN PortIpAllocStats AS pias ON pias.port_id = pbws.PORT_ID ORDER BY pbws.VIF_TYPE NULLS FIRST, pbws.PORT_ID NULLS FIRST), VifTypeAverages AS (SELECT vif_type, AVG(ip_allocation_count) AS avg_ip_allocations FROM inner_cte GROUP BY vif_type) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.network_id, i.mac_address, i.host, i.vif_type, i.vnic_type, i.status, i.device_owner, i.avg_standard_attr_id_window, i.ip_allocation_count, i.subnet_id_rows FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN VifTypeAverages AS vta ON vta.vif_type = i.vif_type WHERE i.ip_allocation_count > vta.avg_ip_allocations ORDER BY i.vif_type NULLS FIRST, i.port_id NULLS FIRST
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH TenantDownPorts AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), NetworkDownPorts AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (ORDER BY p.ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.NETWORK_ID IN ('0a1d0a27-cffa-4de3-92c5-9d3fd3f2e74d')), CombinedDownPorts AS (SELECT t.port_id AS port_identifier, t.tenant_id AS scope_id, t.mac_address, t.window_column AS window_metric, 'Tenant Filter' AS reason FROM TenantDownPorts AS t UNION ALL SELECT n.NAME AS port_identifier, n.NETWORK_ID AS scope_id, n.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, n.window_column AS window_metric, 'Network Filter' AS reason FROM NetworkDownPorts AS n) SELECT * FROM CombinedDownPorts ORDER BY reason NULLS FIRST, scope_id NULLS FIRST, port_identifier NULLS FIRST), ReasonCounts AS (SELECT reason, COUNT(*) AS total_ports FROM inner_cte GROUP BY reason) SELECT reason, total_ports FROM ReasonCounts WHERE total_ports > (SELECT AVG(total_ports) FROM ReasonCounts) ORDER BY total_ports DESC NULLS LAST, reason NULLS FIRST
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH sq1 AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS min_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS spb ON p.ID = spb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), sq2 AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT sq2.VIF_TYPE, sq2.DEVICE_OWNER, sq1.port_status, sq1.port_count, sq1.min_standard_attr_id, COUNT(DISTINCT sq2.PORT_ID) AS admin_up_port_count, AVG(sq2.window_column) AS avg_window_standard_attr_id FROM sq2 JOIN sq1 ON sq2.DEVICE_OWNER = sq1.device_owner GROUP BY sq2.VIF_TYPE, sq2.DEVICE_OWNER, sq1.port_status, sq1.port_count, sq1.min_standard_attr_id ORDER BY admin_up_port_count DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT VIF_TYPE, MAX(admin_up_port_count) AS max_admin_up_port_count FROM inner_cte GROUP BY VIF_TYPE
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH NetworkStatusSecurityStats AS (SELECT n.STATUS AS network_status, VAR_SAMP(nsb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED) AS port_security_enabled_variance, STDDEV(nsb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED) AS port_security_enabled_stddev FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN NETWORKSECURITYBINDINGS AS nsb ON n.ID = nsb.NETWORK_ID WHERE NOT n.ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY n.STATUS ORDER BY port_security_enabled_variance DESC NULLS LAST), ActiveNetworkRbacDetails AS (SELECT n.name, n.status, n.admin_state_up, r.action, r.target_tenant, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY n.id ORDER BY n.id NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM networksecuritybindings AS b JOIN networks AS n ON b.network_id = n.id JOIN networkrbacs AS r ON n.id = r.object_id WHERE n.status = 'ACTIVE' AND NOT n.id IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b'))), = anc.network_status ORDER BY anc.active_network_count DESC NULLS LAST, nsss.port_security_enabled_variance DESC NULLS LAST), tenant_active_networks AS (SELECT n.tenant_id, COUNT(DISTINCT n.id) AS tan.total_active_networks, CASE WHEN trp.total_rbac_rows > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_any_network_rbac, COUNT(DISTINCT ic.network_status) AS statuses_covered_in_inner_cte FROM tenant_active_networks AS tan LEFT JOIN tenant_rbac_presence AS trp ON trp.tenant_id = tan.tenant_id LEFT JOIN networks AS n ON n.tenant_id = tan.tenant_id LEFT JOIN inner_cte AS ic ON ic.network_status = n.status GROUP BY tan.tenant_id, tan.total_active_networks, trp.total_rbac_rows ORDER BY tan.total_active_networks DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH SubnetPoolAndAttrStats AS (SELECT s.NAME, s.TENANT_ID, s.NETWORK_ID, s.IP_VERSION, s.CIDR, s.GATEWAY_IP, s.ENABLE_DHCP, p.FIRST_IP, p.LAST_IP, MAX(s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (ORDER BY s.ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_standard_attr_id_next2 FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN IPALLOCATIONPOOLS AS p ON s.ID = p.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND s.ID IN ('012993ae-5d73-4aad-834b-e87a31e99d48')), SubnetRouteCountDispersion AS (SELECT s.NAME AS subnet_name, MAX(route_count) - MIN(route_count) AS route_count_range, VAR_SAMP(route_count) AS route_count_variance, STDDEV(route_count) AS route_count_stddev FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN (SELECT sr.SUBNET_ID, COUNT(*) AS route_count FROM SUBNETROUTES AS sr GROUP BY sr.SUBNET_ID, sr.NEXTHOP) AS route_stats ON s.ID = route_stats.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND s.ID IN ('012993ae-5d73-4aad-834b-e87a31e99d48') GROUP BY s.NAME ORDER BY route_count_range DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT spa.NAME AS subnet_name, spa.TENANT_ID, spa.NETWORK_ID, spa.IP_VERSION, spa.CIDR, spa.GATEWAY_IP, spa.ENABLE_DHCP, spa.FIRST_IP AS allocation_pool_first_ip, spa.LAST_IP AS allocation_pool_last_ip, spa.max_standard_attr_id_next2, srcd.route_count_range, srcd.route_count_variance, srcd.route_count_stddev FROM SubnetPoolAndAttrStats AS spa JOIN SubnetRouteCountDispersion AS srcd ON spa.NAME = srcd.subnet_name ORDER BY spa.NAME NULLS FIRST) SELECT i.subnet_name, i.TENANT_ID, i.NETWORK_ID, i.IP_VERSION, i.CIDR, i.GATEWAY_IP, i.ENABLE_DHCP, i.allocation_pool_first_ip, i.allocation_pool_last_ip, i.max_standard_attr_id_next2, i.route_count_range, i.route_count_variance, i.route_count_stddev, avg_stats.avg_route_count_range FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN (SELECT AVG(route_count_range) AS avg_route_count_range FROM (SELECT route_count_range FROM inner_cte) AS sub) AS avg_stats ON 1 = 1

WITH inner_cte AS (WITH PortSecurityAndDNSStats AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.name AS port_name, COUNT(sgpb.security_group_id) AS security_group_binding_count, SUM(CASE WHEN pd.port_id IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END) AS dns_record_count FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id LEFT JOIN PORTDNSES AS pd ON p.id = pd.port_id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.name), PortBindingWindowStats AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT pbws.PORT_ID AS port_id, pbws.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, psds.port_name, pbws.HOST, pbws.VIF_TYPE, pbws.VNIC_TYPE, pbws.NETWORK_ID, pbws.MAC_ADDRESS, pbws.STATUS, pbws.DEVICE_OWNER, pbws.window_column, psds.security_group_binding_count, psds.dns_record_count FROM PortBindingWindowStats AS pbws JOIN PortSecurityAndDNSStats AS psds ON pbws.PORT_ID = psds.port_id ORDER BY pbws.PORT_ID NULLS FIRST) SELECT port_id, tenant_id, port_name, host, vif_type, vnic_type, network_id, mac_address, status, device_owner, window_column, security_group_binding_count, dns_record_count FROM inner_cte WHERE security_group_binding_count > (SELECT AVG(security_group_binding_count) FROM inner_cte) ORDER BY port_id NULLS FIRST

WITH inner_cte AS (WITH TenantDownPorts AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), NetworkDownPorts AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (ORDER BY p.ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.NETWORK_ID IN ('0a1d0a27-cffa-4de3-92c5-9d3fd3f2e74d')), CombinedDownPorts AS (SELECT t.port_id AS port_identifier, t.tenant_id AS scope_id, t.mac_address, t.window_column AS window_metric, 'Tenant Filter' AS reason FROM TenantDownPorts AS t UNION ALL SELECT n.NAME AS port_identifier, n.NETWORK_ID AS scope_id, n.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, n.window_column AS window_metric, 'Network Filter' AS reason FROM NetworkDownPorts AS n) SELECT * FROM CombinedDownPorts ORDER BY reason NULLS FIRST, scope_id NULLS FIRST, port_identifier NULLS FIRST), AverageWindowMetric AS (SELECT AVG(window_metric) AS avg_window_metric FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.port_identifier, i.scope_id, i.mac_address, i.window_metric, i.reason, (i.window_metric - a.avg_window_metric) AS deviation_from_avg_window_metric FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN AverageWindowMetric AS a ORDER BY i.reason NULLS FIRST, i.scope_id NULLS FIRST, i.port_identifier NULLS FIRST

WITH AdminUpPorts AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1), DownPortsBySecurityGroup AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, sgpb.security_group_id, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.security_group_id ORDER BY p.id NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN') SELECT aup.TENANT_ID, AVG(aup.window_column) AS Avg_AdminUp_StdAttr_Window, COUNT(DISTINCT dpsg.security_group_id) AS Down_Security_Group_Count, CASE WHEN COUNT(dpsg.port_id) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS Has_Down_Ports FROM AdminUpPorts AS aup LEFT JOIN DownPortsBySecurityGroup AS dpsg ON dpsg.tenant_id = aup.TENANT_ID WHERE aup.STATUS = 'DOWN' GROUP BY aup.TENANT_ID HAVING AVG(aup.window_column) > 100 ORDER BY Avg_AdminUp_StdAttr_Window DESC NULLS LAST
WITH HaprxoyLBCountsByOpStatus AS (SELECT lb.OPERATING_STATUS, COUNT(*) AS total_loadbalancers FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb JOIN PROVIDERRESOURCEASSOCIATIONS AS pra ON lb.ID = pra.RESOURCE_ID WHERE pra.PROVIDER_NAME = 'haproxy' GROUP BY lb.OPERATING_STATUS HAVING COUNT(*) > 1), ActiveLBBytesInByOpStatus AS (SELECT lb.OPERATING_STATUS, SUM(s.BYTES_IN) AS total_bytes_in FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCER_STATISTICS AS s ON lb.ID = s.LOADBALANCER_ID WHERE lb.PROVISIONING_STATUS = 'ACTIVE' GROUP BY lb.OPERATING_STATUS HAVING SUM(s.BYTES_IN) > 1000000) SELECT h.OPERATING_STATUS, h.total_loadbalancers, a.total_bytes_in FROM HaprxoyLBCountsByOpStatus AS h JOIN ActiveLBBytesInByOpStatus AS a ON h.OPERATING_STATUS = a.OPERATING_STATUS ORDER BY a.total_bytes_in DESC NULLS LAST
WITH DownPortBindingIPs AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, b.HOST AS host, b.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ia.IP_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), DownPortMacAttrSums AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(sa.ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sa.RESOURCE_TYPE ORDER BY sa.ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT d1.port_id, d1.tenant_id, d1.host, d1.vif_type, d1.ip_address, d1.window_column AS ip_row_number_window, d2.mac_address, d2.window_column AS standard_attr_running_sum_window FROM DownPortBindingIPs AS d1 JOIN DownPortMacAttrSums AS d2 ON d1.port_id = d2.port_id ORDER BY d1.tenant_id NULLS FIRST, d1.port_id NULLS FIRST, d1.ip_address NULLS FIRST
WITH DownPortsBySecurityGroup AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), Drive59PortsByVifType AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, p.TENANT_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE b.HOST = 'drive-59'), CombinedPorts AS (SELECT port_id, tenant_id, mac_address, window_column, 'DOWN (by Security Group)' AS reason FROM DownPortsBySecurityGroup UNION ALL SELECT PORT_ID AS port_id, TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, window_column, 'Host drive-59 (by VIF Type)' AS reason FROM Drive59PortsByVifType) SELECT port_id, tenant_id, mac_address, window_column, reason FROM CombinedPorts ORDER BY tenant_id NULLS FIRST, port_id NULLS FIRST
WITH DownPortsWindow AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, AVG(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST), HostStandardAttrVariance AS (SELECT b.HOST, VAR_SAMP(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS standard_attr_id_variance FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND NOT ia.PORT_ID IS NULL GROUP BY b.HOST ORDER BY standard_attr_id_variance DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT dpw.VIF_TYPE, COUNT(*) AS down_ports_count, AVG(dpw.window_column) AS avg_window_admin_state_up, AVG(hsav.standard_attr_id_variance) AS avg_standard_attr_id_variance_across_hosts FROM DownPortsWindow AS dpw JOIN HostStandardAttrVariance AS hsav ON dpw.HOST = hsav.HOST GROUP BY dpw.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY down_ports_count DESC NULLS LAST
WITH ActiveStatusBytes AS (SELECT lb.OPERATING_STATUS, SUM(s.BYTES_IN) AS total_bytes_in FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCER_STATISTICS AS s ON lb.ID = s.LOADBALANCER_ID WHERE lb.PROVISIONING_STATUS = 'ACTIVE' GROUP BY lb.OPERATING_STATUS HAVING SUM(s.BYTES_IN) > 1000000), HaproxyAdminUpLoadBalancers AS (SELECT lb.name AS loadbalancer_name, MAX(lb.admin_state_up) AS max_admin_state_up, lb.operating_status FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb JOIN PROVIDERRESOURCEASSOCIATIONS AS pra ON lb.id = pra.resource_id WHERE pra.provider_name = 'haproxy' GROUP BY lb.name, lb.operating_status HAVING MAX(lb.admin_state_up) = 1) SELECT h.loadbalancer_name, h.operating_status, a.total_bytes_in FROM HaproxyAdminUpLoadBalancers AS h JOIN ActiveStatusBytes AS a ON h.operating_status = a.OPERATING_STATUS ORDER BY h.operating_status NULLS FIRST, h.loadbalancer_name NULLS FIRST
WITH administratively_up_ports AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1), port_status_binding_stats AS (SELECT p.status, MAX(ps.binding_count) - MIN(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_range, VAR_SAMP(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_variance, STDDEV(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_stddev FROM ports AS p JOIN (SELECT p2.id AS port_id, COUNT(*) AS binding_count FROM ports AS p2 JOIN portsecuritybindings AS b ON p2.id = b.port_id GROUP BY p2.id) AS ps ON p.id = ps.port_id GROUP BY p.status ORDER BY binding_count_range DESC NULLS LAST), up_port_counts_by_status AS (SELECT aup.STATUS, COUNT(*) AS administratively_up_port_count FROM administratively_up_ports AS aup GROUP BY aup.STATUS) SELECT psbs.status, ucs.administratively_up_port_count, psbs.binding_count_range, psbs.binding_count_variance, psbs.binding_count_stddev FROM port_status_binding_stats AS psbs JOIN up_port_counts_by_status AS ucs ON psbs.status = ucs.STATUS ORDER BY psbs.binding_count_range DESC NULLS LAST
WITH PortDownMacWindow AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, AVG(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST), PortDownIpRowNumber AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, b.HOST AS host, b.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ia.IP_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT p2.vif_type, COUNT(DISTINCT p2.port_id) AS Total_Down_Ports, AVG(p1.window_column) AS Avg_AdminStateUp_Window_Across_Ports, MAX(p2.window_column) AS Max_Ip_RowNumber_Window, COUNT(DISTINCT p2.tenant_id) AS Distinct_Tenants FROM PortDownIpRowNumber AS p2 JOIN PortDownMacWindow AS p1 ON p1.HOST = p2.host AND p1.VIF_TYPE = p2.vif_type GROUP BY p2.vif_type ORDER BY Total_Down_Ports DESC NULLS LAST
WITH NetworkSecurityStats AS (SELECT n.STATUS AS network_status, VAR_SAMP(nsb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED) AS port_security_enabled_variance, STDDEV(nsb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED) AS port_security_enabled_stddev FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN NETWORKSECURITYBINDINGS AS nsb ON n.ID = nsb.NETWORK_ID GROUP BY n.STATUS ORDER BY port_security_enabled_variance DESC NULLS LAST), PortOwnerStats AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), NetworkPortCountsByStatus AS (SELECT n.STATUS AS network_status, COUNT(p.ID) AS total_ports_in_status FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN PORTS AS p ON p.NETWORK_ID = n.ID JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' GROUP BY n.STATUS) SELECT nss.network_status, nss.port_security_enabled_variance, nss.port_security_enabled_stddev, npc.total_ports_in_status, pos.port_status, pos.device_owner, pos.port_count, pos.earliest_standard_attr_update FROM NetworkSecurityStats AS nss JOIN NetworkPortCountsByStatus AS npc ON nss.network_status = npc.network_status JOIN PortOwnerStats AS pos ON pos.port_status = nss.network_status ORDER BY npc.total_ports_in_status DESC NULLS LAST, nss.port_security_enabled_variance DESC NULLS LAST, pos.port_count DESC NULLS LAST
WITH route_metrics AS (SELECT s.NAME AS subnet_name, MAX(route_count) - MIN(route_count) AS route_count_range, VAR_SAMP(route_count) AS route_count_variance, STDDEV(route_count) AS route_count_stddev FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN (SELECT sr.SUBNET_ID, COUNT(*) AS route_count FROM SUBNETROUTES AS sr GROUP BY sr.SUBNET_ID, sr.NEXTHOP) AS route_stats ON s.ID = route_stats.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 GROUP BY s.NAME ORDER BY route_count_range DESC NULLS LAST), shared_rbac_max_ip AS (SELECT s.name, MAX(s.ip_version) AS max_ip_version, s.gateway_ip FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.object_id = s.network_id WHERE n.action = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY s.name, s.gateway_ip HAVING MAX(s.ip_version) > 3) SELECT rm.subnet_name, srm.gateway_ip, srm.max_ip_version, rm.route_count_range, rm.route_count_variance, rm.route_count_stddev FROM route_metrics AS rm JOIN shared_rbac_max_ip AS srm ON rm.subnet_name = srm.name ORDER BY rm.route_count_range DESC NULLS LAST
WITH PortStatusOwnerStats AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), PortStatusVariation AS (SELECT p.status, CAST(STDDEV(p.standard_attr_id) AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(AVG(p.standard_attr_id), 0), 0) AS coefficient_of_variation FROM securitygroupportbindings AS sgpb JOIN ports AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id GROUP BY p.status HAVING AVG(p.standard_attr_id) > 0 ORDER BY coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST), CombinedPortStatusMetrics AS (SELECT psos.port_status AS port_status, psos.device_owner AS device_owner, psos.port_count AS port_count, psos.earliest_standard_attr_update AS earliest_standard_attr_update, psv.coefficient_of_variation AS coefficient_of_variation FROM PortStatusOwnerStats AS psos JOIN PortStatusVariation AS psv ON psos.port_status = psv.status) SELECT port_status, device_owner, port_count, earliest_standard_attr_update, coefficient_of_variation FROM CombinedPortStatusMetrics ORDER BY port_status NULLS FIRST, device_owner NULLS FIRST
WITH PortSecurityWindow AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY psb.port_security_enabled NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN'), PortBindingRank AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.NAME AS port_name, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, b.HOST AS binding_host, b.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY p.STATUS ORDER BY p.ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT pbr.port_id, pbr.port_name, pbr.mac_address, pbr.binding_host, pbr.vif_type, pbr.window_column AS rank_within_down_status_by_port_id, psw.window_column AS dense_rank_by_port_security_with_2_preceding_frame FROM PortBindingRank AS pbr JOIN PortSecurityWindow AS psw ON pbr.port_id = psw.port_id ORDER BY pbr.port_id NULLS FIRST
WITH SubnetPoolStats AS (SELECT s.name AS subnet_name, MAX(pool_count) - MIN(pool_count) AS pool_count_range, VAR_SAMP(pool_count) AS pool_count_variance, STDDEV(pool_count) AS pool_count_stddev FROM subnets AS s JOIN dnsnameservers AS d ON d.subnet_id = s.id JOIN (SELECT s2.id AS subnet_id, COUNT(*) AS pool_count FROM subnets AS s2 JOIN ipallocationpools AS p ON p.subnet_id = s2.id GROUP BY s2.id, p.id) AS pool_stats ON pool_stats.subnet_id = s.id GROUP BY s.name ORDER BY pool_count_range DESC NULLS LAST), ActiveNetworks AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE') SELECT an.tenant_id, an.network_id, an.network_name, an.admin_state_up, an.mtu, an.standard_attr_updated_at, an.window_column, sps.subnet_name, sps.pool_count_range, sps.pool_count_variance, sps.pool_count_stddev FROM ActiveNetworks AS an JOIN subnets AS s ON s.network_id = an.network_id JOIN SubnetPoolStats AS sps ON sps.subnet_name = s.name WHERE an.window_column = 1 ORDER BY sps.pool_count_range DESC NULLS LAST, an.tenant_id NULLS FIRST, an.network_name NULLS FIRST
WITH ActiveNetworks AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE'), AdminUpPorts AS (SELECT p.NAME AS port_name, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, psb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED AS port_security_enabled, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1), TenantPortStats AS (SELECT p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, COUNT(*) AS admin_up_port_count, COUNT(DISTINCT p.MAC_ADDRESS) AS unique_mac_count FROM PORTS AS p WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 GROUP BY p.TENANT_ID) SELECT an.tenant_id, COUNT(DISTINCT an.network_id) AS active_networks, MAX(an.standard_attr_updated_at) AS latest_network_standard_attr_updated_at, tps.admin_up_port_count, tps.unique_mac_count, MIN(aup.mac_address) AS min_admin_up_port_mac_address, MAX(aup.mac_address) AS max_admin_up_port_mac_address FROM ActiveNetworks AS an LEFT JOIN TenantPortStats AS tps ON tps.tenant_id = an.tenant_id LEFT JOIN AdminUpPorts AS aup ON 1 = 1 GROUP BY an.tenant_id, tps.admin_up_port_count, tps.unique_mac_count ORDER BY active_networks DESC NULLS LAST, an.tenant_id NULLS FIRST
WITH DownPortsWithIPs AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.NAME AS port_name, p.NETWORK_ID AS network_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY p.NETWORK_ID ORDER BY p.ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), DownPortsWithSecurityWindow AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (ORDER BY p.ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT dpi.port_id, dpi.tenant_id, dpi.port_name, dpi.network_id, dpi.mac_address, dpi.device_owner, dpi.ip_address, dpi.window_column AS row_num_within_network, dpsw.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, dpsw.ADMIN_STATE_UP, dpsw.STATUS, dpsw.window_column AS min_admin_state_up_window FROM DownPortsWithIPs AS dpi JOIN DownPortsWithSecurityWindow AS dpsw ON dpi.network_id = dpsw.NETWORK_ID AND dpi.port_name = dpsw.NAME AND dpi.mac_address = dpsw.MAC_ADDRESS AND dpi.device_owner = dpsw.DEVICE_OWNER ORDER BY dpi.network_id NULLS FIRST, dpi.port_id NULLS FIRST
WITH port_status_owner_sg AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS min_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS spb ON p.ID = spb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), host_standard_attr_variance AS (SELECT b.HOST, VAR_SAMP(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS standard_attr_id_variance FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND NOT ia.PORT_ID IS NULL GROUP BY b.HOST ORDER BY standard_attr_id_variance DESC NULLS LAST), host_count AS (SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT b.HOST) AS host_count FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT psos.port_status, psos.device_owner, psos.port_count, psos.min_standard_attr_id, hc.host_count, (SELECT MAX(hsav.standard_attr_id_variance) FROM host_standard_attr_variance AS hsav) AS max_standard_attr_id_variance_across_hosts FROM port_status_owner_sg AS psos CROSS JOIN host_count AS hc
WITH shared_subnets AS (SELECT s.NAME AS subnet_name, s.CIDR AS subnet_cidr, n.TARGET_TENANT AS rbac_target_tenant, SUM(s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY s.IP_VERSION ORDER BY s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS n ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared'), ipv4_dense_ranks AS (SELECT s.NAME, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY sa.UPDATED_AT NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4) SELECT ss.subnet_name, ss.subnet_cidr, ss.rbac_target_tenant, ss.window_column AS running_sum_standard_attr_ids_ipv_group, idr.window_column AS dense_rank_by_updated_at_ipv4 FROM shared_subnets AS ss JOIN ipv4_dense_ranks AS idr ON idr.NAME = ss.subnet_name
WITH DnsOrderStddevBySubnet AS (SELECT s.name, STDDEV(d.ORDER) AS order_stddev FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN DNSNAMESERVERS AS d ON s.id = d.subnet_id WHERE s.ip_version = 4 GROUP BY s.name HAVING COUNT(*) > 5 ORDER BY order_stddev DESC NULLS LAST), StandardAttrDenseRankBySubnet AS (SELECT s.NAME, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY sa.UPDATED_AT NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4), CombinedSubnetMetrics AS (SELECT name, order_stddev AS metric_value, 'DNS Order Stddev' AS metric_type FROM DnsOrderStddevBySubnet UNION ALL SELECT NAME, window_column AS metric_value, 'Standard Attr Dense Rank' AS metric_type FROM StandardAttrDenseRankBySubnet) SELECT * FROM CombinedSubnetMetrics ORDER BY name NULLS FIRST, metric_type NULLS FIRST
WITH sq1 AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(ia.ip_address) AS ip_allocation_count, SUM(CASE WHEN NOT ia.subnet_id IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS subnet_id_rows FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON ia.port_id = p.id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND NOT ia.ip_address IS NULL GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address), sq2 AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY psb.port_security_enabled NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN') SELECT sq1.port_id, sq1.tenant_id, sq1.mac_address, sq1.ip_allocation_count, sq1.subnet_id_rows, sq2.window_column FROM sq1 JOIN sq2 ON sq1.port_id = sq2.port_id ORDER BY sq1.ip_allocation_count DESC NULLS LAST
WITH sq1 AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.NAME AS port_name, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, b.HOST AS binding_host, b.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY p.STATUS ORDER BY p.ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), sq2 AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.NAME AS port_name, p.NETWORK_ID AS network_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY p.NETWORK_ID ORDER BY p.ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), top_networks AS (SELECT network_id, COUNT(DISTINCT port_id) AS down_port_count FROM sq2 GROUP BY network_id ORDER BY down_port_count DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10) SELECT tn.network_id, tn.down_port_count, sq2.port_id, sq2.tenant_id, sq2.port_name, sq2.mac_address, sq2.device_owner, sq2.ip_address, sq2.window_column AS port_row_number_within_network, sq1.binding_host, sq1.vif_type, sq1.window_column AS port_rank_within_down_status FROM top_networks AS tn JOIN sq2 ON tn.network_id = sq2.network_id LEFT JOIN sq1 ON sq2.port_id = sq1.port_id ORDER BY tn.down_port_count DESC NULLS LAST, tn.network_id NULLS FIRST, sq2.window_column NULLS FIRST, sq2.port_id NULLS FIRST
WITH DownPortSecurityWindow AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (ORDER BY p.ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), HostDownPortAttrVariance AS (SELECT b.HOST, VAR_SAMP(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS standard_attr_id_variance FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND NOT ia.PORT_ID IS NULL GROUP BY b.HOST ORDER BY standard_attr_id_variance DESC NULLS LAST), DownPortsWithIpOnHosts AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, b.HOST FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON ia.PORT_ID = p.ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND NOT ia.PORT_ID IS NULL) SELECT dpsw.NAME, dpsw.NETWORK_ID, dpsw.MAC_ADDRESS, dpsw.ADMIN_STATE_UP, dpsw.STATUS, dpsw.DEVICE_OWNER, dpsw.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, dpsw.window_column, hdpav.HOST, hdpav.standard_attr_id_variance FROM DownPortSecurityWindow AS dpsw JOIN PORTS AS p ON p.NAME = dpsw.NAME AND p.NETWORK_ID = dpsw.NETWORK_ID AND p.MAC_ADDRESS = dpsw.MAC_ADDRESS AND p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = dpsw.ADMIN_STATE_UP AND p.STATUS = dpsw.STATUS AND p.DEVICE_OWNER = dpsw.DEVICE_OWNER JOIN DownPortsWithIpOnHosts AS dpwh ON dpwh.port_id = p.ID JOIN HostDownPortAttrVariance AS hdpav ON hdpav.HOST = dpwh.HOST ORDER BY hdpav.standard_attr_id_variance DESC NULLS LAST, dpsw.NETWORK_ID NULLS FIRST, dpsw.NAME NULLS FIRST
WITH IPv4SubnetDNSOrderStats AS (SELECT s.name AS subnet_name, MAX(d.ORDER) - MIN(d.ORDER) AS order_range, VAR_SAMP(d.ORDER) AS order_variance FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN DNSNAMESERVERS AS d ON s.id = d.subnet_id WHERE s.ip_version = 4 GROUP BY s.name ORDER BY order_range DESC NULLS LAST), SharedNetworkSubnets AS (SELECT s.NAME AS subnet_name, s.CIDR AS subnet_cidr, n.TARGET_TENANT AS rbac_target_tenant, SUM(s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY s.IP_VERSION ORDER BY s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS n ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared'), SharedIPv4SubnetDNSAndRBACDetails AS (SELECT sns.subnet_name, sns.subnet_cidr, sns.rbac_target_tenant, sns.window_column, ids.order_range, ids.order_variance FROM SharedNetworkSubnets AS sns JOIN IPv4SubnetDNSOrderStats AS ids ON ids.subnet_name = sns.subnet_name JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON s.NAME = sns.subnet_name WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4) SELECT * FROM SharedIPv4SubnetDNSAndRBACDetails ORDER BY subnet_name NULLS FIRST, rbac_target_tenant NULLS FIRST
WITH DownPortsWithBindings AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.NAME AS port_name, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, b.HOST AS binding_host, b.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY p.STATUS ORDER BY p.ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), AdminUpPortsWithSecurity AS (SELECT p.NAME AS port_name, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, psb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED AS port_security_enabled, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1) SELECT dpwb.port_id, dpwb.port_name, dpwb.mac_address, dpwb.binding_host, dpwb.vif_type, dpwb.window_column AS down_status_rank, aupws.port_security_enabled, aupws.window_column AS admin_up_row_number FROM DownPortsWithBindings AS dpwb JOIN AdminUpPortsWithSecurity AS aupws ON dpwb.mac_address = aupws.mac_address WHERE aupws.port_security_enabled = 1 ORDER BY dpwb.port_id NULLS FIRST
WITH PortIpAllocStats AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(ia.ip_address) AS ip_allocation_count, SUM(CASE WHEN NOT ia.subnet_id IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS subnet_id_rows FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON ia.port_id = p.id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND NOT ia.ip_address IS NULL GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address), HostVifWindowSums AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, p.TENANT_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE b.HOST = 'drive-59') SELECT h.PORT_ID AS port_id, h.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, h.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, pias.ip_allocation_count, pias.subnet_id_rows, h.window_column FROM HostVifWindowSums AS h JOIN PortIpAllocStats AS pias ON pias.port_id = h.PORT_ID AND pias.tenant_id = h.TENANT_ID AND pias.mac_address = h.MAC_ADDRESS ORDER BY h.window_column DESC NULLS LAST, h.PORT_ID NULLS FIRST
WITH DnsOrderStats AS (SELECT s.name AS subnet_name, MAX(d.ORDER) - MIN(d.ORDER) AS order_range, VAR_SAMP(d.ORDER) AS order_variance FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN DNSNAMESERVERS AS d ON s.id = d.subnet_id WHERE s.ip_version = 4 GROUP BY s.name ORDER BY order_range DESC NULLS LAST), SubnetRoutePoolDetails AS (SELECT r.DESTINATION, r.NEXTHOP, s.NAME AS subnet_name, s.CIDR AS subnet_cidr, p.FIRST_IP, p.LAST_IP, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY r.SUBNET_ID ORDER BY r.SUBNET_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SUBNETROUTES AS r JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON r.SUBNET_ID = s.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONPOOLS AS p ON s.ID = p.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4) SELECT srpd.subnet_name, srpd.subnet_cidr, srpd.DESTINATION, srpd.NEXTHOP, srpd.FIRST_IP, srpd.LAST_IP, srpd.window_column, dos.order_range, dos.order_variance FROM SubnetRoutePoolDetails AS srpd JOIN DnsOrderStats AS dos ON srpd.subnet_name = dos.subnet_name ORDER BY srpd.subnet_name NULLS FIRST, srpd.window_column NULLS FIRST, srpd.DESTINATION NULLS FIRST
WITH AdminUpPortsWithIPs AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(ia.ip_address) AS ip_allocation_count, SUM(CASE WHEN NOT ia.subnet_id IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS subnet_id_rows FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON ia.port_id = p.id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND NOT ia.ip_address IS NULL GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address), DownPortsWithBindings AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.NAME AS port_name, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, b.HOST AS binding_host, b.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY p.STATUS ORDER BY p.ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT dpwb.port_id, dpwb.port_name, apwi.tenant_id, dpwb.mac_address, dpwb.binding_host, dpwb.vif_type, apwi.ip_allocation_count, apwi.subnet_id_rows, dpwb.window_column FROM DownPortsWithBindings AS dpwb JOIN AdminUpPortsWithIPs AS apwi ON dpwb.port_id = apwi.port_id ORDER BY dpwb.window_column NULLS FIRST, dpwb.port_id NULLS FIRST
WITH SubnetStdAttrWindow AS (SELECT s.NAME, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY sa.UPDATED_AT NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4), SubnetRoutesPoolsWindow AS (SELECT r.DESTINATION, r.NEXTHOP, s.NAME AS subnet_name, s.CIDR AS subnet_cidr, p.FIRST_IP, p.LAST_IP, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY r.SUBNET_ID ORDER BY r.SUBNET_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SUBNETROUTES AS r JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON r.SUBNET_ID = s.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONPOOLS AS p ON s.ID = p.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4) SELECT srpw.subnet_name, srpw.subnet_cidr, srpw.DESTINATION, srpw.NEXTHOP, srpw.FIRST_IP, srpw.LAST_IP, ssaw.window_column AS std_attr_dense_rank_updated_at_window, srpw.window_column AS route_dense_rank_within_subnet FROM SubnetRoutesPoolsWindow AS srpw JOIN SubnetStdAttrWindow AS ssaw ON srpw.subnet_name = ssaw.NAME
WITH AdminUpPorts AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1), DownPortsWithIPs AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, b.HOST AS host, b.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ia.IP_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT aup.TENANT_ID, aup.VIF_TYPE, COUNT(DISTINCT aup.PORT_ID) AS Admin_Up_Port_Count, COUNT(DISTINCT dpi.port_id) AS Down_Port_Count, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT dpi.port_id) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS Has_Down_Ports_With_IPs FROM AdminUpPorts AS aup LEFT JOIN DownPortsWithIPs AS dpi ON aup.TENANT_ID = dpi.tenant_id AND aup.VIF_TYPE = dpi.vif_type WHERE aup.window_column > 0 GROUP BY aup.TENANT_ID, aup.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY Admin_Up_Port_Count DESC NULLS LAST, Down_Port_Count DESC NULLS LAST
WITH DnsOrderStatsBySubnet AS (SELECT s.NAME AS subnet_name, AVG(d.ORDER) AS avg_dns_order, VAR_SAMP(d.ORDER) AS dns_order_variance FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN DNSNAMESERVERS AS d ON s.ID = d.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 GROUP BY s.NAME ORDER BY dns_order_variance DESC NULLS LAST), SharedNetworkSubnetMaxIpVersion AS (SELECT s.name, MAX(s.ip_version) AS max_ip_version, s.gateway_ip FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.object_id = s.network_id WHERE n.action = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY s.name, s.gateway_ip HAVING MAX(s.ip_version) > 3), CombinedSubnetInsights AS (SELECT d.subnet_name AS subnet_name, NULL AS gateway_ip, d.avg_dns_order AS avg_dns_order, d.dns_order_variance AS dns_order_variance, NULL AS max_ip_version, 'IPv4 DNS Order Stats' AS reason FROM DnsOrderStatsBySubnet AS d UNION ALL SELECT s.name AS subnet_name, s.gateway_ip AS gateway_ip, NULL AS avg_dns_order, NULL AS dns_order_variance, s.max_ip_version AS max_ip_version, 'Shared Network (Max IP Version > 3)' AS reason FROM SharedNetworkSubnetMaxIpVersion AS s) SELECT subnet_name, gateway_ip, avg_dns_order, dns_order_variance, max_ip_version, reason FROM CombinedSubnetInsights ORDER BY subnet_name NULLS FIRST, reason NULLS FIRST
WITH DownPortsWithStdAttrWindow AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(sa.ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sa.RESOURCE_TYPE ORDER BY sa.ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), AdminUpPortsWithSecurityRowNum AS (SELECT p.NAME AS port_name, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, psb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED AS port_security_enabled, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1) SELECT a.port_name, a.mac_address, a.port_security_enabled, a.window_column AS admin_up_row_number_window, d.port_id, d.tenant_id, d.window_column AS down_ports_stdattr_running_sum FROM AdminUpPortsWithSecurityRowNum AS a JOIN DownPortsWithStdAttrWindow AS d ON a.mac_address = d.mac_address ORDER BY a.mac_address NULLS FIRST, d.port_id NULLS FIRST
