WITH Subq1 AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), Subq2 AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.STATUS AS port_status, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY p.NETWORK_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID = '0008e629-5619-41e0-b86c-4eecfeaacb6c') SELECT s1.port_id, s1.tenant_id, s1.mac_address, s2.port_status, s2.ip_address, s1.window_column AS running_sum_standard_attr_id_within_security_group, s2.window_column AS running_sum_standard_attr_id_within_network FROM Subq1 AS s1 JOIN Subq2 AS s2 ON s1.port_id = s2.port_id ORDER BY s1.port_id NULLS FIRST
WITH SubnetDetails AS (SELECT s.NAME, s.TENANT_ID, s.NETWORK_ID, s.IP_VERSION, s.CIDR, s.GATEWAY_IP, s.ENABLE_DHCP, p.FIRST_IP, p.LAST_IP, MAX(s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (ORDER BY s.ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_standard_attr_id_next2 FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN IPALLOCATIONPOOLS AS p ON s.ID = p.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4), SubnetRouteDetails AS (SELECT r.DESTINATION, r.NEXTHOP, s.NAME AS subnet_name, s.CIDR AS subnet_cidr, p.FIRST_IP, p.LAST_IP, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY r.SUBNET_ID ORDER BY r.SUBNET_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SUBNETROUTES AS r JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON r.SUBNET_ID = s.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONPOOLS AS p ON s.ID = p.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4) SELECT sd.NAME AS subnet_name, sd.TENANT_ID, sd.NETWORK_ID, sd.IP_VERSION, sd.CIDR, sd.GATEWAY_IP, sd.ENABLE_DHCP, sd.FIRST_IP AS allocation_first_ip, sd.LAST_IP AS allocation_last_ip, sd.max_standard_attr_id_next2, srd.DESTINATION AS route_destination, srd.NEXTHOP AS route_nexthop, srd.window_column AS route_dense_rank_within_subnet FROM SubnetDetails AS sd LEFT JOIN SubnetRouteDetails AS srd ON sd.NAME = srd.subnet_name AND sd.CIDR = srd.subnet_cidr AND sd.FIRST_IP = srd.FIRST_IP AND sd.LAST_IP = srd.LAST_IP ORDER BY sd.NAME NULLS FIRST, srd.window_column NULLS FIRST, srd.DESTINATION NULLS FIRST
WITH HostPortMaxAttr AS (SELECT b.HOST, MAX(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS max_standard_attr_id, p.STATUS FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN ALLOWEDADDRESSPAIRS AS a ON p.ID = a.PORT_ID WHERE a.IP_ADDRESS = '10.232.210.41/8' GROUP BY b.HOST, p.STATUS HAVING MAX(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) > 1), StatusAttrVariance AS (SELECT p.STATUS, VAR_SAMP(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS standard_attr_id_variance FROM PORTS AS p JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE NOT p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID IS NULL GROUP BY p.STATUS ORDER BY standard_attr_id_variance DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT hpma.HOST, hpma.STATUS, hpma.max_standard_attr_id, sav.standard_attr_id_variance FROM HostPortMaxAttr AS hpma JOIN StatusAttrVariance AS sav ON hpma.STATUS = sav.STATUS
WITH DownPorts AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.NAME AS port_name, p.NETWORK_ID AS network_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY p.NETWORK_ID ORDER BY p.ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), ActiveNetworks AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE') SELECT an.tenant_id, an.network_id, an.network_name, an.admin_state_up, an.mtu, an.standard_attr_updated_at, COUNT(DISTINCT dp.port_id) AS down_port_count FROM ActiveNetworks AS an LEFT JOIN DownPorts AS dp ON dp.network_id = an.network_id AND dp.tenant_id = an.tenant_id WHERE an.admin_state_up = 1 GROUP BY an.tenant_id, an.network_id, an.network_name, an.admin_state_up, an.mtu, an.standard_attr_updated_at HAVING COUNT(DISTINCT dp.port_id) > 0 ORDER BY down_port_count DESC NULLS LAST, an.network_name NULLS FIRST
WITH PortStatusIPLenCV AS (SELECT p.status, CAST(STDDEV(LENGTH(ia.ip_address)) AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(AVG(LENGTH(ia.ip_address)), 0), 0) AS coefficient_of_variation FROM portsecuritybindings AS psb JOIN ports AS p ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON p.id = ia.port_id WHERE psb.port_security_enabled = 1 GROUP BY p.status HAVING AVG(LENGTH(ia.ip_address)) > 0 ORDER BY coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST), DownPortsWithSecurityGroup AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (ORDER BY p.ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT d.NAME, d.NETWORK_ID, d.MAC_ADDRESS, d.ADMIN_STATE_UP, d.STATUS, d.DEVICE_OWNER, d.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, d.window_column, cv.coefficient_of_variation AS status_ip_length_coefficient_of_variation FROM DownPortsWithSecurityGroup AS d JOIN PortStatusIPLenCV AS cv ON d.STATUS = cv.status ORDER BY cv.coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST, d.NAME NULLS FIRST
WITH sq1 AS (SELECT s.NAME AS subnet_name, MAX(route_count) - MIN(route_count) AS route_count_range, VAR_SAMP(route_count) AS route_count_variance, STDDEV(route_count) AS route_count_stddev FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN (SELECT sr.SUBNET_ID, COUNT(*) AS route_count FROM SUBNETROUTES AS sr GROUP BY sr.SUBNET_ID, sr.NEXTHOP) AS route_stats ON s.ID = route_stats.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 GROUP BY s.NAME ORDER BY route_count_range DESC NULLS LAST), sq2 AS (SELECT r.DESTINATION, r.NEXTHOP, s.NAME AS subnet_name, s.CIDR AS subnet_cidr, p.FIRST_IP, p.LAST_IP, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY r.SUBNET_ID ORDER BY r.SUBNET_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SUBNETROUTES AS r JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON r.SUBNET_ID = s.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONPOOLS AS p ON s.ID = p.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4) SELECT sq2.subnet_name, sq2.subnet_cidr, sq2.DESTINATION, sq2.NEXTHOP, sq2.FIRST_IP, sq2.LAST_IP, sq2.window_column, sq1.route_count_range, sq1.route_count_variance, sq1.route_count_stddev FROM sq2 JOIN sq1 ON sq2.subnet_name = sq1.subnet_name ORDER BY sq1.route_count_range DESC NULLS LAST, sq2.subnet_name NULLS FIRST, sq2.window_column NULLS FIRST
WITH DownPortsBySecurityGroup AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), AdminUpPortsWithRowNumbers AS (SELECT p.NAME AS port_name, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, psb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED AS port_security_enabled, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1) SELECT dpsg.port_id, dpsg.tenant_id, dpsg.mac_address, dpsg.window_column AS down_ports_running_sum_standard_attr_id, aup.port_name, aup.port_security_enabled, aup.window_column AS admin_up_ports_row_number FROM DownPortsBySecurityGroup AS dpsg JOIN AdminUpPortsWithRowNumbers AS aup ON dpsg.mac_address = aup.mac_address WHERE dpsg.window_column > 0 ORDER BY dpsg.window_column DESC NULLS LAST, dpsg.mac_address NULLS FIRST
WITH PortStandardAttrVarianceByStatus AS (SELECT p.STATUS, VAR_SAMP(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS standard_attr_id_variance FROM PORTS AS p JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE NOT p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID IS NULL GROUP BY p.STATUS ORDER BY standard_attr_id_variance DESC NULLS LAST), PortSecurityBindingStatsByStatus AS (SELECT p.status, MAX(ps.binding_count) - MIN(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_range, VAR_SAMP(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_variance, STDDEV(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_stddev FROM ports AS p JOIN (SELECT p2.id AS port_id, COUNT(*) AS binding_count FROM ports AS p2 JOIN portsecuritybindings AS b ON p2.id = b.port_id GROUP BY p2.id) AS ps ON p.id = ps.port_id GROUP BY p.status ORDER BY binding_count_range DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT psav.STATUS, psav.standard_attr_id_variance, psbs.binding_count_range, psbs.binding_count_variance, psbs.binding_count_stddev FROM PortStandardAttrVarianceByStatus AS psav JOIN PortSecurityBindingStatsByStatus AS psbs ON psav.STATUS = psbs.status ORDER BY psav.standard_attr_id_variance DESC NULLS LAST
WITH SubnetOrderRangeVariance AS (SELECT s.name AS subnet_name, MAX(d.ORDER) - MIN(d.ORDER) AS order_range, VAR_SAMP(d.ORDER) AS order_variance FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN DNSNAMESERVERS AS d ON s.id = d.subnet_id WHERE s.ip_version = 4 GROUP BY s.name ORDER BY order_range DESC NULLS LAST), SubnetAvgVariance AS (SELECT s.NAME AS subnet_name, AVG(d.ORDER) AS avg_dns_order, VAR_SAMP(d.ORDER) AS dns_order_variance FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN DNSNAMESERVERS AS d ON s.ID = d.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 GROUP BY s.NAME ORDER BY dns_order_variance DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT sorv.subnet_name, sorv.order_range, sorv.order_variance, sav.avg_dns_order, sav.dns_order_variance FROM SubnetOrderRangeVariance AS sorv JOIN SubnetAvgVariance AS sav ON sorv.subnet_name = sav.subnet_name WHERE (CAST(sorv.order_variance * 1.0 AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(sav.dns_order_variance, 0), 0)) > 0.75
WITH q1 AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, sgpb.security_group_id, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.security_group_id ORDER BY p.id NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN'), q2 AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE') SELECT q2.network_id, q2.network_name, q2.tenant_id, q2.admin_state_up, q2.mtu, q2.standard_attr_updated_at, q1.port_id, q1.mac_address, q1.security_group_id, q1.window_column AS port_rank_within_security_group, q2.window_column AS network_row_number_within_tenant FROM q2 JOIN PORTS AS p ON p.network_id = q2.network_id JOIN q1 ON q1.port_id = p.id AND q1.tenant_id = q2.tenant_id ORDER BY q2.tenant_id NULLS FIRST, q2.network_name NULLS FIRST, q1.security_group_id NULLS FIRST, q1.window_column NULLS FIRST, q1.port_id NULLS FIRST
WITH q1 AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE'), q2 AS (SELECT r.DESTINATION, r.NEXTHOP, s.NAME AS subnet_name, s.CIDR AS subnet_cidr, p.FIRST_IP, p.LAST_IP, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY r.SUBNET_ID ORDER BY r.SUBNET_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SUBNETROUTES AS r JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON r.SUBNET_ID = s.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONPOOLS AS p ON s.ID = p.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4) SELECT q1.network_id, q1.network_name, q1.tenant_id, q1.admin_state_up, q1.mtu, q1.standard_attr_updated_at, q2.DESTINATION, q2.NEXTHOP, q2.subnet_name, q2.subnet_cidr, q2.FIRST_IP, q2.LAST_IP FROM q1 JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON s.NETWORK_ID = q1.network_id JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa_s ON sa_s.ID = s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID JOIN q2 ON q2.subnet_name = s.NAME AND q2.subnet_cidr = s.CIDR ORDER BY q1.tenant_id NULLS FIRST, q1.network_name NULLS FIRST, q2.subnet_name NULLS FIRST, q2.DESTINATION NULLS FIRST
WITH PortStatusOwnerStats AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), ActiveTenantNetworks AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE'), ActiveTenantPortStatusOwnerStats AS (SELECT atn.tenant_id, atn.network_id, atn.network_name, atn.admin_state_up, atn.mtu, atn.standard_attr_updated_at, atn.window_column, psos.port_status, psos.device_owner, psos.port_count, psos.earliest_standard_attr_update FROM ActiveTenantNetworks AS atn JOIN PORTS AS p ON p.NETWORK_ID = atn.network_id JOIN PortStatusOwnerStats AS psos ON psos.port_status = p.STATUS AND psos.device_owner = p.DEVICE_OWNER JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa_p ON sa_p.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa_p.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' GROUP BY atn.tenant_id, atn.network_id, atn.network_name, atn.admin_state_up, atn.mtu, atn.standard_attr_updated_at, atn.window_column, psos.port_status, psos.device_owner, psos.port_count, psos.earliest_standard_attr_update) SELECT * FROM ActiveTenantPortStatusOwnerStats ORDER BY tenant_id NULLS FIRST, window_column NULLS FIRST, network_id NULLS FIRST, port_status NULLS FIRST, device_owner NULLS FIRST
WITH RouteStatsBySubnetName AS (SELECT s.NAME AS subnet_name, MAX(route_count) - MIN(route_count) AS route_count_range, VAR_SAMP(route_count) AS route_count_variance, STDDEV(route_count) AS route_count_stddev FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN (SELECT sr.SUBNET_ID, COUNT(*) AS route_count FROM SUBNETROUTES AS sr GROUP BY sr.SUBNET_ID, sr.NEXTHOP) AS route_stats ON s.ID = route_stats.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 GROUP BY s.NAME ORDER BY route_count_range DESC NULLS LAST), PoolStatsBySubnetNameWithDNS AS (SELECT s.name AS subnet_name, MAX(pool_count) - MIN(pool_count) AS pool_count_range, VAR_SAMP(pool_count) AS pool_count_variance, STDDEV(pool_count) AS pool_count_stddev FROM subnets AS s JOIN dnsnameservers AS d ON d.subnet_id = s.id JOIN (SELECT s2.id AS subnet_id, COUNT(*) AS pool_count FROM subnets AS s2 JOIN ipallocationpools AS p ON p.subnet_id = s2.id GROUP BY s2.id, p.id) AS pool_stats ON pool_stats.subnet_id = s.id GROUP BY s.name ORDER BY pool_count_range DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT r.subnet_name, r.route_count_range, r.route_count_variance, r.route_count_stddev, p.pool_count_range, p.pool_count_variance, p.pool_count_stddev FROM RouteStatsBySubnetName AS r JOIN PoolStatsBySubnetNameWithDNS AS p ON p.subnet_name = r.subnet_name ORDER BY r.route_count_range DESC NULLS LAST, p.pool_count_range DESC NULLS LAST
WITH NetworkMtuStatsByStatus AS (SELECT n.status, AVG(n.mtu) AS avg_mtu, VAR_SAMP(n.mtu) AS mtu_variance FROM EXTERNALNETWORKS AS e JOIN NETWORKS AS n ON e.network_id = n.id JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r ON n.id = r.object_id WHERE r.action = 'access_as_shared' AND NOT n.mtu IS NULL GROUP BY n.status ORDER BY mtu_variance DESC NULLS LAST), ActiveExternalNetworksRanked AS (SELECT t0.NETWORK_ID, t0.IS_DEFAULT, t1.NAME, t1.STATUS, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY t0.IS_DEFAULT ORDER BY t0.NETWORK_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM EXTERNALNETWORKS AS t0 JOIN NETWORKS AS t1 ON t0.NETWORK_ID = t1.ID WHERE t1.STATUS = 'ACTIVE') SELECT aenr.STATUS AS status, aenr.IS_DEFAULT, COUNT(DISTINCT aenr.NETWORK_ID) AS active_external_network_count, m.avg_mtu, m.mtu_variance, AVG(aenr.window_column) AS avg_dense_rank_within_default_group FROM ActiveExternalNetworksRanked AS aenr JOIN NetworkMtuStatsByStatus AS m ON aenr.STATUS = m.status JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r ON aenr.NETWORK_ID = r.OBJECT_ID WHERE r.action = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY aenr.STATUS, aenr.IS_DEFAULT, m.avg_mtu, m.mtu_variance ORDER BY m.mtu_variance DESC NULLS LAST, active_external_network_count DESC NULLS LAST
WITH DownPortsWindow AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, AVG(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST), Drive59PortsRunningSum AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, p.TENANT_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE b.HOST = 'drive-59'), DownDrive59PortDetails AS (SELECT dpw.NAME, dpw.MAC_ADDRESS, dpw.ADMIN_STATE_UP, dpw.HOST, dpw.VIF_TYPE, dpw.window_column AS down_ports_avg_admin_state_up_window, d59.PORT_ID, d59.TENANT_ID, d59.window_column AS drive59_running_sum_standard_attr_id FROM DownPortsWindow AS dpw JOIN Drive59PortsRunningSum AS d59 ON dpw.MAC_ADDRESS = d59.MAC_ADDRESS WHERE dpw.HOST = 'drive-59') SELECT * FROM DownDrive59PortDetails ORDER BY VIF_TYPE NULLS FIRST, MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST, PORT_ID NULLS FIRST
WITH DownPorts AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.NAME AS port_name, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, b.HOST AS binding_host, b.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY p.STATUS ORDER BY p.ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), ActiveNetworks AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE') SELECT an.network_id, an.network_name, an.tenant_id, an.admin_state_up, an.mtu, an.standard_attr_updated_at, dp.port_id, dp.port_name, dp.mac_address, dp.binding_host, dp.vif_type, dp.window_column AS down_port_rank_within_status FROM ActiveNetworks AS an JOIN PORTS AS p ON p.NETWORK_ID = an.network_id JOIN DownPorts AS dp ON dp.port_id = p.ID
WITH subq1 AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(sa.ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sa.RESOURCE_TYPE ORDER BY sa.ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), subq2 AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE'), top_tenants AS (SELECT tenant_id, COUNT(*) AS down_port_count FROM subq1 GROUP BY tenant_id ORDER BY down_port_count DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10) SELECT tt.tenant_id, tt.down_port_count, s2.network_id, s2.network_name, s2.admin_state_up, s2.mtu, s2.standard_attr_updated_at, s2.window_column AS network_row_number_within_tenant, s1.port_id, s1.mac_address, s1.window_column AS running_sum_standard_attr_ids_within_resource_type FROM top_tenants AS tt JOIN subq2 AS s2 ON s2.tenant_id = tt.tenant_id LEFT JOIN subq1 AS s1 ON s1.tenant_id = tt.tenant_id ORDER BY tt.down_port_count DESC NULLS LAST, tt.tenant_id NULLS FIRST, s2.window_column NULLS FIRST, s2.network_name NULLS FIRST, s1.port_id NULLS FIRST
WITH SharedRbacSubnets AS (SELECT s.NAME AS subnet_name, s.CIDR AS subnet_cidr, n.TARGET_TENANT AS rbac_target_tenant, SUM(s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY s.IP_VERSION ORDER BY s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS n ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared'), IPv4SubnetDenseRanks AS (SELECT s.NAME, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY sa.UPDATED_AT NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4) SELECT srs.subnet_name, srs.subnet_cidr, srs.rbac_target_tenant, srs.window_column AS running_sum_standard_attr_id_within_ip_version, idr.window_column AS dense_rank_by_standardattribute_updated_at_for_ipv4 FROM SharedRbacSubnets AS srs JOIN IPv4SubnetDenseRanks AS idr ON idr.NAME = srs.subnet_name
WITH PortBindingsWindow AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, AVG(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST), DownPortsWithIPs AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.NAME AS port_name, p.NETWORK_ID AS network_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY p.NETWORK_ID ORDER BY p.ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT dpi.tenant_id, pbw.VIF_TYPE, COUNT(*) AS down_port_count, AVG(pbw.window_column) AS avg_admin_state_up_window_2_pre_2_fol FROM DownPortsWithIPs AS dpi JOIN PortBindingsWindow AS pbw ON dpi.mac_address = pbw.MAC_ADDRESS WHERE dpi.window_column <= 3 GROUP BY dpi.tenant_id, pbw.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY down_port_count DESC NULLS LAST, avg_admin_state_up_window_2_pre_2_fol DESC NULLS LAST
WITH PortStatusOwnerAgg AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS min_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS spb ON p.ID = spb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), DownPortDetails AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.NAME AS port_name, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, b.HOST AS binding_host, b.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY p.STATUS ORDER BY p.ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT psa.port_status, psa.device_owner, psa.port_count, psa.min_standard_attr_id, dpd.port_id, dpd.port_name, dpd.mac_address, dpd.binding_host, dpd.vif_type, dpd.window_column FROM PortStatusOwnerAgg AS psa CROSS JOIN DownPortDetails AS dpd
WITH port_status_owner_sg_summary AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS min_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS spb ON p.ID = spb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), down_ports_with_ip_window AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, b.HOST AS host, b.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ia.IP_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT d.port_id, d.tenant_id, d.host, d.vif_type, d.ip_address, d.window_column, s.port_status, s.device_owner, s.port_count, s.min_standard_attr_id FROM down_ports_with_ip_window AS d JOIN PORTS AS p ON p.ID = d.port_id JOIN port_status_owner_sg_summary AS s ON s.port_status = p.STATUS AND s.device_owner = p.DEVICE_OWNER
WITH sq1 AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS min_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS spb ON p.ID = spb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), sq2 AS (SELECT b.HOST, VAR_SAMP(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS standard_attr_id_variance FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND NOT ia.PORT_ID IS NULL GROUP BY b.HOST ORDER BY standard_attr_id_variance DESC NULLS LAST), top_hosts AS (SELECT HOST, standard_attr_id_variance FROM sq2 ORDER BY standard_attr_id_variance DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10) SELECT th.HOST, th.standard_attr_id_variance, sq1.port_status, sq1.device_owner, sq1.port_count, sq1.min_standard_attr_id FROM top_hosts AS th CROSS JOIN sq1 ORDER BY th.standard_attr_id_variance DESC NULLS LAST, sq1.port_count DESC NULLS LAST
WITH haproxy_status_counts AS (SELECT lb.OPERATING_STATUS, COUNT(*) AS total_loadbalancers FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb JOIN PROVIDERRESOURCEASSOCIATIONS AS pra ON lb.ID = pra.RESOURCE_ID WHERE pra.PROVIDER_NAME = 'haproxy' GROUP BY lb.OPERATING_STATUS HAVING COUNT(*) > 1), haproxy_admin_up_lbs AS (SELECT lb.name AS loadbalancer_name, MAX(lb.admin_state_up) AS max_admin_state_up, lb.operating_status FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb JOIN PROVIDERRESOURCEASSOCIATIONS AS pra ON lb.id = pra.resource_id WHERE pra.provider_name = 'haproxy' GROUP BY lb.name, lb.operating_status HAVING MAX(lb.admin_state_up) = 1) SELECT hsc.OPERATING_STATUS, hsc.total_loadbalancers, COUNT(hal.loadbalancer_name) AS admin_up_loadbalancers_in_status FROM haproxy_status_counts AS hsc LEFT JOIN haproxy_admin_up_lbs AS hal ON hal.operating_status = hsc.OPERATING_STATUS GROUP BY hsc.OPERATING_STATUS, hsc.total_loadbalancers
WITH DnsOrderStats AS (SELECT s.NAME AS subnet_name, AVG(d.ORDER) AS avg_dns_order, VAR_SAMP(d.ORDER) AS dns_order_variance FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN DNSNAMESERVERS AS d ON s.ID = d.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 GROUP BY s.NAME ORDER BY dns_order_variance DESC NULLS LAST), RoutePoolDetails AS (SELECT r.DESTINATION, r.NEXTHOP, s.NAME AS subnet_name, s.CIDR AS subnet_cidr, p.FIRST_IP, p.LAST_IP, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY r.SUBNET_ID ORDER BY r.SUBNET_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SUBNETROUTES AS r JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON r.SUBNET_ID = s.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONPOOLS AS p ON s.ID = p.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4) SELECT rpd.subnet_name, rpd.subnet_cidr, rpd.DESTINATION, rpd.NEXTHOP, rpd.FIRST_IP, rpd.LAST_IP, rpd.window_column, dos.avg_dns_order, dos.dns_order_variance FROM RoutePoolDetails AS rpd JOIN DnsOrderStats AS dos ON rpd.subnet_name = dos.subnet_name ORDER BY dos.dns_order_variance DESC NULLS LAST, rpd.subnet_name NULLS FIRST, rpd.window_column NULLS FIRST
WITH PortStatusOwnerStats AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), DefaultExternalNetworks AS (SELECT e.NETWORK_ID, e.IS_DEFAULT, n.TENANT_ID, n.NAME, n.STATUS, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP, n.MTU, n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID, MIN(n.MTU) OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.TENANT_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM EXTERNALNETWORKS AS e JOIN NETWORKS AS n ON e.NETWORK_ID = n.ID WHERE e.IS_DEFAULT = 0), TenantPortCounts AS (SELECT n.TENANT_ID, COUNT(p.ID) AS tenant_port_count FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN PORTS AS p ON p.NETWORK_ID = n.ID JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' GROUP BY n.TENANT_ID) SELECT den.NETWORK_ID, den.TENANT_ID, den.NAME AS network_name, den.STATUS AS network_status, den.ADMIN_STATE_UP, den.MTU, den.window_column AS tenant_min_mtu, tpc.tenant_port_count, pso.port_status, pso.device_owner, pso.port_count, pso.earliest_standard_attr_update FROM DefaultExternalNetworks AS den JOIN TenantPortCounts AS tpc ON tpc.TENANT_ID = den.TENANT_ID CROSS JOIN PortStatusOwnerStats AS pso WHERE pso.port_count > (0.75 * tpc.tenant_port_count)
WITH ExternalActiveNetworks AS (SELECT t0.NETWORK_ID, t0.IS_DEFAULT, t1.NAME, t1.STATUS, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY t0.IS_DEFAULT ORDER BY t0.NETWORK_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM EXTERNALNETWORKS AS t0 JOIN NETWORKS AS t1 ON t0.NETWORK_ID = t1.ID WHERE t1.STATUS = 'ACTIVE'), DefaultExternalNetworkRbacRanks AS (SELECT n.NAME, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY r.ACTION NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM EXTERNALNETWORKS AS e JOIN NETWORKS AS n ON e.NETWORK_ID = n.ID JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r ON n.ID = r.OBJECT_ID WHERE e.IS_DEFAULT = 0) SELECT ean.IS_DEFAULT, COUNT(DISTINCT ean.NETWORK_ID) AS Active_External_Networks, AVG(ean.window_column * 1.0) AS Avg_Dense_Rank_By_NetworkId_Within_DefaultFlag, AVG(denrr.window_column * 1.0) AS Avg_Dense_Rank_By_Rbac_Action_For_Default_External_Networks FROM ExternalActiveNetworks AS ean LEFT JOIN DefaultExternalNetworkRbacRanks AS denrr ON ean.NAME = denrr.NAME WHERE ean.IS_DEFAULT = 0 GROUP BY ean.IS_DEFAULT ORDER BY Active_External_Networks DESC NULLS LAST
WITH DownPortsWithIPs AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.NAME AS port_name, p.NETWORK_ID AS network_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY p.NETWORK_ID ORDER BY p.ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), PortSecurityBindingStatsByStatus AS (SELECT p.status, MAX(ps.binding_count) - MIN(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_range, VAR_SAMP(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_variance, STDDEV(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_stddev FROM ports AS p JOIN (SELECT p2.id AS port_id, COUNT(*) AS binding_count FROM ports AS p2 JOIN portsecuritybindings AS b ON p2.id = b.port_id GROUP BY p2.id) AS ps ON p.id = ps.port_id GROUP BY p.status ORDER BY binding_count_range DESC NULLS LAST), DownPortCountsByNetwork AS (SELECT network_id, COUNT(DISTINCT port_id) AS down_port_count FROM DownPortsWithIPs GROUP BY network_id) SELECT d.port_id, d.tenant_id, d.port_name, d.network_id, d.mac_address, d.device_owner, d.ip_address, d.window_column, ps.status, ps.binding_count_range, ps.binding_count_variance, ps.binding_count_stddev, c.down_port_count FROM DownPortsWithIPs AS d JOIN PortSecurityBindingStatsByStatus AS ps ON ps.status = 'DOWN' JOIN DownPortCountsByNetwork AS c ON d.network_id = c.network_id ORDER BY d.network_id NULLS FIRST, d.window_column NULLS FIRST
WITH DownPortsBySecurityGroup AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), AdminUpPortsSecurity AS (SELECT p.NAME AS port_name, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, psb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED AS port_security_enabled, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1) SELECT dpsg.port_id, dpsg.tenant_id, dpsg.mac_address, dpsg.window_column AS down_ports_running_sum_standard_attr_id, aups.port_name, aups.port_security_enabled, aups.window_column AS admin_up_ports_row_number FROM DownPortsBySecurityGroup AS dpsg JOIN AdminUpPortsSecurity AS aups ON dpsg.mac_address = aups.mac_address ORDER BY dpsg.port_id NULLS FIRST
WITH PortSecurityGroupStats AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS min_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS spb ON p.ID = spb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), PortStatusStdAttrVariance AS (SELECT p.STATUS, VAR_SAMP(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS standard_attr_id_variance FROM PORTS AS p JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE NOT p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID IS NULL GROUP BY p.STATUS ORDER BY standard_attr_id_variance DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT psgs.port_status, psgs.device_owner, psgs.port_count, psgs.min_standard_attr_id, pssav.standard_attr_id_variance FROM PortSecurityGroupStats AS psgs JOIN PortStatusStdAttrVariance AS pssav ON psgs.port_status = pssav.STATUS ORDER BY psgs.port_status NULLS FIRST, psgs.device_owner NULLS FIRST
WITH port_status_owner_stats AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), bound_admin_ports AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.name AS port_name, COUNT(sgpb.security_group_id) AS security_group_binding_count, SUM(CASE WHEN pd.port_id IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END) AS dns_record_count FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id LEFT JOIN PORTDNSES AS pd ON p.id = pd.port_id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.name), top10_status_owner_by_bound_ports AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS bound_admin_port_count FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb ON sgpb.port_id = p.id WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' AND p.admin_state_up = 1 GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER ORDER BY bound_admin_port_count DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10) SELECT t.port_status, t.device_owner, t.bound_admin_port_count, psos.port_count AS total_ports_with_standard_attr_resource_type_ports, psos.earliest_standard_attr_update, SUM(bap.security_group_binding_count) AS total_security_group_bindings_across_bound_admin_ports, SUM(bap.dns_record_count) AS total_dns_records_across_bound_admin_ports FROM top10_status_owner_by_bound_ports AS t JOIN port_status_owner_stats AS psos ON psos.port_status = t.port_status AND ((psos.device_owner = t.device_owner) OR (psos.device_owner IS NULL AND t.device_owner IS NULL)) JOIN PORTS AS p ON p.STATUS = t.port_status AND ((p.DEVICE_OWNER = t.device_owner) OR (p.DEVICE_OWNER IS NULL AND t.device_owner IS NULL)) JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID AND sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' JOIN bound_admin_ports AS bap ON bap.port_id = p.id GROUP BY t.port_status, t.device_owner, t.bound_admin_port_count, psos.port_count, psos.earliest_standard_attr_update ORDER BY t.bound_admin_port_count DESC NULLS LAST
WITH down_ports_window AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, AVG(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST), status_binding_stats AS (SELECT p.status, MAX(ps.binding_count) - MIN(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_range, VAR_SAMP(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_variance, STDDEV(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_stddev FROM ports AS p JOIN (SELECT p2.id AS port_id, COUNT(*) AS binding_count FROM ports AS p2 JOIN portsecuritybindings AS b ON p2.id = b.port_id GROUP BY p2.id) AS ps ON p.id = ps.port_id GROUP BY p.status ORDER BY binding_count_range DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT dpw.NAME, dpw.MAC_ADDRESS, dpw.ADMIN_STATE_UP, dpw.HOST, dpw.VIF_TYPE, dpw.window_column, sbs.status, sbs.binding_count_range, sbs.binding_count_variance, sbs.binding_count_stddev FROM down_ports_window AS dpw CROSS JOIN status_binding_stats AS sbs WHERE sbs.status = 'DOWN' ORDER BY dpw.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST
WITH subq1 AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS min_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS spb ON p.ID = spb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), subq2 AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (ORDER BY p.ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT subq1.port_status, subq1.device_owner, subq1.port_count, subq1.min_standard_attr_id, subq2.NAME AS port_name, subq2.NETWORK_ID, subq2.MAC_ADDRESS, subq2.ADMIN_STATE_UP, subq2.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, subq2.window_column FROM subq1 JOIN subq2 ON subq1.port_status = subq2.STATUS AND subq1.device_owner = subq2.DEVICE_OWNER ORDER BY subq1.port_count DESC NULLS LAST, subq1.device_owner NULLS FIRST, subq2.NETWORK_ID NULLS FIRST, subq2.NAME NULLS FIRST
WITH DownPortsWithBindings AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, AVG(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST), DownPortsWithSecurityGroups AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (ORDER BY p.ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT dpb.NAME AS port_name, dpsg.NETWORK_ID, dpb.MAC_ADDRESS, dpb.ADMIN_STATE_UP, dpsg.STATUS, dpsg.DEVICE_OWNER, dpb.HOST AS binding_host, dpb.VIF_TYPE, dpsg.SECURITY_GROUP_ID AS bound_security_group_id, dpb.window_column AS avg_admin_state_up_viftype_mac_window, dpsg.window_column AS min_admin_state_up_portid_window FROM DownPortsWithBindings AS dpb JOIN DownPortsWithSecurityGroups AS dpsg ON dpb.NAME = dpsg.NAME AND dpb.MAC_ADDRESS = dpsg.MAC_ADDRESS AND dpb.ADMIN_STATE_UP = dpsg.ADMIN_STATE_UP ORDER BY dpb.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST
WITH SecurityGroupAndDnsByPort AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.name AS port_name, COUNT(sgpb.security_group_id) AS security_group_binding_count, SUM(CASE WHEN pd.port_id IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END) AS dns_record_count FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id LEFT JOIN PORTDNSES AS pd ON p.id = pd.port_id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.name), DownPortBindings AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.NAME AS port_name, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, b.HOST AS binding_host, b.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY p.STATUS ORDER BY p.ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT sgd.port_id, sgd.tenant_id, sgd.port_name, sgd.security_group_binding_count, sgd.dns_record_count, dpb.mac_address, dpb.binding_host, dpb.vif_type, dpb.window_column FROM SecurityGroupAndDnsByPort AS sgd JOIN DownPortBindings AS dpb ON sgd.port_id = dpb.port_id
WITH PortStatusOwnerStats AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), HostStandardAttrVariance AS (SELECT b.HOST, VAR_SAMP(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS standard_attr_id_variance FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND NOT ia.PORT_ID IS NULL GROUP BY b.HOST ORDER BY standard_attr_id_variance DESC NULLS LAST), DownPortsWithIPAndPortsResourceType AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, b.HOST FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' AND p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND NOT ia.PORT_ID IS NULL) SELECT d.port_status, d.device_owner, psos.port_count, psos.earliest_standard_attr_update, d.host, hsav.standard_attr_id_variance FROM DownPortsWithIPAndPortsResourceType AS d JOIN PortStatusOwnerStats AS psos ON psos.port_status = d.port_status AND psos.device_owner = d.device_owner JOIN HostStandardAttrVariance AS hsav ON hsav.HOST = d.host ORDER BY d.port_status NULLS FIRST, d.device_owner NULLS FIRST, hsav.standard_attr_id_variance DESC NULLS LAST, d.host NULLS FIRST
WITH SG_Bound_Ports AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.STATUS AS port_status, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY p.NETWORK_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID = '0008e629-5619-41e0-b86c-4eecfeaacb6c'), Down_Ports_DenseRank AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY psb.port_security_enabled NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN') SELECT sbp.port_id, sbp.port_status, sbp.ip_address, sbp.window_column AS running_sum_standard_attr_id_within_network, dpdr.window_column AS dense_rank_by_port_security_for_down_ports FROM SG_Bound_Ports AS sbp JOIN Down_Ports_DenseRank AS dpdr ON sbp.port_id = dpdr.port_id WHERE sbp.port_status = 'DOWN' ORDER BY sbp.port_id NULLS FIRST
WITH PortDetailsForSecurityGroup AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.STATUS AS port_status, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY p.NETWORK_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID = '0008e629-5619-41e0-b86c-4eecfeaacb6c'), PortSecurityBindingStatsByStatus AS (SELECT p.status, MAX(ps.binding_count) - MIN(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_range, VAR_SAMP(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_variance, STDDEV(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_stddev FROM ports AS p JOIN (SELECT p2.id AS port_id, COUNT(*) AS binding_count FROM ports AS p2 JOIN portsecuritybindings AS b ON p2.id = b.port_id GROUP BY p2.id) AS ps ON p.id = ps.port_id GROUP BY p.status ORDER BY binding_count_range DESC NULLS LAST), SecurityGroupPortStatusCounts AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS ports_in_security_group FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID WHERE sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID = '0008e629-5619-41e0-b86c-4eecfeaacb6c' GROUP BY p.STATUS) SELECT pd.port_id, pd.port_status, pd.ip_address, pd.window_column, pss.binding_count_range, pss.binding_count_variance, pss.binding_count_stddev, sgc.ports_in_security_group FROM PortDetailsForSecurityGroup AS pd JOIN PortSecurityBindingStatsByStatus AS pss ON pd.port_status = pss.status JOIN SecurityGroupPortStatusCounts AS sgc ON pd.port_status = sgc.port_status ORDER BY pd.port_status NULLS FIRST, pd.window_column NULLS FIRST, pd.port_id NULLS FIRST
WITH PortStatusOwnerStandardAttrAgg AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), AdminUpPortSecurityWindow AS (SELECT p.NAME AS port_name, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, psb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED AS port_security_enabled, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1) SELECT a.port_name, a.mac_address, a.port_security_enabled, a.window_column, s.port_status, s.device_owner, s.port_count, s.earliest_standard_attr_update FROM AdminUpPortSecurityWindow AS a JOIN PORTS AS p ON p.MAC_ADDRESS = a.mac_address JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID JOIN PortStatusOwnerStandardAttrAgg AS s ON s.port_status = p.STATUS AND s.device_owner = p.DEVICE_OWNER WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' ORDER BY a.mac_address NULLS FIRST, a.window_column NULLS FIRST
WITH DownPorts AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.NAME AS port_name, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, b.HOST AS binding_host, b.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY p.STATUS ORDER BY p.ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), ActiveNetworks AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE') SELECT an.tenant_id, an.network_id, an.network_name, an.admin_state_up, an.mtu, an.standard_attr_updated_at, an.window_column AS network_row_number_within_tenant, dp.port_id, dp.port_name, dp.mac_address, dp.binding_host, dp.vif_type, dp.window_column AS port_rank_within_down_status FROM ActiveNetworks AS an JOIN PORTS AS p ON p.NETWORK_ID = an.network_id AND p.STATUS = 'DOWN' JOIN DownPorts AS dp ON dp.port_id = p.ID ORDER BY an.tenant_id NULLS FIRST, an.window_column NULLS FIRST, dp.port_id NULLS FIRST
WITH DownPortsWithSecurityGroupRank AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, sgpb.security_group_id, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.security_group_id ORDER BY p.id NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN'), Drive59PortsWithRunningStdAttrSum AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, p.TENANT_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE b.HOST = 'drive-59') SELECT d.port_id, d.tenant_id, d.mac_address, d.security_group_id, d.window_column AS security_group_port_rank, r.window_column AS running_std_attr_id_sum_within_vif_type FROM DownPortsWithSecurityGroupRank AS d JOIN Drive59PortsWithRunningStdAttrSum AS r ON d.port_id = r.PORT_ID ORDER BY d.security_group_id NULLS FIRST, d.port_id NULLS FIRST
WITH DownPortsBySecurityGroup AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), PortsInGivenSecurityGroup AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.STATUS AS port_status, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY p.NETWORK_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID = '0008e629-5619-41e0-b86c-4eecfeaacb6c'), DownPortsInGivenSecurityGroup AS (SELECT dpsg.port_id, dpsg.tenant_id, dpsg.mac_address, pisg.port_status, pisg.ip_address, dpsg.window_column AS down_ports_sg_running_sum_standard_attr_id, pisg.window_column AS sg_ports_network_running_sum_standard_attr_id FROM DownPortsBySecurityGroup AS dpsg JOIN PortsInGivenSecurityGroup AS pisg ON dpsg.port_id = pisg.port_id) SELECT port_id, tenant_id, mac_address, port_status, ip_address, down_ports_sg_running_sum_standard_attr_id, sg_ports_network_running_sum_standard_attr_id FROM DownPortsInGivenSecurityGroup
WITH AdminUpBoundPorts AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.name AS port_name, COUNT(sgpb.security_group_id) AS security_group_binding_count, SUM(CASE WHEN pd.port_id IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END) AS dns_record_count FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id LEFT JOIN PORTDNSES AS pd ON p.id = pd.port_id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.name), DownPortsWithIPs AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.NAME AS port_name, p.NETWORK_ID AS network_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY p.NETWORK_ID ORDER BY p.ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT d.network_id, d.window_column AS down_port_row_number_in_network, d.port_id, d.tenant_id, d.port_name, d.mac_address, d.device_owner, d.ip_address, a.security_group_binding_count, a.dns_record_count FROM DownPortsWithIPs AS d JOIN AdminUpBoundPorts AS a ON d.port_id = a.port_id ORDER BY d.network_id NULLS FIRST, d.window_column NULLS FIRST, d.port_id NULLS FIRST
WITH SubnetDNSStats AS (SELECT s.NAME AS subnet_name, AVG(d.ORDER) AS avg_dns_order, VAR_SAMP(d.ORDER) AS dns_order_variance FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN DNSNAMESERVERS AS d ON s.ID = d.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 GROUP BY s.NAME ORDER BY dns_order_variance DESC NULLS LAST), SharedNetworkSubnetMaxIP AS (SELECT s.name, MAX(s.ip_version) AS max_ip_version, s.gateway_ip FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.object_id = s.network_id WHERE n.action = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY s.name, s.gateway_ip HAVING MAX(s.ip_version) > 3) SELECT sds.subnet_name, snsm.gateway_ip, sds.avg_dns_order, sds.dns_order_variance, snsm.max_ip_version FROM SubnetDNSStats AS sds JOIN SharedNetworkSubnetMaxIP AS snsm ON sds.subnet_name = snsm.name ORDER BY sds.dns_order_variance DESC NULLS LAST
WITH HostDrive59Ports AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, p.TENANT_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE b.HOST = 'drive-59'), AdminUpPorts AS (SELECT p.NAME AS port_name, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, psb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED AS port_security_enabled, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1), CombinedPortWindows AS (SELECT h.PORT_ID AS port_identifier, h.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, h.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, h.window_column AS host_viftype_running_sum_standard_attr_id, NULL AS admin_up_port_name, NULL AS port_security_enabled, NULL AS admin_up_row_number_window FROM HostDrive59Ports AS h UNION ALL SELECT NULL AS port_identifier, NULL AS tenant_id, a.mac_address AS mac_address, NULL AS host_viftype_running_sum_standard_attr_id, a.port_name AS admin_up_port_name, a.port_security_enabled AS port_security_enabled, a.window_column AS admin_up_row_number_window FROM AdminUpPorts AS a) SELECT * FROM CombinedPortWindows ORDER BY mac_address NULLS FIRST
WITH AdminUpWithIPAllocations AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(ia.ip_address) AS ip_allocation_count, SUM(CASE WHEN NOT ia.subnet_id IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS subnet_id_rows FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON ia.port_id = p.id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND NOT ia.ip_address IS NULL GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address), DownPortsRunningStdAttrSumBySecurityGroup AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT d.port_id, d.tenant_id, d.mac_address, d.window_column, a.ip_allocation_count, a.subnet_id_rows FROM DownPortsRunningStdAttrSumBySecurityGroup AS d JOIN AdminUpWithIPAllocations AS a ON a.port_id = d.port_id ORDER BY d.tenant_id NULLS FIRST, d.port_id NULLS FIRST
WITH subq1 AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(sa.ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sa.RESOURCE_TYPE ORDER BY sa.ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), subq2 AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, p.TENANT_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE b.HOST = 'drive-59') SELECT s2.PORT_ID AS port_id, s2.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, s2.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, s1.window_column AS down_ports_running_sum_by_resource_type, s2.window_column AS drive_59_ports_running_sum_by_vif_type FROM subq2 AS s2 JOIN subq1 AS s1 ON s1.port_id = s2.PORT_ID AND s1.tenant_id = s2.TENANT_ID AND s1.mac_address = s2.MAC_ADDRESS ORDER BY s2.PORT_ID NULLS FIRST
WITH SharedRbacSubnets AS (SELECT s.NAME AS subnet_name, s.CIDR AS subnet_cidr, n.TARGET_TENANT AS rbac_target_tenant, SUM(s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY s.IP_VERSION ORDER BY s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS n ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared'), Ipv4SubnetRoutesAndPools AS (SELECT r.DESTINATION, r.NEXTHOP, s.NAME AS subnet_name, s.CIDR AS subnet_cidr, p.FIRST_IP, p.LAST_IP, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY r.SUBNET_ID ORDER BY r.SUBNET_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SUBNETROUTES AS r JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON r.SUBNET_ID = s.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONPOOLS AS p ON s.ID = p.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4) SELECT srs.rbac_target_tenant, COUNT(DISTINCT srs.subnet_name) AS shared_subnet_count, AVG(srs.window_column) AS avg_running_sum_standard_attr_id, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT isp.subnet_name) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS Has_IPv4_Routes_And_Pools FROM SharedRbacSubnets AS srs LEFT JOIN Ipv4SubnetRoutesAndPools AS isp ON srs.subnet_name = isp.subnet_name AND srs.subnet_cidr = isp.subnet_cidr WHERE srs.subnet_cidr LIKE '10.%' GROUP BY srs.rbac_target_tenant HAVING COUNT(DISTINCT srs.subnet_name) >= 2 ORDER BY shared_subnet_count DESC NULLS LAST, avg_running_sum_standard_attr_id DESC NULLS LAST
WITH HapLoadBalancersByOpStatus AS (SELECT lb.OPERATING_STATUS, COUNT(*) AS total_loadbalancers FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb JOIN PROVIDERRESOURCEASSOCIATIONS AS pra ON lb.ID = pra.RESOURCE_ID WHERE pra.PROVIDER_NAME = 'haproxy' GROUP BY lb.OPERATING_STATUS HAVING COUNT(*) > 1), ActiveBytesInByOpStatus AS (SELECT lb.OPERATING_STATUS, SUM(s.BYTES_IN) AS total_bytes_in FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCER_STATISTICS AS s ON lb.ID = s.LOADBALANCER_ID WHERE lb.PROVISIONING_STATUS = 'ACTIVE' GROUP BY lb.OPERATING_STATUS HAVING SUM(s.BYTES_IN) > 1000000) SELECT h.OPERATING_STATUS, h.total_loadbalancers, a.total_bytes_in FROM HapLoadBalancersByOpStatus AS h JOIN ActiveBytesInByOpStatus AS a ON h.OPERATING_STATUS = a.OPERATING_STATUS
WITH DownPortsWindowAvg AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, AVG(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST), DownPortsRank AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.NAME AS port_name, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, b.HOST AS binding_host, b.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY p.STATUS ORDER BY p.ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), CombinedDownPortAnalytics AS (SELECT NULL AS port_id, d1.NAME AS port_name, d1.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, d1.HOST AS binding_host, d1.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, d1.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, d1.window_column AS window_metric, 'VIF-type window avg(admin_state_up)' AS metric_type FROM DownPortsWindowAvg AS d1 UNION ALL SELECT d2.port_id AS port_id, d2.port_name AS port_name, d2.mac_address AS mac_address, d2.binding_host AS binding_host, d2.vif_type AS vif_type, NULL AS admin_state_up, d2.window_column AS window_metric, 'Status-group rank by port_id' AS metric_type FROM DownPortsRank AS d2) SELECT * FROM CombinedDownPortAnalytics ORDER BY vif_type NULLS FIRST, mac_address NULLS FIRST, metric_type NULLS FIRST, port_id NULLS FIRST
WITH DownPortsWithBindings AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, AVG(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST), AdminUpPortsWithSecurity AS (SELECT p.NAME AS port_name, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, psb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED AS port_security_enabled, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1) SELECT dp.NAME AS down_port_name, dp.MAC_ADDRESS AS down_mac_address, dp.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS down_admin_state_up, dp.HOST AS binding_host, dp.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, dp.window_column AS avg_admin_state_up_window_2p_2f, au.port_name AS admin_up_port_name, au.mac_address AS admin_up_mac_address, au.port_security_enabled, au.window_column AS row_number_window_2p_1f FROM DownPortsWithBindings AS dp JOIN AdminUpPortsWithSecurity AS au ON dp.MAC_ADDRESS = au.mac_address ORDER BY dp.MAC_ADDRESS NULLS FIRST
WITH SubnetPoolWindow AS (SELECT s.NAME, s.TENANT_ID, s.NETWORK_ID, s.IP_VERSION, s.CIDR, s.GATEWAY_IP, s.ENABLE_DHCP, p.FIRST_IP, p.LAST_IP, MAX(s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (ORDER BY s.ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_standard_attr_id_next2 FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN IPALLOCATIONPOOLS AS p ON s.ID = p.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4), SharedNetworkSubnetMaxIP AS (SELECT s.name, MAX(s.ip_version) AS max_ip_version, s.gateway_ip FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.object_id = s.network_id WHERE n.action = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY s.name, s.gateway_ip HAVING MAX(s.ip_version) > 3) SELECT spw.NAME AS subnet_name, spw.TENANT_ID, spw.NETWORK_ID, spw.IP_VERSION, spw.CIDR, spw.GATEWAY_IP, spw.ENABLE_DHCP, spw.FIRST_IP, spw.LAST_IP, spw.max_standard_attr_id_next2, sns.max_ip_version FROM SubnetPoolWindow AS spw JOIN SharedNetworkSubnetMaxIP AS sns ON spw.NAME = sns.name AND spw.GATEWAY_IP = sns.gateway_ip ORDER BY spw.NAME NULLS FIRST, spw.GATEWAY_IP NULLS FIRST
WITH DownPortsWithIPs AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.NAME AS port_name, p.NETWORK_ID AS network_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY p.NETWORK_ID ORDER BY p.ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), DownPortsWithSecurityGroupWindow AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.NAME, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (ORDER BY p.ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT dpi.port_id, dpi.tenant_id, dpi.port_name, dpi.network_id, dpi.mac_address, dpsgw.ADMIN_STATE_UP, dpsgw.STATUS, dpi.device_owner, dpsgw.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, dpi.ip_address, dpi.window_column AS network_row_number, dpsgw.window_column AS admin_state_up_min_window FROM DownPortsWithIPs AS dpi JOIN DownPortsWithSecurityGroupWindow AS dpsgw ON dpi.port_id = dpsgw.port_id
WITH q1 AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), q2 AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.STATUS AS port_status, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY p.NETWORK_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID = '0008e629-5619-41e0-b86c-4eecfeaacb6c') SELECT q2.port_id, q1.tenant_id, q1.mac_address, q2.port_status, q2.ip_address, q1.window_column AS running_sum_standard_attr_id_within_security_group, q2.window_column AS running_sum_standard_attr_id_within_network FROM q2 JOIN q1 ON q2.port_id = q1.port_id ORDER BY q2.port_id NULLS FIRST
WITH PortIpAllocationStats AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(ia.ip_address) AS ip_allocation_count, SUM(CASE WHEN NOT ia.subnet_id IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS subnet_id_rows FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON ia.port_id = p.id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND NOT ia.ip_address IS NULL GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address), SecurityGroupBoundPortIps AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.STATUS AS port_status, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY p.NETWORK_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID = '0008e629-5619-41e0-b86c-4eecfeaacb6c'), PortSubnetCoverage AS (SELECT ia.port_id, COUNT(DISTINCT ia.subnet_id) AS distinct_subnet_count FROM ipallocations AS ia JOIN securitygroupportbindings AS sgpb ON sgpb.port_id = ia.port_id WHERE sgpb.security_group_id = '0008e629-5619-41e0-b86c-4eecfeaacb6c' GROUP BY ia.port_id) SELECT pis.port_id, pis.tenant_id, pis.mac_address, sgp.port_status, sgp.ip_address, sgp.window_column, pis.ip_allocation_count, psc.distinct_subnet_count FROM PortIpAllocationStats AS pis JOIN SecurityGroupBoundPortIps AS sgp ON pis.port_id = sgp.port_id JOIN PortSubnetCoverage AS psc ON pis.port_id = psc.port_id WHERE (CAST(pis.subnet_id_rows * 1.0 AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(pis.ip_allocation_count, 0)) > 0.75
WITH ports_admin_up_tenant_window AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), status_variance_for_ip AS (SELECT p.STATUS, VAR_SAMP(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS standard_attr_id_variance FROM PORTS AS p JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE NOT p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID IS NULL AND ia.IP_ADDRESS IN ('10.150.64.113/8') GROUP BY p.STATUS ORDER BY standard_attr_id_variance DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT COUNT(*) AS admin_up_ports_for_tenant_count, (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM status_variance_for_ip) AS ip_filtered_status_groups_count FROM ports_admin_up_tenant_window AS paw CROSS JOIN status_variance_for_ip AS svi
WITH DownPortsWithWindow AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (ORDER BY p.ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.NETWORK_ID IN ('0a1d0a27-cffa-4de3-92c5-9d3fd3f2e74d')), SecurityGroupGeomMean AS (SELECT s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, EXP(AVG(LN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID))) AS geometric_mean_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID > 0 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY geometric_mean_standard_attr_id DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT dpw.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, COUNT(*) AS down_port_count_in_network, AVG(dpw.window_column) AS avg_window_min_admin_state_up, MAX(sgg.geometric_mean_standard_attr_id) AS geometric_mean_standard_attr_id_for_tenant FROM DownPortsWithWindow AS dpw LEFT JOIN SecurityGroupGeomMean AS sgg ON dpw.SECURITY_GROUP_ID = sgg.SECURITY_GROUP_ID GROUP BY dpw.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY down_port_count_in_network DESC NULLS LAST
WITH ActiveTenantNetworks AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE' AND n.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), AdminUpTenantPorts AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), CombinedNetworkPortView AS (SELECT n.tenant_id, n.network_id, n.network_name, n.admin_state_up AS network_admin_state_up, n.mtu, n.standard_attr_updated_at, n.window_column AS network_row_number_by_name, p.PORT_ID AS port_id, p.HOST AS host, p.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, p.VNIC_TYPE AS vnic_type, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, p.window_column AS avg_standard_attr_id_window_2_pre_2_fol_by_vif_type FROM ActiveTenantNetworks AS n JOIN AdminUpTenantPorts AS p ON p.TENANT_ID = n.tenant_id AND p.NETWORK_ID = n.network_id) SELECT * FROM CombinedNetworkPortView ORDER BY tenant_id NULLS FIRST, network_name NULLS FIRST, port_id NULLS FIRST
WITH RbacIpVersionStatsShared AS (SELECT n.ACTION, AVG(s.IP_VERSION) AS avg_ip_version, VAR_SAMP(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_variance, STDDEV(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_stddev FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY n.ACTION ORDER BY ip_version_variance DESC NULLS LAST), RbacIpVersionStddevDhcpNonShared AS (SELECT n.ACTION, STDDEV(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_stddev FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.ENABLE_DHCP = 1 AND NOT s.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY n.ACTION HAVING COUNT(*) > 5 ORDER BY ip_version_stddev DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT r1.ACTION, r1.avg_ip_version, r1.ip_version_variance, r1.ip_version_stddev AS shared_access_ip_version_stddev, r2.ip_version_stddev AS dhcp_nonshared_ip_version_stddev FROM RbacIpVersionStatsShared AS r1 JOIN RbacIpVersionStddevDhcpNonShared AS r2 ON r1.ACTION = r2.ACTION WHERE r1.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' ORDER BY r1.ip_version_variance DESC NULLS LAST
WITH Subq1 AS (SELECT n.ACTION, AVG(s.IP_VERSION) AS avg_ip_version, VAR_SAMP(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_variance, STDDEV(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_stddev FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY n.ACTION ORDER BY ip_version_variance DESC NULLS LAST), Subq2 AS (SELECT n.name, n.status, n.admin_state_up, r.action, r.target_tenant, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY n.id ORDER BY n.id NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM networksecuritybindings AS b JOIN networks AS n ON b.network_id = n.id JOIN networkrbacs AS r ON n.id = r.object_id WHERE n.status = 'ACTIVE' AND NOT n.id IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b'))) SELECT s2.name, s2.status, s2.admin_state_up, s2.action, s2.target_tenant, s2.window_column, s1.avg_ip_version, s1.ip_version_variance, s1.ip_version_stddev FROM Subq2 AS s2 CROSS JOIN Subq1 AS s1 WHERE s1.action = 'access_as_shared'
WITH PortIpAllocStats AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(ia.ip_address) AS ip_allocation_count, SUM(CASE WHEN NOT ia.subnet_id IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS subnet_id_rows FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON ia.port_id = p.id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND NOT ia.ip_address IS NULL AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address), PortBindingWindowStats AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT pbws.PORT_ID AS port_id, pbws.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, pbws.NETWORK_ID AS network_id, pbws.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, pbws.HOST AS host, pbws.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, pbws.VNIC_TYPE AS vnic_type, pbws.STATUS AS status, pbws.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, pbws.window_column AS avg_standard_attr_id_window, pias.ip_allocation_count AS ip_allocation_count, pias.subnet_id_rows AS subnet_id_rows FROM PortBindingWindowStats AS pbws JOIN PortIpAllocStats AS pias ON pias.port_id = pbws.PORT_ID ORDER BY pbws.VIF_TYPE NULLS FIRST, pbws.PORT_ID NULLS FIRST
WITH DefaultExternalNonSharedNetworks AS (SELECT e.NETWORK_ID, e.IS_DEFAULT, n.TENANT_ID, n.NAME, n.STATUS, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP, n.MTU, n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID, MIN(n.MTU) OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.TENANT_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM EXTERNALNETWORKS AS e JOIN NETWORKS AS n ON e.NETWORK_ID = n.ID WHERE e.IS_DEFAULT = 0 AND NOT n.ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b'))), ActiveNetworksWithRbacNonShared AS (SELECT n.name, n.status, n.admin_state_up, r.action, r.target_tenant, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY n.id ORDER BY n.id NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM networksecuritybindings AS b JOIN networks AS n ON b.network_id = n.id JOIN networkrbacs AS r ON n.id = r.object_id WHERE n.status = 'ACTIVE' AND NOT n.id IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b'))), DefaultExternalNonSharedNetworkCount AS (SELECT COUNT(*) AS Default_External_NonShared_Network_Count FROM DefaultExternalNonSharedNetworks) SELECT c.Default_External_NonShared_Network_Count, a.name, a.status, a.admin_state_up, a.action, a.target_tenant, a.window_column AS Active_Network_Rbac_Dense_Rank FROM DefaultExternalNonSharedNetworkCount AS c CROSS JOIN ActiveNetworksWithRbacNonShared AS a
WITH IPAllocStatusVariance AS (SELECT p.STATUS, VAR_SAMP(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS standard_attr_id_variance FROM PORTS AS p JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE NOT p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID IS NULL AND ia.IP_ADDRESS IN ('10.150.64.113/8') GROUP BY p.STATUS ORDER BY standard_attr_id_variance DESC NULLS LAST), OVSDeviceStatusStats AS (SELECT p.STATUS, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) AS min_admin_state_up, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS avg_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE b.VIF_TYPE = 'ovs' AND p.DEVICE_ID IN ('da811c75-0f9b-462c-b020-8ea55f6ca7c7') GROUP BY p.STATUS) SELECT o.STATUS, o.min_admin_state_up, o.avg_standard_attr_id, i.standard_attr_id_variance FROM OVSDeviceStatusStats AS o JOIN IPAllocStatusVariance AS i ON o.STATUS = i.STATUS ORDER BY i.standard_attr_id_variance DESC NULLS LAST
WITH DownPortsBySecurityGroup AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, sgpb.security_group_id, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.security_group_id ORDER BY p.id NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN' AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), AdminUpPortsSecurityGroupAndDNSCounts AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.name AS port_name, COUNT(sgpb.security_group_id) AS security_group_binding_count, SUM(CASE WHEN pd.port_id IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END) AS dns_record_count FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id LEFT JOIN PORTDNSES AS pd ON p.id = pd.port_id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.name) SELECT dp.port_id, dp.tenant_id, dp.mac_address, dp.security_group_id, dp.window_column AS down_port_rank_within_security_group, au.port_name, au.security_group_binding_count, au.dns_record_count FROM DownPortsBySecurityGroup AS dp JOIN AdminUpPortsSecurityGroupAndDNSCounts AS au ON au.port_id = dp.port_id AND au.tenant_id = dp.tenant_id
WITH DownPortsBySecurityGroup AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, sgpb.security_group_id, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.security_group_id ORDER BY p.id NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN' AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), ActiveNetworksByTenant AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE' AND n.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM ActiveNetworksByTenant) AS total_active_networks, (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM DownPortsBySecurityGroup) AS total_down_ports, ROUND(CAST(CAST((SELECT COUNT(*) FROM DownPortsBySecurityGroup) AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF((SELECT COUNT(*) FROM ActiveNetworksByTenant), 0), 0) AS DECIMAL), 2) AS avg_down_ports_per_active_network
WITH PortStatusBindingStats AS (SELECT p.status, MAX(ps.binding_count) - MIN(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_range, VAR_SAMP(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_variance, STDDEV(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_stddev FROM ports AS p JOIN (SELECT p2.id AS port_id, COUNT(*) AS binding_count FROM ports AS p2 JOIN portsecuritybindings AS b ON p2.id = b.port_id WHERE p2.tenant_id IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p2.id) AS ps ON p.id = ps.port_id GROUP BY p.status ORDER BY binding_count_range DESC NULLS LAST), AdminUpPortWindowDetails AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), PortStatusCounts AS (SELECT p.status, COUNT(*) AS port_count FROM PORTS AS p JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p.status) SELECT psbs.status, psc.port_count, psbs.binding_count_range, psbs.binding_count_variance, psbs.binding_count_stddev FROM PortStatusBindingStats AS psbs JOIN PortStatusCounts AS psc ON psbs.status = psc.status WHERE psbs.status IN (SELECT STATUS FROM AdminUpPortWindowDetails)
WITH TenantDownPortsBySecurityGroup AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, sgpb.security_group_id, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.security_group_id ORDER BY p.id NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN' AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), NetworkDownPortsWithMinAdminStateWindow AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (ORDER BY p.ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.NETWORK_ID IN ('0a1d0a27-cffa-4de3-92c5-9d3fd3f2e74d')), SecurityGroupDownPortCounts AS (SELECT sgpb.security_group_id, COUNT(DISTINCT p.id) AS down_port_count FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN' AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') AND p.network_id IN ('0a1d0a27-cffa-4de3-92c5-9d3fd3f2e74d') GROUP BY sgpb.security_group_id) SELECT tdp.port_id, tdp.tenant_id, ndp.network_id, ndp.name AS port_name, tdp.mac_address, ndp.admin_state_up, ndp.status, ndp.device_owner, tdp.security_group_id, tdp.window_column AS port_rank_within_security_group_by_port_id, ndp.window_column AS min_admin_state_up_in_3_preceding_1_following_window, sgc.down_port_count FROM TenantDownPortsBySecurityGroup AS tdp JOIN NetworkDownPortsWithMinAdminStateWindow AS ndp ON tdp.security_group_id = ndp.security_group_id AND tdp.mac_address = ndp.mac_address JOIN SecurityGroupDownPortCounts AS sgc ON tdp.security_group_id = sgc.security_group_id WHERE sgc.down_port_count >= 2 ORDER BY sgc.down_port_count DESC NULLS LAST, tdp.security_group_id NULLS FIRST, tdp.port_id NULLS FIRST
