WITH port_status_owner_sg_summary AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS min_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS spb ON p.ID = spb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), admin_up_ports_with_window AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT pso.port_status, pso.device_owner, pso.port_count, pso.min_standard_attr_id, COUNT(DISTINCT aup.PORT_ID) AS admin_up_port_rows, AVG(aup.window_column) AS avg_window_column FROM port_status_owner_sg_summary AS pso CROSS JOIN admin_up_ports_with_window AS aup GROUP BY pso.port_status, pso.device_owner, pso.port_count, pso.min_standard_attr_id
WITH Subq1 AS (SELECT n.ACTION AS rbac_action, AVG(s.IP_VERSION) AS avg_ip_version, MAX(s.IP_VERSION) - MIN(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_range, VAR_SAMP(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_variance FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION IN (4, 6) AND n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY n.ACTION ORDER BY ip_version_range DESC NULLS LAST), Subq2 AS (SELECT n.name, n.status, n.admin_state_up, r.action, r.target_tenant, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY n.id ORDER BY n.id NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM networksecuritybindings AS b JOIN networks AS n ON b.network_id = n.id JOIN networkrbacs AS r ON n.id = r.object_id WHERE n.status = 'ACTIVE' AND NOT n.id IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b'))) SELECT s2.name, s2.status, s2.admin_state_up, s2.action, s2.target_tenant, s2.window_column, s1.rbac_action, s1.avg_ip_version, s1.ip_version_range, s1.ip_version_variance FROM Subq2 AS s2 JOIN Subq1 AS s1 ON s2.action = s1.rbac_action ORDER BY s1.ip_version_range DESC NULLS LAST, s2.name NULLS FIRST
WITH PortStatusOwnerSecurityGroupStats AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS min_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS spb ON p.ID = spb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), AdminUpPortBindingWindow AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), AdminUpPortBindingWindow_WithSecurityGroup AS (SELECT aupbw.PORT_ID, aupbw.HOST, aupbw.VIF_TYPE, aupbw.VNIC_TYPE, aupbw.TENANT_ID, aupbw.NETWORK_ID, aupbw.MAC_ADDRESS, aupbw.STATUS, aupbw.DEVICE_OWNER, aupbw.window_column FROM AdminUpPortBindingWindow AS aupbw JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS spb ON aupbw.PORT_ID = spb.PORT_ID) SELECT psosgs.port_status, psosgs.device_owner, psosgs.port_count, psosgs.min_standard_attr_id, aupbwsg.PORT_ID, aupbwsg.HOST, aupbwsg.VIF_TYPE, aupbwsg.VNIC_TYPE, aupbwsg.TENANT_ID, aupbwsg.NETWORK_ID, aupbwsg.MAC_ADDRESS, aupbwsg.STATUS, aupbwsg.window_column FROM PortStatusOwnerSecurityGroupStats AS psosgs JOIN AdminUpPortBindingWindow_WithSecurityGroup AS aupbwsg ON psosgs.device_owner = aupbwsg.DEVICE_OWNER AND psosgs.port_status = aupbwsg.STATUS
WITH SubnetPoolAndMaxAttrNext2 AS (SELECT s.NAME, s.TENANT_ID, s.NETWORK_ID, s.IP_VERSION, s.CIDR, s.GATEWAY_IP, s.ENABLE_DHCP, p.FIRST_IP, p.LAST_IP, MAX(s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (ORDER BY s.ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_standard_attr_id_next2 FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN IPALLOCATIONPOOLS AS p ON s.ID = p.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND s.ID IN ('012993ae-5d73-4aad-834b-e87a31e99d48')), SubnetAttrDenseRankWindow AS (SELECT s.NAME, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY sa.UPDATED_AT NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND s.ID IN ('012993ae-5d73-4aad-834b-e87a31e99d48')) SELECT sp.NAME AS subnet_name, sp.TENANT_ID, sp.NETWORK_ID, sp.IP_VERSION, sp.CIDR, sp.GATEWAY_IP, sp.ENABLE_DHCP, sp.FIRST_IP, sp.LAST_IP, sp.max_standard_attr_id_next2, sadr.window_column AS standard_attr_dense_rank_window FROM SubnetPoolAndMaxAttrNext2 AS sp JOIN SubnetAttrDenseRankWindow AS sadr ON sp.NAME = sadr.NAME
WITH RbacIpVersionStats AS (SELECT n.ACTION, AVG(s.IP_VERSION) AS avg_ip_version, VAR_SAMP(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_variance, STDDEV(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_stddev FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY n.ACTION ORDER BY ip_version_variance DESC NULLS LAST), HighMtuRbacNetworks AS (SELECT n.name AS network_name, r.action AS rbac_action, MAX(n.mtu) AS max_mtu FROM NETWORKSECURITYBINDINGS AS nsb JOIN NETWORKS AS n ON nsb.network_id = n.id JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r ON n.id = r.object_id WHERE n.status = 'ACTIVE' AND r.action IN ('access_as_shared', 'access_as_external') GROUP BY n.name, r.action HAVING MAX(n.mtu) > 8000), FilteredHighMtuSharedNetworks AS (SELECT hmn.network_name, hmn.rbac_action, hmn.max_mtu FROM HighMtuRbacNetworks AS hmn WHERE hmn.rbac_action = 'access_as_shared') SELECT f.network_name, f.rbac_action, f.max_mtu, r.avg_ip_version, r.ip_version_variance, r.ip_version_stddev FROM FilteredHighMtuSharedNetworks AS f JOIN RbacIpVersionStats AS r ON r.ACTION = f.rbac_action ORDER BY f.max_mtu DESC NULLS LAST, f.network_name NULLS FIRST
WITH q1 AS (SELECT n.ACTION, STDDEV(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_stddev FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.ENABLE_DHCP = 1 AND NOT s.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY n.ACTION HAVING COUNT(*) > 5 ORDER BY ip_version_stddev DESC NULLS LAST), q2 AS (SELECT n.name AS network_name, r.action AS rbac_action, MAX(n.mtu) AS max_mtu FROM NETWORKSECURITYBINDINGS AS nsb JOIN NETWORKS AS n ON nsb.network_id = n.id JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r ON n.id = r.object_id WHERE n.status = 'ACTIVE' AND r.action IN ('access_as_shared', 'access_as_external') GROUP BY n.name, r.action HAVING MAX(n.mtu) > 8000) SELECT q2.network_name, q2.rbac_action, q2.max_mtu, q1.ip_version_stddev FROM q2 JOIN q1 ON q2.rbac_action = q1.ACTION ORDER BY q2.max_mtu DESC NULLS LAST, q1.ip_version_stddev DESC NULLS LAST
WITH SecurityGroupAndDnsByPort AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.name AS port_name, COUNT(sgpb.security_group_id) AS security_group_binding_count, SUM(CASE WHEN pd.port_id IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END) AS dns_record_count FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id LEFT JOIN PORTDNSES AS pd ON p.id = pd.port_id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.name), IpAllocationsByPort AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(ia.ip_address) AS ip_allocation_count, SUM(CASE WHEN NOT ia.subnet_id IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS subnet_id_rows FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON ia.port_id = p.id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND NOT ia.ip_address IS NULL AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address) SELECT sgdp.port_id, sgdp.tenant_id, sgdp.port_name, iap.mac_address, sgdp.security_group_binding_count, sgdp.dns_record_count, iap.ip_allocation_count, iap.subnet_id_rows FROM SecurityGroupAndDnsByPort AS sgdp JOIN IpAllocationsByPort AS iap ON sgdp.port_id = iap.port_id AND sgdp.tenant_id = iap.tenant_id WHERE sgdp.security_group_binding_count >= 1 ORDER BY sgdp.security_group_binding_count DESC NULLS LAST, iap.ip_allocation_count DESC NULLS LAST
WITH PortStatusVariation AS (SELECT p.status, CAST(STDDEV(p.standard_attr_id) AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(AVG(p.standard_attr_id), 0), 0) AS coefficient_of_variation FROM securitygroupportbindings AS sgpb JOIN ports AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.tenant_id IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p.status HAVING AVG(p.standard_attr_id) > 0 ORDER BY coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST), PortStatusDeviceOwnerStats AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' AND NOT p.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER) SELECT psdo.port_status, psdo.device_owner, psdo.port_count, psdo.earliest_standard_attr_update, psv.coefficient_of_variation FROM PortStatusDeviceOwnerStats AS psdo JOIN PortStatusVariation AS psv ON psdo.port_status = psv.status ORDER BY psdo.port_count DESC NULLS LAST, psv.coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST
WITH PortBindingWindow AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), PortStatusVariance AS (SELECT p.STATUS, VAR_SAMP(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS standard_attr_id_variance FROM PORTS AS p JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE NOT p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID IS NULL AND ia.IP_ADDRESS IN ('10.150.64.113/8') GROUP BY p.STATUS ORDER BY standard_attr_id_variance DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT pbw.PORT_ID, pbw.HOST, pbw.VIF_TYPE, pbw.VNIC_TYPE, pbw.TENANT_ID, pbw.NETWORK_ID, pbw.MAC_ADDRESS, pbw.STATUS, pbw.DEVICE_OWNER, pbw.window_column, psv.standard_attr_id_variance FROM PortBindingWindow AS pbw JOIN PortStatusVariance AS psv ON pbw.STATUS = psv.STATUS ORDER BY pbw.VIF_TYPE NULLS FIRST, pbw.PORT_ID NULLS FIRST
WITH sq1 AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' AND NOT p.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), sq2 AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (ORDER BY p.ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.NETWORK_ID IN ('0a1d0a27-cffa-4de3-92c5-9d3fd3f2e74d')) SELECT sq2.NETWORK_ID, sq2.NAME AS port_name, sq2.MAC_ADDRESS, sq2.ADMIN_STATE_UP, sq2.STATUS AS port_status, sq2.DEVICE_OWNER, sq2.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, sq2.window_column AS min_admin_state_up_window, sq1.port_count, sq1.earliest_standard_attr_update FROM sq2 JOIN sq1 ON sq1.port_status = sq2.STATUS AND sq1.device_owner = sq2.DEVICE_OWNER ORDER BY sq1.port_count DESC NULLS LAST, sq2.NAME NULLS FIRST
WITH RbacIpVersionStats AS (SELECT n.ACTION, AVG(s.IP_VERSION) AS avg_ip_version, VAR_SAMP(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_variance, STDDEV(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_stddev FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY n.ACTION ORDER BY ip_version_variance DESC NULLS LAST), ActiveHighMtuNetworks AS (SELECT n.name AS network_name, r.action AS rbac_action, MAX(n.mtu) AS max_mtu FROM NETWORKSECURITYBINDINGS AS nsb JOIN NETWORKS AS n ON nsb.network_id = n.id JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r ON n.id = r.object_id WHERE n.status = 'ACTIVE' AND r.action IN ('access_as_shared', 'access_as_external') GROUP BY n.name, r.action HAVING MAX(n.mtu) > 8000) SELECT ahmn.network_name, ahmn.rbac_action, ahmn.max_mtu, rivs.avg_ip_version, rivs.ip_version_variance, rivs.ip_version_stddev FROM ActiveHighMtuNetworks AS ahmn JOIN RbacIpVersionStats AS rivs ON ahmn.rbac_action = rivs.ACTION WHERE ahmn.rbac_action = 'access_as_shared' ORDER BY ahmn.max_mtu DESC NULLS LAST
WITH q1 AS (SELECT n.ACTION, VAR_SAMP(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_variance FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' AND n.TARGET_TENANT = '*' GROUP BY n.ACTION ORDER BY ip_version_variance DESC NULLS LAST), q2 AS (SELECT n.ACTION AS rbac_action, AVG(s.IP_VERSION) AS avg_ip_version, MAX(s.IP_VERSION) - MIN(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_range, VAR_SAMP(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_variance FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION IN (4, 6) AND n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY n.ACTION ORDER BY ip_version_range DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT q1.ACTION AS rbac_action, q2.avg_ip_version, q2.ip_version_range, q1.ip_version_variance AS public_access_ip_version_variance, q2.ip_version_variance AS overall_ip_version_variance FROM q1 JOIN q2 ON q1.ACTION = q2.rbac_action ORDER BY q2.ip_version_range DESC NULLS LAST
WITH SubnetDetails AS (SELECT s.NAME, s.TENANT_ID, s.NETWORK_ID, s.IP_VERSION, s.CIDR, s.GATEWAY_IP, s.ENABLE_DHCP, p.FIRST_IP, p.LAST_IP, MAX(s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (ORDER BY s.ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_standard_attr_id_next2 FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN IPALLOCATIONPOOLS AS p ON s.ID = p.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND s.ID IN ('012993ae-5d73-4aad-834b-e87a31e99d48')), SubnetRouteStats AS (SELECT s.NAME AS subnet_name, MAX(route_count) - MIN(route_count) AS route_count_range, VAR_SAMP(route_count) AS route_count_variance, STDDEV(route_count) AS route_count_stddev FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN (SELECT sr.SUBNET_ID, COUNT(*) AS route_count FROM SUBNETROUTES AS sr GROUP BY sr.SUBNET_ID, sr.NEXTHOP) AS route_stats ON s.ID = route_stats.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND s.ID IN ('012993ae-5d73-4aad-834b-e87a31e99d48') GROUP BY s.NAME ORDER BY route_count_range DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT sd.NAME AS subnet_name, sd.TENANT_ID, sd.NETWORK_ID, sd.IP_VERSION, sd.CIDR, sd.GATEWAY_IP, sd.ENABLE_DHCP, sd.FIRST_IP, sd.LAST_IP, sd.max_standard_attr_id_next2, srs.route_count_range, srs.route_count_variance, srs.route_count_stddev FROM SubnetDetails AS sd JOIN SubnetRouteStats AS srs ON sd.NAME = srs.subnet_name
WITH PortDetails AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), StatusVariance AS (SELECT p.STATUS, VAR_SAMP(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS standard_attr_id_variance FROM PORTS AS p JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE NOT p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID IS NULL AND ia.IP_ADDRESS IN ('10.150.64.113/8') GROUP BY p.STATUS ORDER BY standard_attr_id_variance DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT pd.PORT_ID, pd.HOST, pd.VIF_TYPE, pd.VNIC_TYPE, pd.TENANT_ID, pd.NETWORK_ID, pd.MAC_ADDRESS, pd.STATUS, pd.DEVICE_OWNER, pd.window_column, sv.standard_attr_id_variance FROM PortDetails AS pd LEFT JOIN StatusVariance AS sv ON pd.STATUS = sv.STATUS
WITH Subq1 AS (SELECT p.status, CAST(STDDEV(p.standard_attr_id) AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(AVG(p.standard_attr_id), 0), 0) AS coefficient_of_variation FROM securitygroupportbindings AS sgpb JOIN ports AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.tenant_id IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p.status HAVING AVG(p.standard_attr_id) > 0 ORDER BY coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST), Subq2 AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS min_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS spb ON p.ID = spb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER) SELECT s2.port_status, s2.device_owner, s2.port_count, s2.min_standard_attr_id, s1.coefficient_of_variation FROM Subq2 AS s2 JOIN Subq1 AS s1 ON s2.port_status = s1.status
WITH ActiveTenantNetworks AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE' AND n.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), AdminUpTenantPorts AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT n.tenant_id, COUNT(DISTINCT n.network_id) AS active_network_count, COUNT(DISTINCT p.PORT_ID) AS admin_up_port_count, ROUND(CAST(CAST(COUNT(DISTINCT p.PORT_ID) AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(COUNT(DISTINCT n.network_id), 0), 0) AS DECIMAL), 2) AS ports_per_active_network_ratio, ROUND(CAST(AVG(p.window_column) AS DECIMAL), 2) AS avg_port_window_standard_attr_id, MAX(n.standard_attr_updated_at) AS latest_network_standard_attr_updated_at FROM ActiveTenantNetworks AS n JOIN AdminUpTenantPorts AS p ON p.tenant_id = n.tenant_id AND p.NETWORK_ID = n.network_id WHERE n.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY n.tenant_id ORDER BY ports_per_active_network_ratio DESC NULLS LAST
WITH sq1 AS (SELECT n.ACTION, VAR_SAMP(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_variance FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' AND n.TARGET_TENANT = '*' GROUP BY n.ACTION ORDER BY ip_version_variance DESC NULLS LAST), sq2 AS (SELECT n.name, n.status, n.admin_state_up, r.action, r.target_tenant, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY n.id ORDER BY n.id NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM networksecuritybindings AS b JOIN networks AS n ON b.network_id = n.id JOIN networkrbacs AS r ON n.id = r.object_id WHERE n.status = 'ACTIVE' AND NOT n.id IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b'))) SELECT sq2.name, sq2.status, sq2.admin_state_up, sq2.action, sq2.target_tenant, sq2.window_column, sq1.ip_version_variance FROM sq2 CROSS JOIN sq1 ORDER BY sq1.ip_version_variance DESC NULLS LAST, sq2.name NULLS FIRST
WITH SubnetDetails AS (SELECT s.NAME, s.TENANT_ID, s.NETWORK_ID, s.IP_VERSION, s.CIDR, s.GATEWAY_IP, s.ENABLE_DHCP, p.FIRST_IP, p.LAST_IP, MAX(s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (ORDER BY s.ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_standard_attr_id_next2 FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN IPALLOCATIONPOOLS AS p ON s.ID = p.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND s.ID IN ('012993ae-5d73-4aad-834b-e87a31e99d48')), SubnetRouteDetails AS (SELECT r.DESTINATION, r.NEXTHOP, s.NAME AS subnet_name, s.CIDR AS subnet_cidr, p.FIRST_IP, p.LAST_IP, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY r.SUBNET_ID ORDER BY r.SUBNET_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SUBNETROUTES AS r JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON r.SUBNET_ID = s.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONPOOLS AS p ON s.ID = p.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND s.ID IN ('012993ae-5d73-4aad-834b-e87a31e99d48')) SELECT sd.NAME AS subnet_name, sd.TENANT_ID, sd.NETWORK_ID, sd.IP_VERSION, sd.CIDR, sd.GATEWAY_IP, sd.ENABLE_DHCP, sd.FIRST_IP AS allocation_first_ip, sd.LAST_IP AS allocation_last_ip, sd.max_standard_attr_id_next2, srd.DESTINATION AS route_destination, srd.NEXTHOP AS route_nexthop, srd.window_column AS route_dense_rank_within_subnet FROM SubnetDetails AS sd LEFT JOIN SubnetRouteDetails AS srd ON sd.NAME = srd.subnet_name AND sd.CIDR = srd.subnet_cidr AND sd.FIRST_IP = srd.FIRST_IP AND sd.LAST_IP = srd.LAST_IP ORDER BY sd.NAME NULLS FIRST, srd.DESTINATION NULLS FIRST
WITH port_security_binding_stats_by_status AS (SELECT p.status, MAX(ps.binding_count) - MIN(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_range, VAR_SAMP(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_variance, STDDEV(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_stddev FROM ports AS p JOIN (SELECT p2.id AS port_id, COUNT(*) AS binding_count FROM ports AS p2 JOIN portsecuritybindings AS b ON p2.id = b.port_id WHERE p2.tenant_id IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p2.id) AS ps ON p.id = ps.port_id GROUP BY p.status ORDER BY binding_count_range DESC NULLS LAST), down_ports_with_security_groups AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (ORDER BY p.ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.NETWORK_ID IN ('0a1d0a27-cffa-4de3-92c5-9d3fd3f2e74d')), down_ports_with_security_groups_tenant_filtered AS (SELECT dpsg.NAME, dpsg.NETWORK_ID, dpsg.MAC_ADDRESS, dpsg.ADMIN_STATE_UP, dpsg.STATUS, dpsg.DEVICE_OWNER, dpsg.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, dpsg.window_column FROM down_ports_with_security_groups AS dpsg JOIN ports AS p ON p.network_id = dpsg.NETWORK_ID AND p.mac_address = dpsg.MAC_ADDRESS WHERE p.tenant_id IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7')) SELECT psbs.status, psbs.binding_count_range, psbs.binding_count_variance, psbs.binding_count_stddev, COUNT(*) AS down_port_rows_for_tenant_and_network FROM port_security_binding_stats_by_status AS psbs CROSS JOIN down_ports_with_security_groups_tenant_filtered AS dptf GROUP BY psbs.status, psbs.binding_count_range, psbs.binding_count_variance, psbs.binding_count_stddev ORDER BY psbs.binding_count_range DESC NULLS LAST
WITH Subq1 AS (SELECT p.STATUS, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) AS min_admin_state_up, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS avg_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE b.VIF_TYPE = 'ovs' AND p.DEVICE_ID IN ('da811c75-0f9b-462c-b020-8ea55f6ca7c7') GROUP BY p.STATUS), Subq2 AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (ORDER BY p.ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.NETWORK_ID IN ('0a1d0a27-cffa-4de3-92c5-9d3fd3f2e74d')) SELECT s1.STATUS AS port_status, s1.min_admin_state_up AS ovs_device_min_admin_state_up, s1.avg_standard_attr_id AS ovs_device_avg_standard_attr_id, COUNT(*) AS down_ports_in_network_with_security_group_count, AVG(s2.window_column) AS avg_window_min_admin_state_up_for_down_ports FROM Subq1 AS s1 CROSS JOIN Subq2 AS s2 GROUP BY s1.STATUS, s1.min_admin_state_up, s1.avg_standard_attr_id
WITH default_external_networks AS (SELECT e.NETWORK_ID, e.IS_DEFAULT, n.TENANT_ID, n.NAME, n.STATUS, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP, n.MTU, n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID, MIN(n.MTU) OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.TENANT_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM EXTERNALNETWORKS AS e JOIN NETWORKS AS n ON e.NETWORK_ID = n.ID WHERE e.IS_DEFAULT = 0 AND NOT n.ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b'))), port_status_device_owner_summary AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' AND NOT p.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER) SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT den.TENANT_ID) AS tenant_count_with_default_external_networks, COUNT(*) AS port_status_device_owner_group_count FROM default_external_networks AS den CROSS JOIN port_status_device_owner_summary AS psdos
WITH PortOwnerStatusStats AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' AND NOT p.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), PortStatusIPLenCV AS (SELECT p.status, CAST(STDDEV(LENGTH(ia.ip_address)) AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(AVG(LENGTH(ia.ip_address)), 0), 0) AS coefficient_of_variation FROM portsecuritybindings AS psb JOIN ports AS p ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON p.id = ia.port_id WHERE psb.port_security_enabled = 1 AND ia.ip_address IN ('10.150.64.113/8') GROUP BY p.status HAVING AVG(LENGTH(ia.ip_address)) > 0 ORDER BY coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT pos.port_status, pos.device_owner, pos.port_count, pos.earliest_standard_attr_update, psc.coefficient_of_variation FROM PortOwnerStatusStats AS pos LEFT JOIN PortStatusIPLenCV AS psc ON pos.port_status = psc.status ORDER BY pos.port_count DESC NULLS LAST
WITH subq1 AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, sgpb.security_group_id, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.security_group_id ORDER BY p.id NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN' AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), subq2 AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(ia.ip_address) AS ip_allocation_count, SUM(CASE WHEN NOT ia.subnet_id IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS subnet_id_rows FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON ia.port_id = p.id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND NOT ia.ip_address IS NULL AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address) SELECT subq1.security_group_id, subq1.port_id AS down_port_id, subq1.tenant_id, subq1.mac_address, subq1.window_column AS down_port_rank_within_security_group, subq2.ip_allocation_count, subq2.subnet_id_rows FROM subq1 LEFT JOIN subq2 ON subq1.port_id = subq2.port_id ORDER BY subq1.security_group_id NULLS FIRST, subq1.window_column NULLS FIRST, subq1.port_id NULLS FIRST
WITH ActiveNetworks AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE' AND n.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), PortSecurityAndDns AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.name AS port_name, COUNT(sgpb.security_group_id) AS security_group_binding_count, SUM(CASE WHEN pd.port_id IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END) AS dns_record_count FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id LEFT JOIN PORTDNSES AS pd ON p.id = pd.port_id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.name) SELECT an.tenant_id, COUNT(DISTINCT an.network_id) AS active_network_count, COUNT(DISTINCT psd.port_id) AS admin_up_sg_bound_port_count, ROUND(CAST(CAST(COUNT(DISTINCT psd.port_id) AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(COUNT(DISTINCT an.network_id), 0), 0) AS DECIMAL), 2) AS avg_ports_per_active_network, SUM(psd.security_group_binding_count) AS total_security_group_bindings_across_ports, SUM(psd.dns_record_count) AS total_dns_records_across_ports FROM ActiveNetworks AS an CROSS JOIN PortSecurityAndDns AS psd GROUP BY an.tenant_id
WITH PortSecurityAndDNSStats AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.name AS port_name, COUNT(sgpb.security_group_id) AS security_group_binding_count, SUM(CASE WHEN pd.port_id IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END) AS dns_record_count FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id LEFT JOIN PORTDNSES AS pd ON p.id = pd.port_id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.name), PortBindingWindowStats AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT pbws.PORT_ID AS port_id, pbws.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, psds.port_name, pbws.HOST, pbws.VIF_TYPE, pbws.VNIC_TYPE, pbws.NETWORK_ID, pbws.MAC_ADDRESS, pbws.STATUS, pbws.DEVICE_OWNER, pbws.window_column, psds.security_group_binding_count, psds.dns_record_count FROM PortBindingWindowStats AS pbws JOIN PortSecurityAndDNSStats AS psds ON pbws.PORT_ID = psds.port_id ORDER BY pbws.PORT_ID NULLS FIRST
WITH default_external_networks AS (SELECT e.NETWORK_ID, e.IS_DEFAULT, n.TENANT_ID, n.NAME, n.STATUS, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP, n.MTU, n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID, MIN(n.MTU) OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.TENANT_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM EXTERNALNETWORKS AS e JOIN NETWORKS AS n ON e.NETWORK_ID = n.ID WHERE e.IS_DEFAULT = 0 AND NOT n.ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b'))), port_status_owner_stats AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' AND NOT p.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), top10_device_owner_port_counts AS (SELECT device_owner, SUM(port_count) AS total_ports FROM port_status_owner_stats GROUP BY device_owner ORDER BY total_ports DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 10) SELECT t.device_owner, t.total_ports, den.NETWORK_ID, den.IS_DEFAULT, den.TENANT_ID, den.NAME AS network_name, den.STATUS AS network_status, den.ADMIN_STATE_UP, den.MTU, den.STANDARD_ATTR_ID AS network_standard_attr_id, den.window_column AS min_mtu_within_tenant, psos.port_status, psos.port_count, psos.earliest_standard_attr_update FROM top10_device_owner_port_counts AS t JOIN port_status_owner_stats AS psos ON psos.device_owner = t.device_owner CROSS JOIN default_external_networks AS den ORDER BY t.total_ports DESC NULLS LAST, t.device_owner NULLS FIRST, psos.port_status NULLS FIRST
WITH StdAttrIdCVByStatus AS (SELECT p.status, CAST(STDDEV(p.standard_attr_id) AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(AVG(p.standard_attr_id), 0), 0) AS coefficient_of_variation FROM securitygroupportbindings AS sgpb JOIN ports AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.tenant_id IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p.status HAVING AVG(p.standard_attr_id) > 0 ORDER BY coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST), IpAddrLenCVByStatus AS (SELECT p.status, CAST(STDDEV(LENGTH(ia.ip_address)) AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(AVG(LENGTH(ia.ip_address)), 0), 0) AS coefficient_of_variation FROM portsecuritybindings AS psb JOIN ports AS p ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON p.id = ia.port_id WHERE psb.port_security_enabled = 1 AND ia.ip_address IN ('10.150.64.113/8') GROUP BY p.status HAVING AVG(LENGTH(ia.ip_address)) > 0 ORDER BY coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST), FilteredStatusComparison AS (SELECT s1.status, s1.coefficient_of_variation AS std_attr_id_coefficient_of_variation, s2.coefficient_of_variation AS ip_address_length_coefficient_of_variation FROM StdAttrIdCVByStatus AS s1 JOIN IpAddrLenCVByStatus AS s2 ON s1.status = s2.status) SELECT * FROM FilteredStatusComparison ORDER BY std_attr_id_coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST, ip_address_length_coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST
WITH PortStatusOwnerSecurityGroupStats AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS min_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS spb ON p.ID = spb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), PortStatusOvsBindingStats AS (SELECT p.STATUS, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) AS min_admin_state_up, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS avg_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE b.VIF_TYPE = 'ovs' AND p.DEVICE_ID IN ('da811c75-0f9b-462c-b020-8ea55f6ca7c7') GROUP BY p.STATUS) SELECT psosgs.port_status, psosgs.device_owner, psosgs.port_count, psosgs.min_standard_attr_id, psobs.min_admin_state_up, psobs.avg_standard_attr_id FROM PortStatusOwnerSecurityGroupStats AS psosgs JOIN PortStatusOvsBindingStats AS psobs ON psosgs.port_status = psobs.STATUS ORDER BY psosgs.port_count DESC NULLS LAST
WITH PortSecurityGroupRanks AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, sgpb.security_group_id, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.security_group_id ORDER BY p.id NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN' AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), ActiveTenantNetworks AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE' AND n.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), DownTenantPortsWithNetwork AS (SELECT psgr.port_id, psgr.tenant_id, psgr.mac_address, psgr.security_group_id, psgr.window_column AS port_rank_within_security_group, p.network_id FROM PortSecurityGroupRanks AS psgr JOIN PORTS AS p ON p.id = psgr.port_id WHERE psgr.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT atn.tenant_id, atn.network_id, atn.network_name, atn.admin_state_up, atn.mtu, atn.standard_attr_updated_at, atn.window_column AS network_row_number_within_tenant, dtp.port_id, dtp.mac_address, dtp.security_group_id, dtp.port_rank_within_security_group FROM ActiveTenantNetworks AS atn JOIN DownTenantPortsWithNetwork AS dtp ON dtp.network_id = atn.network_id AND dtp.tenant_id = atn.tenant_id ORDER BY atn.tenant_id NULLS FIRST, atn.network_name NULLS FIRST, dtp.security_group_id NULLS FIRST, dtp.port_id NULLS FIRST
WITH sq1 AS (SELECT p.status, CAST(STDDEV(LENGTH(ia.ip_address)) AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(AVG(LENGTH(ia.ip_address)), 0), 0) AS coefficient_of_variation FROM portsecuritybindings AS psb JOIN ports AS p ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON p.id = ia.port_id WHERE psb.port_security_enabled = 1 AND ia.ip_address IN ('10.150.64.113/8') GROUP BY p.status HAVING AVG(LENGTH(ia.ip_address)) > 0 ORDER BY coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST), sq2 AS (SELECT p.status, MAX(ps.binding_count) - MIN(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_range, VAR_SAMP(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_variance, STDDEV(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_stddev FROM ports AS p JOIN (SELECT p2.id AS port_id, COUNT(*) AS binding_count FROM ports AS p2 JOIN portsecuritybindings AS b ON p2.id = b.port_id WHERE p2.tenant_id IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p2.id) AS ps ON p.id = ps.port_id GROUP BY p.status ORDER BY binding_count_range DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT sq2.status, sq1.coefficient_of_variation, sq2.binding_count_range, sq2.binding_count_variance, sq2.binding_count_stddev FROM sq2 JOIN sq1 ON sq2.status = sq1.status ORDER BY sq2.binding_count_range DESC NULLS LAST
WITH DownPortsBySecurityGroup AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, sgpb.security_group_id, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.security_group_id ORDER BY p.id NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN' AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), AdminUpPortBindingAndDNSCounts AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.name AS port_name, COUNT(sgpb.security_group_id) AS security_group_binding_count, SUM(CASE WHEN pd.port_id IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END) AS dns_record_count FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id LEFT JOIN PORTDNSES AS pd ON p.id = pd.port_id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.name), DownPortsThatAreAlsoAdminUp AS (SELECT dpsg.port_id, dpsg.tenant_id, dpsg.mac_address, dpsg.security_group_id, dpsg.window_column, aup.port_name, aup.security_group_binding_count, aup.dns_record_count FROM DownPortsBySecurityGroup AS dpsg JOIN AdminUpPortBindingAndDNSCounts AS aup ON dpsg.port_id = aup.port_id AND dpsg.tenant_id = aup.tenant_id) SELECT port_id, tenant_id, mac_address, security_group_id, window_column AS port_rank_within_security_group_by_port_id, port_name, security_group_binding_count, dns_record_count FROM DownPortsThatAreAlsoAdminUp ORDER BY security_group_id NULLS FIRST, port_id NULLS FIRST
WITH port_status_owner_summary AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' AND NOT p.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), active_network_rbac_details AS (SELECT n.name, n.status, n.admin_state_up, r.action, r.target_tenant, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY n.id ORDER BY n.id NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM networksecuritybindings AS b JOIN networks AS n ON b.network_id = n.id JOIN networkrbacs AS r ON n.id = r.object_id WHERE n.status = 'ACTIVE' AND NOT n.id IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b'))), active_network_rbac_counts AS (SELECT action, target_tenant, COUNT(*) AS active_network_rbac_row_count FROM active_network_rbac_details GROUP BY action, target_tenant) SELECT p.port_status, p.device_owner, p.port_count, p.earliest_standard_attr_update, c.action, c.target_tenant, c.active_network_rbac_row_count FROM port_status_owner_summary AS p CROSS JOIN active_network_rbac_counts AS c
WITH Subq1 AS (SELECT n.ACTION, VAR_SAMP(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_variance FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' AND n.TARGET_TENANT = '*' GROUP BY n.ACTION ORDER BY ip_version_variance DESC NULLS LAST), Subq2 AS (SELECT n.ACTION AS rbac_action, AVG(s.IP_VERSION) AS avg_ip_version, MAX(s.IP_VERSION) - MIN(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_range, VAR_SAMP(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_variance FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION IN (4, 6) AND n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY n.ACTION ORDER BY ip_version_range DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT s1.ACTION, s1.ip_version_variance AS public_access_ip_version_variance, s2.avg_ip_version, s2.ip_version_range, s2.ip_version_variance AS overall_ip_version_variance FROM Subq1 AS s1 JOIN Subq2 AS s2 ON s1.ACTION = s2.rbac_action
WITH SubnetAttributeWindow AS (SELECT s.NAME, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY sa.UPDATED_AT NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND s.ID IN ('012993ae-5d73-4aad-834b-e87a31e99d48')), SubnetRouteStats AS (SELECT s.NAME AS subnet_name, MAX(route_count) - MIN(route_count) AS route_count_range, VAR_SAMP(route_count) AS route_count_variance, STDDEV(route_count) AS route_count_stddev FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN (SELECT sr.SUBNET_ID, COUNT(*) AS route_count FROM SUBNETROUTES AS sr GROUP BY sr.SUBNET_ID, sr.NEXTHOP) AS route_stats ON s.ID = route_stats.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND s.ID IN ('012993ae-5d73-4aad-834b-e87a31e99d48') GROUP BY s.NAME ORDER BY route_count_range DESC NULLS LAST), CombinedSubnetMetrics AS (SELECT saw.NAME AS subnet_name, saw.window_column, NULL AS route_count_range, NULL AS route_count_variance, NULL AS route_count_stddev, 'Standard Attribute Window Rank' AS reason FROM SubnetAttributeWindow AS saw UNION ALL SELECT srs.subnet_name, NULL AS window_column, srs.route_count_range, srs.route_count_variance, srs.route_count_stddev, 'Route Count Stats' AS reason FROM SubnetRouteStats AS srs) SELECT * FROM CombinedSubnetMetrics ORDER BY subnet_name NULLS FIRST, reason NULLS FIRST
WITH CoeffOfVarByStatus AS (SELECT p.status, CAST(STDDEV(LENGTH(ia.ip_address)) AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(AVG(LENGTH(ia.ip_address)), 0), 0) AS coefficient_of_variation FROM portsecuritybindings AS psb JOIN ports AS p ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON p.id = ia.port_id WHERE psb.port_security_enabled = 1 AND ia.ip_address IN ('10.150.64.113/8') GROUP BY p.status HAVING AVG(LENGTH(ia.ip_address)) > 0 ORDER BY coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST), StdAttrIdVarByStatus AS (SELECT p.STATUS, VAR_SAMP(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS standard_attr_id_variance FROM PORTS AS p JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE NOT p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID IS NULL AND ia.IP_ADDRESS IN ('10.150.64.113/8') GROUP BY p.STATUS ORDER BY standard_attr_id_variance DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT cv.status, cv.coefficient_of_variation, sv.standard_attr_id_variance FROM CoeffOfVarByStatus AS cv JOIN StdAttrIdVarByStatus AS sv ON cv.status = sv.status
WITH port_status_cov AS (SELECT p.status, CAST(STDDEV(p.standard_attr_id) AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(AVG(p.standard_attr_id), 0), 0) AS coefficient_of_variation FROM securitygroupportbindings AS sgpb JOIN ports AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.tenant_id IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p.status HAVING AVG(p.standard_attr_id) > 0 ORDER BY coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST), down_ports_by_owner AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS min_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS spb ON p.ID = spb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER) SELECT psc.status, psc.coefficient_of_variation, dpo.device_owner, dpo.port_count, dpo.min_standard_attr_id FROM port_status_cov AS psc JOIN down_ports_by_owner AS dpo ON psc.status = dpo.port_status
WITH SubnetWindowRanks AS (SELECT s.NAME, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY sa.UPDATED_AT NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND s.ID IN ('012993ae-5d73-4aad-834b-e87a31e99d48')), SubnetRouteStats AS (SELECT s.NAME AS subnet_name, MAX(route_count) - MIN(route_count) AS route_count_range, VAR_SAMP(route_count) AS route_count_variance, STDDEV(route_count) AS route_count_stddev FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN (SELECT sr.SUBNET_ID, COUNT(*) AS route_count FROM SUBNETROUTES AS sr GROUP BY sr.SUBNET_ID, sr.NEXTHOP) AS route_stats ON s.ID = route_stats.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND s.ID IN ('012993ae-5d73-4aad-834b-e87a31e99d48') GROUP BY s.NAME ORDER BY route_count_range DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT swr.NAME AS subnet_name, swr.window_column, srs.route_count_range, srs.route_count_variance, srs.route_count_stddev FROM SubnetWindowRanks AS swr JOIN SubnetRouteStats AS srs ON srs.subnet_name = swr.NAME ORDER BY swr.NAME NULLS FIRST, swr.window_column NULLS FIRST
WITH PortStatusIPLenCV AS (SELECT p.status, CAST(STDDEV(LENGTH(ia.ip_address)) AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(AVG(LENGTH(ia.ip_address)), 0), 0) AS coefficient_of_variation FROM portsecuritybindings AS psb JOIN ports AS p ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON p.id = ia.port_id WHERE psb.port_security_enabled = 1 AND ia.ip_address IN ('10.150.64.113/8') GROUP BY p.status HAVING AVG(LENGTH(ia.ip_address)) > 0 ORDER BY coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST), AdminUpTenantPorts AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT a.PORT_ID, a.HOST, a.VIF_TYPE, a.VNIC_TYPE, a.TENANT_ID, a.NETWORK_ID, a.MAC_ADDRESS, a.STATUS, a.DEVICE_OWNER, a.window_column, cv.coefficient_of_variation FROM AdminUpTenantPorts AS a JOIN PortStatusIPLenCV AS cv ON a.STATUS = cv.status
WITH PortIpAllocationStats AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(ia.ip_address) AS ip_allocation_count, SUM(CASE WHEN NOT ia.subnet_id IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS subnet_id_rows FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON ia.port_id = p.id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND NOT ia.ip_address IS NULL AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address), DownPortsWindowBySecurityGroup AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), SecurityGroupCountsForTenant AS (SELECT sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID AS security_group_id, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS total_ports_in_sg_for_tenant, COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN p.STATUS = 'DOWN' THEN p.ID END) AS down_ports_in_sg_for_tenant FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID WHERE p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID) SELECT sct.security_group_id, sct.total_ports_in_sg_for_tenant, sct.down_ports_in_sg_for_tenant, AVG(dpw.window_column) AS avg_down_port_running_sum_standard_attr_id, COUNT(DISTINCT pia.port_id) AS admin_up_ports_with_ip_allocations_in_sg, AVG(pia.ip_allocation_count) AS avg_ip_allocations_per_admin_up_port_in_sg, MAX(pia.subnet_id_rows) AS max_subnet_id_rows_among_admin_up_ports_in_sg FROM SecurityGroupCountsForTenant AS sct JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb ON sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID = sct.security_group_id LEFT JOIN DownPortsWindowBySecurityGroup AS dpw ON dpw.port_id = sgpb.PORT_ID LEFT JOIN PortIpAllocationStats AS pia ON pia.port_id = sgpb.PORT_ID WHERE sct.down_ports_in_sg_for_tenant > 0 AND (sct.total_ports_in_sg_for_tenant - sct.down_ports_in_sg_for_tenant) > 0 GROUP BY sct.security_group_id, sct.total_ports_in_sg_for_tenant, sct.down_ports_in_sg_for_tenant ORDER BY sct.total_ports_in_sg_for_tenant DESC NULLS LAST
WITH PortStatusOwnerStats AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' AND NOT p.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), OvsBoundDevicePortStats AS (SELECT p.STATUS, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) AS min_admin_state_up, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS avg_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE b.VIF_TYPE = 'ovs' AND p.DEVICE_ID IN ('da811c75-0f9b-462c-b020-8ea55f6ca7c7') GROUP BY p.STATUS) SELECT psos.port_status, psos.device_owner, psos.port_count, psos.earliest_standard_attr_update, obdps.min_admin_state_up, obdps.avg_standard_attr_id FROM PortStatusOwnerStats AS psos JOIN OvsBoundDevicePortStats AS obdps ON psos.port_status = obdps.STATUS ORDER BY psos.port_status NULLS FIRST, psos.device_owner NULLS FIRST
WITH PortIpAllocationStats AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(ia.ip_address) AS ip_allocation_count, SUM(CASE WHEN NOT ia.subnet_id IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS subnet_id_rows FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON ia.port_id = p.id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND NOT ia.ip_address IS NULL AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address), DownPortsSecurityGroupWindow AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT dpsgw.tenant_id, dpsgw.port_id, dpsgw.mac_address, dpsgw.window_column, pis.ip_allocation_count, pis.subnet_id_rows FROM DownPortsSecurityGroupWindow AS dpsgw JOIN PortIpAllocationStats AS pis ON pis.port_id = dpsgw.port_id AND pis.tenant_id = dpsgw.tenant_id WHERE pis.ip_allocation_count >= 1 ORDER BY dpsgw.window_column DESC NULLS LAST, dpsgw.port_id NULLS FIRST
WITH RbacIpVersionStddev AS (SELECT n.ACTION, STDDEV(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_stddev FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.ENABLE_DHCP = 1 AND NOT s.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY n.ACTION HAVING COUNT(*) > 5 ORDER BY ip_version_stddev DESC NULLS LAST), AccessAsSharedIpVersionStats AS (SELECT n.ACTION AS rbac_action, AVG(s.IP_VERSION) AS avg_ip_version, MAX(s.IP_VERSION) - MIN(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_range, VAR_SAMP(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_variance FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION IN (4, 6) AND n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY n.ACTION ORDER BY ip_version_range DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT aasis.rbac_action, aasis.avg_ip_version, aasis.ip_version_range, aasis.ip_version_variance, rivs.ip_version_stddev FROM AccessAsSharedIpVersionStats AS aasis JOIN RbacIpVersionStddev AS rivs ON aasis.rbac_action = rivs.ACTION ORDER BY aasis.ip_version_range DESC NULLS LAST
WITH sq1 AS (SELECT p.status, CAST(STDDEV(p.standard_attr_id) AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(AVG(p.standard_attr_id), 0), 0) AS coefficient_of_variation FROM securitygroupportbindings AS sgpb JOIN ports AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.tenant_id IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p.status HAVING AVG(p.standard_attr_id) > 0 ORDER BY coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST), sq2 AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT sq2.PORT_ID, sq2.HOST, sq2.VIF_TYPE, sq2.VNIC_TYPE, sq2.TENANT_ID, sq2.NETWORK_ID, sq2.MAC_ADDRESS, sq2.STATUS, sq2.DEVICE_OWNER, sq2.window_column, sq1.coefficient_of_variation FROM sq2 JOIN sq1 ON sq2.STATUS = sq1.status ORDER BY sq1.coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST, sq2.window_column DESC NULLS LAST
WITH sq1 AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' AND NOT p.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), sq2 AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT sq2.VIF_TYPE, sq2.STATUS AS port_status, sq2.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, sq1.port_count, sq1.earliest_standard_attr_update, COUNT(sq2.PORT_ID) AS tenant_port_rows, AVG(sq2.window_column) AS avg_window_standard_attr_id FROM sq2 JOIN sq1 ON sq2.STATUS = sq1.port_status AND sq2.DEVICE_OWNER = sq1.device_owner WHERE NOT sq2.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY sq2.VIF_TYPE, sq2.STATUS, sq2.DEVICE_OWNER, sq1.port_count, sq1.earliest_standard_attr_update ORDER BY tenant_port_rows DESC NULLS LAST
WITH ports_rank_within_sg AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, sgpb.security_group_id, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.security_group_id ORDER BY p.id NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN' AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), ports_running_sum_std_attr AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT prws.port_id, prws.tenant_id, prws.mac_address, prws.security_group_id, prws.window_column AS rank_within_security_group_by_port_id, prssa.window_column AS running_sum_standard_attr_id_within_security_group FROM ports_rank_within_sg AS prws JOIN ports_running_sum_std_attr AS prssa ON prssa.port_id = prws.port_id AND prssa.tenant_id = prws.tenant_id AND prssa.mac_address = prws.mac_address
WITH ActiveTenantNetworks AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE' AND n.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), AdminUpTenantPorts AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), TenantPortCounts AS (SELECT ap.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, COUNT(DISTINCT ap.PORT_ID) AS admin_up_port_count FROM AdminUpTenantPorts AS ap GROUP BY ap.TENANT_ID) SELECT an.tenant_id, tpc.admin_up_port_count, COUNT(DISTINCT an.network_id) AS active_network_count, MAX(an.standard_attr_updated_at) AS latest_network_standard_attr_updated_at FROM ActiveTenantNetworks AS an JOIN TenantPortCounts AS tpc ON an.tenant_id = tpc.tenant_id WHERE tpc.admin_up_port_count >= 2 GROUP BY an.tenant_id, tpc.admin_up_port_count ORDER BY tpc.admin_up_port_count DESC NULLS LAST, active_network_count DESC NULLS LAST
WITH RankedPortsBySecurityGroup AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, sgpb.security_group_id, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.security_group_id ORDER BY p.id NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN' AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), RunningSumStandardAttrIdBySecurityGroup AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT r.port_id, r.tenant_id, r.mac_address, r.security_group_id, r.window_column AS rank_within_security_group_by_port_id, s.window_column AS running_sum_standard_attr_id_within_security_group FROM RankedPortsBySecurityGroup AS r JOIN RunningSumStandardAttrIdBySecurityGroup AS s ON s.port_id = r.port_id AND s.tenant_id = r.tenant_id AND s.mac_address = r.mac_address
WITH DownPortsBySecurityGroup AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), AdminUpPortsByVifType AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT a.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, COUNT(DISTINCT a.PORT_ID) AS admin_up_port_count, COUNT(DISTINCT d.port_id) AS down_port_count, ROUND(CAST(CAST(COUNT(DISTINCT d.port_id) AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(COUNT(DISTINCT a.PORT_ID), 0), 0) AS DECIMAL), 2) AS down_to_admin_up_port_ratio, AVG(a.window_column) AS avg_admin_up_window_avg_standard_attr_id, AVG(d.window_column) AS avg_down_window_running_sum_standard_attr_id FROM AdminUpPortsByVifType AS a LEFT JOIN DownPortsBySecurityGroup AS d ON d.tenant_id = a.TENANT_ID GROUP BY a.TENANT_ID ORDER BY down_to_admin_up_port_ratio DESC NULLS LAST
WITH SubnetPoolsAndMaxStdAttrNext2 AS (SELECT s.NAME, s.TENANT_ID, s.NETWORK_ID, s.IP_VERSION, s.CIDR, s.GATEWAY_IP, s.ENABLE_DHCP, p.FIRST_IP, p.LAST_IP, MAX(s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (ORDER BY s.ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_standard_attr_id_next2 FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN IPALLOCATIONPOOLS AS p ON s.ID = p.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND s.ID IN ('012993ae-5d73-4aad-834b-e87a31e99d48')), SubnetStdAttrDenseRankWindow AS (SELECT s.NAME, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY sa.UPDATED_AT NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND s.ID IN ('012993ae-5d73-4aad-834b-e87a31e99d48')) SELECT sp.NAME AS subnet_name, sp.TENANT_ID, sp.NETWORK_ID, sp.IP_VERSION, sp.CIDR, sp.GATEWAY_IP, sp.ENABLE_DHCP, sp.FIRST_IP, sp.LAST_IP, sp.max_standard_attr_id_next2, sr.window_column AS dense_rank_by_updated_at_window FROM SubnetPoolsAndMaxStdAttrNext2 AS sp JOIN SubnetStdAttrDenseRankWindow AS sr ON sp.NAME = sr.NAME
WITH PortStatusOwnerSecurityGroupStats AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS min_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS spb ON p.ID = spb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), PortStatusBindingCountDispersion AS (SELECT p.status, MAX(ps.binding_count) - MIN(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_range, VAR_SAMP(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_variance, STDDEV(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_stddev FROM ports AS p JOIN (SELECT p2.id AS port_id, COUNT(*) AS binding_count FROM ports AS p2 JOIN portsecuritybindings AS b ON p2.id = b.port_id WHERE p2.tenant_id IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p2.id) AS ps ON p.id = ps.port_id GROUP BY p.status ORDER BY binding_count_range DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT psosgs.port_status, psosgs.device_owner, psosgs.port_count, psosgs.min_standard_attr_id, psbcd.binding_count_range, psbcd.binding_count_variance, psbcd.binding_count_stddev FROM PortStatusOwnerSecurityGroupStats AS psosgs JOIN PortStatusBindingCountDispersion AS psbcd ON psosgs.port_status = psbcd.status ORDER BY psosgs.port_status NULLS FIRST, psosgs.device_owner NULLS FIRST
WITH DefaultExternalNetworks AS (SELECT e.NETWORK_ID, e.IS_DEFAULT, n.TENANT_ID, n.NAME, n.STATUS, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP, n.MTU, n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID, MIN(n.MTU) OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.TENANT_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM EXTERNALNETWORKS AS e JOIN NETWORKS AS n ON e.NETWORK_ID = n.ID WHERE e.IS_DEFAULT = 0 AND NOT n.ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b'))), PortStatusOwnerAgg AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' AND NOT p.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), PortsOnDefaultExternalNetworks AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.NETWORK_ID, p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner FROM PORTS AS p WHERE p.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT NETWORK_ID FROM DefaultExternalNetworks)) SELECT den.NETWORK_ID, den.IS_DEFAULT, den.TENANT_ID, den.NAME AS network_name, den.STATUS AS network_status, den.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS network_admin_state_up, den.MTU, den.STANDARD_ATTR_ID AS network_standard_attr_id, den.window_column AS tenant_min_mtu, psa.port_status, psa.device_owner, psa.port_count, psa.earliest_standard_attr_update FROM DefaultExternalNetworks AS den JOIN PortsOnDefaultExternalNetworks AS pden ON pden.NETWORK_ID = den.NETWORK_ID JOIN PortStatusOwnerAgg AS psa ON psa.port_status = pden.port_status AND psa.device_owner = pden.device_owner ORDER BY den.TENANT_ID NULLS FIRST, den.NETWORK_ID NULLS FIRST, psa.port_status NULLS FIRST, psa.device_owner NULLS FIRST
WITH ActiveTenantNetworks AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE' AND n.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), AdminUpTenantPorts AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), NetworkPortRollup AS (SELECT atn.tenant_id, atn.network_id, atn.network_name, atn.admin_state_up, atn.mtu, atn.standard_attr_updated_at, atn.window_column AS network_row_number_within_tenant, COUNT(DISTINCT autp.PORT_ID) AS total_admin_up_ports, COUNT(DISTINCT autp.VIF_TYPE) AS distinct_vif_types, AVG(autp.window_column) AS avg_port_window_avg_standard_attr_id FROM ActiveTenantNetworks AS atn LEFT JOIN AdminUpTenantPorts AS autp ON autp.NETWORK_ID = atn.network_id AND autp.TENANT_ID = atn.tenant_id GROUP BY atn.tenant_id, atn.network_id, atn.network_name, atn.admin_state_up, atn.mtu, atn.standard_attr_updated_at, atn.window_column) SELECT tenant_id, network_id, network_name, admin_state_up, mtu, standard_attr_updated_at, network_row_number_within_tenant, total_admin_up_ports, distinct_vif_types, avg_port_window_avg_standard_attr_id FROM NetworkPortRollup ORDER BY total_admin_up_ports DESC NULLS LAST, network_name ASC NULLS FIRST
WITH PortStatusVariation AS (SELECT p.status, CAST(STDDEV(p.standard_attr_id) AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(AVG(p.standard_attr_id), 0), 0) AS coefficient_of_variation FROM securitygroupportbindings AS sgpb JOIN ports AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.tenant_id IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p.status HAVING AVG(p.standard_attr_id) > 0 ORDER BY coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST), PortStatusDeviceOwnerStats AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS min_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS spb ON p.ID = spb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER) SELECT psds.port_status, psds.device_owner, psds.port_count, psds.min_standard_attr_id, psv.coefficient_of_variation FROM PortStatusDeviceOwnerStats AS psds JOIN PortStatusVariation AS psv ON psv.status = psds.port_status WHERE psv.coefficient_of_variation > 0 ORDER BY psv.coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST, psds.port_count DESC NULLS LAST
WITH rbac_ip_version_stats AS (SELECT n.ACTION AS rbac_action, AVG(s.IP_VERSION) AS avg_ip_version, MAX(s.IP_VERSION) - MIN(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_range, VAR_SAMP(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_variance FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION IN (4, 6) AND n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY n.ACTION ORDER BY ip_version_range DESC NULLS LAST), active_network_rbac_details AS (SELECT n.name, n.status, n.admin_state_up, r.action, r.target_tenant, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY n.id ORDER BY n.id NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM networksecuritybindings AS b JOIN networks AS n ON b.network_id = n.id JOIN networkrbacs AS r ON n.id = r.object_id WHERE n.status = 'ACTIVE' AND NOT n.id IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b'))) SELECT d.name, d.status, d.admin_state_up, d.action, d.target_tenant, d.window_column, s.rbac_action, s.avg_ip_version, s.ip_version_range, s.ip_version_variance FROM active_network_rbac_details AS d CROSS JOIN rbac_ip_version_stats AS s WHERE s.rbac_action = 'access_as_shared'
WITH PortSecurityAndDnsCounts AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.name AS port_name, COUNT(sgpb.security_group_id) AS security_group_binding_count, SUM(CASE WHEN pd.port_id IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END) AS dns_record_count FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id LEFT JOIN PORTDNSES AS pd ON p.id = pd.port_id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.name), DownPortsWindowedAttrSum AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT psc.tenant_id, COUNT(DISTINCT psc.port_id) AS admin_up_bound_port_count, AVG(psc.security_group_binding_count) AS avg_security_group_bindings_per_admin_up_port, SUM(psc.dns_record_count) AS total_dns_records_on_admin_up_ports, COUNT(DISTINCT dpa.port_id) AS down_bound_port_count, AVG(dpa.window_column) AS avg_running_sum_standard_attr_id_within_security_group_for_down_ports FROM PortSecurityAndDnsCounts AS psc JOIN DownPortsWindowedAttrSum AS dpa ON psc.tenant_id = dpa.tenant_id WHERE psc.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY psc.tenant_id ORDER BY admin_up_bound_port_count DESC NULLS LAST
WITH PortStatusBindingStats AS (SELECT p.status, MAX(ps.binding_count) - MIN(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_range, VAR_SAMP(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_variance, STDDEV(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_stddev FROM ports AS p JOIN (SELECT p2.id AS port_id, COUNT(*) AS binding_count FROM ports AS p2 JOIN portsecuritybindings AS b ON p2.id = b.port_id WHERE p2.tenant_id IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p2.id) AS ps ON p.id = ps.port_id GROUP BY p.status ORDER BY binding_count_range DESC NULLS LAST), AdminUpPortWindowDetails AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT aupwd.STATUS, psbs.binding_count_range, psbs.binding_count_variance, psbs.binding_count_stddev, COUNT(DISTINCT aupwd.PORT_ID) AS admin_up_ports_with_security_bindings, AVG(aupwd.window_column) AS avg_window_standard_attr_id FROM AdminUpPortWindowDetails AS aupwd JOIN PortStatusBindingStats AS psbs ON aupwd.STATUS = psbs.status GROUP BY aupwd.STATUS, psbs.binding_count_range, psbs.binding_count_variance, psbs.binding_count_stddev ORDER BY admin_up_ports_with_security_bindings DESC NULLS LAST
WITH PortStatusOwnerStats AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS min_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS spb ON p.ID = spb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), AdminUpPortWindowStats AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT psos.port_status, psos.device_owner, psos.port_count, psos.min_standard_attr_id, aupws.VIF_TYPE, COUNT(DISTINCT aupws.PORT_ID) AS admin_up_ports, AVG(aupws.window_column) AS avg_window_standard_attr_id FROM PortStatusOwnerStats AS psos JOIN AdminUpPortWindowStats AS aupws ON psos.port_status = aupws.STATUS AND psos.device_owner = aupws.DEVICE_OWNER GROUP BY psos.port_status, psos.device_owner, psos.port_count, psos.min_standard_attr_id, aupws.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY psos.port_count DESC NULLS LAST
WITH PortSecurityGroupStats AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS min_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS spb ON p.ID = spb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), OvsBindingPortStats AS (SELECT p.STATUS, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) AS min_admin_state_up, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS avg_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE b.VIF_TYPE = 'ovs' AND p.DEVICE_ID IN ('da811c75-0f9b-462c-b020-8ea55f6ca7c7') GROUP BY p.STATUS) SELECT psgs.port_status, psgs.device_owner, psgs.port_count, psgs.min_standard_attr_id, obs.min_admin_state_up, obs.avg_standard_attr_id FROM PortSecurityGroupStats AS psgs JOIN OvsBindingPortStats AS obs ON psgs.port_status = obs.STATUS
WITH PortIPStats AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(ia.ip_address) AS ip_allocation_count, SUM(CASE WHEN NOT ia.subnet_id IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS subnet_id_rows FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON ia.port_id = p.id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND NOT ia.ip_address IS NULL AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address), PortBindingWindow AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), TenantAvgIPAlloc AS (SELECT p.tenant_id, AVG(ips.ip_allocation_count) AS avg_ip_allocation_count FROM ports AS p JOIN PortIPStats AS ips ON ips.port_id = p.id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.tenant_id) SELECT pbw.PORT_ID, pbw.TENANT_ID, pbw.NETWORK_ID, pbw.MAC_ADDRESS, pbw.HOST, pbw.VIF_TYPE, pbw.VNIC_TYPE, pbw.STATUS, pbw.DEVICE_OWNER, pbw.window_column, pis.ip_allocation_count, pis.subnet_id_rows FROM PortBindingWindow AS pbw JOIN PortIPStats AS pis ON pis.port_id = pbw.PORT_ID JOIN TenantAvgIPAlloc AS ta ON ta.tenant_id = pbw.TENANT_ID WHERE pis.ip_allocation_count > ta.avg_ip_allocation_count
WITH DownPortsBySecurityGroup AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, sgpb.security_group_id, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.security_group_id ORDER BY p.id NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN' AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), AdminUpPortsWithIPAllocations AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(ia.ip_address) AS ip_allocation_count, SUM(CASE WHEN NOT ia.subnet_id IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS subnet_id_rows FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON ia.port_id = p.id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND NOT ia.ip_address IS NULL AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address) SELECT dpsg.security_group_id, COUNT(DISTINCT dpsg.port_id) AS down_port_count, COUNT(DISTINCT aup.port_id) AS admin_up_port_with_ip_count FROM DownPortsBySecurityGroup AS dpsg LEFT JOIN AdminUpPortsWithIPAllocations AS aup ON aup.tenant_id = dpsg.tenant_id GROUP BY dpsg.security_group_id HAVING (CAST(COUNT(DISTINCT aup.port_id) * 1.0 AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(COUNT(DISTINCT dpsg.port_id), 0), 0)) > 0.75
