WITH PortStatusOwnerStats AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' AND NOT p.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), PortStatusBindingStats AS (SELECT p.status, MAX(ps.binding_count) - MIN(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_range, VAR_SAMP(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_variance, STDDEV(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_stddev FROM ports AS p JOIN (SELECT p2.id AS port_id, COUNT(*) AS binding_count FROM ports AS p2 JOIN portsecuritybindings AS b ON p2.id = b.port_id WHERE p2.tenant_id IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p2.id) AS ps ON p.id = ps.port_id GROUP BY p.status ORDER BY binding_count_range DESC NULLS LAST), CombinedPortStatusStats AS (SELECT pso.port_status, pso.device_owner, pso.port_count, pso.earliest_standard_attr_update, pbs.binding_count_range, pbs.binding_count_variance, pbs.binding_count_stddev FROM PortStatusOwnerStats AS pso JOIN PortStatusBindingStats AS pbs ON pso.port_status = pbs.status) SELECT port_status, device_owner, port_count, earliest_standard_attr_update, binding_count_range, binding_count_variance, binding_count_stddev FROM CombinedPortStatusStats ORDER BY port_status NULLS FIRST, device_owner NULLS FIRST
WITH PortStatusVariation AS (SELECT p.status, CAST(STDDEV(p.standard_attr_id) AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(AVG(p.standard_attr_id), 0), 0) AS coefficient_of_variation FROM securitygroupportbindings AS sgpb JOIN ports AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.tenant_id IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p.status HAVING AVG(p.standard_attr_id) > 0 ORDER BY coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST), PortStatusDeviceOwnerStats AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' AND NOT p.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER) SELECT psdo.port_status AS status, psdo.device_owner, psdo.port_count, psdo.earliest_standard_attr_update, psv.coefficient_of_variation FROM PortStatusDeviceOwnerStats AS psdo JOIN PortStatusVariation AS psv ON psdo.port_status = psv.status ORDER BY psdo.port_count DESC NULLS LAST
WITH NetworkStatusSecurityBindingStats AS (SELECT n.STATUS AS network_status, VAR_SAMP(nsb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED) AS port_security_enabled_variance, STDDEV(nsb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED) AS port_security_enabled_stddev FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN NETWORKSECURITYBINDINGS AS nsb ON n.ID = nsb.NETWORK_ID WHERE NOT n.ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY n.STATUS ORDER BY port_security_enabled_variance DESC NULLS LAST), DefaultExternalNetworksByTenant AS (SELECT e.NETWORK_ID, e.IS_DEFAULT, n.TENANT_ID, n.NAME, n.STATUS, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP, n.MTU, n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID, MIN(n.MTU) OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.TENANT_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM EXTERNALNETWORKS AS e JOIN NETWORKS AS n ON e.NETWORK_ID = n.ID WHERE e.IS_DEFAULT = 0 AND NOT n.ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b'))) SELECT den.NETWORK_ID, den.IS_DEFAULT, den.TENANT_ID, den.NAME, den.STATUS, den.ADMIN_STATE_UP, den.MTU, den.STANDARD_ATTR_ID, den.window_column AS min_mtu_within_tenant, nssbs.port_security_enabled_variance, nssbs.port_security_enabled_stddev FROM DefaultExternalNetworksByTenant AS den JOIN NetworkStatusSecurityBindingStats AS nssbs ON den.STATUS = nssbs.network_status
WITH SubnetDNSOrderVariation AS (SELECT s.name AS subnet_name, CAST(STDDEV(d.order) AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(AVG(d.order), 0), 0) AS coefficient_of_variation FROM subnets AS s JOIN dnsnameservers AS d ON d.subnet_id = s.id JOIN ipallocationpools AS p ON p.subnet_id = s.id JOIN networks AS n ON n.id = s.network_id WHERE s.ip_version = 4 AND NOT n.id IN (SELECT r.object_id FROM networkrbacs AS r WHERE r.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (r.target_tenant = '*' OR r.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY s.name HAVING AVG(d.order) > 0 ORDER BY coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST), SharedSubnetWindowedAttrs AS (SELECT s.NAME AS subnet_name, s.CIDR AS subnet_cidr, n.TARGET_TENANT AS rbac_target_tenant, SUM(s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY s.IP_VERSION ORDER BY s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS n ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' AND n.TARGET_TENANT IN ('1ee6b5b4fe8c45cfa16c3ecdbb8ee02b')) SELECT sswa.subnet_name, sswa.subnet_cidr, sswa.rbac_target_tenant, sswa.window_column, sdo.coefficient_of_variation FROM SharedSubnetWindowedAttrs AS sswa JOIN SubnetDNSOrderVariation AS sdo ON sswa.subnet_name = sdo.subnet_name WHERE sdo.coefficient_of_variation > 0 ORDER BY sdo.coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST
WITH network_status_security_stats AS (SELECT n.STATUS AS network_status, VAR_SAMP(nsb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED) AS port_security_enabled_variance, STDDEV(nsb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED) AS port_security_enabled_stddev FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN NETWORKSECURITYBINDINGS AS nsb ON n.ID = nsb.NETWORK_ID WHERE NOT n.ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY n.STATUS ORDER BY port_security_enabled_variance DESC NULLS LAST), default_external_network_details AS (SELECT e.NETWORK_ID, e.IS_DEFAULT, n.TENANT_ID, n.NAME, n.STATUS, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP, n.MTU, n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID, MIN(n.MTU) OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.TENANT_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM EXTERNALNETWORKS AS e JOIN NETWORKS AS n ON e.NETWORK_ID = n.ID WHERE e.IS_DEFAULT = 0 AND NOT n.ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b'))) SELECT den.NETWORK_ID, den.IS_DEFAULT, den.TENANT_ID, den.NAME, den.STATUS, den.ADMIN_STATE_UP, den.MTU, den.STANDARD_ATTR_ID, den.window_column, nsss.port_security_enabled_variance, nsss.port_security_enabled_stddev FROM default_external_network_details AS den JOIN network_status_security_stats AS nsss ON den.STATUS = nsss.network_status
WITH TenantDownPortsBySecurityGroup AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, sgpb.security_group_id, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.security_group_id ORDER BY p.id NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN' AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), NetworkDownPortsWithAdminStateWindow AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (ORDER BY p.ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.NETWORK_ID IN ('0a1d0a27-cffa-4de3-92c5-9d3fd3f2e74d')) SELECT n.NAME, n.NETWORK_ID, n.MAC_ADDRESS, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP, n.STATUS, n.DEVICE_OWNER, n.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, n.window_column AS min_admin_state_up_window_3_preceding_1_following, t.port_id, t.tenant_id, t.window_column AS rank_within_security_group_by_port_id FROM NetworkDownPortsWithAdminStateWindow AS n JOIN TenantDownPortsBySecurityGroup AS t ON n.SECURITY_GROUP_ID = t.security_group_id ORDER BY n.NETWORK_ID NULLS FIRST, n.SECURITY_GROUP_ID NULLS FIRST, t.port_id NULLS FIRST
WITH PortStatusVariation AS (SELECT p.status, CAST(STDDEV(p.standard_attr_id) AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(AVG(p.standard_attr_id), 0), 0) AS coefficient_of_variation FROM securitygroupportbindings AS sgpb JOIN ports AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.tenant_id IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p.status HAVING AVG(p.standard_attr_id) > 0 ORDER BY coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST), PortStatusDeviceOwnerStats AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS min_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS spb ON p.ID = spb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER) SELECT psd.port_status, psd.device_owner, psd.port_count, psd.min_standard_attr_id, psv.coefficient_of_variation FROM PortStatusDeviceOwnerStats AS psd JOIN PortStatusVariation AS psv ON psd.port_status = psv.status ORDER BY psd.port_count DESC NULLS LAST, psv.coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST
WITH RbacSharedIpVersionStats AS (SELECT n.ACTION, AVG(s.IP_VERSION) AS avg_ip_version, VAR_SAMP(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_variance, STDDEV(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_stddev FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY n.ACTION ORDER BY ip_version_variance DESC NULLS LAST), RbacDhcpNonSharedStddev AS (SELECT n.ACTION, STDDEV(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_stddev FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.ENABLE_DHCP = 1 AND NOT s.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY n.ACTION HAVING COUNT(*) > 5 ORDER BY ip_version_stddev DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT rs.ACTION, rs.avg_ip_version, rs.ip_version_variance, rs.ip_version_stddev AS shared_ip_version_stddev, rd.ip_version_stddev AS dhcp_nonshared_ip_version_stddev FROM RbacSharedIpVersionStats AS rs JOIN RbacDhcpNonSharedStddev AS rd ON rs.ACTION = rd.ACTION
WITH SecurityGroupBoundPorts AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS min_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS spb ON p.ID = spb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), OvsBoundPorts AS (SELECT p.STATUS, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) AS min_admin_state_up, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS avg_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE b.VIF_TYPE = 'ovs' AND p.DEVICE_ID IN ('da811c75-0f9b-462c-b020-8ea55f6ca7c7') GROUP BY p.STATUS), CombinedPortStatusMetrics AS (SELECT sg.port_status AS status, sg.device_owner AS device_owner, sg.port_count AS port_count, sg.min_standard_attr_id AS min_standard_attr_id, NULL AS min_admin_state_up, NULL AS avg_standard_attr_id, 'Security Group Bound Ports' AS reason FROM SecurityGroupBoundPorts AS sg UNION ALL SELECT ov.STATUS AS status, NULL AS device_owner, NULL AS port_count, NULL AS min_standard_attr_id, ov.min_admin_state_up AS min_admin_state_up, ov.avg_standard_attr_id AS avg_standard_attr_id, 'OVS VIF Bound Ports' AS reason FROM OvsBoundPorts AS ov) SELECT * FROM CombinedPortStatusMetrics ORDER BY status NULLS FIRST, device_owner NULLS FIRST, reason NULLS FIRST
WITH subq1 AS (SELECT n.ACTION, STDDEV(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_stddev FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.ENABLE_DHCP = 1 AND NOT s.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY n.ACTION HAVING COUNT(*) > 5 ORDER BY ip_version_stddev DESC NULLS LAST), subq2 AS (SELECT n.ACTION AS rbac_action, AVG(s.IP_VERSION) AS avg_ip_version, MAX(s.IP_VERSION) - MIN(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_range, VAR_SAMP(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_variance FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION IN (4, 6) AND n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY n.ACTION ORDER BY ip_version_range DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT subq1.ACTION AS action, subq1.ip_version_stddev, subq2.avg_ip_version, subq2.ip_version_range, subq2.ip_version_variance FROM subq1 JOIN subq2 ON subq1.ACTION = subq2.rbac_action ORDER BY subq1.ip_version_stddev DESC NULLS LAST
WITH TenantUpPortsWithIPs AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(ia.ip_address) AS ip_allocation_count, SUM(CASE WHEN NOT ia.subnet_id IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS subnet_id_rows FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON ia.port_id = p.id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND NOT ia.ip_address IS NULL AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address), DownPortsOnNetworkWithSG AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (ORDER BY p.ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.NETWORK_ID IN ('0a1d0a27-cffa-4de3-92c5-9d3fd3f2e74d')), TenantUpPortsWithIPsOnNetwork AS (SELECT t.port_id, t.tenant_id, t.mac_address, t.ip_allocation_count, t.subnet_id_rows FROM TenantUpPortsWithIPs AS t JOIN PORTS AS p ON p.ID = t.port_id WHERE p.NETWORK_ID IN ('0a1d0a27-cffa-4de3-92c5-9d3fd3f2e74d')) SELECT d.NETWORK_ID, COUNT(DISTINCT d.NAME) AS down_port_name_count, COUNT(DISTINCT d.SECURITY_GROUP_ID) AS down_port_security_group_count, COUNT(DISTINCT t.port_id) AS tenant_up_port_count_with_ips, SUM(t.ip_allocation_count) AS tenant_up_total_ip_allocations, SUM(t.subnet_id_rows) AS tenant_up_total_subnet_id_rows FROM DownPortsOnNetworkWithSG AS d LEFT JOIN TenantUpPortsWithIPsOnNetwork AS t ON t.mac_address = d.MAC_ADDRESS GROUP BY d.NETWORK_ID
WITH active_networks AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE' AND n.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), admin_up_ports_with_ip_allocs AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(ia.ip_address) AS ip_allocation_count, SUM(CASE WHEN NOT ia.subnet_id IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS subnet_id_rows FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON ia.port_id = p.id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND NOT ia.ip_address IS NULL AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address), tenant_port_ip_summary AS (SELECT p.tenant_id, COUNT(*) AS admin_up_port_count, SUM(p.ip_allocation_count) AS total_ip_allocations, SUM(p.subnet_id_rows) AS total_subnet_id_rows FROM admin_up_ports_with_ip_allocs AS p GROUP BY p.tenant_id) SELECT an.tenant_id, COUNT(an.network_id) AS active_network_count, MAX(an.standard_attr_updated_at) AS latest_network_standard_attr_updated_at, tps.admin_up_port_count, tps.total_ip_allocations, tps.total_subnet_id_rows FROM active_networks AS an JOIN tenant_port_ip_summary AS tps ON tps.tenant_id = an.tenant_id WHERE an.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY an.tenant_id, tps.admin_up_port_count, tps.total_ip_allocations, tps.total_subnet_id_rows
WITH ActiveTenantNetworks AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE' AND n.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), AdminUpTenantPorts AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT n.network_id, n.network_name, n.tenant_id, n.admin_state_up AS network_admin_state_up, n.mtu, n.standard_attr_updated_at AS network_standard_attr_updated_at, n.window_column AS network_row_number_within_tenant, p.PORT_ID AS port_id, p.HOST AS port_host, p.VIF_TYPE AS port_vif_type, p.VNIC_TYPE AS port_vnic_type, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS port_mac_address, p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS port_device_owner, p.window_column AS port_avg_standard_attr_id_window FROM ActiveTenantNetworks AS n JOIN AdminUpTenantPorts AS p ON p.NETWORK_ID = n.network_id AND p.TENANT_ID = n.tenant_id ORDER BY n.network_name NULLS FIRST, p.PORT_ID NULLS FIRST
WITH port_status_device_owner_summary AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS min_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS spb ON p.ID = spb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), port_status_binding_stats AS (SELECT p.status, MAX(ps.binding_count) - MIN(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_range, VAR_SAMP(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_variance, STDDEV(ps.binding_count) AS binding_count_stddev FROM ports AS p JOIN (SELECT p2.id AS port_id, COUNT(*) AS binding_count FROM ports AS p2 JOIN portsecuritybindings AS b ON p2.id = b.port_id WHERE p2.tenant_id IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p2.id) AS ps ON p.id = ps.port_id GROUP BY p.status ORDER BY binding_count_range DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT psdo.port_status, psdo.device_owner, psdo.port_count, psdo.min_standard_attr_id, psbs.binding_count_range, psbs.binding_count_variance, psbs.binding_count_stddev FROM port_status_device_owner_summary AS psdo JOIN port_status_binding_stats AS psbs ON psdo.port_status = psbs.status
WITH PortCountsAndEarliestUpdate AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' AND NOT p.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), PortStatusIpLengthVariation AS (SELECT p.status, CAST(STDDEV(LENGTH(ia.ip_address)) AS DOUBLE PRECISION) / NULLIF(NULLIF(AVG(LENGTH(ia.ip_address)), 0), 0) AS coefficient_of_variation FROM portsecuritybindings AS psb JOIN ports AS p ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON p.id = ia.port_id WHERE psb.port_security_enabled = 1 AND ia.ip_address IN ('10.150.64.113/8') GROUP BY p.status HAVING AVG(LENGTH(ia.ip_address)) > 0 ORDER BY coefficient_of_variation DESC NULLS LAST), CombinedPortStatusMetrics AS (SELECT pc.port_status AS port_status, pc.device_owner AS device_owner, pc.port_count AS port_count, pc.earliest_standard_attr_update AS earliest_standard_attr_update, pv.coefficient_of_variation AS coefficient_of_variation FROM PortCountsAndEarliestUpdate AS pc JOIN PortStatusIpLengthVariation AS pv ON pv.status = pc.port_status) SELECT port_status, device_owner, port_count, earliest_standard_attr_update, coefficient_of_variation FROM CombinedPortStatusMetrics ORDER BY port_status NULLS FIRST, device_owner NULLS FIRST
WITH SubnetIpStddevByAction AS (SELECT n.ACTION, STDDEV(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_stddev FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.ENABLE_DHCP = 1 AND NOT s.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY n.ACTION HAVING COUNT(*) > 5 ORDER BY ip_version_stddev DESC NULLS LAST), SubnetIpVarianceForPublicShared AS (SELECT n.ACTION, VAR_SAMP(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_variance FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' AND n.TARGET_TENANT = '*' GROUP BY n.ACTION ORDER BY ip_version_variance DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT s1.ACTION, s1.ip_version_stddev, s2.ip_version_variance FROM SubnetIpStddevByAction AS s1 JOIN SubnetIpVarianceForPublicShared AS s2 ON s1.ACTION = s2.ACTION ORDER BY s1.ip_version_stddev DESC NULLS LAST
WITH subnet_details AS (SELECT s.NAME, s.TENANT_ID, s.NETWORK_ID, s.IP_VERSION, s.CIDR, s.GATEWAY_IP, s.ENABLE_DHCP, p.FIRST_IP, p.LAST_IP, MAX(s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (ORDER BY s.ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_standard_attr_id_next2 FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN IPALLOCATIONPOOLS AS p ON s.ID = p.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND s.ID IN ('012993ae-5d73-4aad-834b-e87a31e99d48')), subnet_routes AS (SELECT r.DESTINATION, r.NEXTHOP, s.NAME AS subnet_name, s.CIDR AS subnet_cidr, p.FIRST_IP, p.LAST_IP, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY r.SUBNET_ID ORDER BY r.SUBNET_ID NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM SUBNETROUTES AS r JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON r.SUBNET_ID = s.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONPOOLS AS p ON s.ID = p.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND s.ID IN ('012993ae-5d73-4aad-834b-e87a31e99d48')) SELECT sd.NAME, sd.TENANT_ID, sd.NETWORK_ID, sd.IP_VERSION, sd.CIDR, sd.GATEWAY_IP, sd.ENABLE_DHCP, sd.FIRST_IP AS allocation_first_ip, sd.LAST_IP AS allocation_last_ip, sd.max_standard_attr_id_next2, sr.DESTINATION AS route_destination, sr.NEXTHOP AS route_nexthop, sr.window_column AS route_dense_rank_within_subnet FROM subnet_details AS sd JOIN subnet_routes AS sr ON sd.NAME = sr.subnet_name AND sd.CIDR = sr.subnet_cidr AND sd.FIRST_IP = sr.FIRST_IP AND sd.LAST_IP = sr.LAST_IP
WITH sq1 AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' AND NOT p.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), sq2 AS (SELECT p.STATUS, VAR_SAMP(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS standard_attr_id_variance FROM PORTS AS p JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE NOT p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID IS NULL AND ia.IP_ADDRESS IN ('10.150.64.113/8') GROUP BY p.STATUS ORDER BY standard_attr_id_variance DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT sq1.port_status, sq1.device_owner, sq1.port_count, sq1.earliest_standard_attr_update, sq2.standard_attr_id_variance FROM sq1 JOIN sq2 ON sq1.port_status = sq2.STATUS ORDER BY sq1.port_count DESC NULLS LAST, sq2.standard_attr_id_variance DESC NULLS LAST
WITH Subq1 AS (SELECT n.ACTION, AVG(s.IP_VERSION) AS avg_ip_version, VAR_SAMP(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_variance, STDDEV(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_stddev FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY n.ACTION ORDER BY ip_version_variance DESC NULLS LAST), Subq2 AS (SELECT n.ACTION AS rbac_action, AVG(s.IP_VERSION) AS avg_ip_version, MAX(s.IP_VERSION) - MIN(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_range, VAR_SAMP(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_variance FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION IN (4, 6) AND n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY n.ACTION ORDER BY ip_version_range DESC NULLS LAST) SELECT s1.ACTION AS rbac_action, s1.avg_ip_version AS avg_ip_version_ipv4_only, s1.ip_version_variance AS ip_version_variance_ipv4_only, s1.ip_version_stddev AS ip_version_stddev_ipv4_only, s2.avg_ip_version AS avg_ip_version_ipv4_or_ipv6, s2.ip_version_range AS ip_version_range_ipv4_or_ipv6, s2.ip_version_variance AS ip_version_variance_ipv4_or_ipv6 FROM Subq1 AS s1 JOIN Subq2 AS s2 ON s1.ACTION = s2.rbac_action
WITH RbacActionIpVersionStddev AS (SELECT n.ACTION, STDDEV(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_stddev FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.ENABLE_DHCP = 1 AND NOT s.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY n.ACTION HAVING COUNT(*) > 5 ORDER BY ip_version_stddev DESC NULLS LAST), ActiveHighMtuNetworksByRbacAction AS (SELECT n.name AS network_name, r.action AS rbac_action, MAX(n.mtu) AS max_mtu FROM NETWORKSECURITYBINDINGS AS nsb JOIN NETWORKS AS n ON nsb.network_id = n.id JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r ON n.id = r.object_id WHERE n.status = 'ACTIVE' AND r.action IN ('access_as_shared', 'access_as_external') GROUP BY n.name, r.action HAVING MAX(n.mtu) > 8000) SELECT ahmn.network_name, ahmn.rbac_action, ahmn.max_mtu, raivs.ip_version_stddev FROM ActiveHighMtuNetworksByRbacAction AS ahmn JOIN RbacActionIpVersionStddev AS raivs ON ahmn.rbac_action = raivs.ACTION ORDER BY ahmn.max_mtu DESC NULLS LAST, raivs.ip_version_stddev DESC NULLS LAST
WITH RbacSubnetIpVersionStats AS (SELECT n.ACTION AS rbac_action, AVG(s.IP_VERSION) AS avg_ip_version, MAX(s.IP_VERSION) - MIN(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_range, VAR_SAMP(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_variance FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION IN (4, 6) AND n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY n.ACTION ORDER BY ip_version_range DESC NULLS LAST), ActiveNetworksRbacDetails AS (SELECT n.name, n.status, n.admin_state_up, r.action, r.target_tenant, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY n.id ORDER BY n.id NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM networksecuritybindings AS b JOIN networks AS n ON b.network_id = n.id JOIN networkrbacs AS r ON n.id = r.object_id WHERE n.status = 'ACTIVE' AND NOT n.id IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b'))), CombinedResults AS (SELECT a.name, a.status, a.admin_state_up, a.action, a.target_tenant, a.window_column, s.rbac_action, s.avg_ip_version, s.ip_version_range, s.ip_version_variance FROM ActiveNetworksRbacDetails AS a JOIN RbacSubnetIpVersionStats AS s ON a.action = s.rbac_action) SELECT * FROM CombinedResults ORDER BY name NULLS FIRST, target_tenant NULLS FIRST
WITH PortSecurityAndDns AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.name AS port_name, COUNT(sgpb.security_group_id) AS security_group_binding_count, SUM(CASE WHEN pd.port_id IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END) AS dns_record_count FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id LEFT JOIN PORTDNSES AS pd ON p.id = pd.port_id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.name), PortBindingWindow AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID NULLS FIRST ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT pbw.PORT_ID, pbw.TENANT_ID, psd.port_name, pbw.HOST, pbw.VIF_TYPE, pbw.VNIC_TYPE, pbw.NETWORK_ID, pbw.MAC_ADDRESS, pbw.STATUS, pbw.DEVICE_OWNER, pbw.window_column, psd.security_group_binding_count, psd.dns_record_count FROM PortBindingWindow AS pbw JOIN PortSecurityAndDns AS psd ON pbw.PORT_ID = psd.port_id AND pbw.TENANT_ID = psd.tenant_id
WITH PortStatusOwnerAgg AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' AND NOT p.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), ActiveNetworkRbacDetails AS (SELECT n.name, n.status, n.admin_state_up, r.action, r.target_tenant, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY n.id ORDER BY n.id NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM networksecuritybindings AS b JOIN networks AS n ON b.network_id = n.id JOIN networkrbacs AS r ON n.id = r.object_id WHERE n.status = 'ACTIVE' AND NOT n.id IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b'))), PortStatusOwnerAggForActiveNetworks AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' AND p.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT DISTINCT r.object_id FROM networkrbacs AS r JOIN networks AS n ON n.id = r.object_id JOIN networksecuritybindings AS b ON b.network_id = n.id WHERE n.status = 'ACTIVE' AND NOT n.id IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b'))) AND NOT p.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER) SELECT d.name AS network_name, d.status AS network_status, d.admin_state_up, d.action AS rbac_action, d.target_tenant, d.window_column, a.port_status, a.device_owner, a.port_count, a.earliest_standard_attr_update FROM ActiveNetworkRbacDetails AS d CROSS JOIN PortStatusOwnerAggForActiveNetworks AS a ORDER BY d.name NULLS FIRST, d.action NULLS FIRST, d.target_tenant NULLS FIRST, a.port_status NULLS FIRST, a.device_owner NULLS FIRST
WITH RbacActionIpVersionStddev AS (SELECT n.ACTION, STDDEV(s.IP_VERSION) AS ip_version_stddev FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.ENABLE_DHCP = 1 AND NOT s.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY n.ACTION HAVING COUNT(*) > 5 ORDER BY ip_version_stddev DESC NULLS LAST), ActiveNetworkRbacDetails AS (SELECT n.name, n.status, n.admin_state_up, r.action, r.target_tenant, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY n.id ORDER BY n.id NULLS FIRST) AS window_column FROM networksecuritybindings AS b JOIN networks AS n ON b.network_id = n.id JOIN networkrbacs AS r ON n.id = r.object_id WHERE n.status = 'ACTIVE' AND NOT n.id IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b'))) SELECT anrd.name, anrd.status, anrd.admin_state_up, anrd.action, anrd.target_tenant, anrd.window_column, raivs.ip_version_stddev FROM ActiveNetworkRbacDetails AS anrd JOIN RbacActionIpVersionStddev AS raivs ON anrd.action = raivs.ACTION ORDER BY raivs.ip_version_stddev DESC NULLS LAST, anrd.name NULLS FIRST
